================================================
FILE: install.sh
================================================
#!/usr/bin/env sh
# =====================================================================
# Spid3rWeb — installer & project bootstrap
# =====================================================================
# Auto-detects the OS and installs, in order:
#   1. The pinned Spid3r language toolchain (verified with SHA-256)
#   2. This framework project: .env (0600), APP_KEY, storage/ perms, migrations
#
# Never invokes sudo or system package managers automatically — if a
# prerequisite downloader is missing, exact manual commands are printed.
#
# Usage:
#   sh install.sh            # install language (if needed) + set up project
#   sh install.sh --serve    # ...then start the dev server on :8000
#   SPID3R_PORT=3000 sh install.sh --serve
# =====================================================================
set -eu

# ---------- pretty output ----------
if [ -t 1 ]; then
  BOLD="\033[1m"; GREEN="\033[32m"; YELLOW="\033[33m"; RED="\033[31m"; CYAN="\033[36m"; OFF="\033[0m"
else
  BOLD=""; GREEN=""; YELLOW=""; RED=""; CYAN=""; OFF=""
fi
say()  { printf "%b\n" "${GREEN}==>${OFF} ${BOLD}$*${OFF}"; }
warn() { printf "%b\n" "${YELLOW}warning:${OFF} $*"; }
die()  { printf "%b\n" "${RED}error:${OFF} $*" >&2; exit 1; }

# ---------- pinned release constants ----------
SPID3R_REPO="needyamin/spid3r"
SPID3R_VERSION="${SPID3R_VERSION:-1.1.1}"
SPID3R_SHA256="${SPID3R_SHA256:-}"
RELEASE_BASE="https://github.com/$SPID3R_REPO/releases/download/v${SPID3R_VERSION}"
APP_PORT="${SPID3R_PORT:-8000}"

SCRIPT_DIR=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)

# =====================================================================
# Platform detection
# =====================================================================
detect_os() {
  UNAME_S=$(uname -s)
  case "$UNAME_S" in
    MINGW*|MSYS*|CYGWIN*) PLATFORM="windows" ;;
    Darwin)               PLATFORM="macos" ;;
    Linux)                PLATFORM="linux" ;;
    *)                    PLATFORM="unix" ;;
  esac
  case "$(uname -m)" in
    arm64|aarch64) ARCH="aarch64" ;;
    *)             ARCH="x86_64" ;;
  esac
}

release_target() {
  case "${PLATFORM}:${ARCH}" in
    linux:x86_64)   printf '%s' "x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu" ;;
    linux:aarch64)  printf '%s' "aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu" ;;
    macos:x86_64)   printf '%s' "x86_64-apple-darwin" ;;
    macos:aarch64)  printf '%s' "aarch64-apple-darwin" ;;
    *)              return 1 ;;
  esac
}

# =====================================================================
# Download & checksum helpers (never auto-sudo)
# =====================================================================
have_downloader() {
  command -v curl >/dev/null 2>&1 || command -v wget >/dev/null 2>&1 || [ "$PLATFORM" = "windows" ]
}

fetch() {
  _url="$1"
  _out="$2"
  if command -v curl >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    curl -fsSL "$_url" -o "$_out"
  elif command -v wget >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    wget -qO "$_out" "$_url"
  elif [ "$PLATFORM" = "windows" ]; then
    powershell.exe -NoProfile -Command "Invoke-WebRequest -UseBasicParsing -Uri '$_url' -OutFile '$_out'"
  else
    return 1
  fi
}

verify_sha256() {
  _file="$1"
  _expected="$2"
  if command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    printf '%s  %s\n' "$_expected" "$_file" | sha256sum -c - >/dev/null
  elif command -v shasum >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    printf '%s  %s\n' "$_expected" "$_file" | shasum -a 256 -c - >/dev/null
  else
    die "neither sha256sum nor shasum found — cannot verify release integrity"
  fi
}

ensure_downloader() {
  if have_downloader; then
    return 0
  fi
  warn "Neither 'curl' nor 'wget' was found."
  printf "Install one manually, then re-run this script:\n"
  printf "  Debian/Ubuntu : sudo apt-get update && sudo apt-get install -y curl ca-certificates\n"
  printf "  Fedora/RHEL   : sudo dnf install -y curl\n"
  printf "  Alpine        : sudo apk add curl\n"
  printf "  Arch          : sudo pacman -S curl\n"
  printf "  macOS         : brew install curl\n"
  die "missing required downloader (curl or wget)"
}

# =====================================================================
# PATH helpers
# =====================================================================
path_has() { case ":$PATH:" in *":$1:"*) return 0 ;; *) return 1 ;; esac; }

add_path_dir() {
  [ -d "$1" ] || return 0
  path_has "$1" && return 0
  PATH="$PATH:$1"
  export PATH
}

fix_session_path() {
  add_path_dir "$HOME/.spid3r/bin"
  add_path_dir "$HOME/.local/bin"
  add_path_dir "/usr/local/bin"
  if [ -n "${LOCALAPPDATA:-}" ]; then
    add_path_dir "$LOCALAPPDATA/Programs/Spid3r"
  fi
}

path_hint() {
  printf "\n"
  warn "spid3r was installed into $HOME/.spid3r/bin, which is not on PATH yet."
  warn "Open a new terminal or run:"
  warn "  export PATH=\"\$PATH:\$HOME/.spid3r/bin\""
}

spid3r_ok() {
  command -v spid3r >/dev/null 2>&1 && spid3r --version >/dev/null 2>&1
}

# =====================================================================
# Step 1 — Pinned Spid3r language runtime (SHA-256 verified)
# =====================================================================
install_language() {
  fix_session_path
  if spid3r_ok; then
    say "Spid3r is already installed: $(spid3r --version 2>&1 | grep -i Version | head -n 1 || echo ok)"
    return 0
  fi

  say "Spid3r runtime not found — installing pinned v${SPID3R_VERSION} for ${PLATFORM} (${ARCH})..."
  ensure_downloader

  TMP=$(mktemp -d)
  if [ "$PLATFORM" = "windows" ]; then
    ASSET="Spid3r-Setup-${SPID3R_VERSION}.exe"
    say "Downloading ${ASSET} ..."
    fetch "${RELEASE_BASE}/${ASSET}" "${TMP}/${ASSET}" || die "could not download ${RELEASE_BASE}/${ASSET}"
    if [ -n "$SPID3R_SHA256" ]; then
      verify_sha256 "${TMP}/${ASSET}" "$SPID3R_SHA256"
    elif fetch "${RELEASE_BASE}/sha256sums.txt" "${TMP}/sha256sums.txt" 2>/dev/null; then
      EXPECTED=$(awk -v a="${ASSET}" '$2 == a || $2 == "*" a {print $1; exit}' "${TMP}/sha256sums.txt")
      [ -n "$EXPECTED" ] && verify_sha256 "${TMP}/${ASSET}" "$EXPECTED"
    fi
    say "Running installer (silent mode)..."
    if ! (cd "$TMP" && "./${ASSET}" /S); then
      warn "silent install failed — launching interactive installer."
      (cd "$TMP" && "./${ASSET}") || true
    fi
  else
    TARGET=$(release_target) || die "unsupported platform ${PLATFORM}/${ARCH} for prebuilt tarball"
    ASSET="spid3r-${TARGET}.tar.gz"
    say "Downloading ${ASSET} ..."
    fetch "${RELEASE_BASE}/${ASSET}" "${TMP}/${ASSET}" || die "could not download ${RELEASE_BASE}/${ASSET}"
    if [ -n "$SPID3R_SHA256" ]; then
      EXPECTED="$SPID3R_SHA256"
    else
      fetch "${RELEASE_BASE}/sha256sums.txt" "${TMP}/sha256sums.txt" || die "could not download ${RELEASE_BASE}/sha256sums.txt"
      EXPECTED=$(awk -v a="${ASSET}" '$2 == a || $2 == "*" a {print $1; exit}' "${TMP}/sha256sums.txt")
    fi
    [ -n "$EXPECTED" ] || die "empty SHA-256 checksum for ${ASSET}"
    say "Verifying SHA-256 checksum..."
    verify_sha256 "${TMP}/${ASSET}" "$EXPECTED"

    tar -xzf "${TMP}/${ASSET}" -C "$TMP"
    mkdir -p "$HOME/.spid3r/bin"
    BIN_SRC=$(find "$TMP" -type f -name spid3r | head -n 1 || true)
    [ -n "$BIN_SRC" ] || die "archive did not contain a 'spid3r' binary"
    cp "$BIN_SRC" "$HOME/.spid3r/bin/spid3r"
    chmod 0755 "$HOME/.spid3r/bin/spid3r"
    SELF_SRC=$(find "$TMP" -type f -name spid3r-self | head -n 1 || true)
    if [ -n "$SELF_SRC" ] && [ -f "$SELF_SRC" ]; then
      cp "$SELF_SRC" "$HOME/.spid3r/bin/spid3r-self"
      chmod 0755 "$HOME/.spid3r/bin/spid3r-self"
    fi
  fi
  rm -rf "$TMP"

  fix_session_path
  if spid3r_ok; then
    say "Spid3r installed cleanly."
  elif [ -x "$HOME/.spid3r/bin/spid3r" ]; then
    path_hint
    die "spid3r not reachable in this shell"
  else
    die "spid3r still not found after installation"
  fi
}

# =====================================================================
# Step 2 — Spid3rWeb project setup (.env 0600, APP_KEY, dirs, migrate)
# =====================================================================
setup_framework() {
  say "Setting up Spid3rWeb in $SCRIPT_DIR ..."
  cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"

  [ -f "server.matrix" ]       || die "server.matrix not found — run this script from the project root"
  [ -f ".env.example" ]        || die ".env.example not found — run this script from the project root"
  [ -d "database/migrations" ] || die "database/migrations not found — incomplete project?"

  if [ -f ".env" ]; then
    say ".env already exists — keeping existing configuration"
  else
    cp .env.example .env
    chmod 0600 .env
    say "Created .env (mode 0600) from .env.example"
  fi

  if [ -f ".env" ]; then
    chmod 0600 .env
    if ! grep -qE '^APP_KEY=.{16,}' .env; then
      say "Generating 256-bit APP_KEY..."
      spid3r magic key:generate
      chmod 0600 .env
    fi
  fi

  mkdir -p storage/logs storage/framework/cache storage/app storage/backups storage/database database
  chmod 0700 storage storage/logs storage/framework storage/framework/cache storage/app storage/backups storage/database database

  say "Running database migrations ..."
  spid3r magic migrate

  say "Running seeder ..."
  spid3r magic db:seed
}

# =====================================================================
# Main
# =====================================================================
detect_os
printf "%b\n" "${CYAN}${BOLD}"
printf "  ____  _     _              \n"
printf " / ___|(_) __| | ___ _ __    \n"
printf " \\___ \\| |/ _\` |/ _ \\ '__|  \n"
printf "  ___) | | (_| |  __/ |      \n"
printf " |____/|_|\\__,_|\\___|_|  web\n"
printf '%b' "$OFF"
printf "installer — %s (%s) detected\n\n" "$PLATFORM" "$ARCH"

install_language
setup_framework

printf "\n"
say "Spid3rWeb is ready!"
printf "  Health check : %bhttp://127.0.0.1:%s/health%b\n" "$BOLD" "$APP_PORT" "$OFF"
printf "  Diagnostics  : %bspid3r magic doctor%b\n\n" "$BOLD" "$OFF"

if [ "${1:-}" = "--serve" ]; then
  say "Starting dev server on http://127.0.0.1:$APP_PORT ..."
  exec spid3r magic serve --port "$APP_PORT"
fi

printf "Start the app:\n"
printf "  %bspid3r magic serve --port %s%b   then open http://127.0.0.1:%s\n" "$BOLD" "$APP_PORT" "$OFF" "$APP_PORT"
printf "  (or run %bsh install.sh --serve%b to start it automatically)\n" "$BOLD" "$OFF"

// magic
// Spid3rWeb Magic Command-Line Tool

import "framework/Console/ConsoleKernel";
import "bootstrap/app";
import "database/seeders/DatabaseSeeder";

fn main() {
    let app = create_application();
    let kernel = new ConsoleKernel(app);

    kernel.register_seeder(fn() {
        run_database_seeder();
    });

    return kernel.handle(args());
}



================================================
FILE: server.matrix
================================================
// server.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Production & Development HTTP/HTTPS Application Server
// Works both via `spid3r run` and as a compiled native AOT executable (`dist/server.exe`).

import "bootstrap/app";

fn server_dirname(path: string) -> string {
    let parts = string(path).replace("\\", "/").split("/");
    if parts.len() <= 1 {
        return "";
    }
    parts.pop();
    return parts.join("/");
}

fn has_project_markers(dir: string) -> bool {
    let p = string(dir).replace("\\", "/");
    if p == "" or p == "." {
        return file_exists("resources/views") or file_exists("spider.toml");
    }
    return file_exists(p + "/resources/views") or file_exists(p + "/spider.toml");
}

fn resolve_and_enter_project_root(explicit_root: string) {
    if explicit_root != "" {
        let cr = chdir(explicit_root);
        if cr.is_err() {
            let err = string(cr.unwrap_err());
            throw "server: cannot chdir to --root '{explicit_root}': {err}";
        }
        return;
    }
    // Already in project root (e.g. `.\dist\server.exe` from repo root)
    if has_project_markers(".") {
        return;
    }
    // Walk up from the executable's directory (e.g. double-clicking `dist/server.exe`
    // or running `cd dist && .\server.exe`).
    let exe_r = exe_path();
    if exe_r.is_ok() {
        let dir = server_dirname(string(exe_r.unwrap()));
        let depth = 0;
        while dir != "" and depth < 5 {
            if has_project_markers(dir) {
                let cr = chdir(dir);
                if cr.is_ok() {
                    return;
                }
            }
            let parent = server_dirname(dir);
            if parent == dir {
                break;
            }
            dir = parent;
            depth = depth + 1;
        }
    }
}

fn print_server_help() {
    println("Spid3rWeb Application Server");
    println("Usage: server [port] [options]");
    println("  --port N          Port to listen on (default: 8000)");
    println("  --host ADDR       Bind address (default: SPID3R_HOST or 127.0.0.1)");
    println("  --tls             Enable native HTTPS (requires --cert and --key)");
    println("  --cert PATH       TLS certificate path");
    println("  --key PATH        TLS private key path");
    println("  --root DIR        Explicit project root directory");
    println("  --help, -h        Show this help message");
}

fn main() {
    let port = 8000;
    let host = "";
    let use_tls = false;
    let cert_path = "";
    let key_path = "";
    let explicit_root = "";

    let cli_args = args();
    let i = 0;
    while i < cli_args.len() {
        let a = string(cli_args[i]);
        if a == "--help" or a == "-h" {
            print_server_help();
            return 0;
        } elif a.starts_with("--port=") {
            port = int(a.slice(7, a.len()));
        } elif a == "--port" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
            i = i + 1;
            port = int(cli_args[i]);
        } elif a.starts_with("--host=") {
            host = a.slice(7, a.len());
        } elif a == "--host" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
            i = i + 1;
            host = string(cli_args[i]);
        } elif a == "--tls" {
            use_tls = true;
        } elif a.starts_with("--cert=") {
            cert_path = a.slice(7, a.len());
            use_tls = true;
        } elif a == "--cert" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
            i = i + 1;
            cert_path = string(cli_args[i]);
            use_tls = true;
        } elif a.starts_with("--key=") {
            key_path = a.slice(6, a.len());
            use_tls = true;
        } elif a == "--key" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
            i = i + 1;
            key_path = string(cli_args[i]);
            use_tls = true;
        } elif a.starts_with("--root=") {
            explicit_root = a.slice(7, a.len());
        } elif a == "--root" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
            i = i + 1;
            explicit_root = string(cli_args[i]);
        } elif not a.starts_with("-") {
            let parsed = 0;
            try {
                parsed = int(a);
            } catch (_e) {
                println("[Spid3rWeb] Invalid port argument '" + a + "', falling back to 8000");
                parsed = 0;
            }
            if parsed > 0 {
                port = parsed;
            }
        }
        i = i + 1;
    }

    if use_tls and (cert_path == "" or key_path == "") {
        println("[Spid3rWeb] Error: --tls requires both --cert <path> and --key <path>");
        return 1;
    }

    // 1. Resolve project root so dist/server.exe works from any working directory
    resolve_and_enter_project_root(explicit_root);

    let root_dir = ".";
    let cwd_r = cwd();
    if cwd_r.is_ok() {
        root_dir = string(cwd_r.unwrap());
    }

    println("==================================================================");
    println("                  Spid3rWeb Application Server                   ");
    println("==================================================================");
    println("[Spid3rWeb] Project root: " + root_dir);

    // 2. Create application, register service providers, routes, and jobs
    let app = create_application();

    // 4. Start HTTP or HTTPS Server
    if use_tls {
        app.listen_tls(port, cert_path, key_path, host);
    } else {
        app.listen(port, host);
    }
    return 0;
}

# Spid3rWeb production runtime guardrails for Spid3r v1.1.1+
# Copy to `spid3r.ini` in the project root (or manage live via `spid3r-control`).
# Every key below is verified against `spid3r config list` / `spid3r config validate`.

[spid3r]
# Bytecode VM is the default engine in v1.1.1+ ("vm" | "tree")
engine = vm
# Worker thread pool ceiling and shared ready-queue cap
max_workers = 256
queue_cap = 65536
# Graceful shutdown drain window in milliseconds
task_drain_ms = 2000

[net]
# HTTP/1.1 keep-alive idle timeout in milliseconds
keepalive_ms = 5000
# Per-request read/write socket framing deadline in milliseconds
timeout_ms = 30000
# Max HTTP request body size (10 MiB; 413 above)
max_body = 10485760
# Max number of HTTP headers per request (431 above)
max_headers = 64
# Note: connection limits are bounded by spid3r.max_workers / reverse proxy.
# Database driver timeouts live in framework/Database/Connection.matrix
# (SQLite busy_timeout=5000, PostgreSQL statement_timeout=30000).

[runtime]
# Soft memory limit in MiB for the script-value heap (0 = unlimited)
memory_limit_mb = 512
# Fatal execution deadline in ms per run (0 = unlimited)
wall_clock_limit_ms = 0
timezone = UTC

[errors]
level = info
display = false

[modules]
# Ensure project-relative framework/*, config/*, and routes/* imports resolve
path = .
# Disable untrusted host/WASM dynamic plugins unless explicitly required
allow_wasm = false
allow_host = false
# Block unused network/desktop stdlib modules in production servers
disable = ssh, ftp, gui

[security]
# Open-basedir allowlist for file-taking natives (S4): confines runtime I/O
# to static assets, storage, database, templates, and config directories.
basedir = ./public, ./storage, ./database, ./resources, ./config
# For dedicated `spid3r run server.matrix` deployments (without `magic migrate`
# child spawns), also disable OS process execution natives:
; disable_natives = spawn_process, exec_capture, run_payload, run_payload_strict

# =====================================================================
# Spid3rWeb environment configuration
# =====================================================================
# Copy this file to `.env` in the project root and adjust values.
#   cp .env.example .env
#
# Precedence: real OS environment variables ALWAYS win over .env —
# the file only fills in values that are not already set.
# `.env` is git-ignored; never commit real credentials.

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Application mode & signing key
#   production (default when unset) | development | testing
#   APP_ENV=production disables debug output, static-asset revalidation,
#   and requires a 16+ char APP_KEY (or SPID3R_APP_KEY).
#   Run `spid3r magic key:generate` to populate APP_KEY.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
APP_ENV=development
APP_KEY=

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Database driver: sqlite (default) | postgres | mysql | mariadb
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
SPID3R_DB_DRIVER=sqlite

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# SQLite (default engine — runtime state kept under storage/database/)
#   Set SPID3R_STATE_DIR to relocate runtime DB/storage outside synced folders,
#   or SPID3R_DB_PATH to specify an exact SQLite file path.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
#SPID3R_STATE_DIR=storage
SPID3R_DB_PATH=storage/database/database.sqlite

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# PostgreSQL
#   Auth note: the runtime client supports md5/trust auth (not SCRAM).
#   On the server run:
#     SET password_encryption = 'md5';
#     ALTER USER app WITH PASSWORD 'secret';
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
#SPID3R_DB_DRIVER=postgres
#SPID3R_PG_HOST=127.0.0.1
#SPID3R_PG_PORT=5432
#SPID3R_PG_USER=spid3r
#SPID3R_PG_PASSWORD=secret
#SPID3R_PG_DATABASE=spid3r

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# MySQL / MariaDB (MariaDB uses the same SPID3R_MYSQL_* variables)
#   Auth note: MySQL 8 users must use mysql_native_password:
#     ALTER USER 'app'@'%' IDENTIFIED WITH mysql_native_password BY 'secret';
#   MariaDB ships mysql_native_password by default — works as-is.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
#SPID3R_DB_DRIVER=mysql
#SPID3R_MYSQL_HOST=127.0.0.1
#SPID3R_MYSQL_PORT=3306
#SPID3R_MYSQL_USER=spid3r
#SPID3R_MYSQL_PASSWORD=secret
#SPID3R_MYSQL_DATABASE=spid3r

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Session (config/session.matrix)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

#SPID3R_LOG_LEVEL=debug

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Mail (config/mail.matrix)
#   The "log" driver writes every message to storage/logs/mail.log-*.log.
#   The smtp driver needs an outbound TCP API the runtime does not expose
#   yet, so keep the log driver until then.

#SPID3R_QUEUE_CONNECTION=database

// app/Controllers/HomeController.matrix
// Default web entry controller handling /, /health, and /info.

import "framework/Http/Controller";
import "framework/Http/Response";

public class HomeController extends Controller {
    fn new () {
        let _ = self;
    }

    public fn index(self, _req: any) -> Response {
        return self.view("welcome");
    }

    public fn health(self, _req: any) -> Response {
        return self.ok({
            "status": "ok"
        });
    }

    public fn info(self, _req: any) -> Response {
        let _ = self;
        return html(string(spid3rinfo()));
    }
}

FILE: benchmarks/bench.matrix

// benchmarks/bench.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Micro-Benchmarks
//
// Measures framework hot paths with wall-clock timing. Run under each
// execution engine and compare honestly:
//
//   spid3r run benchmarks/bench.matrix                    # tree-walk interpreter
//   spid3r run --engine vm benchmarks/bench.matrix        # bytecode VM
//
// Rules: no assertions (a benchmark that flakes is a benchmark nobody
// runs); fixed iteration counts; warm-up pass before timing; only measured
// numbers get published (benchmarks/RESULTS.md).

import "framework/Http/Router";
import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Database/Schema";
import "framework/View/BD";

fn compute_rate(ops: int, ms: int) -> int {
    if ms < 1 {
        return ops * 1_000;
    }
    return int(ops * 1_000 / ms);
}

fn fmt_rate(ops: int, ms: int) -> string {
    if ms < 1 {
        return string(ops) + " ops (<1 ms)";
    }
    let rate = int(ops * 1_000 / ms);
    return string(rate) + " ops/sec";
}

fn time_block(iterations: int, body: any) -> int {
    // Warm-up (JIT-free, but primes caches and the template loader).
    body();
    let start = now_ms();
    let i = 0;
    while i < iterations {
        body();
        i += 1;
    }
    return now_ms() - start;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn bench_route_matching() -> int {
    let r = new Router();
    let i = 0;
    while i < 200 {
        r.get("/static/path/{i}", fn(_req) {
            return null;
        });
        r.get("/users/{i}/posts/:id", fn(_req) {
            return null;
        });
        i += 1;
    }
    let hits = {
        "n": 0
    };
    let target = "/users/128/posts/4567";
    let ms = time_block(2000, fn() {
        let m = r.match_route("GET", target);
        if m != null {
            hits["n"] = hits["n"] + 1;
        }
    });
    let rate = fmt_rate(2000, ms);
    println(
        "route matching (200 routes, param capture) : {rate}  ({ms} ms)"
    );
    return compute_rate(2000, ms);
}

fn bench_query_compilation() -> int {
    let ms = time_block(3000, fn() {
        let _sql = DB.table("bench_items")
        .where("status", "=", "active")
        .where("score", ">", 10)
        .whereIn("kind", ["a", "b", "c"])
        .orderBy("created_at", "DESC")
        .limit(20)
        .toSql();
    });
    let rate = fmt_rate(3000, ms);
    println(
        "query compilation (4 clauses + IN + order)  : {rate}  ({ms} ms)"
    );
    return compute_rate(3000, ms);
}

fn bench_json_roundtrip() -> int {
    let payload = {
        "id": 42,
        "name": "bench",
        "tags": ["a", "b", "c"],
        "meta": {
            "nested": true, "score": 1.5, "when": "2026-11-06"
        }
    };
    let sink = {
        "n": 0
    };
    let ms = time_block(2000, fn() {
        let parsed = json_parse(json_write(payload));
        if parsed["id"] == 42 {
            sink["n"] = sink["n"] + 1;
        }
    });
    let rate = fmt_rate(2000, ms);
    println(
        "JSON write+parse round-trip (nested)        : {rate}  ({ms} ms)"
    );
    return compute_rate(2000, ms);
}

fn bench_view_render() -> int {
    let data = {
        "title": "Benchmark",
        "items": ["one", "two", "three", "four", "five"]
    };
    // Fail fast if the template is missing — a bench of error strings is
    // worthless.
    let probe = global_bd.render("bench", data);
    if probe.contains("BD Error") {
        throw "bench_view template missing: {probe}";
    }
    let ms = time_block(2000, fn() {
        let _html = global_bd.render("bench", data);
    });
    let rate = fmt_rate(2000, ms);
    println(
        "BD template render (interpolation+loop)     : {rate}  ({ms} ms)"
    );
    return compute_rate(2000, ms);
}

fn bench_sqlite_crud() -> int {
    if DB.driver_name() != "sqlite" {
        println(
            "sqlite CRUD                                 : skipped (driver is " + DB.driver_name() + ")"
        );
        return -1;
    }
    Schema.dropIfExists("bench_items");
    Schema.create("bench_items", fn(t) {
        t.id();
        t.string("title");
        t.integer("score");
        t.string("status");
        t.timestamps();
    });

    // INSERT x 200
    let n = 200;
    let start = now_ms();
    let i = 0;
    while i < n {
        DB.table("bench_items").insert({
            "title": "item {i}",
            "score": i,
            "status": "active",
            "created_at": "2026-11-06",
            "updated_at": "2026-11-06"
        });
        i += 1;
    }
    let ins_ms = now_ms() - start;

    // SELECT (full scan with WHERE) x 300
    start = now_ms();
    i = 0;
    while i < 300 {
        let rows = DB.table("bench_items").where("score", ">", 100).get();
        if rows.len() != 99 {
            throw "bench select returned wrong row count";
        }
        i += 1;
    }
    let sel_ms = now_ms() - start;

    // UPDATE x 200
    start = now_ms();
    i = 0;
    while i < n {
        DB.table("bench_items").where("id", "=", i + 1).update({
            "status": "seen"
        });
        i += 1;
    }
    let upd_ms = now_ms() - start;

    Schema.dropIfExists("bench_items");
    let ins_rate = fmt_rate(n, ins_ms);
    let sel_rate = fmt_rate(300, sel_ms);
    let upd_rate = fmt_rate(n, upd_ms);
    println(
        "sqlite insert x{n}                          : {ins_rate}  ({ins_ms} ms)"
    );
    println(
        "sqlite select x300 (WHERE >, 200 rows)      : {sel_rate}  ({sel_ms} ms)"
    );
    println(
        "sqlite update x{n}                          : {upd_rate}  ({upd_ms} ms)"
    );
    return compute_rate(300, sel_ms);
}

fn main() -> int {
    let gate = env("SPID3R_BENCH_GATE").unwrap_or("").trim() == "1";
    for a in args() {
        if string(a) == "--gate" {
            gate = true;
        }
    }
    let epoch = now_ms();
    println("==================================================================");
    println(" Spid3rWeb micro-benchmarks — {epoch} ms epoch");
    println("==================================================================");
    let r_route = bench_route_matching();
    let r_query = bench_query_compilation();
    let r_json = bench_json_roundtrip();
    let r_view = bench_view_render();
    let r_sql = bench_sqlite_crud();
    DB.close();
    println("==================================================================");
    if gate {
        let checks = [
        ["route_matching", r_route, 20_000],
        ["query_compilation", r_query, 4_000],
        ["json_roundtrip", r_json, 60_000],
        ["view_render", r_view, 1_500]
        ];
        if r_sql >= 0 {
            checks.push(["sqlite_select", r_sql, 800]);
        }
        let gate_fails = 0;
        for c in checks {
            let label = string(c[0]);
            let actual = int(c[1]);
            let floor_val = int(c[2]);
            if actual >= floor_val {
                println("[Perf Gate] {label}: PASS ({actual} >= {floor_val} ops/sec)");
            } else {
                println("[Perf Gate] {label}: FAIL ({actual} < {floor_val} ops/sec)");
                gate_fails = gate_fails + 1;
            }
        }
        if gate_fails > 0 {
            throw "Framework perf gate failed ({gate_fails} regression(s))";
        }
        println("[Perf Gate] Passed against benchmarks/RESULTS.md floors.");
    }
    return 0;
}



================================================
FILE: benchmarks/RESULTS.md
================================================
# Spid3rWeb Benchmark Results

**Date:** 2026-11-20 · **Toolchain:** Spid3r `v1.1.1` (Language `1.1.1`, Bytecode VM default + LLVM 23.1.0 AOT) · **Machine:** Windows 11, local NVMe (developer desktop; absolute numbers are host-specific, compare ratios) · **Method:** `benchmarks/bench.matrix` — fixed iterations, warm-up pass, wall-clock `now_ms()`. SQLite (file) driver.

## Results (v1.1.1 vs 2026-11-07 v1.0.0 Baseline)

| Benchmark | 2026-11-07 Tree (old default) | 2026-11-07 VM | v1.1.1 VM (default) | v1.1.1 LLVM-23 AOT (`--release`) | AOT vs old default |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Route matching (200 routes, `:param` capture) | 55,555 ops/sec | 133,333 ops/sec | **153,846 ops/sec** (13 ms) | **200,000 ops/sec** (10 ms) | **3.60×** |
| Query compilation (4 clauses + whereIn + orderBy) | 13,888 ops/sec | 20,547 ops/sec | **21,428 ops/sec** (140 ms) | **27,027 ops/sec** (111 ms) | **1.95×** |
| JSON write+parse round-trip (nested) | 250,000 ops/sec | 333,333 ops/sec | **666,666 ops/sec** (3 ms) | **1,000,000 ops/sec** (2 ms) | **4.00×** |
| BD template render (interpolation + loop) | 6,153 ops/sec | 9,569 ops/sec | **9,661 ops/sec** (207 ms) | **12,500 ops/sec** (160 ms) | **2.03×** |
| SQLite INSERT ×200 (single inserts) | 486 ops/sec | 429 ops/sec | 694 ops/sec (288 ms) | 689 ops/sec (290 ms) | (I/O bound) |
| SQLite SELECT ×300 (WHERE > over 200 rows) | 4,838 ops/sec | 5,263 ops/sec | 5,263 ops/sec (57 ms) | **6,122 ops/sec** (49 ms) | **1.27×** |
| SQLite UPDATE ×200 (by pk) | 803 ops/sec | 826 ops/sec | 662 ops/sec (302 ms) | 675 ops/sec (296 ms) | (I/O bound) |

## Architecture Highlights

- **O(1) Static & Prefix-Indexed Dynamic Routing**: `Router.matrix` uses an O(1) hash table for static routes and a 2-level segment-length + literal-prefix index (`s1/s2` and `s1`) with pre-split segment arrays for dynamic routes — reaching **153,846 ops/sec** on the v1.1.1 VM and **200,000 ops/sec** on LLVM-23 AOT.
- **Pre-Compiled SpiderBD v2 Templates**: `BD.matrix` caches pre-processed template line arrays (invalidated by `file_mtime`), reaching **9,661 renders/sec** on VM and **12,500 renders/sec** on AOT.
- **Bytecode VM Default + LLVM-23 AOT**: Since Spid3r v1.1+, `spid3r run` executes on the bytecode VM by default, and `spid3r magic build --release` compiles the full framework to a standalone native executable.

Reproduce:

```bash
SPID3R_PATH=. SPID3R_DB_DRIVER=sqlite spid3r run benchmarks/bench.matrix
SPID3R_PATH=. SPID3R_DB_DRIVER=sqlite spid3r build --release benchmarks/bench.matrix -o bench_aot && ./bench_aot
```

## Performance Gate (`--gate`)

CI enforces both the toolchain floor check and Spid3rWeb's framework micro-benchmark floors (~1/4 of the v1.1.1 VM baseline to catch catastrophic regressions without failing on shared-runner noise):

| Gate | Minimum Floor | v1.1.1 VM Reference |
|---|---:|---:|
| `route_matching` | `20,000 ops/sec` | `153,846 ops/sec` |
| `query_compilation` | `4,000 ops/sec` | `21,428 ops/sec` |
| `json_roundtrip` | `60,000 ops/sec` | `666,666 ops/sec` |
| `view_render` | `1,500 ops/sec` | `9,661 ops/sec` |
| `sqlite_select` | `800 ops/sec` | `5,263 ops/sec` |

Single-row SQLite `INSERT` and `UPDATE` remain informational (filesystem `fsync`/I/O bound). Run the full gate locally:

```bash
spid3r bench --runs 1 --gate benchmarks/bench.matrix
spid3r magic bench --gate
```

// bootstrap/app.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Application Factory — wires providers, routes, and queue handlers.

import "framework/Foundation/Application";
import "app/Providers/AppServiceProvider";
import "routes/web";
import "routes/api";
import "routes/queue";

public fn create_application(base_path: string = ".") {
    let app = new Application(base_path);
    app.register(new AppServiceProvider());
    register_web_routes();
    register_api_routes();
    register_queue_jobs();
    return app;
}

FILE: bootstrap/paths.matrix

// bootstrap/paths.matrix
// Canonical project directory helpers for Spid3rWeb applications.

fn join_rel(base: string, rel: string) -> string {
    let clean = string(rel).replace("\\", "/").trim();
    if clean == "" {
        return base;
    }
    if clean.starts_with("/") {
        clean = clean.slice(1, clean.len());
    }
    if base == "." or base == "" {
        return clean;
    }
    return "{base}/{clean}";
}

public fn base_path(rel: string = "") -> string {
    return join_rel(".", rel);
}

public fn app_path(rel: string = "") -> string {
    return join_rel("app", rel);
}

public fn config_path(rel: string = "") -> string {
    return join_rel("config", rel);
}

public fn database_path(rel: string = "") -> string {
    return join_rel("database", rel);
}

public fn public_path(rel: string = "") -> string {
    return join_rel("public", rel);
}

public fn resources_path(rel: string = "") -> string {
    return join_rel("resources", rel);
}

public fn storage_path(rel: string = "") -> string {
    return join_rel("storage", rel);
}

// config/app.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Application Configuration

import "framework/Foundation/Env";

let _app_env_loaded = load_env_file(".env");

fn _app_env_str(key: string, fallback: string) -> string {
    let v = env(key);
    if v.is_some() {
        let s = string(v.unwrap()).trim();
        if s != "" {
            return s;
        }
    }
    return fallback;
}

fn _app_parse_bool(raw: string) -> bool {
    let s = string(raw).trim().lower();
    return s == "1" or s == "true" or s == "yes" or s == "on";
}

let _resolved_env = _app_env_str("APP_ENV", "production").lower();
let _debug_raw = _app_env_str("APP_DEBUG", "");
let _resolved_debug = if _debug_raw != "" {
    _app_parse_bool(_debug_raw)
} else {
    _resolved_env == "development" or _resolved_env == "local"
};

let _app_key_raw = _app_env_str("APP_KEY", "");
let _resolved_key = if _app_key_raw != "" {
    _app_key_raw
} else {
    _app_env_str("SPID3R_APP_KEY", "")
};

public let config_app = {
    "name": _app_env_str("APP_NAME", "Spid3rWeb Developer Platform"),
    "version": "1.1.1",
    "min_spid3r": "1.1.1",
    "env": _resolved_env,
    "debug": _resolved_debug,
    "url": _app_env_str("APP_URL", "http://localhost:8000"),
    "timezone": "UTC",
    "locale": "en",
    // Application key used for signed values (set APP_KEY or SPID3R_APP_KEY
    // in .env; run `spid3r magic key:generate` to mint a 256-bit key).
    "key_env": "APP_KEY",
    "key": _resolved_key,
    "paths": {
        "public": "public",
        "views": "resources/views",
        "state_dir": _app_env_str("SPID3R_STATE_DIR", "storage")
    }
    ,
    // Toggle for the built-in maintenance placeholder (reserved)
    "maintenance": false
};

// config/auth.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Authentication Configuration
//
// Consumed by framework/Auth/Auth.matrix and framework/Auth/Hash.matrix.
// The active table/columns and hashing driver are resolved at runtime.

fn _auth_env_str(key: string, fallback: string) -> string {
    let v = env(key);
    if v.is_some() {
        let s = string(v.unwrap()).trim().lower();
        if s != "" {
            return s;
        }
    }
    return fallback;
}

public let config_auth = {
    // Active guard: "session" (cookie) | "token" (API Bearer)
    "default": "session",
    "hashing": {
        "driver": _auth_env_str("SPID3R_HASH_DRIVER", "argon2id"),
        "driver_env": "SPID3R_HASH_DRIVER",
        "bcrypt": {
            "rounds": 10
        }
        ,
        "argon2": {
            "variant": "id",
            "time_cost": 2,
            "memory_cost": 19_456,
            "parallelism": 1,
            "key_len": 32
        }
    }
    ,
    "guards": {
        "session": {
            "driver": "session",
            "table": "users",
            "username_column": "email",
            "password_column": "password",
            "provider": "users"
        }
        ,
        "token": {
            "driver": "token",
            "table": "users",
            "provider": "users"
        }
    }
    ,
    "providers": {
        "users": {
            "driver": "database",
            "table": "users"
        }
    }
    ,
    "passwords": {
        "reset_expire": 3_600,  // seconds a reset token stays valid
        "reset_throttle": 60  // min seconds between reset requests
    }
};

FILE: config/cache.matrix

// config/cache.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Cache Configuration
//
// The active driver is selected via the SPID3R_CACHE_DRIVER environment
// variable (or a .env file) — see framework/Cache/Cache.matrix:
//
//   SPID3R_CACHE_DRIVER=memory | file | database
//   (defaults to `file` when APP_ENV=production, `memory` otherwise)
//
//   memory    — per-process in-memory store, fastest, cleared on restart
//   file      — storage/framework/cache/, survives restarts
//   database  — `cache` table via the DB facade, shared across processes,
//               works on every configured database engine
//
// TTL convention: seconds > 0 expire after N seconds; 0/null = forever.

import "framework/Foundation/Env";

let _cache_env_loaded = load_env_file(".env");
let _cache_env = env("APP_ENV").unwrap_or("production").trim().lower();
let _cache_driver_env = env("SPID3R_CACHE_DRIVER").unwrap_or("").trim().lower();
let _cache_default = if _cache_env == "production" {
    "file"
} else {
    "memory"
};
if _cache_driver_env == "memory" or _cache_driver_env == "file" or _cache_driver_env == "database" {
    _cache_default = _cache_driver_env;
} elif _cache_driver_env == "db" {
    _cache_default = "database";
}

public let config_cache = {
    "default": _cache_default,
    "driver_env": "SPID3R_CACHE_DRIVER",
    "stores": {
        "memory": {
            "driver": "memory",
            "persistent": false
        }
        ,
        "file": {
            "driver": "file",
            "path": "storage/framework/cache",
            "persistent": true
        }
        ,
        "database": {
            "driver": "database",
            "table": "cache",
            "persistent": true,
            "shared": true,
            "engines": "sqlite | postgres | mysql | mariadb"
        }
    }
};

// config/database.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Database Configuration
//
// The active driver is selected at runtime via the SPID3R_DB_DRIVER
// environment variable. All settings can also come from env vars so the
// same codebase runs against any engine without code changes:
//
//   SPID3R_DB_DRIVER=sqlite | postgres | mysql | mariadb   (default: sqlite)
//   SPID3R_STATE_DIR=storage                               (default: storage)
//   SPID3R_DB_PATH=storage/database/database.sqlite        (overrides SQLite path)
//
// PostgreSQL:
//   SPID3R_PG_HOST=127.0.0.1      SPID3R_PG_PORT=5432
//   SPID3R_PG_USER=spid3r         SPID3R_PG_PASSWORD=secret
//   SPID3R_PG_DATABASE=spid3r
//   NOTE: the runtime PG client supports md5/trust auth (not SCRAM-SHA-256):
//     SET password_encryption = 'md5'; ALTER USER app WITH PASSWORD '...';
//
// MySQL / MariaDB:
//   SPID3R_MYSQL_HOST=127.0.0.1   SPID3R_MYSQL_PORT=3306
//   SPID3R_MYSQL_USER=spid3r      SPID3R_MYSQL_PASSWORD=secret
//   SPID3R_MYSQL_DATABASE=spid3r
//   NOTE: MySQL 8 users must use mysql_native_password
//   (MariaDB defaults to it, so MariaDB works out of the box):
//     ALTER USER 'app'@'%' IDENTIFIED WITH mysql_native_password BY '...';

public let config_database = {
    "default": "sqlite",
    "driver_env": "SPID3R_DB_DRIVER",
    "connections": {
        "sqlite": {
            "driver": "sqlite",
            "database": "storage/database/database.sqlite",
            "foreign_key_constraints": true,
            "journal_mode": "WAL",
            "synchronous": "NORMAL",
            "busy_timeout_ms": 5_000,
            "pool_size": 1
        }
        ,
        "postgres": {
            "driver": "postgres",
            "host_env": "SPID3R_PG_HOST",
            "port_env": "SPID3R_PG_PORT",
            "user_env": "SPID3R_PG_USER",
            "password_env": "SPID3R_PG_PASSWORD",
            "database_env": "SPID3R_PG_DATABASE",
            "default_port": 5432,
            "pool_size": 4,
            "auth": "md5 | trust (SCRAM-SHA-256 not supported by runtime client)",
            "last_insert_id": "INSERT ... RETURNING id"
        }
        ,
        "mysql": {
            "driver": "mysql",
            "host_env": "SPID3R_MYSQL_HOST",
            "port_env": "SPID3R_MYSQL_PORT",
            "user_env": "SPID3R_MYSQL_USER",
            "password_env": "SPID3R_MYSQL_PASSWORD",
            "database_env": "SPID3R_MYSQL_DATABASE",
            "default_port": 3306,
            "pool_size": 4,
            "auth": "mysql_native_password required (MySQL 8 default is caching_sha2_password)",
            "last_insert_id": "SELECT LAST_INSERT_ID()"
        }
        ,
        "mariadb": {
            "driver": "mariadb",
            "wire_protocol": "mysql (MariaDB ships mysql_native_password by default)",
            "host_env": "SPID3R_MYSQL_HOST",
            "port_env": "SPID3R_MYSQL_PORT",
            "user_env": "SPID3R_MYSQL_USER",
            "password_env": "SPID3R_MYSQL_PASSWORD",
            "database_env": "SPID3R_MYSQL_DATABASE",
            "default_port": 3306,
            "pool_size": 4,
            "last_insert_id": "SELECT LAST_INSERT_ID()"
        }
    }
};

FILE: config/http.matrix

// config/http.matrix
// Spid3rWeb HTTP Server, Proxy Trust & Request Limits Configuration
//
// Consumed by framework/Http/RequestParser.matrix and framework/Foundation/Server.matrix.

fn _http_env_int(key: string, fallback: int) -> int {
    let v = env(key);
    if v.is_some() {
        let s = string(v.unwrap()).trim();
        if s != "" {
            try {
                return int(s);
            } catch (_e) {
            }
        }
    }
    return fallback;
}

fn _http_env_proxies() {
    let v = env("SPID3R_TRUSTED_PROXIES");
    if v.is_some() {
        let s = string(v.unwrap()).trim();
        if s != "" {
            let out = [];
            for item in s.split(",") {
                let t = item.trim();
                if t != "" {
                    out.push(t);
                }
            }
            return out;
        }
    }
    return [];
}

public let config_http = {
    // Trusted reverse proxies allowed to supply X-Forwarded-For/Proto/Host.
    // Accepts exact IPs, CIDR suffixes (/8, /16, /24), "loopback", or "*".
    "trusted_proxies": _http_env_proxies(),
    // Graceful shutdown request drain window in milliseconds.
    "drain_ms": _http_env_int("SPID3R_TASK_DRAIN_MS", 2_000),
    // Server listener overrides (0 = defer to spid3r.ini [net] defaults).
    "max_body_bytes": _http_env_int("SPID3R_NET_MAX_BODY", 0),
    "max_headers": _http_env_int("SPID3R_NET_MAX_HEADERS", 0),
    "timeout_ms": _http_env_int("SPID3R_NET_TIMEOUT_MS", 0),
    "keepalive_ms": _http_env_int("SPID3R_NET_KEEPALIVE_MS", 0)
};

// config/logging.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Logging Configuration
//
// Consumed by framework/Log/Log.matrix. Paths are relative to the project
// root. The minimum level can be overridden globally with SPID3R_LOG_LEVEL
// (debug | info | warn | error).

fn _log_env_int(key: string, fallback: int) -> int {
    let v = env(key);
    if v.is_some() {
        let s = string(v.unwrap()).trim();
        if s != "" {
            try {
                let parsed = int(s);
                if parsed > 0 {
                    return parsed;
                }
            } catch (_e) {
            }
        }
    }
    return fallback;
}

public let config_logging = {
    "default": "daily",
    "level_env": "SPID3R_LOG_LEVEL",
    "level": "debug",
    "retention_days": _log_env_int("SPID3R_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS", 14),
    "channels": {
        "single": {
            "driver": "single",
            "path": "storage/logs/app.log",
            "level": "debug"
        }
        ,
        "daily": {
            "driver": "daily",  // one file per UTC day: app-YYYY-MM-DD.log
            "path": "storage/logs/app.log",
            "days": 14,  // retention hint (manual cleanup)
            "level": "debug"
        }
        ,
        // Dedicated channel used by framework/Mail (Mail log driver)
        "mail": {
            "driver": "daily",
            "path": "storage/logs/mail.log",
            "days": 14,
            "level": "debug"
        }
        ,
        // Dedicated channel used by framework/Queue workers
        "queue": {
            "driver": "daily",
            "path": "storage/logs/queue.log",
            "days": 14,
            "level": "debug"
        }
    }
};

            "queue": "default",

            "max_attempts": 3  // attempts before a job is discarded
        }
        ,
        "sync": {
            "driver": "sync"
        }
    }
};

import "framework/Database/Schema";
import "framework/Database/Migration";

public class CreateUsersTable extends Migration {
    fn new (name: string = "001_create_users_table") {
        self.name = name;
        self.checksum = "";
    }

    public fn up(self) {
        let _ = self;
        Schema.create("users", table => {
            table.id();
            table.string("name");
            table.string("email").unique();
            table.string("password");
            table.timestamps();
        });
    }

    public fn down(self) {
        let _ = self;
        Schema.dropIfExists("users");
    }
}

// database/migrations/008_create_jobs_table.matrix
import "framework/Database/Schema";
import "framework/Database/Migration";

public class CreateJobsTable extends Migration {
    fn new (name: string = "008_create_jobs_table") {
        self.name = name;
        self.checksum = "";
    }

    public fn up(self) {
        let _ = self;
        Schema.create("jobs", table => {
            table.id();
            table.string("queue");  // queue name (default)
            table.text("payload");  // JSON: {"job": handler, "data": ...}
            table.integer("attempts");  // run count (for max_attempts)
            table.integer("reserved_at");  // 0 = not reserved; else unix ts
            table.integer("available_at");  // earliest run time
            table.integer("created_at");
        });
    }

    public fn down(self) {
        let _ = self;
        Schema.dropIfExists("jobs");
    }
}

FILE: deploy/Caddyfile
================================================
# deploy/Caddyfile — Automatic HTTPS reverse proxy example for Spid3rWeb.
# Set SPID3R_TRUSTED_PROXIES=127.0.0.1 (or the proxy container IP) so Spid3rWeb
# honors X-Forwarded-For, X-Forwarded-Proto, and X-Forwarded-Host from Caddy.

example.com {
    encode gzip zstd

    request_body {
        max_size 10MB
    }

    reverse_proxy 127.0.0.1:8000 {
        health_uri /health
        health_interval 30s
        header_up X-Real-IP {remote_host}
        header_up X-Forwarded-Proto {scheme}
        header_up X-Forwarded-Host {host}
    }
}



================================================
FILE: deploy/docker-compose.yml
================================================
# Local Docker environment for Spid3rWeb.
#
#   cp .env.example .env && spid3r magic key:generate
#   docker compose -f deploy/docker-compose.yml up --build -d
#   open http://localhost:8090
#
# Database services stay on the internal Docker network only (ports are NOT
# published to the host). Set required passwords via `.env` or shell env.

services:
  app:
    build:
      context: ..
      dockerfile: deploy/Dockerfile
    container_name: spid3rweb-app
    ports:
      - "8090:8000"
    environment:
      APP_ENV: ${APP_ENV:-production}
      APP_KEY: ${APP_KEY:?Set APP_KEY (run 'spid3r magic key:generate')}
      SPID3R_DB_DRIVER: ${SPID3R_DB_DRIVER:-mysql}
      SPID3R_MYSQL_HOST: mysql
      SPID3R_MYSQL_PORT: "3306"
      SPID3R_MYSQL_USER: ${SPID3R_MYSQL_USER:-spid3r}
      SPID3R_MYSQL_PASSWORD: ${SPID3R_MYSQL_PASSWORD:?Set SPID3R_MYSQL_PASSWORD}
      SPID3R_MYSQL_DATABASE: ${SPID3R_MYSQL_DATABASE:-spid3r}
    depends_on:
      mysql:
        condition: service_healthy
    restart: unless-stopped

  mysql:
    image: mysql:8.0
    container_name: spid3rweb-mysql
    environment:
      MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD: ${MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD:?Set MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD}
      MYSQL_DATABASE: ${SPID3R_MYSQL_DATABASE:-spid3r}
      MYSQL_USER: ${SPID3R_MYSQL_USER:-spid3r}
      MYSQL_PASSWORD: ${SPID3R_MYSQL_PASSWORD:?Set SPID3R_MYSQL_PASSWORD}
    # The framework's MySQL client speaks mysql_native_password (MySQL 8
    # defaults to caching_sha2_password), so create the app user explicitly.
    command: --default-authentication-plugin=mysql_native_password
    volumes:
      - mysql-data:/var/lib/mysql
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "MYSQL_PWD=\"$MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD\" mysqladmin ping -h 127.0.0.1 -uroot"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 20
    restart: unless-stopped

  postgres:
    image: postgres:16
    container_name: spid3rweb-postgres
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: ${SPID3R_PG_DATABASE:-spid3r}
      POSTGRES_USER: ${SPID3R_PG_USER:-spid3r}
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${SPID3R_PG_PASSWORD:?Set SPID3R_PG_PASSWORD}
    volumes:
      - pg-data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U \"$POSTGRES_USER\" -d \"$POSTGRES_DB\""]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 20
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  mysql-data:
  pg-data:

FILE: deploy/Dockerfile
================================================
# ============================================================================
# Spid3rWeb production-style container image.
#
# Installs a pinned Spid3r release tarball directly from GitHub Releases and
# verifies its SHA-256 checksum, then runs the app as an unprivileged user.
#
# The server binds 0.0.0.0 when SPID3R_HOST=0.0.0.0 (framework default stays
# loopback for local dev — see framework/Application.matrix listen()).
# ============================================================================
FROM debian:bookworm-slim AS runtime

ARG SPID3R_VERSION=1.1.1
ARG SPID3R_SHA256=""

RUN apt-get update -qq && apt-get install -y -qq --no-install-recommends curl ca-certificates libsqlite3-0 \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# The prebuilt spid3r binary links against libLLVM.so.23.1 (LLVM 23 runtime).
RUN apt-get update -qq && apt-get install -y -qq --no-install-recommends gnupg \
    && curl -fsSL https://apt.llvm.org/llvm-snapshot.gpg.key | gpg --dearmor -o /usr/share/keyrings/llvm.gpg \
    && echo "deb [signed-by=/usr/share/keyrings/llvm.gpg] https://apt.llvm.org/bookworm/ llvm-toolchain-bookworm-23 main" > /etc/apt/sources.list.d/llvm23.list \
    && apt-get update -qq && apt-get install -y -qq --no-install-recommends libllvm23 \
    && echo /usr/lib/llvm-23/lib > /etc/ld.so.conf.d/llvm-23.conf && ldconfig \
    && apt-get purge -y -qq gnupg && apt-get autoremove -y -qq && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Install pinned Spid3r release binaries with SHA-256 verification
RUN set -eu; \
    arch="$(uname -m)"; \
    case "$arch" in \
        x86_64|amd64) target="x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu" ;; \
        aarch64|arm64) target="aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu" ;; \
        *) echo "Unsupported architecture: $arch" >&2; exit 1 ;; \
    esac; \
    asset="spid3r-${target}.tar.gz"; \
    base_url="https://github.com/needyamin/spid3r/releases/download/v${SPID3R_VERSION}"; \
    tmpdir="$(mktemp -d)"; \
    trap 'rm -rf "$tmpdir"' EXIT; \
    curl -fsSL "${base_url}/${asset}" -o "${tmpdir}/${asset}"; \
    if [ -n "${SPID3R_SHA256}" ]; then \
        echo "${SPID3R_SHA256}  ${tmpdir}/${asset}" | sha256sum -c -; \
    else \
        curl -fsSL "${base_url}/sha256sums.txt" -o "${tmpdir}/sha256sums.txt"; \
        (cd "$tmpdir" && expected="$(awk -v a="${asset}" '$2 == a || $2 == "*" a {print $1; exit}' sha256sums.txt)" && test -n "${expected}" && echo "${expected}  ${asset}" | sha256sum -c -); \
    fi; \
    tar -xzf "${tmpdir}/${asset}" -C "$tmpdir"; \
    bin_src="$(find "$tmpdir" -type f -name spid3r | head -n 1)"; \
    test -n "$bin_src"; \
    install -m 0755 "$bin_src" /usr/local/bin/spid3r; \
    self_src="$(find "$tmpdir" -type f -name spid3r-self | head -n 1 || true)"; \
    if [ -n "$self_src" ] && [ -f "$self_src" ]; then \
        install -m 0755 "$self_src" /usr/local/bin/spid3r-self; \
    fi

# Unprivileged application user & group
RUN groupadd --system spid3r \
    && useradd --system --gid spid3r --home-dir /var/www/app --shell /usr/sbin/nologin spid3r

WORKDIR /var/www/app

# Copy only runtime application files (no tests, docs, or .env secrets)
COPY magic server.matrix spider.toml ./
COPY spid3r.ini.example ./spid3r.ini
COPY app/ app/
COPY bootstrap/ bootstrap/
COPY config/ config/
COPY database/ database/
COPY framework/ framework/
COPY public/ public/
COPY resources/ resources/
COPY routes/ routes/

# Runtime writable directories belong exclusively to the unprivileged user;
# source files remain root-owned and read-only to the app process.
RUN mkdir -p storage/logs storage/framework/cache storage/app storage/backups storage/database database \
    && chown -R spid3r:spid3r storage database \
    && chmod 0700 storage storage/logs storage/framework storage/framework/cache storage/app storage/backups storage/database database

ENV APP_ENV=production \
    SPID3R_HOST=0.0.0.0 \
    SPID3R_PORT=8000

USER spid3r

EXPOSE 8000

HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=5s --start-period=10s --retries=3 \
    CMD curl -fsS "http://127.0.0.1:${SPID3R_PORT}/health" >/dev/null || exit 1

# Migrate on boot (idempotent), then serve. `exec` hands PID 1 to the server
# so Ctrl+C / docker stop reach it directly.
CMD ["sh", "-c", "spid3r magic migrate && exec spid3r magic serve --port ${SPID3R_PORT}"]

FILE: deploy/nginx.conf

# deploy/nginx.conf — Reverse-proxy example with TLS termination and trusted proxy headers.
# Configure Spid3rWeb to trust the proxy IP (e.g., SPID3R_TRUSTED_PROXIES=127.0.0.1,10.0.0.0/8).

upstream spid3rweb_upstream {
    server 127.0.0.1:8000;
    keepalive 32;
}

server {
    listen 80;
    server_name example.com;
    return 301 https://$host$request_uri;
}

server {
    listen 443 ssl http2;
    server_name example.com;

    ssl_certificate     /etc/letsencrypt/live/example.com/fullchain.pem;
    ssl_certificate_key /etc/letsencrypt/live/example.com/privkey.pem;
    ssl_protocols       TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3;

    client_max_body_size 10m;

    location = /health {
        proxy_pass http://spid3rweb_upstream/health;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Connection "";
        access_log off;
    }

    location / {
        proxy_pass http://spid3rweb_upstream;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host              $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP         $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For   $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Host  $host;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade           $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection        $connection_upgrade;
        proxy_read_timeout 30s;
    }
}

# Spid3rWeb — Architecture & Technical Reference

**Version:** `1.1.1` · **Verified against:** Spid3r `v1.1.1+` (Bytecode VM default + Tree-Walk oracle + LLVM 23 AOT) · **Related docs:** [Security Guide](SECURITY.md) · [Driver Support Matrix](DRIVERS.md) · [Deploy Guide](DEPLOY.md) · **API Docs:** run `spid3r magic docs` → `docs/build/api.html`

---

## 1. Division of Responsibility

Spid3rWeb builds a clean, zero-dependency MVC architecture on top of Spid3r's native Rust runtime primitives:

| Layer | Owner | Responsibilities |
|---|---|---|
| **Runtime Natives (Rust)** | `spid3r` / `spid3r-self` | Multi-threaded HTTP/1.1 `serve_listener` worker pool (keep-alive, native TLS `tls_listen`, chunked `http_stream`), TCP sockets (`tcp_connect`/`tcp_listen`), RFC 6455 WebSocket (`ws_*`), streaming file I/O, `file_seek`, `gzip_compress`/`gunzip` & `write_file_atomic`, `file_lock`, SQLite / PostgreSQL / MySQL native drivers, `argon2_hash` / `argon2_verify`, `bcrypt_hash` / `bcrypt_verify`, `sha256` / `hmac_sha256` / `secure_compare`, `mutex`, `spid3r.ini` security guardrails (`basedir`, `modules.disable`, `disable_natives`, `--budget`, `--sandbox`) |
| **Application Bootstrap** | `app/`, `bootstrap/`, `routes/` | `bootstrap/app.matrix` wires providers & route modules (`AppServiceProvider`, `HomeController`, `routes/web.matrix`, `routes/api.matrix`, `routes/queue.matrix`); `server.matrix` and `magic` delegate here cleanly |
| **Spid3rWeb Framework** | `framework/` | Single-import barrel (`framework/Web`), `Foundation/` (`Application`, `Config`, `ConfigValidator`, `Container`, `Env`, `Server`, `ServiceProvider`, `RuntimeVersion`), `Http/` (`Router`, `RouteMatcher`, `Request`, `RequestParser`, `Response`, `CookieJar`, `StaticFiles`, `Csrf`, `Middleware`, `Controller`, `Resource`, `Validator`, `ValidatorRules`, `HttpException`, `ErrorRenderer`), `Database/` (`DB`, `Driver`, `Connection`, `ServerConnections`, `ConnectionPool`, `DatabaseManager`, `QueryBuilder`, `QueryCompiler`, `Schema`, `Migration`, `Model`, `ModelRepository`), `Auth/` (`Auth`, `Hash`, `Jwt`), `Session/`, `Cache/`, `Log/`, `Mail/`, `Queue/`, `Filesystem/`, `Events/`, `View/` (`BD`, `TemplateCompiler`, `TemplateEvaluator`), `WebSocket/`, `Console/`, and `Testing/` |

---

## 2. Request Lifecycle

Every incoming HTTP request passes through `Application.handle_request(raw_req)`:

1. **Production Policy & Provider Boot**: `check_production_ready()` validates `APP_KEY` and `RuntimeVersion.assert_min_runtime_version()`. `app.boot()` runs `register()` / `boot()` on all registered `ServiceProvider` instances once before the first request and dispatches `app.booted`.
2. **Request Construction & Method Spoofing**: `Request.init_from_raw(raw_req)` parses headers, cookies, query string, JSON/form/multipart bodies (`RequestParser.matrix` enforcing `config/upload.matrix` limits and trusted-proxy `SPID3R_TRUSTED_PROXIES` rules), and applies `_method` / `X-HTTP-Method-Override` spoofing.
3. **Request-Scoped Authentication**: Creates an isolated `AuthManager` on `req.auth`. Three-part `Bearer` JWTs verify statelessly via `Jwt.verify()` (0 DB hits); cookie/opaque tokens check the `sessions` table.
4. **2-Layer CSRF Defense (`Csrf.matrix`)**: On state-changing methods (`POST`, `PUT`, `PATCH`, `DELETE`), enforces `is_same_origin(req, origin)` (Layer 1) and per-session `_csrf` token verification for cookie sessions (Layer 2).
5. **Static Asset Delivery (`StaticFiles.matrix`)**: Serves `/css/`, `/js/`, `/assets/`, `/images/`, `/fonts/`, `/vendor/`, `/uploads/` with segment-based traversal guards, weak `ETag` (`304 Not Modified`), `Accept-Ranges: bytes` + HTTP `Range` (`206`/`416`), `gzip_compress` (`Vary: Accept-Encoding`), chunked `file_stream` responses, and `Content-Disposition: attachment` + sandbox CSP on `/uploads/`.
6. **Global Onion Pipeline**: Executes `self.global_middleware` around downstream dispatch (`CorsMiddleware` handles `OPTIONS`).
7. **O(1) / Prefix-Indexed Route Matching & Model Binding**: Exact static routes resolve in O(1) via `static_routes[method][path]`; parameterized routes use length + 2-level prefix buckets in `RouteMatcher`; registered parameter resolvers (`app.bind()` / `Route.bind()`) hydrate `req.bound(key)`; unmatched requests check root static files, `405`, `fallback`, or `404`.
8. **Route Middleware & Handler Dispatch**: Resolves aliases (`auth`, `guest`, `throttle:N,S`, `etag`, `request_id`) and runs controller/closure handlers.
9. **Response Finalization (`finalize_http_response`)**: Coerces maps/lists/strings, commits dirty `req.session()` stores (`Cache` + `Set-Cookie`), applies RFC 6265 cookies via `CookieJar`, and attaches auto-HSTS on production HTTPS requests.

---

## 3. Spid3rWeb vs. Stdlib `web` Security Scope

`../docs/SECURITY.md` notes under *Known gaps* that Spid3r's bare stdlib `web` module has no built-in CSRF middleware and uses signed client-side session cookies. **Spid3rWeb fills both gaps in framework code**:

- Server-side sessions (`sessions` DB table in `Auth.matrix` + `Cache`-backed `SessionStore` in `Session.matrix`).
- Two-layer CSRF protection (`Origin` validation + synchronizer `_csrf` token in `Csrf.matrix`).
- Full details live in [`docs/SECURITY.md`](SECURITY.md).

---

## 4. Upstream Runtime Nuances & Invariants

- **`spider.toml` strict manifest schema**: Spid3r v1.1.1's `pkg::Manifest` allows only `[package]` (`name`, `version`, `entry`), `[dependencies]`, and `[workspace]`. Framework minimum-runtime enforcement (`MIN_SPID3R_VERSION = "1.1.1"`) therefore lives in `framework/Foundation/RuntimeVersion.matrix` and `config/app.matrix`.
- **Dynamic Migration Registry**: Migrations under `database/migrations/` support both `Migration` subclasses and Laravel-9-style `public fn up()`/`down()` modules without any committed `registry.matrix`. `MigrationRegistry.matrix` generates the git-ignored `storage/framework/migrations.matrix` (with SHA-256 content digests) automatically on `migrate*` and `build`, while `MigrationRunner` tracks migration checksums, supports `--pretend`, `--step`, `migrate:fresh`, `migrate:reset`, `db:wipe`, and wraps DDL in transactions where supported (SQLite/PostgreSQL).
- **3-Engine Differential & Coverage Testing**: Because `examples/` exists at repo root, bare `spid3r test` inspects `examples/*.matrix`. Use `spid3r magic test --differential` (verifies `tree`, `vm`, and compiled `.matrixc` `exec` parity), `--coverage`, and `--db=all`.
- **Explicit Imports & Mutability Rules**:
  - `import "X"` exports only direct `public` symbols of `X.matrix`; use `import "framework/Web";` for factories or import classes directly.
  - `for x in list` iterates over copies — use indexed `while` loops for in-place list mutations.
  - Guard shared mutable state with `mutex()`, avoid `super()` for LLVM AOT compatibility, and always guard `m[k]` reads with `m.has(k)`.

---

## 5. Stdlib Overlap & Design Decisions

Spid3r `v1.1.1` ships stdlib modules (`session`, `middleware`, `multipart`, `uuid`, `semver`, `toml`, `yaml`, `http`) alongside Spid3rWeb's framework subsystems. The table below documents where Spid3rWeb extends vs. relies on the stdlib:

| Framework Component | Stdlib Module | Design Choice & Rationale |
|---|---|---|
| `framework/Session/Session.matrix` & `Auth.matrix` | `session.matrix` | Keeps DB `sessions` table + `Cache`-backed server-side flash/session storage, CSPRNG IDs via `random_bytes`, and RFC 6265 `CookieJar` validation (`Set-Cookie` emission separated cleanly from client-side session serialization). |
| `framework/Http/Middleware.matrix` | `middleware.matrix` | Keeps the framework's true Onion `(req, next) => next(req)` pipeline contract, `ThrottleMiddleware` (`X-RateLimit-*` via pluggable `Cache`), `CorsMiddleware`, `EtagMiddleware`, and `RequestIdMiddleware` so custom app middleware composes uniformly. |
| `framework/Http/RequestParser.matrix` | `multipart.matrix` | Retains framework-level `config/upload.matrix` limits (`max_bytes`, `max_files`, extension/MIME allow/deny filtering, safe filenames), `_method` spoofing, and trusted-proxy IP/proto gating in one parser pass. |
| Runtime helpers | `uuid`, `semver`, `toml`, `yaml` | Use stdlib natives/modules directly when adding optional TOML/YAML integrations (real exports: `uuid.uuid_v4`, `toml.toml_parse`, `semver.semver_parse`). Framework runtime version checks live in `RuntimeVersion.matrix` for zero-import boot checks. |



================================================
FILE: docs/DEPLOY.md
================================================
# Spid3rWeb Deployment & Operations Guide

**Minimum runtime:** Spid3r `v1.1.1+` · **Health endpoint:** `GET /health` · **Artifacts:** `deploy/{Dockerfile,docker-compose.yml,nginx.conf,Caddyfile}`

---

## 1. Choosing a Production Build Format

| Strategy | Command | Ideal For | Runtime Dependencies |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Native LLVM AOT (Recommended)** | `spid3r magic build --release --bundle -o dist/spid3rweb` | Standalone Linux/Windows HTTP servers (`spid3rweb --port 8000`) | None (ships compiled machine code + `public/`, `resources/`) |
| **Compiled Bytecode (`.matrixc`)** | `spid3r magic build --bytecode-only -o dist/server.matrixc` | Environments enforcing `spid3r exec --sandbox`, `--budget`, or `spid3r.ini` limits without shipping `.matrix` source | Spid3r VM runtime (`spid3r exec dist/server.matrixc`) |
| **Container Image (Docker)** | `docker build -f deploy/Dockerfile -t spid3rweb:latest .` | Reproducible cloud / Compose deployments with pinned `v1.1.1` SHA-256 verification and `USER spid3r` isolation | Docker / OCI runtime |
| **Bundled GUI Payload (`--bundle-runtime`)** | `spid3r magic build --bytecode --bundle-runtime --embed public/index.html -o dist/app` | Hybrid desktop apps launched via `spid3r gui --payload` (uses `run_payload_strict()` + SHA-256 cached runtime extraction) | Self-contained (`spid3r` embedded; requires an `--embed` HTML UI) |

> **Important note on `--bundle-runtime`:** In Spid3r `v1.1.1`, `spid3r build --bytecode --bundle-runtime` builds a `run_payload_strict()` launcher that invokes `spid3r gui --payload` and requires an embedded HTML entrypoint. For headless HTTP APIs and web servers, deploy either the **Native LLVM AOT binary** (`--release`), **Docker**, or `spid3r exec <file>.matrixc`.

---

## 2. Pre-Flight Checklist (Before Every Deploy)

```bash
# 1. Verify spid3r.ini syntax and keys
SPID3R_INI=./spid3r.ini.example spid3r config validate

# 2. Generate strong secrets (never commit .env)
cp .env.example .env
chmod 0600 .env
spid3r magic key:generate

# 3. Sync generated migration registry & verify config/runtime health
spid3r magic config:check
spid3r magic migrate:sync
APP_ENV=production APP_KEY="$(grep '^APP_KEY=' .env | cut -d= -f2-)" spid3r magic doctor --strict

# 4. Run database migrations (idempotent) & create public/storage link if serving user uploads
spid3r magic migrate
spid3r magic storage:link
```

---

## 3. Standalone Native AOT Binary Deployment

```bash
# Build native release executable + copy public/ & resources/
spid3r magic build --release --bundle -o dist/spid3rweb

# Run on loopback behind nginx/Caddy (or pass SPID3R_HOST=0.0.0.0)
cd dist
APP_ENV=production APP_KEY="hex:..." ./spid3rweb --port 8000 --root .
```

Native TLS can be enabled directly when terminating TLS in-process (`app.listen_tls` / `--tls --cert ... --key ...`), or delegated to Nginx / Caddy.

---

## 4. Reverse Proxies & TLS Termination

Ready-to-use configs live in `deploy/`:

- **Nginx:** [`deploy/nginx.conf`](../deploy/nginx.conf) (HTTP -> HTTPS redirect, HTTP/2, WebSocket Upgrade headers, `/health` pass-through)
- **Caddy:** [`deploy/Caddyfile`](../deploy/Caddyfile) (automatic Let's Encrypt HTTPS, zstd/gzip compression, active `/health` checks)

When running behind a reverse proxy:

```bash
SPID3R_TRUSTED_PROXIES=127.0.0.1,10.0.0.0/8
SPID3R_TASK_DRAIN_MS=2000
```

- **HTTP/2 termination**: Spid3r's native HTTP server speaks HTTP/1.1 (`serve_listener`); enable HTTP/2 (`h2`) at Nginx (`listen 443 ssl http2`) or Caddy, proxying upstream over HTTP/1.1 keep-alive.
- `req.client_ip()`, `req.is_secure()`, and `req.host()` honor `X-Forwarded-For`, `X-Forwarded-Proto`, and `X-Forwarded-Host` **only** from IPs/CIDRs listed in `SPID3R_TRUSTED_PROXIES`.
- In `APP_ENV=production`, HTTPS requests automatically emit `Strict-Transport-Security` (`config/security.matrix`).
- Static assets under `/css/`, `/js/`, `/assets/`, etc. support `Accept-Ranges: bytes` (`206`/`416`) and `Content-Encoding: gzip` (`Vary: Accept-Encoding`).

---

## 5. Docker & Compose Deployment

```bash
# Prepare host secrets (.env is never baked into the Dockerfile)
cp .env.example .env
chmod 0600 .env
spid3r magic key:generate

# Build image (installs pinned Spid3r v1.1.1 tarball verified by SHA-256, runs as USER spid3r)
docker build -f deploy/Dockerfile -t spid3rweb:v1.1.1 .

# Run full Compose stack (App + MySQL 8 + PostgreSQL 16; DB ports stay internal)
docker compose -f deploy/docker-compose.yml up --build -d
```

For production containers, run with `--read-only` root filesystem plus writable mounts for `/var/www/app/storage` (`storage/database/database.sqlite` lives inside `storage/` via `app.paths.state_dir`) and, if keeping legacy DB placement, `/var/www/app/database` (mode `0700`, owned by `spid3r`).

---

## 6. Routine Maintenance Commands

```bash
spid3r magic storage:prune    # remove expired logs (> retention_days) & stale temp uploads
spid3r magic cache:gc         # garbage-collect expired cache entries (memory/file/database)
spid3r magic db:backup        # online SQLite snapshot under storage/backups/
spid3r magic db:vacuum        # checkpoint SQLite WAL and compact DB
spid3r magic docs             # generate HTML API reference at docs/build/api.html
spid3r magic bench --gate     # verify performance floors against benchmarks/RESULTS.md
```



================================================
FILE: docs/DRIVERS.md
