# Spid3rWeb Driver Support Matrix

**Runtime baseline:** Spid3r `v1.1.1+` · **Framework modules:** `framework/Database/{Driver,Connection,ServerConnections,ConnectionPool,DatabaseManager,Schema}.matrix` · **Config:** `config/database.matrix`

Spid3rWeb routes all database queries and migrations through the uniform `Driver` interface (`quote_identifier`, `placeholder`, `limit_sql`, `begin_sql`/`commit_sql`/`rollback_sql`, `ping`, `query`, `run`, `exec`, `statement`, `close`).

---

## 1. Engine Capabilities & Compatibility

| Capability | SQLite 3 (Default) | PostgreSQL (14–16+) | MySQL (8.0+) | MariaDB (10.6+) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Env driver value** | `sqlite` | `postgres` (`postgresql`, `pg`) | `mysql` | `mariadb` (MySQL wire) |
| **Native bindings** | Dynamic `sqlite3` (`db_open`) | Native wire (`pg_connect`) | Native wire (`mysql_connect`) | Native wire (`mysql_connect`) |
| **Supported auth** | Local file permissions | `md5`, `trust` (**no SCRAM-SHA-256**) | `mysql_native_password` (**no `caching_sha2_password`**) | `mysql_native_password` (default) |
| **Empty password** | n/a | Supported under `trust` | Quirk on `root` with empty pass; always set a non-empty app password | Set a non-empty app user password |
| **TLS to DB socket** | Local file only | **Not exposed** (use localhost / WireGuard / stunnel / Docker internal network) | **Not exposed** (use private VPC / Docker internal network / stunnel) | **Not exposed** (use private VPC / Docker internal network / stunnel) |
| **Identifier quoting** | `"ident"` | `"ident"` | `` `ident` `` | `` `ident` `` |
| **Placeholder syntax** | `?` | `$1, $2, ...` | `?` | `?` |
| **Last insert ID** | `last_insert_rowid()` | `INSERT ... RETURNING id` | `SELECT LAST_INSERT_ID()` | `SELECT LAST_INSERT_ID()` |
| **Transactional DDL** | Yes (`BEGIN`/`COMMIT`) | Yes (`BEGIN`/`COMMIT`) | No (DDL implicitly commits) | No (DDL implicitly commits) |
| **1-shot reconnect** | Not applicable | Yes, outside active tx on transient disconnect | Yes, outside active tx on transient disconnect | Yes, outside active tx on transient disconnect |
| **Connection pool** | `pool_size = 1` (`ConnectionPool` mutex) | `pool_size = 4` (`ConnectionPool` mutex + `ping()`) | `pool_size = 4` (`ConnectionPool` mutex + `ping()`) | `pool_size = 4` (`ConnectionPool` mutex + `ping()`) |
| **Default timeouts** | `busy_timeout = 5000` ms | `statement_timeout = 30000` ms, `lock_timeout = 10000` ms | `max_execution_time = 30000` ms | Server session default (`30000` ms where supported) |

---

## 2. Known Upstream Driver Gaps (Action Required on Managed Clouds)

Managed database providers (AWS RDS, Google Cloud SQL, Azure Database, Neon, Supabase) default to modern auth plugins that Spid3r v1.1.1's built-in wire clients do not negotiate natively:

1. **PostgreSQL `SCRAM-SHA-256` (PG 14+ default)**
   - **Symptom:** Authentication failure mentioning SCRAM / SASL (`10`).
   - **Remediation:** Configure the database role with `md5` password encryption on private networks, or terminate SCRAM/TLS through an adjacent PgBouncer/cloud-sql-proxy on loopback:
     ```sql
     SET password_encryption = 'md5';
     ALTER USER spid3r WITH PASSWORD 'your-strong-password';
     ```
2. **MySQL 8 `caching_sha2_password` (MySQL 8.0 default)**
   - **Symptom:** Auth plugin error mentioning `caching_sha2_password` or `sha256_password`.
   - **Remediation:** Create or alter the application database user with `mysql_native_password` (see `deploy/docker-compose.yml` for an automated setup):
     ```sql
     ALTER USER 'spid3r'@'%' IDENTIFIED WITH mysql_native_password BY 'your-strong-password';
     FLUSH PRIVILEGES;
     ```
   - `ServerConnections.matrix` (`enhance_db_auth_error`) intercepts both errors at `connect()` / `magic migrate` time and appends explicit plugin remediation instructions instead of a generic socket failure.

---

## 3. SQLite Pragmas, File Placement & Windows OneDrive Warning

Default new installations place the SQLite database under `storage/database/database.sqlite` (`app.paths.state_dir = "storage"`, separating runtime state from version-controlled `database/{migrations,seeders}/`, while still honoring an existing legacy `database/database.sqlite` if present). `SqliteConnection` creates parent directories automatically.

On every open, `SqliteConnection` (`framework/Database/Connection.matrix`) applies:

- `PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;`
- `PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL;` (configured in `config/database.matrix`)
- `PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;`
- `PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000;`

### OneDrive / Network Filesystem Warning (Windows & NFS)

- SQLite `WAL` (`-wal` and `-shm`) uses shared-memory locks that can corrupt or throw `database is locked` inside **OneDrive-synced** folders or over **NFS/SMB** network shares, and synced folders can upload database state or `.env` to cloud storage.
- For Windows development or heavy local writes, keep the repository outside `OneDrive`, exclude `storage/`, or relocate runtime state (`SPID3R_STATE_DIR` / `SPID3R_DB_PATH`) outside synced paths:
  ```powershell
  $env:SPID3R_STATE_DIR = "$env:LOCALAPPDATA\Spid3rWeb"
  # or pinpoint the SQLite file directly:
  $env:SPID3R_DB_PATH = "$env:LOCALAPPDATA\Spid3rWeb\dev.sqlite"
  ```
- See [Upstream Feature Requests](UPSTREAM_REQUESTS.md) for tracked language runtime improvements (`SCRAM-SHA-256`, `caching_sha2_password`, DB TLS).
- `spid3r magic doctor` detects OneDrive workspaces automatically and emits an actionable warning.

---

## 4. Housekeeping & Multi-DB Local Testing

```bash
# Check database health & SQLite permissions
spid3r magic doctor

# Online SQLite backup (`VACUUM INTO`, with checkpoint + copy fallback)
spid3r magic db:backup [storage/backups/manual.sqlite]

# Checkpoint SQLite WAL and vacuum storage (PostgreSQL runs VACUUM)
spid3r magic db:vacuum

# Start local MySQL 8 + PostgreSQL 16 test services (ports stay internal in compose, or expose locally for test runs)
docker compose -f deploy/docker-compose.yml up -d mysql postgres
```



================================================
FILE: docs/SECURITY.md
================================================
# Spid3rWeb Security Architecture & Operations Guide

**Verified against:** Spid3r `v1.1.1+` · **Modules:** `config/{security,upload,http,auth}.matrix`, `framework/Foundation/{Config,Env,RuntimeVersion}.matrix`, `framework/Http/{CookieJar,Csrf,StaticFiles,RequestParser,Response,Middleware}.matrix`, `framework/Auth/{Hash,Jwt,Auth}.matrix`, `spid3r.ini.example`

---

## 1. Spid3rWeb vs. Spid3r Stdlib `web` Module

The upstream Spid3r language manual (`../docs/SECURITY.md`, "Known gaps") notes:
*"No CSRF middleware … Sessions are client-side (`web.SessionStore` signs the data; the client can read it)."*

That note applies **only** to the minimal built-in `web` module shipped in Spid3r's standard library. **Spid3rWeb (`spid3r_web`) implements full server-side protections:**

- **2-Layer CSRF Defense (`framework/Http/Csrf.matrix`, `Application.handle_request`)**:
  1. **Origin validation (`is_same_origin`)**: For `POST`/`PUT`/`PATCH`/`DELETE`, verifies any `Origin` header matches `Host` (normalizing scheme default ports).
  2. **Per-session synchronizer token (`csrf_valid`)**: Cookie-authenticated mutations without an `Origin` header must present the CSPRNG `_csrf` token issued at login and stored server-side.
- **Server-Side Sessions (`sessions` table & `SessionStore`)**:
  - Authenticated login sessions (`Auth.matrix`) issue an opaque CSPRNG `sess_<hex>` token stored in the database `sessions` table (`payload` never lives in browser cookies).
  - General session/flash data (`SessionStore`, `framework/Session/Session.matrix`) is kept server-side via `Cache` (`session:<id>`) and referenced by an `HttpOnly`, `SameSite=Lax`, validated cookie (`CookieJar`).

---

## 2. Production Runtime Policy (`spid3r.ini` & CLI Flags)

### Recommended `spid3r.ini` Guardrails (`spid3r.ini.example`)

Verify configuration files before deploying:

```bash
SPID3R_INI=./spid3r.ini.example spid3r config validate
```

Key directives:

- `[modules] disable = ssh, ftp, gui`, `allow_wasm = false`, `allow_host = false`: blocks unused stdlib modules across tree, VM, and AOT compilation.
- `[security] basedir = ./public, ./storage, ./database, ./resources, ./config`: confines file-taking runtime natives (`read_file`, `write_file_atomic`, `db_open`, etc.) component-wise after canonicalization.
- `[net] max_body = 10485760`, `max_headers = 64`, `timeout_ms = 30000`, `keepalive_ms = 5000`: bounds HTTP body sizes (`413`), header counts (`431`), and slowloris windows.
- `[spid3r] task_drain_ms = 2000`, `max_workers = 256`, `queue_cap = 65536`.
- `[env.<name>]`: per-environment overrides without changing code.

### Important Confinement Boundary (`basedir` Alone Is Not a Jail)

Per Spid3r's `SECURITY.md`, `[security] basedir` restricts path-taking natives, **not child OS processes** spawned through `exec_capture` / `spawn_process`. When running a dedicated HTTP server via `spid3r run server.matrix` (without running CLI migrations inside the same process), also enable in `[security]`:

```ini
disable_natives = spawn_process, exec_capture, run_payload, run_payload_strict
```

And pair with container/OS isolation (`USER spid3r`, unprivileged Linux UID, `--sandbox`, `--budget <steps>`, or `--max-call-depth` for untrusted sub-scripts).

---

## 3. Cryptographic & HTTP Controls

| Control | Implementation | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| **Fail-closed `APP_ENV` / `APP_KEY`** | `Config.matrix`, `Application.check_production_ready()` | `APP_ENV` defaults to `"production"` (`debug = false`). Production refuses to start if `APP_KEY` / `SPID3R_APP_KEY` is missing, `< 16` chars, or a known placeholder. Run `spid3r magic key:generate`. |
| **Runtime minimum check** | `RuntimeVersion.matrix` | Verifies Spid3r runtime >= `1.1.1` when CLI binary is inspectable; skipped cleanly in standalone AOT or when `exec_capture` is disabled. |
| **Password hashing** | `Hash.matrix`, `config/auth.matrix`, `Auth.matrix` | Defaults to **Argon2id** (`m=19456`, `t=2`, `p=1`) with **Bcrypt** verification compatibility and automatic `Hash.needs_rehash()` upgrade on successful login. |
| **RFC 6265 cookies** | `CookieJar.matrix`, `Response.matrix` | Validates cookie token names, control chars/semicolons, path leading `/`, emits separate `Set-Cookie` headers, and sets `Secure` automatically on HTTPS / production. |
| **Security headers & CSP** | `config/security.matrix`, `Response.matrix`, `Application.matrix` | Default strict CSP (`default-src 'self'; object-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'`), `nosniff`, `SAMEORIGIN`, `COOP`, `Permissions-Policy`, per-route `.csp()`/`.security_headers()`, and automatic production TLS HSTS. |
| **Static files & uploads** | `StaticFiles.matrix`, `RequestParser.matrix`, `Storage.matrix`, `config/upload.matrix` | Segment-based traversal guard, upload size/count/extension/MIME validation (`blocked_extensions`: `html`, `htm`, `svg`, `js`, `php`, `exe`, etc.), server-side randomized upload names, and `/uploads/` `Content-Disposition: attachment` + `default-src 'none'; sandbox` CSP. |
| **Trusted proxies** | `config/http.matrix`, `Request.matrix` | Honors `X-Forwarded-For`, `X-Forwarded-Proto`, and `X-Forwarded-Host` **only** when the peer IP matches `SPID3R_TRUSTED_PROXIES`. |
| **WebSockets** | `WsServer.matrix` | Atomic `max_clients` slot cap, `max_message_bytes` enforcement, and `allowed_origins` policy helper. |

---

## 4. File Permissions & Windows / OneDrive Checklist

On POSIX hosts (Linux / macOS / Docker):

- `.env`: `chmod 0600 .env` (never readable by group/others; never copied into Docker images).
- `storage/` (and subdirectories) & `database/`: `chmod 0700`; SQLite files and logs `chmod 0600`.
- Application source (`app/`, `bootstrap/`, `config/`, `framework/`, `routes/`, `public/`): root-owned / read-only to the unprivileged `spid3r` service user (`USER spid3r` in `deploy/Dockerfile`).
- Run `spid3r magic doctor --fix --strict` before releasing to verify permissions, directory presence, config validity, `APP_KEY`, and DB connectivity.

On Windows development workstations:

- Keep development clones outside **OneDrive-synced** folders (e.g. `C:\dev\spid3r_web`), or exclude `.env`, `storage/`, and `database/` from sync so SQLite WAL/SHM locks do not conflict and secrets are not uploaded to cloud storage. Set `SPID3R_STATE_DIR=%LOCALAPPDATA%\Spid3rWeb` (or `SPID3R_DB_PATH`) when developing inside a synced folder.
- Runtime state isolation: SQLite runtime state resolves via `app.paths.state_dir` (`SPID3R_STATE_DIR`, default `storage`, placing the file at `storage/database/database.sqlite`) cleanly separated from code in `app/` and `database/migrations/`.

---

## 5. Single-Process vs. Multi-Process State Matrix

| Subsystem | Single Process | Multiple Workers / Containers | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Cache (`config/cache.matrix`)** | `memory` (dev/test) or `file` | **`file`** (shared volume) or **`database`** | `APP_ENV=production` defaults cache driver to **`file`** automatically when `SPID3R_CACHE_DRIVER` is unset. |
| **Throttle (`ThrottleMiddleware`)** | `Cache` (`memory` ok) | Set **`SPID3R_CACHE_DRIVER=database`** (or edge rate-limiting in nginx/Caddy) | `ThrottleMiddleware` stores counters in `Cache`; in-memory counters do not synchronize across separate OS processes. |
| **Sessions (`sessions` table)** | DB (`sqlite`/`postgres`/`mysql`) | DB (`postgres`/`mysql`/`mariadb`) | Server-side DB table; share across replicas seamlessly. |
| **Queue (`jobs` table)** | DB or `sync` | DB (`postgres`/`mysql`/`mariadb`) | Uses optimistic compare-and-swap on `reserved_at` (`retry_after` reclamation). |



================================================
FILE: docs/UPSTREAM_REQUESTS.md
================================================
# Upstream Spid3r Runtime Feature Requests

**Target Repository:** `needyamin/spid3r`  
**Observed Toolchain:** `spid3r 1.1.1`  
**Context:** Items identified while hardening and modernizing `spid3r_web` that cannot be implemented cleanly in user-space framework code without language runtime changes.

---

## 1. Dynamic Module Loading for Migration Auto-Discovery (Option D)

- **Area:** Module loader / VM / CLI runtime
- **Problem:** `import "..."` statements are resolved statically before execution, with no `load_module(path)` native. As a result, Spid3rWeb generates git-ignored `storage/framework/migrations.matrix` (Option A) and wraps function-based migrations (`public fn up()` / `public fn down()`) via aliased imports (Option C).
- **Requested Feature:**
  - Expose a sandboxed `load_module(path: string) -> Result<map, string>` native subject to `[security] basedir`, `[modules] allow_wasm` / `allow_host`, and `[modules] disable`.
  - Return exported symbols (`up`, `down`, classes) from dynamic modules when running interpreted (`tree`/`vm`), while allowing `spid3r build` AOT to continue pre-bundling static entrypoints or registered registries.
- **Payoff:** Once `load_module()` lands in Spid3r, Spid3rWeb can eliminate `storage/framework/migrations.matrix` generation entirely in favor of pure runtime discovery like Laravel.

---

## 2. PostgreSQL `SCRAM-SHA-256` & TLS Wire Client Support

- **Area:** Database stdlib (`pg_connect`)
- **Problem:** Managed PostgreSQL 14+ providers (AWS RDS, Google Cloud SQL, Neon, Supabase) default to `SCRAM-SHA-256` authentication and require TLS (`sslmode=require`). Spid3r v1.1.1's built-in `pg_connect` only supports `md5`/`trust` over unencrypted sockets.
- **Requested Feature:**
  - Add SASL `SCRAM-SHA-256` authentication exchange and `sslmode` / TLS stream negotiation to `pg_connect`.
- **Current Workaround in Spid3rWeb:** `ServerConnections.matrix` (`enhance_db_auth_error`) intercepts auth errors and prints explicit `password_encryption = 'md5'` / PgBouncer remediation steps (see `docs/DRIVERS.md`).

---

## 3. MySQL 8 `caching_sha2_password` & TLS Support

- **Area:** Database stdlib (`mysql_connect`)
- **Problem:** MySQL 8.0+ defaults to `caching_sha2_password`, and empty passwords on `root` are mishandled in `mysql_connect`. Managed MySQL clouds also mandate TLS connections.
- **Requested Feature:**
  - Add `caching_sha2_password` (RSA / TLS fast-path) negotiation, TLS socket support, and clean empty-password packet handling in `mysql_connect`.
- **Current Workaround in Spid3rWeb:** `ServerConnections.matrix` (`enhance_db_auth_error`) surfaces explicit `ALTER USER ... IDENTIFIED WITH mysql_native_password BY '...'` instructions; CI and `deploy/docker-compose.yml` configure `mysql_native_password` automatically.

---

## 4. Semantic Checker & Package Manifest Ergonomics

- **Area:** `src/semantic/mod.rs` & `src/pkg/mod.rs` (`spider.toml`)
- **Requests:**
  1. **Implicit `self` in static/interface/empty methods:** Do not emit `warning[semantic]: unused variable 'self'` for `static fn` methods (which have no runtime `self` object), empty constructors, or concrete overrides of abstract/interface contracts.
  2. **`spider.toml` `min_spid3r` manifest field:** Allow an optional `[package] min_spid3r = ">=1.1.1"` key in `spider.toml` (currently rejected by strict TOML manifest parsing; Spid3rWeb validates `MIN_SPID3R_VERSION` in `framework/Foundation/RuntimeVersion.matrix` instead).

# Spid3rWeb — Architectural Diagrams 🎨

Seven technical schematics for the Spid3rWeb MVC framework. Every diagram ships as a standalone vector **SVG** (used on this page and in `developer-docs.html`) plus an editable **Mermaid** source (`.mmd`). All numbers match `benchmarks/RESULTS.md` and `developer-docs.html` — update both together if they change.

| # | Diagram | SVG | Mermaid | What it shows |
|---|---------|-----|---------|---------------|
| 1 | Architecture Overview | [architecture-overview.svg](architecture-overview.svg) | [.mmd](architecture-overview.mmd) | 3-tier separation: `app/` → `framework/` → Spid3r Rust runtime |
| 2 | Request Lifecycle | [request-lifecycle.svg](request-lifecycle.svg) | [.mmd](request-lifecycle.mmd) | 9 steps from TCP socket to HTTP response (`Application.handle_request`) |
| 3 | Onion Middleware | [onion-middleware.svg](onion-middleware.svg) | [.mmd](onion-middleware.mmd) | Dual-pass wrapping: `(req, next) => next(req)` |
| 4 | O(1) & Prefix Router | [router-architecture.svg](router-architecture.svg) | [.mmd](router-architecture.mmd) | Static hash → prefix buckets → wildcards · 153,846 ops/s VM / 200k AOT |
| 5 | MVC & ORM Flow | [mvc-orm-flow.svg](mvc-orm-flow.svg) | [.mmd](mvc-orm-flow.mmd) | 1-line models, auto-casts, QueryBuilder, transactions, native drivers |
| 6 | Dual Authentication | [auth-flow.svg](auth-flow.svg) | [.mmd](auth-flow.mmd) | Stateless JWT (0 DB queries) vs session cookies + 2-layer CSRF |
| 7 | Fluent TestClient | [testclient-flow.svg](testclient-flow.svg) | [.mmd](testclient-flow.mmd) | In-memory testing: `acting_as()`, kernel traversal, JSON assertions |

## Preview

|---|---|
| ![Architecture](architecture-overview.svg) | ![Lifecycle](request-lifecycle.svg) |
| ![Middleware](onion-middleware.svg) | ![Router](router-architecture.svg) |

## Editing a diagram

1. Change the `.mmd` source (paste it into the [Mermaid Live Editor](https://mermaid.live) for a quick preview).
2. Re-export the SVG so it stays in sync with the source:

   ```bash
   npx @mermaid-js/mermaid-cli -i <name>.mmd -o <name>.svg -b white
   ```

3. Keep labels short — long strings wrap poorly and break layout at small sizes.

> **Note:** SVGs must contain only SVG elements inside `<text>` (use `<tspan>`, never HTML tags like `<code>`/`<strong>` — browsers silently drop them when the SVG is embedded via `<img>`).

FILE: docs/diagrams/auth-flow.mmd
================================================
flowchart TD
    classDef client fill:#f8fafc,stroke:#64748b,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#0f172a;
    classDef dec fill:#fffbeb,stroke:#d97706,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#78350f;
    classDef jwt fill:#f0fdf4,stroke:#16a34a,stroke-width:2px,color:#14532d;
    classDef sess fill:#eff6ff,stroke:#2563eb,stroke-width:2px,color:#1e40af;
    classDef err fill:#fef2f2,stroke:#dc2626,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#991b1b;
    classDef ok fill:#10b981,stroke:#047857,stroke-width:2px,color:#ffffff;

    Req(["Incoming HTTP Request"]):::client
    Check{"Auth Header or Cookie?"}:::dec

    subgraph JWTFlow ["Stateless JWT Flow (0 DB Hits)"]
        Split["1. Split Token (header.payload.signature)"]:::jwt
        HMAC["2. Calculate HMAC-SHA256 signature"]:::jwt
        Comp{"3. secure_compare(sig, expected)?"}:::jwt
        Exp{"4. exp > current_time()?"}:::jwt
        JWTUser["Populate req.auth.user() from claims"]:::ok
    end

    subgraph SessFlow ["Stateful Session Flow"]
        FindSess["1. Lookup session_id in Cache / DB"]:::sess
        SessFound{"2. Session active?"}:::sess
        Mutating{"3. POST / PUT / PATCH / DELETE?"}:::dec
        CSRF{"4. Verify CSRF (Origin vs Host + _csrf)"}:::sess
        SessUser["Populate req.auth.user() from session"]:::ok
    end

    AuthFail["401 Unauthorized / 403 Forbidden"]:::err
    Handler["Proceed to Route Handler"]:::ok

    Req --> Check
    Check -- Bearer Token --> Split
    Split --> HMAC --> Comp
    Comp -- Match --> Exp
    Exp -- Valid --> JWTUser --> Handler
    Comp -- Fail --> AuthFail
    Exp -- Expired --> AuthFail

    Check -- Cookie spid3r_session --> FindSess
    FindSess --> SessFound
    SessFound -- Yes --> Mutating
    SessFound -- No --> AuthFail
    Mutating -- Yes --> CSRF
    Mutating -- No (GET/HEAD) --> SessUser --> Handler
    CSRF -- Valid --> SessUser
    CSRF -- Invalid --> AuthFail

FILE: docs/diagrams/onion-middleware.mmd
================================================
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    actor Client
    participant L1 as Layer 1: CORS & Security
    participant L2 as Layer 2: Request ID & Logger
    participant L3 as Layer 3: Rate Limiter (Throttle)
    participant L4 as Layer 4: Auth & Guards
    participant Handler as Route Handler / Controller

    Note over Client, Handler: Inbound Pass: Down the Onion Layers
    Client->>L1: HTTP Request
    L1->>L2: next(req)
    L2->>L3: next(req) [start timer]
    L3->>L4: next(req) [check quota]
    L4->>Handler: next(req) [req.user verified]

    Note over Handler: Execute Controller logic & query models
    Handler-->>L4: return Response(200 / 201)

    Note over Handler, Client: Outbound Pass: Up through the Onion Layers
    L4-->>L3: return res
    L3-->>L2: res.header("X-RateLimit-Remaining", N)
    L2-->>L1: log_request(status, duration_ms)
    L1-->>Client: res.header("Access-Control-*") -> Send to Client

FILE: docs/diagrams/request-lifecycle.mmd

flowchart TD
    classDef startEnd fill:#1e1b4b,stroke:#4338ca,stroke-width:2px,color:#ffffff;
    classDef step fill:#ffffff,stroke:#3b82f6,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#0f172a;
    classDef success fill:#f0fdf4,stroke:#16a34a,stroke-width:2px,color:#14532d;
    classDef branch fill:#fffbeb,stroke:#d97706,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#78350f;

    ClientReq(["Incoming HTTP Request<br/>(TCP serve_listener worker)"]):::startEnd
    S1["1. Idempotent Provider Boot<br/><code>app.boot()</code> runs register() / boot() once"]:::step
    S2["2. Request & Method Spoofing<br/>Parses headers, cookies, query, body & _method"]:::step
    S3["3. Request-Scoped Auth<br/>Stateless JWT (0 DB queries!) or DB session"]:::step
    S4{"4. State-Changing Request?<br/>(POST / PUT / PATCH / DELETE)"}:::branch
    CSRF["CSRF Defense-in-Depth<br/>Origin vs Host + session _csrf token"]:::step
    S5{"5. Static Asset Match?<br/>(/css, /js, /images, /uploads)"}:::branch
    StaticRes["Static File Handler<br/>Weak ETag check -> 304 Not Modified"]:::success
    S6["6. Global Onion Pipeline<br/>CORS preflight, Logging, Security headers"]:::step
    S7["7. O(1) & Prefix-Indexed Routing<br/>Static O(1) -> 2-level prefix buckets -> Wildcard"]:::step
    S8["8. Route Middleware & Handler<br/>auth, throttle, validate_or_fail() & Controller"]:::step
    S9["9. Response Coercion & Session Commit<br/>Map/List -> 200 JSON, String -> HTML, commit session"]:::step
    ClientRes(["HTTP Client Response<br/>(Status, Headers, Body stream)"]):::success

    ClientReq --> S1
    S1 --> S2
    S2 --> S3
    S3 --> S4
    S4 -- Yes --> CSRF
    S4 -- No --> S5
    CSRF --> S5
    S5 -- Yes (Static file) --> StaticRes
    S5 -- No (Dynamic route) --> S6
    S6 --> S7
    S7 --> S8
    S8 --> S9
    S9 --> ClientRes
    StaticRes --> ClientRes

FILE: docs/diagrams/router-architecture.mmd
================================================
flowchart TD
    classDef path fill:#f8fafc,stroke:#64748b,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#0f172a;
    classDef stage1 fill:#eff6ff,stroke:#2563eb,stroke-width:2px,color:#1e40af;
    classDef stage2 fill:#f0fdf4,stroke:#16a34a,stroke-width:2px,color:#14532d;
    classDef stage3 fill:#fffbeb,stroke:#d97706,stroke-width:2px,color:#78350f;
    classDef match fill:#10b981,stroke:#047857,stroke-width:2px,color:#ffffff;
    classDef err fill:#fef2f2,stroke:#dc2626,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#991b1b;

    ReqPath["Incoming Request: [METHOD] /path/to/resource<br/>Pre-split segments: ['path', 'to', 'resource']"]:::path
    S1{"Stage 1: Static Route?<br/><code>static_routes[method][path]</code>"}:::stage1
    DisStatic["Dispatch Static Route (O(1))"]:::match

    S2{"Stage 2: Segment Count Buckets?<br/><code>dynamic_by_len[method][seg_count]</code>"}:::stage2
    Prefix2{"Two-Level Prefix Match?<br/><code>bucket[s1/s2]</code> or <code>bucket[s1]</code>"}:::stage2
    Extract["Extract Route Params<br/><code>:id</code> or <code>{id}</code> into <code>req.params</code>"]:::match
    DisDynamic["Dispatch Dynamic Route Handler"]:::match

    S3{"Stage 3: Wildcard Route?<br/><code>wildcard_routes[method]</code> (*path)"}:::stage3
    DisWildcard["Dispatch Wildcard Handler"]:::match

    S4{"Stage 4: Root Static File?<br/>(robots.txt, favicon.ico, sitemap.xml)"}:::stage3
    ServeRoot["Serve Static File (ETag 304)"]:::match

    S5{"Stage 5: Path matches other method?"}:::err
    Err405["405 Method Not Allowed<br/>(Header: Allow: GET, POST)"]:::err
    Err404["404 Not Found<br/>(Or router.fallback_handler)"]:::err

    ReqPath --> S1
    S1 -- Hit (O(1)) --> DisStatic
    S1 -- Miss --> S2
    S2 -- Found Seg Bucket --> Prefix2
    Prefix2 -- Prefix Hit --> Extract --> DisDynamic
    Prefix2 -- Miss --> S3
    S2 -- No Seg Match --> S3
    S3 -- Wildcard Hit --> DisWildcard
    S3 -- Miss --> S4
    S4 -- Found File --> ServeRoot
    S4 -- Miss --> S5
    S5 -- Yes --> Err405
    S5 -- No --> Err404

sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    actor TestSuite as Test Suite (tests/*.matrix)
    participant Client as TestClient
    participant Kernel as Application.handle_request()
    participant MW as Onion Pipeline & Router
    participant App as Controller / Models / DB
    participant Res as TestResponse

    Note over TestSuite, Client: 1. Fluent Setup & Request Assembly
    TestSuite->>Client: app.test_client()
    TestSuite->>Client: .acting_as(user) [mints HS256 JWT]
    TestSuite->>Client: .post_json("/api/posts", payload)

    Note over Client, Kernel: 2. In-Memory Direct Kernel Dispatch (0 Sockets / 0 Ports)
    Client->>Kernel: handle_request(raw_req_map)
    Kernel->>MW: Run Global MW, Auth & Prefix Router
    MW->>App: Invoke Controller & QueryBuilder
    App-->>MW: Return Model / JSON Map
    MW-->>Kernel: Return HTTP Response Map

    Note over Kernel, Res: 3. Fluent Assertions & Dot-Path Inspection
    Kernel-->>Client: raw_res {status, headers, body}
    Client->>Res: new TestResponse(raw_res)
    TestSuite->>Res: .assert_created() [checks 201]
    TestSuite->>Res: .assert_json_path("data.title", expected)
    TestSuite->>Res: .assert_header("content-type", "application/json")

================================================================================
SPID3R_WEB v2.0 — AI AGENT CHANGELOG & MIGRATION GUIDE (changes.txt)
================================================================================
Repository : https://github.com/needyamin/spid3r_web
Commit     : 514791a (feat: evolve Spid3rWeb to v2 Modern MVC framework)
Target     : AI Coding Agents & Developers upgrading downstream Spid3rWeb apps
Engine     : Spid3r Tree-Walk Interpreter & Bytecode VM (`--engine vm`)
================================================================================

1. EXECUTIVE SUMMARY FOR AI AGENTS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Spid3rWeb has evolved into a v2 Modern MVC & Express/Fastify-style framework.
All changes are 100% BACKWARDS-COMPATIBLE with existing v1 routes, controllers,
middleware, models, and views, while introducing major syntax simplifications,
new subsystems (JWT, Sessions/Flash, File Uploads, Signed Cookies, TestClient),
SpiderBD v2 template directives, thread-safety mutexes, and a 153x faster router
(133,333 ops/sec on `--engine vm`).

When updating an existing application that uses Spid3rWeb, read Section 3
(New APIs & Syntax) and Section 4 (Step-by-Step Upgrade Instructions).

================================================================================
2. FILE & MODULE INVENTORY (WHAT CHANGED IN THE FRAMEWORK)
================================================================================

NEW FILES ADDED:
- `framework/Web.matrix`
  Single-import barrel module exporting `web()`, `router()`, `model()`,
  `route()`, `abort()`, `abort_if()`, `db()`, `schema()`, `cache()`, `auth()`,
  `jwt()`, `views()`, `validate()`, `json_out()`, `html_out()`, `text_out()`,
  `redirect_to()`, `test_client()`.
- `framework/Auth/Jwt.matrix`
  Zero-dependency RFC 7519 HS256 JWT implementation (`Jwt.sign`, `Jwt.verify`,
  `Jwt.decode_unverified`) using constant-time `crypto_equals`.
- `framework/Session/Session.matrix`
  Cookie-backed (`spid3r_sid`) `SessionStore` with key-value persistence
  (`get`, `put`, `forget`, `flush`, `regenerate`) and one-time flash messages
  (`flash`, `get_flash`).
- `framework/Testing/TestClient.matrix`
  In-memory HTTP integration testing client (`TestClient`) + `TestResponse`
  fluent assertions (`assert_status`, `assert_ok`, `assert_created`,
  `assert_not_found`, `assert_unprocessable`, `assert_json_path`,
  `assert_header`, `assert_see`).
- `tests/test_modern_mvc.matrix`
  End-to-end test suite verifying all v2 MVC features.

MODIFIED FILES:
- `framework/Router.matrix`          (O(1) static + 2-level indexed dynamic router, `:param`, `*wildcard`, `mount()`, `url()`)
- `framework/Application.matrix`     (Direct routing `app.get/post/put/patch/delete/group/mount/fallback`, auto-coercion, `(req, res)` handlers, static ETag `304`)
- `framework/Middleware.matrix`      (True Onion `(req, next)` pipeline, closure middleware, `etag`, `request_id`, `throttle` rate-limit headers)
- `framework/Request.matrix`         (`_method` spoofing, cookies, `signed_cookie`, multipart `file`/`has_file`/`store_file`, `only`/`except`/`bool`/`float`/`page`/`per_page`, `validate_or_fail`, `session`/`flash`)
- `framework/Response.matrix`        (Fluent `.status().json()/.html()/.text()/.send()/.redirect()`, `signed_cookie`, `clear_cookie`, cached HSTS)
- `framework/Validator.matrix`       (`.validated()` type coercion, `url`, `uuid`, `regex`, `between`, `list`, `map`, `unique:table,col`, `exists:table,col`)
- `framework/Database/Model.matrix`  (`define_model` / `ModelRepository`, `casts`, `hidden`, `is_dirty()`, `get_dirty()`, `find_or_fail`, `first_or_fail`)
- `framework/Database/QueryBuilder.matrix` (`find_or_fail`, `first_or_fail`, `latest`, `oldest`)
- `framework/Database/DatabaseManager.matrix` (Mutex-serialized `transaction()`)
- `framework/Auth/Auth.matrix`       (Automatic stateless JWT `Bearer` verification fallback + `issue_jwt()`)
- `framework/View/BD.matrix`         (SpiderBD v2: `file_mtime` cache, `@if` operators `== != >= <= > < and or not`, `@elif`, inline `@if`, `@foreach` `loop`, `@csrf`, `@method`, `share()`)
- `framework/Cache/CacheStore.matrix` & `Cache.matrix` (Mutex thread-safety, 10k max-entry eviction, atomic `add`/`increment`/`decrement`)
- `framework/Log/Log.matrix`         (Fixed file append bug via `append_file`, added mutex, cached log level)
- `framework/HttpException.matrix`   (Added `errors` field for 422 validation exceptions + `ValidationException()`)
- `framework/Controller.matrix`      (Cleaned imports, added `created()`, `no_content()`, `paginate()`)
- `framework/Console/ConsoleKernel.matrix` & `TestRunner.matrix` (Updated `make:controller`, `make:model`, `make:middleware`, `make:test` generators to v2 syntax)

DELETED FILES:
- `docs/history/PROJECT.md`          (Removed obsolete storefront history)

================================================================================
3. DETAILED API CHANGES & NEW CAPABILITIES
================================================================================

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.1 Single-Import Barrel (`framework/Web`)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
BEFORE (v1):
```spid3r
import "framework/Application";
import "framework/Router";
import "framework/Response";
import "framework/Database/DatabaseManager";
import "framework/Database/Model";
import "framework/Validator";
```

NOW (v2):
```spid3r
import "framework/Web";

let app = web(); // Bootstraps Application + ServiceProviders (or empty app if no config/app.matrix)
```

Exported helper functions from `import "framework/Web"`:
- `web(base_path = "")`         -> Returns bootstrapped `Application` instance
- `router()`                    -> Returns new `Router` instance (for sub-routers)
- `model(table, options = {})`  -> Defines a zero-boilerplate `ModelRepository`
- `route(name, params = {})`    -> Generates URL for a named route
- `abort(status, message = "")` -> Throws `HttpException(status, message)`
- `abort_if(cond, status, msg)` -> Conditionally throws `HttpException`
- `db()` / `schema()`           -> Returns `DatabaseManager` / `Schema` builder
- `cache()` / `auth()` / `jwt()` / `views()` -> Returns core singletons
- `validate(data, rules)`       -> Runs validator and returns coerced map or throws 422
- `test_client(app = nil)`      -> Returns `TestClient` for integration testing

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.2 Application Direct Routing, Sub-Routers & Return Auto-Coercion
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. Direct Routing on `app`:
   - `app.get(path, handler)`
   - `app.post(path, handler)`
   - `app.put(path, handler)`
   - `app.patch(path, handler)`
   - `app.delete(path, handler)`
   - `app.any(path, handler)`
   - `app.group(prefix_or_attrs, callback)`
   - `app.mount(prefix, sub_router)`
   - `app.fallback(handler)`
   - `app.use(middleware)`
   - `app.listen(port = 8000, host = "0.0.0.0")` (alias for `app.serve()`)

2. Route Parameter Syntaxes (All 3 supported):
   - Express-style `:param`:   `app.get("/users/:id/posts/:post_id", ...)`
   - Brace-style `{param}`:    `app.get("/users/{id}/posts/{post_id}", ...)`
   - Catch-all `*wildcard`:    `app.get("/files/*filepath", req => req.param("filepath"))`

3. Reverse Routing (URL Generation):
   ```spid3r
   app.get("/users/:id", handler).name("users.show");
   let url = route("users.show", {"id": 42, "tab": "profile"});
   // -> "/users/42?tab=profile"
   ```

4. Handler Signatures & Return Auto-Coercion:
   - 1-arg `req => ...` OR 2-arg `(req, res) => ...`
   - Returning a `map` or `list` automatically serializes to `200 application/json`.
   - Returning a `string` automatically wraps in `200 text/html; charset=utf-8`.
   - Returning a `Response` object uses it directly.

   ```spid3r
   // Auto-coerced 200 JSON:
   app.get("/api/ping", req => {"ok": true});

   // Express-style 2-arg handler with custom status:
   app.post("/api/items", (req, res) => {
       return res.status(201).json({"created": true});
   });
   ```

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.3 True Onion Middleware Pipeline
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Middleware now supports full before- AND after-response interception via `next`:

1. Closure Middleware:
   ```spid3r
   app.use((req, next) => {
       let res = next(req);
       return res.header("X-Powered-By", "Spid3rWeb");
   });
   ```

2. Class Middleware (2-arg `handle(req, next)` or legacy 1-arg `handle(req)`):
   ```spid3r
   class TimingMiddleware {
       fn handle(req, next) {
           let t0 = clock_ms();
           let res = next(req);
           return res.header("X-Response-Time", to_string(clock_ms() - t0) + "ms");
       }
   }
   ```

3. Built-in Named Middleware Aliases:
   - `"auth"`             -> Verifies session token OR stateless JWT (`Bearer <token>`)
   - `"guest"`            -> Rejects authenticated requests
   - `"cors"`             -> Injects CORS headers & handles `OPTIONS` preflight
   - `"logger"`           -> Structured HTTP request log
   - `"etag"`             -> Computes weak ETag (`W/"<sha256>"`) and returns `304 Not Modified` on `If-None-Match`
   - `"request_id"`       -> Propagates or generates `X-Request-Id` UUID v4 on `req.id` and response header
   - `"throttle:max,win"` -> Atomic rate limiter; attaches `X-RateLimit-Limit`, `X-RateLimit-Remaining`, and `Retry-After` headers

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.4 Request Helpers, Validation, Cookies & Multipart File Uploads
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
New methods on `Request` (`req`):
- `req.validate_or_fail(rules_map)`
  Validates input against rules, returns type-coerced map of validated fields,
  or automatically throws 422 `HttpException` with `{"error": "Unprocessable Entity", "errors": {...}}`.
- `req.only(["name", "email"])`  -> Subset map of present keys
- `req.except(["password"])`     -> All input keys except listed keys
- `req.bool(key, default = false)` -> Coerces `"true"`, `"1"`, `"yes"`, `"on"` to `true`
- `req.float(key, default = 0.0)`  -> Safe float conversion
- `req.page()`                     -> Current page (`>= 1`, default `1`)
- `req.per_page(default = 15, max_limit = 100)` -> Clamped per-page limit
- `req.bearer_token()`             -> Extracts token from `Authorization: Bearer ...`
- `req.cookies()` / `req.cookie(name, default = nil)` -> Parsed request cookies
- `req.signed_cookie(name, default = nil)` -> Verifies HMAC-SHA256 signed cookie (`val.sig`) using `APP_KEY`
- `req.has_file(field)` / `req.file(field)` -> Multipart file upload metadata `{"name", "filename", "content_type", "content", "size"}`
- `req.store_file(field, subdir = "uploads")` -> Sanitizes filename and stores via `Storage`
- `req.session()`                  -> Lazy-loaded `SessionStore`
- `req.flash(key, value)` / `req.get_flash(key, default = nil)` -> One-time flash messages
- HTML Form Method Spoofing: `POST` requests with `_method=PUT|PATCH|DELETE` or `X-HTTP-Method-Override` header automatically route as `PUT`/`PATCH`/`DELETE`.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.5 Response Fluent Chainable API
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
New/updated methods on `Response` (`res`):
- `res.status(code)`               -> Sets HTTP status and returns `self`
- `res.json(data)`                 -> Sets JSON body + `application/json` content-type
- `res.html(html_str)`             -> Sets HTML body + `text/html; charset=utf-8`
- `res.text(text_str)`             -> Sets plain text body + `text/plain; charset=utf-8`
- `res.send(payload)`              -> Auto-detects `map`/`list` (JSON) vs `string` (HTML)
- `res.redirect(url, status = 302)`-> Redirects to `url`
- `res.cookie(key, value, options = {})` -> Supports `path`, `max_age`, `domain`, `http_only`, `secure`, `same_site` (also backwards-compatible if 3rd arg is a path string)
- `res.signed_cookie(key, value, options = {})` -> Signs value with HMAC-SHA256 using `APP_KEY`
- `res.clear_cookie(key, path = "/")` -> Expires cookie immediately (`Max-Age=0`)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.6 Validator v2 Rules & Type Coercion
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
`Validator.make(data, rules)` now supports:
- `v.validated()` -> Returns ONLY declared rule keys, with `"int"`/`"integer"` coerced to `int`, `"float"`/`"numeric"` coerced to `float`, and `"bool"`/`"boolean"` coerced to `bool`.
- New rules:
  - `"nullable"` (skips validation when empty/nil)
  - `"float"` / `"boolean"` / `"list"` / `"map"`
  - `"url"` (http/https URL)
  - `"uuid"` (UUID format)
  - `"regex:pattern"` (PCRE/regex match)
  - `"between:min,max"` (numeric value or string length between min and max)
  - `"unique:table,column"` or `"unique:table,column,ignore_id"` (DB uniqueness check)
  - `"exists:table,column"` (DB foreign key existence check)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.7 Zero-Boilerplate ORM (`model()` / `ModelRepository`)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Define models in 1 line without writing a class, or subclass `Model`:

```spid3r
let Post = model("posts", {
    "primary_key": "id",
    "fillable": ["title", "body", "published", "meta", "secret_hash"],
    "hidden": ["secret_hash"],
    "casts": {
        "published": "bool",
        "meta": "json"
    }
});
```

Available methods on `Post` (`ModelRepository`):
- `Post.query()`
- `Post.all()`
- `Post.find(id)` / `Post.find_or_fail(id)` (throws 404 `HttpException` if missing)
- `Post.where(col, val)` / `Post.where_op(col, op, val)` / `Post.where_in(col, list)`
- `Post.first()` / `Post.first_or_fail()`
- `Post.latest(col = "id")` / `Post.oldest(col = "id")`
- `Post.create(data_map)`
- `Post.update_or_create(match_map, values_map)`
- `Post.paginate(page = 1, per_page = 15)`
- `Post.count()`
- `Post.load_has_many(records, rel_name, ForeignRepo, foreign_key, local_key = "id")`
- `Post.load_belongs_to(records, rel_name, OwnerRepo, foreign_key, owner_key = "id")`

Instance features on `Model` records:
- `record.to_map()` / `record.to_json()` -> Automatically applies `casts` (`int`, `float`, `bool`, `string`, `json`) and strips `hidden` attributes!
- `record.is_dirty(key = nil)` / `record.get_dirty()` -> Tracks modified fields since load/save so `save()` only updates changed columns.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.8 Stateless JWT (`Jwt`) & Hybrid `Auth`
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. `Jwt` (`framework/Auth/Jwt.matrix` or `jwt()` from `framework/Web`):
   ```spid3r
   let token = jwt().sign({"sub": 42, "role": "admin"}, 3600); // 1h TTL
   let res = jwt().verify(token); // Result (is_ok / is_err)
   let claims = unwrap(res);
   ```
2. `Auth` (`auth()`):
   - `auth().issue_jwt(user_map, ttl_seconds = 86400)` -> Issues a signed JWT for the user.
   - `auth().user(req)` / `"auth"` middleware -> Automatically checks DB `sessions` table first, and falls back to verifying stateless HS256 JWT tokens (`eyJ...`) with zero DB queries.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.9 SpiderBD v2 Template Engine (`framework/View/BD.matrix`)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
New SpiderBD v2 capabilities:
- Rich `@if` / `@elif` / `@else` expressions:
  Supports `==`, `!=`, `>=`, `<=`, `>`, `<`, `and`, `or`, `not`, string/number/boolean literals, and dot paths (`@if(user.role == 'admin' and user.points >= 100)`).
- Inline `@if(cond) ... @else ... @endif` on a single line.
- `@foreach(items as item)` injects `loop` metadata:
  - `{{ loop.index }}` (0-based)
  - `{{ loop.iteration }}` (1-based)
  - `{{ loop.first }}` (`true` on first item)
  - `{{ loop.last }}` (`true` on last item)
  - `{{ loop.count }}` (total items)
- `@csrf` -> Renders `<input type="hidden" name="_csrf" value="...">`
- `@method('PUT')` -> Renders `<input type="hidden" name="_method" value="PUT">`
- Global shared variables: `views().share("app_name", "MyApp")`
- `file_mtime` template line cache so templates are not re-read from disk on every request unless modified.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3.10 Integration Testing (`TestClient`)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Test routes, middleware, auth, sessions, and JSON responses in-memory without starting a socket server:

```spid3r
import "framework/Web";

let client = app.test_client();
client.acting_as({"id": 1, "name": "Alice", "role": "admin"})
      .post_json("/api/tasks", {"title": "Ship v2"})
      .assert_created()
      .assert_json_path("title", "Ship v2");
```

================================================================================
4. STEP-BY-STEP UPGRADE INSTRUCTIONS FOR DOWNSTREAM APPLICATIONS
================================================================================
If you are an AI agent updating another project that depends on `spid3r_web`:

1. COPY UPDATED FRAMEWORK DIRECTORY:
   Replace the downstream project's `framework/` folder with the `framework/`
   folder from this repository.

2. SIMPLIFY IMPORTS (OPTIONAL BUT RECOMMENDED):
   Replace multi-line `import "framework/..."` blocks in entrypoints, controllers,
   and tests with:
   ```spid3r
   import "framework/Web";
   ```

3. UPGRADE MIDDLEWARE CLASSES (IF CUSTOM MIDDLEWARE EXISTS):
   Existing 1-arg `fn handle(req)` middleware continues to work without changes.
   To inspect or modify the response in custom middleware, update the signature
   to `fn handle(req, next)` and call `let res = next(req);`.

4. UPGRADE VALIDATION BOILERPLATE IN CONTROLLERS/ROUTES:
   Replace manual `Validator.make(...)` + `if v.fails() { return Response.json(..., 422); }`
   blocks with:
   ```spid3r
   let data = req.validate_or_fail({
       "field": "required|..."
   });
   ```

5. UPGRADE MODEL BOILERPLATE:
   Replace repetitive model subclasses with `model("table_name", {"fillable": [...], "casts": {...}, "hidden": [...]})`
   and replace manual `find` + `if nil return 404` checks with `Model.find_or_fail(id)`.

6. RUN VERIFICATION:
   Execute both test runners from the application root:
   ```bash
   SPID3R_DB_DRIVER=sqlite spid3r magic test
   SPID3R_DB_DRIVER=sqlite spid3r magic test --engine=vm
   ```
================================================================================



================================================
FILE: examples/task-api/main.matrix
================================================
// examples/task-api/main.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Task API â€” a complete, runnable full-stack MVC example.
//
// Demonstrates the modern Spid3rWeb framework end to end WITHOUT opening a port:
// single-import barrel (`framework/Web`), zero-boilerplate `model()` repository,
// service providers, model events, `req.validate_or_fail()`, `find_or_fail()`,
// REST resource envelopes, pagination, and `TestClient` assertions.
//
// Run it:
//   SPID3R_DB_DRIVER=sqlite spid3r run examples/task-api/main.matrix
// Under the bytecode VM:
//   SPID3R_DB_DRIVER=sqlite spid3r run --engine vm examples/task-api/main.matrix

import "framework/Web";
import "framework/Foundation/ServiceProvider";
import "framework/Http/Resource";

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 1. Zero-boilerplate domain model + service provider
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

let Task = model("tasks", {
    "fillable": ["title", "done", "priority"],
    "casts": {
        "done": "bool",
        "priority": "int"
    }
});

class TaskProvider extends ServiceProvider {
    public fn register(self, container: any) {
        let _ = self;
        container.singleton("task.events_fired", fn(_c) {
            return {
                "created": 0, "deleted": 0
            };
        });
    }

    public fn boot(self, container: any) {
        let _ = self;
        let counter = container.make("task.events_fired");
        events().listen("model.tasks.created", fn(_task, _name) {
            counter["created"] = counter["created"] + 1;
            return null;
        });
        events().listen("model.tasks.deleted", fn(_task, _name) {
            counter["deleted"] = counter["deleted"] + 1;
            return null;
        });
    }
}

let task_transformer = fn(t) {
    return {
        "id": t["id"],
        "title": t["title"],
        "done": t["done"] == 1 or t["done"] == true,
        "priority": int(t["priority"])
    };
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 2. Clean MVC Controller
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TaskController {
    public fn index(self, req: any) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        let page = Task.orderBy("priority", "DESC").paginate(req.page(), req.per_page(10));
        return resource_paginated(page, task_transformer);
    }

    public fn store(self, req: any, res: any) -> Response {
        let _ = self;
        let data = req.validate_or_fail({
            "title": "required|min:3|max:200",
            "priority": "nullable|integer|between:1,9"
        });
        let prio = 1;
        if data["priority"] != null {
            prio = data["priority"];
        }
        let task = Task.create({
            "title": data["title"],
            "priority": prio,
            "done": false
        });
        return res.status(201).json(resource_item(task, task_transformer));
    }

    public fn show(self, req: any) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        let task = Task.find_or_fail(req.int("id"), "No task with that id.");
        return resource_item(task, task_transformer);
    }

    public fn destroy(self, req: any) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        let task = Task.find_or_fail(req.int("id"), "No task with that id.");
        let snapshot = task.to_map();
        task.delete();
        return resource_item(snapshot, task_transformer);
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 3. App assembly + demo run
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn show(label: string, test_res) {
    let st = test_res.status;
    println("---- {label} -> {st}");
    let body = test_res.body;
    if len(body) > 220 {
        body = body.slice(0, 220) + " ...";
    }
    println(body);
}

fn main() {
    schema().dropIfExists("tasks");
    schema().create("tasks", t => {
        t.id();
        t.string("title");
        t.integer("done").default(0);
        t.integer("priority").default(1);
        t.timestamps();
    });

    let app = web();
    app.register(new TaskProvider());

    let ctl = new TaskController();
    app.get("/api/tasks", req => ctl.index(req));
    app.post("/api/tasks", (req, res) => ctl.store(req, res));
    app.get("/api/tasks/:id", req => ctl.show(req));
    app.delete("/api/tasks/:id", req => ctl.destroy(req));

    let client = app.test_client();

    println("=== Spid3rWeb Task API demo ===\n");

    // 1. Validation failure -> 422 with field errors.
    let r_invalid = client.post_json("/api/tasks", {
        "priority": "2"
    }).assert_unprocessable();
    show("POST invalid (no title)", r_invalid);

    // 2. Create -> 201 + resource envelope.
    let r_created = client.post_json("/api/tasks", {
        "title": "Ship Spid3rWeb 2.0", "priority": "5"
    })
    .assert_created()
    .assert_json_path("data.title", "Ship Spid3rWeb 2.0")
    .assert_json_path("data.priority", 5);
    show("POST valid", r_created);

    // 3. Index -> paginated collection envelope.
    let r_index = client.get("/api/tasks").assert_ok().assert_json_path("meta.total", 1);
    show("GET /api/tasks", r_index);

    // 4. Typed 404 from a missing id.
    let r_missing = client.get("/api/tasks/999").assert_not_found();
    show("GET /api/tasks/999", r_missing);

    // 5. Show a real one, then delete it.
    let r_show = client.get("/api/tasks/1").assert_ok().assert_json_path("data.id", 1);
    show("GET /api/tasks/1", r_show);

    let r_del = client.delete("/api/tasks/1").assert_ok();
    show("DELETE /api/tasks/1", r_del);

    // 6. Proof the provider's model-event listeners ran.
    let counter = app.container.make("task.events_fired");
    let created_n = counter["created"];
    let deleted_n = counter["deleted"];
    println(
        "---- model.tasks.created events observed: {created_n}, deleted: {deleted_n}"
    );

    schema().dropIfExists("tasks");
    db().close();
    println("\n=== Demo finished cleanly ===");
    return 0;
}

// framework/Web.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Single-Import Barrel Module
//
// Build complete apps or microservices with a single import:
//   import "framework/Web";
//   let app = web();
//   let Post = model("posts", {"fillable": ["title", "body"]});
//   app.get("/api/posts", req => Post.paginate(req.page(), req.per_page()));

import "framework/Foundation/Application";
import "framework/Http/Router";
import "framework/Http/Request";
import "framework/Http/Response";
import "framework/Http/Controller";
import "framework/Http/Middleware";
import "framework/Http/Validator";
import "framework/Http/Resource";
import "framework/Http/HttpException";

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Application, Router, Model & TestClient Factories

/// Create a new Spid3rWeb Application kernel rooted at `base_path`.
public fn web(base_path: string = ".") -> Application {
    return new Application(base_path);
}

/// Create an isolated sub-Router suitable for mounting on an Application.
public fn router() -> Router {
    return new Router();
}

/// Generate a URL for a named route with optional parameter substitution.
public fn route(name: string, params: any = null) -> string {
    return Route.url(name, params);
}

/// Create a zero-boilerplate ModelRepository for `table_name`.
public fn model(table_name: string, opts: map = {
}) -> ModelRepository {
    return new ModelRepository(table_name, opts);
}

/// Abort the active request with a typed HTTP status code and optional errors.
public fn abort(status: int, message: string = "", errors: any = null) {
    abort_http(status, message, errors);
}

/// Create a fluent in-memory TestClient bound to `app`.
public fn test_client(app: any) -> TestClient {
    return new TestClient(app);
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Response & View Helpers
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Build a JSON Response with the given payload and HTTP status code.
public fn send_json(data: any, status: int = 200) -> Response {
    return json(data, status);
}

/// Build an HTML Response with security headers applied.
public fn send_html(content: string, status: int = 200) -> Response {
    return html(content, status);
}

/// Build a plain-text Response with UTF-8 content type.
public fn send_text(content: string, status: int = 200) -> Response {
    return text(content, status);
}

/// Build an HTTP redirect Response to `url` with default status 302.
public fn redirect_to(url: string, status: int = 302) -> Response {
    return redirect(url, status);
}

/// Build a chunked streaming HTTP Response backed by a callback or list.
public fn send_stream(
    source: any,
    content_type: string = "application/octet-stream",
    status: int = 200
) -> Response {
    return stream(source, content_type, status);
}

/// Build a Server-Sent Events (text/event-stream) Response.
public fn send_sse(source: any, status: int = 200) -> Response {
    return sse(source, status);
}

/// Render a SpiderBD v2 template by dot-notation view name.
public fn render_view(view_name: string, data: any = null) -> Response {
    return view(view_name, data);
}

/// Return the global SpiderBD v2 view engine instance.
public fn views() -> BD {
    return global_bd;
}

// Subsystem Accessors
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Return the DB facade, or a QueryBuilder scoped to `table` if non-empty.
public fn db(table: string = "") -> any {
    if table != "" {
        return DB.table(table);
    }
    return DB;
}

    return Schema;
}

}

public fn hash() -> HashManager {

}

}

/// Return the background Queue manager facade.
public fn queue() -> QueueManager {
    return Queue;
}

/// Return the Mail delivery facade.
public fn mail() -> Mailer {
    return Mail;
}

/// Return the Storage manager, or a configured disk when `disk_name` is non-empty.
public fn storage(disk_name: string = "") -> any {
    if disk_name != "" {
        return Storage.disk(disk_name);
    }
    return Storage;
}

/// Return the global synchronous Event dispatcher.
public fn events() -> EventDispatcher {
    return Event;
}

public fn config(key: string = "", default_val: any = null) -> any {
    if key != "" {
        return Config.get(key, default_val);
    }
    return Config;
}

// framework/Auth/Auth.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Authentication State Manager & Facade

import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Auth/Hash";
import "framework/Auth/Jwt";
import "framework/Foundation/Config";

/// Request-scoped authentication and CSRF manager supporting DB sessions and stateless Bearer JWTs.
public class AuthManager {
    public let current_user;
    public let current_token;
    // Per-session CSRF token (stored in the sessions.payload column). Only
    // meaningful for cookie-based sessions â€” Bearer-token API clients are
    // not CSRF-attackable, so they never need it.
    public let csrf_token;

    fn new () {
        self.current_user = null;
        self.current_token = "";
        self.csrf_token = "";
    }

    public fn attempt(self, email: string, password: string) -> bool {
        let clean = string(email).trim().lower();
        let table = string(Config.get("auth.guards.session.table", "users"));
        let username_col = string(Config.get("auth.guards.session.username_column", "email"));
        let password_col = string(
            Config.get("auth.guards.session.password_column", "password")
        );
        let users = DB.table(table).where(username_col, "=", clean).limit(1).get();
        if users.len() == 0 {
            return false;
        }

        let user = users[0];
        let hash_val = string(user[password_col]);

        if Hash.check(password, hash_val) {
            if Hash.needs_rehash(hash_val) {
                try {
                    let upgraded = Hash.make(password);
                    DB.table(table).where(username_col, "=", clean).update({
                        password_col: upgraded
                    });
                } catch (_e) {
                }
            }
            self.login(user);
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    private fn sanitize_user(self, row: map) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        let safe = {
        };
        for (k, v) in row {
            if k != "password" and k != "session_payload" {
                safe[k] = v;
            }
        }
        return safe;
    }

    // Mint a stateless JWT for a user map and mark the current AuthManager as logged in.
    public fn jwt(self, user: map, ttl: int = 3600) -> string {
        let safe_user = self.sanitize_user(user);
        let token = Jwt.sign(safe_user, ttl);
        self.current_user = safe_user;
        self.current_token = token;
        return token;
    }

    public fn login(self, user: map) {
        // Password hashes must never linger in auth state â€” views and API
        // responses render from this map.
        let safe_user = self.sanitize_user(user);
        self.current_user = safe_user;
        // Unpredictable CSPRNG session token (192 bits)
        self.current_token = "sess_" + hex_encode(random_bytes(24).unwrap());
        // Unpredictable per-session CSRF token (128 bits)
        self.csrf_token = "csrf_" + hex_encode(random_bytes(16).unwrap());

        // Store active session in sessions table if exists. The payload
        // column carries the CSRF token so authenticate_token() can verify
        // form submissions against it.
        try {
            let tbl = self.sessions_table();
            DB.statement(
                "INSERT INTO {tbl} (token, user_id, payload, last_activity) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                [self.current_token,
                user["id"],
                self.csrf_token,
                unix_time()]
            );
        } catch (_e) {
            // sessions table optional
        }
    }

    public fn authenticate_token(self, token: string) -> bool {
        if token == "" {
            return false;
        }

        // Stateless JWT fast-path: if token has 3 dot-separated segments, verify HS256 JWT without DB hit
        if token.contains(".") and token.split(".").len() == 3 {
            let claims = Jwt.verify(token);
            if claims != null {
                self.current_user = claims;
                self.current_token = token;
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        try {
            // Server-side session TTL: session.lifetime seconds of inactivity
            // expires a session (config/session.matrix).
            let cutoff = unix_time() - self.session_lifetime();
            let tbl = self.sessions_table();
            let rows = DB.select(
                "SELECT u.*, s.payload AS session_payload FROM users u JOIN {tbl} s ON u.id = s.user_id WHERE s.token = ? AND s.last_activity >= ?",
                [token,
                cutoff]
            );
            if rows.len() > 0 {
                let user_row = rows[0];
                let session_payload = "";
                if user_row.has("session_payload") and user_row["session_payload"] != null {
                    session_payload = string(user_row["session_payload"]);
                }
                // Never expose the password hash through auth state.
                self.current_user = self.sanitize_user(user_row);
                self.current_token = token;
                if session_payload != "" {
                    self.csrf_token = session_payload;
                }
                // Sliding expiration: touch last_activity on use
                try {
                    DB.statement(
                        "UPDATE {tbl} SET last_activity = ? WHERE token = ?",
                        [unix_time(),
                        token]
                    );
                } catch (_e2) {
                }
                return true;
            }
            // Expired or unknown token â€” clean it up so dead sessions
            // don't accumulate forever.
            try {
                DB.statement(
                    "DELETE FROM {tbl} WHERE token = ?",
                    [token]
                );
            } catch (_e3) {
            }
        } catch (_e) {
            // fallback
        }
        return false;
    }

    public fn logout(self) {
        if self.current_token != "" and not self.current_token.contains(".") {
            try {
                let tbl = self.sessions_table();
                DB.statement(
                    "DELETE FROM {tbl} WHERE token = ?",
                    [self.current_token]
                );
            } catch (_e) {
            }
        }
        self.current_user = null;
        self.current_token = "";
        self.csrf_token = "";
    }

    // Config-driven knobs (config/auth.matrix + config/session.matrix)
    // SQL-identifier safety: config-sourced table names are interpolated
    // into raw SQL, so they are validated against [A-Za-z0-9_] before use.
    private fn safe_identifier(self, name: string, fallback: string) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        let allowed = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789_";
        let s = string(name).trim();
        if s.len() == 0 {
            return fallback;
        }
        let i = 0;
        while i < s.len() {
            if not allowed.contains(s.slice(i, i + 1)) {
                throw "auth: invalid identifier '{s}' (allowed: A-Z a-z 0-9 _)";
            }
            i = i + 1;
        }
        return s;
    }

    private fn sessions_table(self) -> string {
        return self.safe_identifier(
            string(Config.get("session.table", "sessions")),
            "sessions"
        );
    }

    private fn session_lifetime(self) -> int {
        let _ = self;
        return int(Config.get("session.lifetime", 86_400));
    }

    public fn clear(self) {
        self.current_user = null;
        self.current_token = "";
        self.csrf_token = "";
    }

    public fn check(self) -> bool {
        return self.current_user != null;
    }

    public fn guest(self) -> bool {
        return self.current_user == null;
    }

    public fn user(self) -> any {
        return self.current_user;
    }

    public fn id(self) -> any {
        if self.current_user != null and self.current_user.has("id") {
            return self.current_user["id"];
        }
        return null;
    }

    public fn token(self) -> string {
        return self.current_token;
    }

    // CSRF token for this session ("" for guests and Bearer sessions)
    public fn csrf(self) -> string {
        return self.csrf_token;
    }
}

public let Auth = new AuthManager();

FILE: framework/Auth/Hash.matrix

// framework/Auth/Hash.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Password Hashing Facade (Argon2id + Bcrypt with Rehash Support)

import "framework/Foundation/Config";

const ARGON2_DEFAULT_MEM = 19_456;
const ARGON2_DEFAULT_TIME = 2;
const ARGON2_DEFAULT_LANES = 1;
const ARGON2_DEFAULT_LEN = 32;
const BCRYPT_DEFAULT_ROUNDS = 10;

fn parse_int_or(text: string, fallback: int) -> int {
    try {
        return int(string(text).trim());
    } catch (_e) {
        return fallback;
    }
}

/// Password hashing facade supporting Argon2id (default), Bcrypt, and transparent rehash checks.
public class HashManager {
    fn new () {
        let _ = self;
    }

    private fn resolve_options(self, opts: any = null) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        let driver = string(Config.get("auth.hashing.driver", "argon2id")).trim().lower();
        let env_drv = env("SPID3R_HASH_DRIVER");
        if env_drv.is_some() and string(env_drv.unwrap()).trim() != "" {
            driver = string(env_drv.unwrap()).trim().lower();
        }
        let variant = string(Config.get("auth.hashing.argon2.variant", "id")).trim().lower();
        let time_cost = int(Config.get("auth.hashing.argon2.time_cost", ARGON2_DEFAULT_TIME));
        let memory_cost = int(
            Config.get("auth.hashing.argon2.memory_cost", ARGON2_DEFAULT_MEM)
        );
        let parallelism = int(
            Config.get("auth.hashing.argon2.parallelism", ARGON2_DEFAULT_LANES)
        );
        let key_len = int(Config.get("auth.hashing.argon2.key_len", ARGON2_DEFAULT_LEN));
        let rounds = int(Config.get("auth.hashing.bcrypt.rounds", BCRYPT_DEFAULT_ROUNDS));

        if opts != null and type_of(opts) == "map" {
            if opts.has("driver") {
                driver = string(opts["driver"]).trim().lower();
            }
            if opts.has("variant") {
                variant = string(opts["variant"]).trim().lower();
            }
            if opts.has("time_cost") {
                time_cost = int(opts["time_cost"]);
            }
            if opts.has("memory_cost") {
                memory_cost = int(opts["memory_cost"]);
            }
            if opts.has("parallelism") {
                parallelism = int(opts["parallelism"]);
            }
            if opts.has("key_len") {
                key_len = int(opts["key_len"]);
            }
            if opts.has("rounds") {
                rounds = int(opts["rounds"]);
            }
        }
        if driver == "argon2i" {
            variant = "i";
            driver = "argon2id";
        } elif driver == "argon2d" {
            variant = "d";
            driver = "argon2id";
        } elif driver == "argon2" {
            driver = "argon2id";
        }
        if variant != "i" and variant != "d" and variant != "id" {
            variant = "id";
        }
        if rounds < 4 {
            rounds = 4;
        } elif rounds > 31 {
            rounds = 31;
        }
        return {
            "driver": driver,
            "variant": variant,
            "time_cost": time_cost,
            "memory_cost": memory_cost,
            "parallelism": parallelism,
            "key_len": key_len,
            "rounds": rounds
        };
    }

    public fn make(self, plain_text: string, opts: any = null) -> string {
        let cfg = self.resolve_options(opts);
        if cfg["driver"] == "bcrypt" {
            return bcrypt_hash(plain_text, int(cfg["rounds"]));
        }
        let salt = random_bytes(16).unwrap();
        let salt_hex = hex_encode(salt);
        let variant = cfg["variant"];
        let m = int(cfg["memory_cost"]);
        let t = int(cfg["time_cost"]);
        let p = int(cfg["parallelism"]);
        let kl = int(cfg["key_len"]);
        let digest = argon2(plain_text, salt, t, m, p, variant, kl);
        let prefix = "$argon2" + variant + "$v=19$m=" + string(m) + ",t=" + string(t) + ",p=" + string(p);
        return prefix + "$" + salt_hex + "$" + digest;
    }

    public fn info(self, hashed: string) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        let h = string(hashed);
        if h.starts_with("$argon2") {
            let parts = h.split("$");
            if parts.len() == 6 {
                let algo_name = parts[1];
                let variant = algo_name.replace("argon2", "");
                let mem = ARGON2_DEFAULT_MEM;
                let time = ARGON2_DEFAULT_TIME;
                let lanes = ARGON2_DEFAULT_LANES;
                for kv in parts[3].split(",") {
                    if kv.starts_with("m=") {
                        mem = parse_int_or(kv.slice(2, kv.len()), mem);
                    } elif kv.starts_with("t=") {
                        time = parse_int_or(kv.slice(2, kv.len()), time);
                    } elif kv.starts_with("p=") {
                        lanes = parse_int_or(kv.slice(2, kv.len()), lanes);
                    }
                }
                let kl = parts[5].len() / 2;
                if kl <= 0 {
                    kl = ARGON2_DEFAULT_LEN;
                }
                return {
                    "algo": algo_name,
                    "options": {
                        "variant": variant,
                        "memory_cost": mem,
                        "time_cost": time,
                        "parallelism": lanes,
                        "key_len": kl
                    }
                };
            }
        }
        if h.starts_with("$2a$") or h.starts_with("$2b$") or h.starts_with("$2y$") {
            let bparts = h.split("$");
            let rounds = BCRYPT_DEFAULT_ROUNDS;
            if bparts.len() >= 4 {
                rounds = parse_int_or(bparts[2], rounds);
            }
            return {
                "algo": "bcrypt",
                "options": {
                    "rounds": rounds
                }
            };
        }
        return {
            "algo": "unknown",
            "options": {
            }
        };
    }

    public fn check(self, plain_text: string, hashed: string) -> bool {
        let h = string(hashed);
        if h.starts_with("$argon2") {
            try {
                let parts = h.split("$");
                if parts.len() != 6 {
                    return false;
                }
                let meta = self.info(h);
                let opts = meta["options"];
                let salt = hex_decode(parts[4]);
                let computed = argon2(
                    plain_text,
                    salt,
                    int(opts["time_cost"]),
                    int(opts["memory_cost"]),
                    int(opts["parallelism"]),
                    string(opts["variant"]),
                    int(opts["key_len"])
                );
                return secure_compare(computed, parts[5]);
            } catch (_e) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        try {
            return bcrypt_verify(plain_text, h);
        } catch (_e) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    public fn needs_rehash(self, hashed: string, opts: any = null) -> bool {
        let target = self.resolve_options(opts);
        let parsed = self.info(hashed);
        let algo = parsed["algo"];
        let cur = parsed["options"];
        if target["driver"] == "bcrypt" {
            if algo != "bcrypt" {
                return true;
            }
            let cur_rounds = if cur.has("rounds") {
                int(cur["rounds"])
            } else {
                0
            };
            return cur_rounds != int(target["rounds"]);
        }
        let target_algo = "argon2" + string(target["variant"]);
        if algo != target_algo {
            return true;
        }
        let cur_m = if cur.has("memory_cost") {
            int(cur["memory_cost"])
        } else {
            0
        };
        let cur_t = if cur.has("time_cost") {
            int(cur["time_cost"])
        } else {
            0
        };
        let cur_p = if cur.has("parallelism") {
            int(cur["parallelism"])
        } else {
            0
        };
        let cur_kl = if cur.has("key_len") {
            int(cur["key_len"])
        } else {
            0
        };
        return cur_m != int(target["memory_cost"])
        or cur_t != int(target["time_cost"])
        or cur_p != int(target["parallelism"])
        or cur_kl != int(target["key_len"]);
    }
}

public let Hash = new HashManager();



================================================
FILE: framework/Auth/Jwt.matrix
================================================
// framework/Auth/Jwt.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Stateless JWT (HS256) Token Engine
//
// Zero-DB-lookup stateless authentication for APIs and microservices.
// Uses Spid3r native `hmac_sha256`, `base64_encode`, `base64_decode`,
// and `secure_compare` for constant-time signature verification.

import "framework/Foundation/Config";

fn b64url_encode(input: string) -> string {
    let b64 = base64_encode(input).replace("+", "-").replace("/", "_");
    while b64.ends_with("=") {
        b64 = b64.slice(0, b64.len() - 1);
    }
    return b64;
}

fn b64url_decode(input: string) -> string {
    let b64 = input.replace("-", "+").replace("_", "/");
    let rem = b64.len() % 4;
    if rem == 2 {
        b64 = b64 + "==";
    } elif rem == 3 {
        b64 = b64 + "=";
    }
    let res = base64_decode(b64);
    let raw_bytes = res;
    if type_of(res) == "result" {
        if res.is_err() {
            return "";
        }
        raw_bytes = res.unwrap();
    }
    if type_of(raw_bytes) == "string" {
        return raw_bytes;
    }
    if type_of(raw_bytes) == "bytes" {
        let chars = [];
        let i = 0;
        let n = len(raw_bytes);
        while i < n {
            chars.push(chr(raw_bytes.get(i)));
            i += 1;
        }
        return chars.join("");
    }
    return "";
}

fn resolve_jwt_secret(custom_secret: string = "") -> string {
    if custom_secret != "" {
        return custom_secret;
    }
    let env_jwt = env("JWT_SECRET");
    if env_jwt.is_some() {
        let s = string(env_jwt.unwrap()).trim();
        if s != "" {
            if is_production() and is_insecure_app_key(s) {
                throw "Security error: JWT_SECRET must be at least 16 characters and not a default placeholder in production.";
            }
            return s;
        }
    }
    return resolve_app_key();
}

/// Stateless HS256 JWT signer and constant-time verifier with expiration validation.
public class JwtEngine {
    public fn sign(self, payload: any, ttl: int = 3600, secret: string = "") -> string {
        let _ = self;
        let key = resolve_jwt_secret(secret);
        let header_json = json_write({
            "alg": "HS256", "typ": "JWT"
        });
        let claims = {
        };
        if type_of(payload) == "map" {
            for (k, v) in payload {
                if k != "password" {
                    claims[k] = v;
                }
            }
        }
        let now = unix_time();
        if not claims.has("iat") {
            claims["iat"] = now;
        }
        if ttl > 0 and not claims.has("exp") {
            claims["exp"] = now + ttl;
        }
        let header_b64 = b64url_encode(header_json);
        let payload_b64 = b64url_encode(json_write(claims));
        let signing_input = header_b64 + "." + payload_b64;
        let sig = b64url_encode(hmac_sha256(key, signing_input));
        return signing_input + "." + sig;
    }

    public fn verify(self, token: string, secret: string = "") -> any {
        let _ = self;
        if token == "" {
            return null;
        }
        let parts = token.split(".");
        if parts.len() != 3 {
            return null;
        }
        let header_b64 = parts[0];
        let payload_b64 = parts[1];
        let provided_sig = parts[2];
        if header_b64 == "" or payload_b64 == "" or provided_sig == "" {
            return null;
        }

        let key = resolve_jwt_secret(secret);
        let signing_input = header_b64 + "." + payload_b64;
        let expected_sig = b64url_encode(hmac_sha256(key, signing_input));
        if not secure_compare(provided_sig, expected_sig) {
            return null;
        }

        let raw_payload = b64url_decode(payload_b64);
        if raw_payload == "" {
            return null;
        }
        let parsed = json_parse(raw_payload);
        if type_of(parsed) == "result" {
            if parsed.is_err() {
                return null;
            }
            parsed = parsed.unwrap();
        }
        let claims = parsed;
        if type_of(claims) != "map" {
            return null;
        }

        if claims.has("exp") and claims["exp"] != null {
            try {
                let exp_time = int(claims["exp"]);
                if unix_time() > exp_time {
                    return null;
                }
            } catch (_e) {
                return null;
            }
        }
        return claims;
    }

    public fn decode_unverified(self, token: string) -> any {
        let _ = self;
        let parts = token.split(".");
        if parts.len() != 3 {
            return null;
        }
        let raw_payload = b64url_decode(parts[1]);
        if raw_payload == "" {
            return null;
        }
        let parsed = json_parse(raw_payload);
        if type_of(parsed) == "result" {
            if parsed.is_ok() {
                return parsed.unwrap();
            }
            return null;
        }
        return parsed;
    }
}

public let Jwt = new JwtEngine();
