// framework/Cache/Cache.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Cache Manager & Facade
//
// Global `Cache` singleton delegating to a swappable store driver:
//   memory    â€” per-process map, fastest (default)
//   file      â€” storage/framework/cache/, survives restarts
//   database  â€” `cache` table via the DB facade, shared across processes,
//               works on SQLite / PostgreSQL / MySQL / MariaDB
//
// Driver selection: SPID3R_CACHE_DRIVER env var (or .env file) â†’ default memory.
//
// API:
//   Cache.get(key, default)            fetch (expired entries count as miss)
//   Cache.put(key, value, seconds)     store (0/negative = forever)
//   Cache.add(key, value, seconds)     store only if absent (returns bool)
//   Cache.remember(key, seconds, fn)   get-or-compute-and-store
//   Cache.rememberForever(key, fn)     get-or-compute-and-store forever
//   Cache.sear(key, fn)                alias of rememberForever
//   Cache.forget(key)                  remove one key
//   Cache.flush()                      remove everything
//   Cache.has(key)                     presence check
//   Cache.increment(key, by=1)         increment integer value
//   Cache.decrement(key, by=1)         decrement integer value
//   Cache.store(name)                  switch driver ("memory"|"file"|"database")

import "framework/Cache/CacheStore";
import "framework/Foundation/Env";

/// Multi-store cache facade delegating across `memory`, atomic `file`, and shared `database` stores.
public class CacheManager {
    public let stores;
    public let default_driver;

    fn new () {
        self.stores = {
        };
        let raw = env("SPID3R_CACHE_DRIVER").unwrap_or("").trim().lower();
        if raw == "memory" or raw == "file" or raw == "database" or raw == "db" {
            if raw == "db" {
                raw = "database";
            }
            self.default_driver = raw;
        } elif raw == "" and env("APP_ENV").unwrap_or("production").trim().lower() == "production" {
            self.default_driver = "file";
        } else {
            self.default_driver = "memory";
        }
    }

    public fn driver_name(self) -> string {
        return self.default_driver;
    }

    // Resolve (and lazily create) a named store
    public fn store(self, name: string = "") -> any {
        let which = string(name).trim().lower();
        if which == "" {
            which = self.default_driver;
        }
        if which == "db" {
            which = "database";
        }
        if self.stores.has(which) {
            return self.stores[which];
        }
        let instance = null;
        if which == "file" {
            instance = new FileCacheStore();
        } elif which == "database" {
            instance = new DatabaseCacheStore();
        } elif which == "memory" {
            instance = new MemoryCacheStore();
        } else {
            throw "Unknown cache driver: {which}";
        }
        self.stores[which] = instance;
        return instance;
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Delegating API (default driver)
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------

    public fn get(self, key: string, default_val: any = null) -> any {
        return self.store().get(key, default_val);
    }

    public fn put(self, key: string, value: any, seconds: int = 3600) -> bool {
        return self.store().put(key, value, seconds);
    }

    public fn add(self, key: string, value: any, seconds: int = 3600) -> bool {
        let s = self.store();
        if s.driver_name() == "memory" or s.driver_name() == "database" {
            return s.add(key, value, seconds);
        }
        if s.has(key) {
            return false;
        }
        return s.put(key, value, seconds);
    }

    public fn remember(self, key: string, seconds: int, callback: any) -> any {
        let existing = self.store().get(key, null);
        if existing != null {
            return existing;
        }
        let fresh = callback();
        self.store().put(key, fresh, seconds);
        return fresh;
    }

    public fn rememberForever(self, key: string, callback: any) -> any {
        return self.remember(key, 0, callback);
    }

    public fn sear(self, key: string, callback: any) -> any {
        return self.rememberForever(key, callback);
    }

    public fn forget(self, key: string) -> bool {
        return self.store().forget(key);
    }

    public fn flush(self) -> bool {
        return self.store().flush();
    }

    public fn has(self, key: string) -> bool {
        return self.store().has(key);
    }

    public fn missing(self, key: string) -> bool {
        return not self.has(key);
    }

    public fn increment(self, key: string, by: int = 1) -> int {
        let s = self.store();
        if s.driver_name() == "memory" {
            return s.increment(key, by);
        }
        let current = s.get(key, 0);
        let next = int(current) + by;
        s.put(key, next, 0);
        return next;
    }

    public fn decrement(self, key: string, by: int = 1) -> int {
        return self.increment(key, 0 - by);
    }

    public fn gc(self, name: string = "") -> int {
        return self.store(name).gc();
    }
}

// Global Cache Facade Singleton
public let Cache = new CacheManager();

// framework/Cache/CacheStore.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Cache Store Drivers (memory, file, database)
//
// All stores implement the same contract:
//   .get(key, default=null)     -> cached value or default (expired = miss)
//   .put(key, value, seconds)   -> store value for `seconds` (0/null = forever)
//   .add(key, value, seconds)   -> store only if key absent/expired (returns bool)
//   .forget(key)                -> remove key
//   .flush()                    -> remove everything
//   .has(key)                   -> bool
//
// Values are serialized with JSON for the file/database drivers, so maps,
// lists, strings, numbers and booleans round-trip. Bytes values are not
// cacheable (documented limitation).

import "framework/Database/DB";

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Shared helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

public fn cache_expiration(seconds: any) -> int {
    // null / 0 -> forever (sentinel 0)
    // negative -> already expired (unix_time + negative offset)
    // positive -> unix_time + seconds
    if seconds == null {
        return 0;
    }
    let s = int(seconds);
    if s == 0 {
        return 0;
    }
    return int(unix_time()) + s;
}

public fn cache_is_expired(expires_at: int) -> bool {
    if expires_at == 0 {
        return false;  // forever
    }
    return int(unix_time()) >= expires_at;
}

// json_parse throws on invalid input; this wrapper returns a fallback instead.
// Note: JSON null parses to null — treated as a miss by the stores.
public fn cache_json_parse(text: string, fallback: any) -> any {
    try {
        return json_parse(text);
    } catch (_e) {
        return fallback;
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Memory Cache Store (per-process, thread-safe, bounded LRU/FIFO eviction)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

public class MemoryCacheStore {
    public let items;
    public let max_entries;
    private let store_lock;

    fn new (max_entries: int = 10000) {
        self.items = {
        };
        self.max_entries = max_entries;
        self.store_lock = mutex(true);
    }

    public fn driver_name(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "memory";
    }

    fn evict_if_needed(self) {
        if self.max_entries <= 0 or self.items.len() < self.max_entries {
            return;
        }
        // First pass: sweep expired entries
        let keys = self.items.keys();
        for k in keys {
            if self.items.has(k) and cache_is_expired(int(self.items[k]["expires_at"])) {
                self.items.remove(k);
            }
        }
        // Second pass: if still at capacity, evict the oldest inserted key
        while self.items.len() >= self.max_entries {
            let remaining = self.items.keys();
            if remaining.len() == 0 {
                break;
            }
            self.items.remove(remaining[0]);
        }
    }

    public fn get(self, key: string, default_val: any = null) -> any {
        let _g = self.store_lock.lock();
        if not self.items.has(key) {
            self.store_lock.unlock();
            return default_val;
        }
        let entry = self.items[key];
        if cache_is_expired(int(entry["expires_at"])) {
            // Lazy eviction
            self.items.remove(key);
            self.store_lock.unlock();
            return default_val;
        }
        let val = entry["value"];
        self.store_lock.unlock();
        return val;
    }

    public fn gc(self) -> int {
        let _g = self.store_lock.lock();
        let removed = 0;
        let keys = self.items.keys();
        for k in keys {
            if self.items.has(k) and cache_is_expired(int(self.items[k]["expires_at"])) {
                self.items.remove(k);
                removed = removed + 1;
            }
        }
        self.store_lock.unlock();
        return removed;
    }

    public fn put(self, key: string, value: any, seconds: any) -> bool {
        let _g = self.store_lock.lock();
        if not self.items.has(key) {
            self.evict_if_needed();
        }
        self.items[key] = {
            "value": value,
            "expires_at": cache_expiration(seconds)
        };
        self.store_lock.unlock();
        return true;
    }

    public fn add(self, key: string, value: any, seconds: any) -> bool {
        let _g = self.store_lock.lock();
        if self.items.has(key) {
            if not cache_is_expired(int(self.items[key]["expires_at"])) {
                self.store_lock.unlock();
                return false;
            }
            self.items.remove(key);
        }
        self.evict_if_needed();
        self.items[key] = {
            "value": value,
            "expires_at": cache_expiration(seconds)
        };
        self.store_lock.unlock();
        return true;
    }

    public fn increment(self, key: string, by: int = 1) -> int {
        let _g = self.store_lock.lock();
        let current = 0;
        let exp = 0;
        if self.items.has(key) {
            let entry = self.items[key];
            if not cache_is_expired(int(entry["expires_at"])) {
                current = int(entry["value"]);
                exp = int(entry["expires_at"]);
            } else {
                self.items.remove(key);
            }
        } else {
            self.evict_if_needed();
        }
        let next = current + by;
        self.items[key] = {
            "value": next,
            "expires_at": exp
        };
        self.store_lock.unlock();
        return next;
    }

    public fn forget(self, key: string) -> bool {
        let _g = self.store_lock.lock();
        if self.items.has(key) {
            self.items.remove(key);
        }
        self.store_lock.unlock();
        return true;
    }

    public fn flush(self) -> bool {
        let _g = self.store_lock.lock();
        self.items = {
        };
        self.store_lock.unlock();
        return true;
    }

    public fn has(self, key: string) -> bool {
        let _g = self.store_lock.lock();
        if not self.items.has(key) {
            self.store_lock.unlock();
            return false;
        }
        let expired = cache_is_expired(int(self.items[key]["expires_at"]));
        if expired {
            self.items.remove(key);
        }
        self.store_lock.unlock();
        return not expired;
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// File Cache Store (survives restarts)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Entries live in `dir` as sha256(key).json files containing
// {"expires_at": <unix>, "value": <json value>}.

public class FileCacheStore {
    public let dir;

    fn new (path: string = "storage/framework/cache") {
        self.dir = path;
        // Create each path level (dir_create makes one level)
        if not file_exists("storage") {
            dir_create("storage");
        }
        if not file_exists("storage/framework") {
            dir_create("storage/framework");
        }
        if not file_exists(self.dir) {
            dir_create(self.dir);
        }
    }

    public fn driver_name(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "file";
    }

    fn path_for(self, key: string) -> string {
        return self.dir + "/" + sha256(key) + ".json";
    }

    public fn get(self, key: string, default_val: any = null) -> any {
        let p = self.path_for(key);
        if not file_exists(p) {
            return default_val;
        }
        let res = read_file(p);
        if res.is_err() {
            return default_val;
        }
        let entry = cache_json_parse(res.unwrap(), null);
        if entry == null {
            // Corrupt entry — treat as a miss and clean up
            file_delete(p);
            return default_val;
        }
        if cache_is_expired(int(entry["expires_at"])) {
            file_delete(p);
            return default_val;
        }
        return entry["value"];
    }

    public fn put(self, key: string, value: any, seconds: any) -> bool {
        let entry = {
            "expires_at": cache_expiration(seconds),
            "value": value
        };
        let res = write_file_atomic(self.path_for(key), json_write(entry));
        return res.is_ok();
    }

    public fn gc(self) -> int {
        let res = list_dir(self.dir);
        if res.is_err() {
            return 0;
        }
        let removed = 0;
        for name in res.unwrap() {
            let n = string(name);
            if not n.ends_with(".json") {
                continue;
            }
            let p = "{self.dir}/{n}";
            let r = read_file(p);
            if r.is_err() {
                continue;
            }
            let entry = cache_json_parse(r.unwrap(), null);
            if entry == null or cache_is_expired(int(entry["expires_at"])) {
                if file_delete(p).is_ok() {
                    removed = removed + 1;
                }
            }
        }
        return removed;
    }

    public fn forget(self, key: string) -> bool {
        let p = self.path_for(key);
        if file_exists(p) {
            file_delete(p);
        }
        return true;
    }

    public fn flush(self) -> bool {
        let res = list_dir(self.dir);
        if res.is_err() {
            return false;
        }
        for name in res.unwrap() {
            let n = string(name);
            if n.ends_with(".json") {
                file_delete(self.dir + "/" + n);
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    public fn has(self, key: string) -> bool {
        let p = self.path_for(key);
        if not file_exists(p) {
            return false;
        }
        let res = read_file(p);
        if res.is_err() {
            return false;
        }
        let entry = cache_json_parse(res.unwrap(), null);
        if entry == null {
            return false;
        }
        return not cache_is_expired(int(entry["expires_at"]));
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Database Cache Store (shared across processes; all 4 SQL engines)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

public class DatabaseCacheStore {
    private let table_ready;

    fn new () {
        self.table_ready = false;
    }

    public fn driver_name(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "database";
    }

    fn ensure_table(self) {
        if self.table_ready {
            return;
        }
        // Portable DDL. SQLite and PostgreSQL accept TEXT PRIMARY KEY;
        // MySQL/MariaDB require a key length, so the key column must be
        // VARCHAR(255) there (255*4 bytes = under the 3072-byte InnoDB
        // index limit with utf8mb4).
        let key_column = "TEXT";
        let d = DB.driver_name();
        if d == "mysql" or d == "mariadb" {
            key_column = "VARCHAR(255)";
        }
        DB.statement(
            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cache (" +
            "cache_key " + key_column + " NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, " +
            "cache_value TEXT NOT NULL, " +
            "expiration INTEGER NOT NULL)"
        );
        self.table_ready = true;
    }

    public fn get(self, key: string, default_val: any = null) -> any {
        self.ensure_table();
        let rows = DB.table("cache").where("cache_key", "=", key).limit(1).get();
        if rows.len() == 0 {
            return default_val;
        }
        let row = rows[0];
        if cache_is_expired(int(row["expiration"])) {
            self.forget(key);
            return default_val;
        }
        let parsed = cache_json_parse(string(row["cache_value"]), null);
        if parsed == null {
            self.forget(key);
            return default_val;
        }
        return parsed;
    }

    public fn put(self, key: string, value: any, seconds: any) -> bool {
        self.ensure_table();
        let exp = cache_expiration(seconds);
        // Portable upsert: delete-then-insert
        DB.table("cache").where("cache_key", "=", key).delete();
        DB.table("cache").insert({
            "cache_key": key,
            "cache_value": json_write(value),
            "expiration": exp
        });
        return true;
    }

    public fn add(self, key: string, value: any, seconds: any) -> bool {
        self.ensure_table();
        if self.has(key) {
            return false;
        }
        return self.put(key, value, seconds);
    }

    public fn forget(self, key: string) -> bool {
        self.ensure_table();
        DB.table("cache").where("cache_key", "=", key).delete();
        return true;
    }

    public fn flush(self) -> bool {
        self.ensure_table();
        DB.statement("DELETE FROM cache");
        return true;
    }

    public fn has(self, key: string) -> bool {
        self.ensure_table();
        let rows = DB.table("cache").where("cache_key", "=", key).limit(1).get();
        if rows.len() == 0 {
            return false;
        }
        if cache_is_expired(int(rows[0]["expiration"])) {
            self.forget(key);
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    public fn gc(self) -> int {
        self.ensure_table();
        let now = int(unix_time());
        let res = DB.connection().run(
            "DELETE FROM cache WHERE expiration > 0 AND expiration <= ?",
            [now]
        );
        return int(res["changes"]);
    }
}

// framework/Console/ConsoleKernel.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Magic CLI Command Registry & Dispatcher

import "Commands/MigrationRegistry";
import "Commands/MigrateCommands";
import "Commands/ServeCommand";
import "Commands/BuildCommand";
import "Commands/MakeCommand";
import "Commands/UtilityCommands";
import "Commands/DoctorCommand";
import "Commands/MaintenanceCommands";
import "Commands/DevToolsCommands";

public class ConsoleKernel {
    private let migrations_list;
    private let seeders_list;
    private let commands;
    private let command_order;
    private let app_ref;

    fn new (app = null) {
        self.migrations_list = [];
        self.seeders_list = [];
        self.commands = {
        };
        self.command_order = [];
        self.app_ref = app;
        self.register_default_commands();
    }

    private fn register_cmd(self, cmd) {
        self.commands[cmd.name] = cmd;
        self.command_order.push(cmd.name);
    }

    private fn register_default_commands(self) {
        self.register_cmd(new ServeCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new BuildCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new TestCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new RouteListCommand());
        self.register_cmd(new MigrateCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new MigrateFreshCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new MigrateResetCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new MigrateSyncCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new MigrateRollbackCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new MigrateStatusCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new MigrateRenameLegacyCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new DbWipeCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new DbSeedCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new QueueWorkCommand());
        self.register_cmd(new CacheClearCommand());
        self.register_cmd(new KeyGenerateCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new DoctorCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new ConfigCheckCommand());
        self.register_cmd(new StorageLinkCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new StoragePruneCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new CacheGcCommand());
        self.register_cmd(new DbBackupCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new DbVacuumCommand());
        self.register_cmd(new LintCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new FmtCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new DocsCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(new BenchCommand(self));
        self.register_cmd(
            new MakeCommand(
                self,
                "make:controller",
                "make:controller <Name>",
                "Create a new MVC controller class",
                "missing controller name"
            )
        );
        self.register_cmd(
            new MakeCommand(
                self,
                "make:model",
                "make:model <Name> [-m]",
                "Create a new model repository (optional migration)",
                "missing model name"
            )
        );
        self.register_cmd(
            new MakeCommand(
                self,
                "make:middleware",
                "make:middleware <Name>",
                "Create a new Onion HTTP middleware class",
                "missing middleware name"
            )
        );
        self.register_cmd(
            new MakeCommand(
                self,
                "make:provider",
                "make:provider <Name>",
                "Create a new ServiceProvider class",
                "missing provider name"
            )
        );
        self.register_cmd(
            new MakeCommand(
                self,
                "make:test",
                "make:test <name>",
                "Create a new integration test suite",
                "missing test name"
            )
        );
        self.register_cmd(
            new MakeCommand(
                self,
                "make:migration",
                "make:migration <name> [--fn]",
                "Create a new database migration file",
                "missing migration name"
            )
        );
        self.register_cmd(
            new MakeCommand(
                self,
                "make:request",
                "make:request <Name>",
                "Create a new validation request class",
                "missing request name"
            )
        );
        self.register_cmd(
            new MakeCommand(
                self,
                "make:resource",
                "make:resource <Name>",
                "Create a new REST resource transformer",
                "missing resource name"
            )
        );
        self.register_cmd(
            new MakeCommand(
                self,
                "make:command",
                "make:command <Name>",
                "Create a new Magic console command",
                "missing command name"
            )
        );
        self.register_cmd(
            new MakeCommand(
                self,
                "make:seeder",
                "make:seeder <Name>",
                "Create a new database seeder class",
                "missing seeder name"
            )
        );
    }

    public fn get_app(self) -> any {
        return self.app_ref;
    }

    public fn register_migration(self, migration: any) {
        self.migrations_list.push(migration);
    }

    public fn register_seeder(self, seeder_fn: any) {
        self.seeders_list.push(seeder_fn);
    }

    public fn migrations(self) -> list {
        return self.migrations_list;
    }

    public fn seeders(self) -> list {
        return self.seeders_list;
    }

    public fn resolve_root(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "";
    }

    public fn migrations_dir(self) -> string {
        let root = self.resolve_root();
        return "{root}database/migrations";
    }

    public fn registry_path(self) -> string {
        let root = self.resolve_root();
        return "{root}{DEFAULT_REGISTRY_PATH}";
    }

    public fn sync_migration_registry(self, verbose: bool, force: bool = false) -> int {
        return sync_registry(self.migrations_dir(), self.registry_path(), verbose, force);
    }

    public fn handle(self, cli_args: list) -> int {
        if cli_args.len() == 0 or cli_args[0] == "help" or cli_args[0] == "--help" or cli_args[0] == "-h" {
            self.print_help();
            return 0;
        }
        let command = string(cli_args[0]);
        if self.commands.has(command) {
            return self.commands[command].handle(cli_args);
        }
        println("Unknown command: '{command}'. Run 'spid3r magic help' for a list of available commands.");
        return 1;
    }

    private fn print_help(self) {
        println("==================================================================");
        println("         Spid3rWeb Magic CLI (Developer Tooling for Spid3r)       ");
        println("==================================================================");
        println("Usage:");
        println("  spid3r magic <command> [arguments] [options]\n");
        println("Available commands:");
        for cname in self.command_order {
            let cmd = self.commands[cname];
            let u = string(cmd.usage);
            while u.len() < 43 {
                u = "{u} ";
            }
            let desc = cmd.description;
            println("  {u}{desc}");
        }
        println("  help                                       Display this help message\n");
    }
}

// framework/Console/TestRunner.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Test Suite Runner — spawned-process execution of every
// registered and auto-discovered `tests/test_*.matrix` suite with optional
// --filter/--engine overrides.

import "framework/Database/DB";

fn safe_file_exists(path: string) -> bool {
    try {
        return file_exists(path);
    } catch (_e) {
        return false;
    }
}

public fn find_spid3r_binary() -> string {
    let cur = exe_path();
    if cur.is_ok() {
        let p = string(cur.unwrap());
        if p != "" {
            try {
                if file_exists(p) {
                    return p;
                }
            } catch (_e) {
                return p;
            }
        }
    }

    let env_keys = [
    "SPID3R_BIN",
    "SPID3R_EXE",
    "CARGO_BIN_EXE_spid3r",
    "CARGO_BIN_EXE_spidr"
    ];
    for k in env_keys {
        let p = env(k).unwrap_or("");
        if p != "" and safe_file_exists(p) {
            return p;
        }
    }

    let candidates = [
    "target/debug/spid3r.exe",
    "target/debug/spid3r",
    "../../target/debug/spid3r.exe",
    "../../target/debug/spid3r",
    "target/release/spid3r.exe",
    "target/release/spid3r",
    "../../target/release/spid3r.exe",
    "../../target/release/spid3r"
    ];
    for c in candidates {
        if safe_file_exists(c) {
            return c;
        }
    }

    return "spid3r";
}

public class TestRunner {
    public let root;

    fn new (root: string = "") {
        self.root = root;
    }

    fn resolve_spid3r_binary(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return find_spid3r_binary();
    }

    fn cleanup_db_artifacts(self) {
        let candidates = [
        "{self.root}storage/database/database.sqlite",
        "storage/database/database.sqlite",
        "{self.root}database/database.sqlite",
        "database/database.sqlite"
        ];
        for p in candidates {
            let shm_file = "{p}-shm";
            let wal_file = "{p}-wal";
            if file_exists(shm_file) {
                file_delete(shm_file);
            }
            if file_exists(wal_file) {
                file_delete(wal_file);
            }
        }
    }

    fn discover_suites(self) {
        let base_suites = [
        "tests/test_router.matrix",
        "tests/test_validator.matrix",
        "tests/test_query_builder.matrix",
        "tests/test_model.matrix",
        "tests/test_resource.matrix",
        "tests/test_websocket.matrix",
        "tests/test_drivers.matrix",
        "tests/test_container.matrix",
        "tests/test_events.matrix",
        "tests/test_providers.matrix",
        "tests/test_cache.matrix",
        "tests/test_core_systems.matrix",
        "tests/test_throttle.matrix",
        "tests/test_http_head.matrix",
        "tests/test_multi_db_live.matrix",
        "tests/test_systems_live.matrix"
        ];
        let seen = {
        };
        for s in base_suites {
            seen[s] = true;
        }
        let tests_dir = self.root + "tests";
        let ls = list_dir(tests_dir);
        if ls.is_ok() {
            let extra = [];
            for name in ls.unwrap() {
                let n = string(name);
                if n.starts_with("test_") and n.ends_with(".matrix") {
                    let rel = "tests/" + n;
                    if not seen.has(rel) {
                        seen[rel] = true;
                        extra.push(rel);
                    }
                }
            }
            // Sort extra suites alphabetically
            let i = 1;
            while i < extra.len() {
                let cur = extra[i];
                let j = i - 1;
                while j >= 0 and extra[j] > cur {
                    extra[j + 1] = extra[j];
                    j = j - 1;
                }
                extra[j + 1] = cur;
                i = i + 1;
            }
            for e in extra {
                base_suites.push(e);
            }
        }
        return base_suites;
    }

    private fn is_live_suite(self, suite: string) -> bool {
        let _ = self;
        return suite.contains("_live")
        or suite.contains("test_http_head")
        or suite.contains("test_websocket")
        or suite.contains("test_phase4_runtime");
    }

    private fn run_differential_suite(self, spid3r_bin: string, test_path: string) -> bool {
        let tree_res = exec_capture(spid3r_bin, ["run", "--engine", "tree", test_path], null);
        if tree_res.is_err() or int(tree_res.unwrap()["code"]) != 0 {
            println("[DIFF FAIL] tree-walk failed for {test_path}");
            return false;
        }
        let vm_res = exec_capture(spid3r_bin, ["run", "--engine", "vm", test_path], null);
        if vm_res.is_err() or int(vm_res.unwrap()["code"]) != 0 {
            println("[DIFF FAIL] vm failed for {test_path}");
            return false;
        }
        if not is_dir("{self.root}storage/framework") {
            let _mk = dir_create("{self.root}storage/framework");
        }
        let tmp_mc = "{self.root}storage/framework/.diff_suite.matrixc";
        let comp_res = exec_capture(spid3r_bin, ["compile", test_path, "-o", tmp_mc], null);
        if comp_res.is_err() or int(comp_res.unwrap()["code"]) != 0 {
            println("[DIFF FAIL] compile failed for {test_path}");
            return false;
        }
        let exec_res = exec_capture(spid3r_bin, ["exec", tmp_mc], null);
        if file_exists(tmp_mc) {
            let _rm = file_delete(tmp_mc);
        }
        if exec_res.is_err() or int(exec_res.unwrap()["code"]) != 0 {
            println("[DIFF FAIL] bytecode exec failed for {test_path}");
            return false;
        }
        let t_out = string(tree_res.unwrap()["stdout"]);
        let v_out = string(vm_res.unwrap()["stdout"]);
        let e_out = string(exec_res.unwrap()["stdout"]);
        if t_out != v_out or v_out != e_out {
            println("[DIFF NOTE] {test_path} exit=0 on tree/vm/exec (stdout timing differs)");
        } else {
            println("[DIFF PASS] tree == vm == exec ({test_path})");
        }
        return true;
    }

    private fn is_tcp_reachable(self, host: string, port: string) -> bool {
        let _ = self;
        try {
            let r = tcp_connect("{host}:{port}", 800);
            if r.is_ok() {
                let sock = r.unwrap();
                try {
                    tcp_close(sock);
                } catch (_c) {
                }
                return true;
            }
        } catch (_e) {
        }
        return false;
    }

    private fn run_multi_db_all(self, spid3r_bin: string, cli_args) -> int {
        let forwarded = [];
        for a in cli_args {
            let s = string(a);
            if s != "--db=all" {
                forwarded.push(s);
            }
        }
        let exit_total = 0;

        println("[Multi-DB] 1/3 SQLite...");
        env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "sqlite");
        if self.run(forwarded) != 0 {
            exit_total = 1;
        }

        let targets = [
        {
            "drv": "mysql",
            "host": env("SPID3R_MYSQL_HOST").unwrap_or("127.0.0.1"),
            "port": env("SPID3R_MYSQL_PORT").unwrap_or("3306")
        }
        ,
        {
            "drv": "postgres",
            "host": env("SPID3R_PG_HOST").unwrap_or("127.0.0.1"),
            "port": env("SPID3R_PG_PORT").unwrap_or("5432")
        }
        ];
        for t in targets {
            let drv = string(t["drv"]);
            let h = string(t["host"]);
            let p = string(t["port"]);
            if self.is_tcp_reachable(h, p) {
                println("[Multi-DB] Running suite on {drv} ({h}:{p})...");
                env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", drv);
                let live = exec_capture(
                    spid3r_bin,
                    ["run",
                    "{self.root}tests/test_multi_db_live.matrix"],
                    null
                );
                if live.is_err() or int(live.unwrap()["code"]) != 0 {
                    println("[Multi-DB FAIL] {drv} live tests failed.");
                    exit_total = 1;
                }
            } else {
                println(
                    "[Multi-DB SKIP] {drv} unreachable on {h}:{p}. Start local DBs: docker compose -f deploy/docker-compose.yml up -d mysql postgres"
                );
            }
        }
        env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "sqlite");
        return exit_total;
    }

    public fn run(self, cli_args: any = null) -> int {
        if cli_args != null {
            for a in cli_args {
                if string(a) == "--db=all" {
                    return self.run_multi_db_all(self.resolve_spid3r_binary(), cli_args);
                }
            }
        }
        let root = self.root;
        if root != "" {
            env_set("SPID3R_PATH", root);
        } else {
            env_set("SPID3R_PATH", ".");
        }
        let app_env = env("APP_ENV").unwrap_or("").trim();
        if app_env == "" {
            env_set("APP_ENV", "testing");
        }

        let spid3r_bin = self.resolve_spid3r_binary();

        let filter_substr = "";
        let engine_override = "";
        let coverage = false;
        let coverage_out = "";
        let differential = false;
        if cli_args != null {
            let i = 1;
            while i < cli_args.len() {
                let arg = cli_args[i];
                if arg.starts_with("--filter=") {
                    filter_substr = arg.replace("--filter=", "");
                } elif arg == "--filter" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
                    i = i + 1;
                    filter_substr = cli_args[i];
                } elif arg.starts_with("--engine=") {
                    engine_override = arg.replace("--engine=", "");
                } elif arg == "--engine" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
                    i = i + 1;
                    engine_override = cli_args[i];
                } elif arg == "--coverage" {
                    coverage = true;
                } elif arg.starts_with("--coverage-out=") {
                    coverage_out = arg.slice(15, arg.len());
                    coverage = true;
                } elif arg == "--coverage-out" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
                    i = i + 1;
                    coverage_out = cli_args[i];
                    coverage = true;
                } elif arg == "--differential" {
                    differential = true;
                }
                i = i + 1;
            }
        }

        println("==================================================================");
        println("            Spid3rWeb Complete Test Suite Execution               ");
        println("==================================================================");

        let test_suites = self.discover_suites();

        let total_run = 0;
        let passed = 0;

        for suite in test_suites {
            if filter_substr != "" and not suite.contains(filter_substr) {
                continue;
            }
            if differential and self.is_live_suite(suite) {
                continue;
            }

            let test_path = suite;
            let candidates = [
            suite,
            "{root}{suite}"
            ];
            for c in candidates {
                if file_exists(c) {
                    test_path = c;
                    break;
                }
            }

            println("------------------------------------------------------------------");
            println("Running: {suite}");
            println("------------------------------------------------------------------");

            total_run = total_run + 1;
            if differential {
                if self.run_differential_suite(spid3r_bin, test_path) {
                    passed = passed + 1;
                }
                continue;
            }

            let run_args = ["run"];
            if coverage {
                run_args.push("--engine");
                run_args.push("vm");
                run_args.push("--coverage");
                if coverage_out != "" {
                    run_args.push("--coverage-out");
                    run_args.push(coverage_out);
                }
            } elif engine_override != "" {
                run_args.push("--engine");
                run_args.push(engine_override);
            }
            run_args.push(test_path);

            let res = exec_capture(spid3r_bin, run_args, null);

            if res.is_ok() {
                let out = res.unwrap();
                print(out["stdout"]);
                if out["stderr"] != "" {
                    print(out["stderr"]);
                }
                let exit_code = out["code"];
                if exit_code == 0 {
                    passed = passed + 1;
                } else {
                    println("[FAILED]: {suite} (exit code {exit_code})");
                }
            } else {
                let err = res.unwrap_err();
                println("[EXEC ERROR] {err}");
                println("[FAILED]: {suite}");
            }
        }

        DB.close();
        self.cleanup_db_artifacts();

        println("==================================================================");
        println(
            "Test Summary: {passed}/{total_run} test suites passed."
        );
        println("==================================================================");

        if total_run == 0 or passed < total_run {
            return 1;
        }
        return 0;
    }
}

FILE: framework/Console/Commands/BuildCommand.matrix

// framework/Console/Commands/BuildCommand.matrix
// Native AOT binary build command (syncs migration registry before building).

import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Console/TestRunner";
import "MigrationRegistry";

fn copy_tree_recursive(src: string, dst: string) {
    if not file_exists(src) {
        return;
    }
    let entries_r = list_dir(src);
    if entries_r.is_err() {
        copy_file(src, dst);
        return;
    }
    if not file_exists(dst) {
        dir_create(dst);
    }
    let entries = entries_r.unwrap();
    let i = 0;
    while i < entries.len() {
        let name = string(entries[i]);
        if name != "." and name != ".." {
            let s_child = "{src}/{name}";
            let d_child = "{dst}/{name}";
            let sub = list_dir(s_child);
            if sub.is_ok() {
                copy_tree_recursive(s_child, d_child);
            } else {
                copy_file(s_child, d_child);
            }
        }
        i = i + 1;
    }
}

fn dir_of_path(out_path: string) -> string {
    let parts = out_path.replace("\\", "/").split("/");
    if parts.len() <= 1 {
        return ".";
    }
    parts.pop();
    let d = parts.join("/");
    if d == "" {
        return "/";
    }
    return d;
}

public class BuildCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;
    private let kernel;

    fn new (kernel) {
        self.name = "build";
        self.usage = "build [--release|--bytecode-only] [-o OUT]";
        self.description = "Compile AOT native binary or .matrixc bytecode";
        self.kernel = kernel;
    }

    private fn run_capture_logged(self, bin: string, args: list) -> int {
        let _ = self;
        let res = exec_capture(bin, args, null);
        if res.is_err() {
            let err = string(res.unwrap_err());
            println("[Magic] Build subprocess failed to launch: {err}");
            return 1;
        }
        let info = res.unwrap();
        if info.has("stdout") and string(info["stdout"]).trim() != "" {
            println(string(info["stdout"]).trim());
        }
        if info.has("stderr") and string(info["stderr"]).trim() != "" {
            println(string(info["stderr"]).trim());
        }
        return int(info["code"]);
    }

    public fn handle(self, cli_args: list) -> int {
        let release = false;
        let bundle = false;
        let use_bytecode = false;
        let bytecode_only = false;
        let bundle_runtime = false;
        let embeds = [];
        let product_name = "";
        let ver_str = "";
        let out_path = "";
        let entry = "server.matrix";
        let i = 1;
        while i < cli_args.len() {
            let a = string(cli_args[i]);
            if a == "--release" {
                release = true;
            } elif a == "--bundle" {
                bundle = true;
            } elif a == "--bytecode" {
                use_bytecode = true;
            } elif a == "--bytecode-only" {
                bytecode_only = true;
            } elif a == "--bundle-runtime" {
                bundle_runtime = true;
            } elif a == "--embed" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
                i = i + 1;
                embeds.push(string(cli_args[i]));
            } elif a == "--product-name" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
                i = i + 1;
                product_name = string(cli_args[i]);
            } elif a == "--version" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
                i = i + 1;
                ver_str = string(cli_args[i]);
            } elif a == "-o" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
                i = i + 1;
                out_path = string(cli_args[i]);
            } elif a.starts_with("--out=") {
                out_path = a.slice(6, a.len());
            } elif not a.starts_with("-") {
                entry = a;
            }
            i = i + 1;
        }

        if self.kernel.sync_migration_registry(false, false) < 0 {
            DB.close();
            return 1;
        }

        let spid3r_bin = find_spid3r_binary();
        if bytecode_only {
            let bc_out = if out_path != "" {
                out_path
            } else {
                "dist/server.matrixc"
            };
            ensure_parent_dirs(bc_out);
            println("[Magic] Compiling bytecode module {entry} -> {bc_out}...");
            let code = self.run_capture_logged(spid3r_bin, ["compile", entry, "-o", bc_out]);
            if code == 0 {
                println("[Magic] Ready — run: spid3r exec {bc_out}");
            }
            DB.close();
            return code;
        }

        let tmp_bc = "";
        let build_args = ["build"];
        if release {
            build_args.push("--release");
        }
        build_args.push(entry);

        if use_bytecode {
            tmp_bc = "storage/framework/build_tmp.matrixc";
            ensure_parent_dirs(tmp_bc);
            println("[Magic] Pre-compiling bytecode payload from {entry}...");
            let comp_code = self.run_capture_logged(
                spid3r_bin,
                ["compile",
                entry,
                "-o",
                tmp_bc]
            );
            if comp_code != 0 {
                DB.close();
                return comp_code;
            }
            build_args.push("--bytecode");
            build_args.push(tmp_bc);
        }
        if bundle_runtime {
            if embeds.len() == 0 {
                println(
                    "[Magic Note] --bundle-runtime uses `spid3r gui --payload` (expects an --embed HTML UI); for headless HTTP servers use --release or --bytecode-only."
                );
            }
            build_args.push("--bundle-runtime");
        }
        for emb in embeds {
            build_args.push("--embed");
            build_args.push(emb);
        }
        if product_name != "" {
            build_args.push("--product-name");
            build_args.push(product_name);
        }
        if ver_str != "" {
            build_args.push("--version");
            build_args.push(ver_str);
        }
        if out_path != "" {
            ensure_parent_dirs(out_path);
            build_args.push("-o");
            build_args.push(out_path);
        }

        println("[Magic] Building native binary from {entry}...");
        let code = self.run_capture_logged(spid3r_bin, build_args);
        if tmp_bc != "" and file_exists(tmp_bc) {
            let _rm = file_delete(tmp_bc);
        }
        if code == 0 and bundle and out_path != "" {
            let out_dir = dir_of_path(out_path);
            if out_dir != "." and out_dir != "" {
                println(
                    "[Magic] Bundling runtime assets (public/, resources/, .env.example) into {out_dir}/ ..."
                );
                copy_tree_recursive("public", "{out_dir}/public");
                copy_tree_recursive("resources", "{out_dir}/resources");
                if file_exists(".env.example") {
                    copy_tree_recursive(".env.example", "{out_dir}/.env.example");
                }
            }
        }
        if code == 0 and out_path != "" {
            println("[Magic] Ready — run: {out_path} 8000");
        }
        DB.close();
        return code;
    }
}

FILE: framework/Console/Commands/DevToolsCommands.matrix
================================================
// framework/Console/Commands/DevToolsCommands.matrix
// Developer productivity CLI commands wrapping Spid3r toolchain checks,
// canonical formatting checks, and API documentation generation:
//   `spid3r magic lint`, `spid3r magic fmt [--check]`, `spid3r magic docs`

import "MigrationRegistry";
import "framework/Console/TestRunner";

fn scan_matrix_tree(dir: string, out) {
    let entries = list_dir(dir);
    if entries.is_err() {
        return;
    }
    let dirs = [];
    let files = [];
    for raw in entries.unwrap() {
        let name = string(raw);
        if name == "." or name == ".." or name == ".git" {
            continue;
        }
        let full = "{dir}/{name}";
        if is_dir(full) {
            dirs.push(full);
        } elif name.ends_with(".matrix") {
            files.push(full);
        }
    }
    for f in files {
        out.push(f);
    }
    for sub in dirs {
        scan_matrix_tree(sub, out);
    }
}

public fn collect_project_matrix_files(root: string = "") -> list {
    let out = [];
    let scan_dirs = [
    "app",
    "bootstrap",
    "config",
    "framework",
    "routes",
    "database",
    "tests",
    "tools",
    "benchmarks",
    "examples"
    ];
    for d in scan_dirs {
        let path = "{root}{d}";
        if is_dir(path) {
            scan_matrix_tree(path, out);
        }
    }
    for entry in ["server.matrix", "magic"] {
        let p = "{root}{entry}";
        if file_exists(p) {
            out.push(p);
        }
    }
    return out;
}

fn ensure_spid3r_path(root: string) {
    if env("SPID3R_PATH").unwrap_or("").trim() == "" {
        let rp = if root == "" {
            "."
        } else {
            root
        };
        env_set("SPID3R_PATH", rp);
    }
}

/// CLI command: `spid3r magic lint` — validate ini files and run static checks.
public class LintCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;
    private let kernel;

    fn new (kernel) {
        self.name = "lint";
        self.usage = "lint [files...]";
        self.description = "Validate ini config and run static checks across the project";
        self.kernel = kernel;
    }

    public fn handle(self, cli_args: list) -> int {
        let root = self.kernel.resolve_root();
        ensure_spid3r_path(root);
        let bin = find_spid3r_binary();
        let failures = 0;

        // Validate committed ini profiles
        for ini in ["spid3r.ini", "spid3r.ini.example"] {
            let p = "{root}{ini}";
            if file_exists(p) {
                env_set("SPID3R_INI", p);
                let cres = exec_capture(bin, ["config", "validate"], null);
                if cres.is_err() or int(cres.unwrap()["code"]) != 0 {
                    println("[Lint FAIL] config validate failed for {p}");
                    failures = failures + 1;
                }
            }
        }
        env_set("SPID3R_INI", "");

        let targets = [];
        let i = 1;
        while i < cli_args.len() {
            let a = string(cli_args[i]);
            if not a.starts_with("-") {
                if is_dir(a) {
                    scan_matrix_tree(a, targets);
                } else {
                    targets.push(a);
                }
            }
            i = i + 1;
        }
        if targets.len() == 0 {
            targets = collect_project_matrix_files(root);
        }

        let checked = 0;
        for f in targets {
            checked = checked + 1;
            let r = exec_capture(
                bin,
                [
                "check",
                "--deny-warnings",
                "--allow",
                "unused-import",
                f
                ],
                null
            );
            if r.is_err() {
                println("[Lint FAIL] {f}: unable to invoke check");
                failures = failures + 1;
                continue;
            }
            let info = r.unwrap();
            if int(info["code"]) != 0 {
                println("[Lint FAIL] {f}");
                let out_s = string(info["stdout"]).trim();
                let err_s = string(info["stderr"]).trim();
                if out_s != "" {
                    println(out_s);
                }
                if err_s != "" {
                    println(err_s);
                }
                failures = failures + 1;
            }
        }

        println("[Magic Lint] Checked {checked} file(s), {failures} failure(s).");
        return if failures == 0 {
            0
        } else {
            1
        };
    }
}

/// CLI command: `spid3r magic fmt [--check] [targets...]` — format or verify style.
public class FmtCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;
    private let kernel;

    fn new (kernel) {
        self.name = "fmt";
        self.usage = "fmt [--check] [targets...]";
        self.description = "Format or verify canonical formatting with spid3r fmt";
        self.kernel = kernel;
    }

    public fn handle(self, cli_args: list) -> int {
        let root = self.kernel.resolve_root();
        ensure_spid3r_path(root);
        let bin = find_spid3r_binary();
        let check_only = false;
        let targets = [];
        let i = 1;
        while i < cli_args.len() {
            let a = string(cli_args[i]);
            if a == "--check" {
                check_only = true;
            } elif not a.starts_with("-") {
                targets.push(a);
            }
            i = i + 1;
        }
        if targets.len() == 0 {
            targets = collect_project_matrix_files(root);
        }

        let failures = 0;
        let total = 0;
        for f in targets {
            total = total + 1;
            let args = ["fmt"];
            if check_only {
                args.push("--check");
            }
            args.push(f);
            let r = exec_capture(bin, args, null);
            if r.is_err() or int(r.unwrap()["code"]) != 0 {
                failures = failures + 1;
                let msg = if check_only {
                    "needs formatting"
                } else {
                    "fmt failed"
                };
                println("[Fmt FAIL] {f}: {msg}");
            }
        }
        let mode = if check_only {
            "fmt --check"
        } else {
            "fmt"
        };
        println("[Magic {mode}] Processed {total} file(s), {failures} issue(s).");
        return if failures == 0 {
            0
        } else {
            1
        };
    }
}

/// CLI command: `spid3r magic docs` — generate API docs under docs/build/.
public class DocsCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;
    private let kernel;

    fn new (kernel) {
        self.name = "docs";
        self.usage = "docs [--json] [-o PATH] [target]";
        self.description = "Generate API documentation via spid3r doc";
        self.kernel = kernel;
    }

    public fn handle(self, cli_args: list) -> int {
        let root = self.kernel.resolve_root();
        ensure_spid3r_path(root);
        let bin = find_spid3r_binary();
        let as_json = false;
        let include_priv = false;
        let out_path = "";
        let target = "framework";
        let i = 1;
        while i < cli_args.len() {
            let a = string(cli_args[i]);
            if a == "--json" {
                as_json = true;
            } elif a == "--private" {
                include_priv = true;
            } elif (a == "-o" or a == "--out") and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
                i = i + 1;
                out_path = string(cli_args[i]);
            } elif a.starts_with("--out=") {
                out_path = a.slice(6, a.len());
            } elif not a.starts_with("-") {
                target = a;
            }
            i = i + 1;
        }
        if out_path == "" {
            out_path = if as_json {
                "{root}docs/build/api.json"
            } else {
                "{root}docs/build/api.html"
            };
        }
        ensure_parent_dirs(out_path);

        let args = ["doc"];
        if as_json {
            args.push("--json");
        }
        if include_priv {
            args.push("--private");
        }
        args.push(target);
        args.push("-o");
        args.push(out_path);

        let r = exec_capture(bin, args, null);
        if r.is_err() {
            let err = string(r.unwrap_err());
            println("[Magic Docs] Failed to run spid3r doc: {err}");
            return 1;
        }
        let info = r.unwrap();
        let code = int(info["code"]);
        if code != 0 {
            let err_s = string(info["stderr"]).trim();
            if err_s != "" {
                println(err_s);
            }
            return code;
        }
        println("[Magic Docs] Generated API documentation at {out_path}");
        return 0;
    }
}

/// CLI command: `spid3r magic bench [--gate] [--runs=N]` — run micro-benchmarks and regression gate.
public class BenchCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;
    private let kernel;

    fn new (kernel) {
        self.name = "bench";
        self.usage = "bench [--gate] [--runs=N]";
        self.description = "Run micro-benchmarks and optional regression perf gate";
        self.kernel = kernel;
    }

    public fn handle(self, cli_args: list) -> int {
        let root = self.kernel.resolve_root();
        ensure_spid3r_path(root);
        let bin = find_spid3r_binary();
        let gate = false;
        let runs = 1;
        let target = "{root}benchmarks/bench.matrix";
        let i = 1;
        while i < cli_args.len() {
            let a = string(cli_args[i]);
            if a == "--gate" {
                gate = true;
            } elif a.starts_with("--runs=") {
                runs = int(a.slice(7, a.len()));
            } elif a == "--runs" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
                i = i + 1;
                runs = int(cli_args[i]);
            } elif not a.starts_with("-") {
                target = a;
            }
            i = i + 1;
        }
        if runs < 1 {
            runs = 1;
        }

        let b_args = ["bench", "--runs", string(runs)];
        if gate {
            b_args.push("--gate");
        }
        b_args.push(target);
        let r = exec_capture(bin, b_args, null);
        if r.is_err() {
            let err = string(r.unwrap_err());
            println("[Magic Bench] Failed to invoke spid3r bench: {err}");
            return 1;
        }
        let info = r.unwrap();
        let out_s = string(info["stdout"]).trim();
        let err_s = string(info["stderr"]).trim();
        if out_s != "" {
            println(out_s);
        }
        if err_s != "" {
            println(err_s);
        }
        if int(info["code"]) != 0 {
            return int(info["code"]);
        }

        if gate {
            let prev = env("SPID3R_BENCH_GATE").unwrap_or("");
            env_set("SPID3R_BENCH_GATE", "1");
            try {
                let gr = exec_capture(bin, ["run", target], null);
                if gr.is_err() {
                    println("[Magic Bench FAIL] Unable to execute benchmark perf gate.");
                    return 1;
                }
                let ginfo = gr.unwrap();
                let gout = string(ginfo["stdout"]).trim();
                let gerr = string(ginfo["stderr"]).trim();
                if gout != "" {
                    println(gout);
                }
                if gerr != "" {
                    println(gerr);
                }
                if int(ginfo["code"]) != 0 {
                    return int(ginfo["code"]);
                }
            } finally {
                env_set("SPID3R_BENCH_GATE", prev);
            }
        }
        return 0;
    }
}

FILE: framework/Console/Commands/DoctorCommand.matrix

// framework/Console/Commands/DoctorCommand.matrix
// `spid3r magic doctor [--fix] [--strict]` — checks permissions, secrets,
// config validity, writable storage, security policy, and DB connectivity.

import "framework/Foundation/Config";
import "framework/Foundation/ConfigValidator";
import "framework/Foundation/RuntimeVersion";
import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Console/TestRunner";

fn detect_windows() -> bool {
    let os_env = env("OS").unwrap_or("").trim().lower();
    if os_env.contains("windows") {
        return true;
    }
    return env("USERPROFILE").is_some() and env("SystemRoot").is_some();
}

fn posix_stat_mode(path: string) -> string {
    let r1 = exec_capture("stat", ["-c", "%a", path], null);
    if r1.is_ok() {
        let out = r1.unwrap();
        if int(out["code"]) == 0 {
            let m = string(out["stdout"]).trim();
            if m.len() >= 3 {
                return m.slice(m.len() - 3, m.len());
            }
            return m;
        }
    }
    let r2 = exec_capture("stat", ["-f", "%Lp", path], null);
    if r2.is_ok() {
        let out2 = r2.unwrap();
        if int(out2["code"]) == 0 {
            let m2 = string(out2["stdout"]).trim();
            if m2.len() >= 3 {
                return m2.slice(m2.len() - 3, m2.len());
            }
            return m2;
        }
    }
    return "";
}

fn chmod_path(mode: string, path: string) -> bool {
    let r = exec_capture("chmod", [mode, path], null);
    if r.is_err() {
        return false;
    }
    return int(r.unwrap()["code"]) == 0;
}

public class DoctorCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;
    private let kernel;

    fn new (kernel) {
        self.name = "doctor";
        self.usage = "doctor [--fix] [--strict]";
        self.description = "Inspect permissions, secrets, ini config, and DB health";
        self.kernel = kernel;
    }

    private fn rec(self, stats: map, kind: string, label: string, detail: string) {
        let _ = self;
        stats[kind] = int(stats[kind]) + 1;
        let tag = string(kind).upper();
        println("  [{tag}] {label}: {detail}");
    }

    private fn check_posix_perms(self, stats, root: string, fix: bool) {
        let env_path = "{root}.env";
        let env_exists = false;
        try {
            env_exists = file_exists(env_path);
        } catch (_e) {
            env_exists = false;
        }
        if env_exists {
            let mode = posix_stat_mode(env_path);
            if mode == "" {
                self.rec(stats, "warn", ".env permissions", "unable to stat mode");
            } elif mode == "600" or mode == "400" {
                self.rec(stats, "pass", ".env permissions", "mode 0{mode}");
            } elif fix and chmod_path("0600", env_path) {
                self.rec(stats, "pass", ".env permissions", "repaired 0{mode} -> 0600");
            } else {
                let lvl = if is_production() {
                    "fail"
                } else {
                    "warn"
                };
                self.rec(stats, lvl, ".env permissions", "mode 0{mode}; expected 0600 (run --fix)");
            }
        } else {
            self.rec(
                stats,
                "pass",
                ".env permissions",
                "no .env file (or confined by basedir; using OS environment)"
            );
        }

        let writable_dirs = [
        "{root}storage",
        "{root}storage/logs",
        "{root}storage/framework",
        "{root}storage/framework/cache",
        "{root}storage/app",
        "{root}storage/backups",
        "{root}storage/database",
        "{root}database"
        ];
        for d in writable_dirs {
            if not is_dir(d) {
                continue;
            }
            let mode = posix_stat_mode(d);
            if mode == "" {
                continue;
            }
            let tail = if mode.len() >= 2 {
                mode.slice(mode.len() - 2, mode.len())
            } else {
                mode
            };
            if tail == "00" or tail == "50" {
                self.rec(stats, "pass", "dir {d}", "mode 0{mode}");
            } elif fix and chmod_path("0700", d) {
                self.rec(stats, "pass", "dir {d}", "repaired 0{mode} -> 0700");
            } else {
                self.rec(stats, "warn", "dir {d}", "mode 0{mode}; expected 0700/0750");
            }
        }

        if DB.driver_name() == "sqlite" and file_exists(DB.db_path) {
            let dbm = posix_stat_mode(DB.db_path);
            if dbm == "600" or dbm == "640" or dbm == "400" {
                self.rec(stats, "pass", "sqlite db perms", "mode 0{dbm}");
            } elif dbm != "" and fix and chmod_path("0600", DB.db_path) {
                self.rec(stats, "pass", "sqlite db perms", "repaired 0{dbm} -> 0600");
            } elif dbm != "" {
                self.rec(stats, "warn", "sqlite db perms", "mode 0{dbm}; expected 0600");
            }
        }
    }

    public fn handle(self, cli_args: list) -> int {
        let fix = false;
        let strict = false;
        for a in cli_args {
            let s = string(a);
            if s == "--fix" {
                fix = true;
            } elif s == "--strict" {
                strict = true;
            }
        }

        let stats = {
            "pass": 0, "warn": 0, "fail": 0, "info": 0
        };
        let root = self.kernel.resolve_root();
        let bin = find_spid3r_binary();
        println("[Doctor] Inspecting Spid3rWeb runtime health...");

        // 1. Runtime version
        let v_res = exec_capture(bin, ["--version"], null);
        if v_res.is_ok() and int(v_res.unwrap()["code"]) == 0 {
            let v_txt = string(v_res.unwrap()["stdout"]).trim();
            let parsed = parse_semver(v_txt);
            if version_at_least(parsed, 1, 1, 1) {
                let ver_label = string(parsed[0]) + "." + string(parsed[1]) + "." + string(parsed[2]);
                self.rec(stats, "pass", "spid3r runtime", "v{ver_label}");
            } else {
                self.rec(
                    stats,
                    "fail",
                    "spid3r runtime",
                    "requires >= {MIN_SPID3R_VERSION} (got {v_txt})"
                );
            }
        } else {
            self.rec(stats, "warn", "spid3r runtime", "unable to query '{bin} --version'");
        }

        // 2. Config validation
        let c_res = exec_capture(bin, ["config", "validate"], null);
        if c_res.is_ok() and int(c_res.unwrap()["code"]) == 0 {
            self.rec(stats, "pass", "spid3r config validate", "ok");
        } else {
            self.rec(
                stats,
                "fail",
                "spid3r config validate",
                "spid3r.ini has invalid or unknown keys"
            );
        }

        let cfg_check = validate_all_config();
        if cfg_check["ok"] == true {
            let ns_count = cfg_check["checked"];
            self.rec(stats, "pass", "config schema", "validated {ns_count} namespace(s)");
        } else {
            let first_err = string(cfg_check["errors"][0]);
            self.rec(stats, "fail", "config schema", first_err);
        }

        // 3. APP_ENV & APP_DEBUG consistency
        let app_env = resolve_app_env();
        let app_dbg = resolve_app_debug();
        if app_env == "production" and app_dbg {
            self.rec(stats, "fail", "debug mode", "APP_ENV=production with debug=true");
        } else {
            self.rec(stats, "pass", "environment", "env={app_env}, debug={app_dbg}");
        }

        // 4. APP_KEY check
        let key = configured_app_key();
        if key == "" or is_insecure_app_key(key) {
            let lvl = if is_production() {
                "fail"
            } else {
                "warn"
            };
            self.rec(
                stats,
                lvl,
                "APP_KEY",
                "missing or insecure placeholder; run 'spid3r magic key:generate'"
            );
        } else {
            self.rec(stats, "pass", "APP_KEY", "configured");
        }

        // 5. Security policy snapshot
        let bd = string(config_get("security.basedir") ?? "").trim();
        let dis_mods = string(config_get("modules.disable") ?? "").trim();
        if bd == "" {
            self.rec(stats, "info", "security policy", "security.basedir unset (dev profile)");
        } else {
            self.rec(stats, "pass", "security policy", "basedir='{bd}', disable='{dis_mods}'");
        }

        // 6. Required writable directories
        let dirs = [
        "{root}storage",
        "{root}storage/logs",
        "{root}storage/framework",
        "{root}storage/framework/cache",
        "{root}storage/app",
        "{root}storage/database",
        "{root}database"
        ];
        for d in dirs {
            if is_dir(d) {
                self.rec(stats, "pass", "directory", "{d} exists");
            } elif fix and dir_create(d).is_ok() {
                self.rec(stats, "pass", "directory", "created {d}");
            } else {
                self.rec(stats, "fail", "directory", "missing writable directory '{d}' (run --fix)");
            }
        }
        if fix and not is_dir("{root}storage/backups") {
            let _bk = dir_create("{root}storage/backups");
        }

        // 7. File permissions / Windows OneDrive guard
        if detect_windows() {
            self.rec(
                stats,
                "info",
                "file permissions",
                "Windows host (POSIX 0600/0700 checks skipped)"
            );
            let cw = cwd();
            if cw.is_ok() and string(cw.unwrap()).lower().contains("onedrive") {
                self.rec(
                    stats,
                    "warn",
                    "OneDrive sync",
                    "workspace is inside OneDrive; set SPID3R_STATE_DIR or SPID3R_DB_PATH outside synced folders for production/heavy SQLite writes"
                );
            }
        } else {
            self.check_posix_perms(stats, root, fix);
        }

        // 8. Database health
        if DB.ping() {
            let drv = DB.driver_name();
            self.rec(stats, "pass", "database", "{drv} reachable (SELECT 1 ok)");
        } else {
            self.rec(stats, "fail", "database", "unable to reach configured database");
        }
        DB.close();

        let p = stats["pass"];
        let w = stats["warn"];
        let f = stats["fail"];
        println("\nDoctor summary: {p} passed, {w} warning(s), {f} failed.");
        if f > 0 or (strict and w > 0) {
            return 1;
        }
        return 0;
    }
}

// framework/Console/Commands/MaintenanceCommands.matrix
// Storage, Cache & Database Housekeeping Commands:
//   storage:prune, cache:gc, db:backup, db:vacuum

import "framework/Foundation/Config";
import "framework/Foundation/ConfigValidator";
import "framework/Cache/Cache";
import "framework/Log/Log";
import "framework/Filesystem/Storage";
import "framework/Database/DB";

public class StoragePruneCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;
    private let kernel;

    fn new (kernel) {
        self.name = "storage:prune";
        self.usage = "storage:prune [--days=N] [--temp-age=S]";
        self.description = "Prune expired log files and stale temporary uploads";
        self.kernel = kernel;
    }

    public fn handle(self, cli_args: list) -> int {
        let days = -1;
        let temp_age = 3_600;
        for a in cli_args {
            let s = string(a);
            if s.starts_with("--days=") {
                days = int(s.slice(7, s.len()));
            } elif s.starts_with("--temp-age=") {
                temp_age = int(s.slice(11, s.len()));
            }
        }
        let root = self.kernel.resolve_root();
        let logs_pruned = Log.prune(days, "{root}storage/logs");
        let tmp_pruned = Storage.prune_temp(temp_age);
        println("[Storage] Pruned {logs_pruned} log file(s) and {tmp_pruned} temp file(s).");
        return 0;
    }
}

public class CacheGcCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;

    fn new () {
        self.name = "cache:gc";
        self.usage = "cache:gc [store]";
        self.description = "Garbage-collect expired entries from cache stores";
    }

    public fn handle(self, cli_args: list) -> int {
        let _ = self;
        if cli_args.len() > 1 {
            let store_name = string(cli_args[1]).trim().lower();
            let n = Cache.gc(store_name);
            println("[Cache] Garbage-collected {n} expired entry/entries from '{store_name}'.");
            if store_name == "database" or store_name == "db" {
                DB.close();
            }
            return 0;
        }
        let mem_n = Cache.gc("memory");
        let file_n = Cache.gc("file");
        let db_n = 0;
        try {
            db_n = Cache.gc("database");
        } catch (_e) {
            db_n = 0;
        }
        DB.close();
        println("[Cache] Garbage-collected expired entries (memory={mem_n}, file={file_n}, database={db_n}).");
        return 0;
    }
}

public class DbBackupCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;
    private let kernel;

    fn new (kernel) {
        self.name = "db:backup";
        self.usage = "db:backup [dest]";
        self.description = "Create an online SQLite database backup in storage/backups";
        self.kernel = kernel;
    }

    public fn handle(self, cli_args: list) -> int {
        let drv = DB.driver_name();
        if drv != "sqlite" {
            println("[DB Backup] Online backup helper supports SQLite; use pg_dump/mysqldump for '{drv}'.");
            return 0;
        }
        let root = self.kernel.resolve_root();
        let ts = int(unix_time());
        let dest = if cli_args.len() > 1 {
            string(cli_args[1]).trim()
        } else {
            "{root}storage/backups/db-{ts}.sqlite"
        };
        if dest == "" or dest.contains("'") or dest.contains("\r") or dest.contains("\n") {
            println("[Magic Error] Invalid backup destination path '{dest}'.");
            return 1;
        }
        let parts = dest.replace("\\", "/").split("/");
        if parts.len() > 1 {
            parts.pop();
            let parent = parts.join("/");
            if parent != "" and not file_exists(parent) {
                dir_create(parent);
            }
        }
        if file_exists(dest) {
            let _ = file_delete(dest);
        }
        let used_vacuum = false;
        try {
            DB.statement("VACUUM INTO '{dest}';");
            used_vacuum = true;
        } catch (_e) {
            used_vacuum = false;
        }
        if not used_vacuum {
            DB.checkpoint();
            let cp = copy_file(DB.db_path, dest);
            if cp.is_err() {
                let err = string(cp.unwrap_err());
                println("[Magic Error] Backup failed: {err}");
                DB.close();
                return 1;
            }
        }
        DB.close();
        println("[DB Backup] Snapshot saved to {dest}");
        return 0;
    }
}

public class DbVacuumCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;

    fn new () {
        self.name = "db:vacuum";
        self.usage = "db:vacuum";
        self.description = "Checkpoint WAL and compact the database file";
    }

    public fn handle(self, _cli_args: list) -> int {
        let _ = self;
        let drv = DB.driver_name();
        if drv == "sqlite" {
            DB.checkpoint();
            DB.statement("VACUUM;");
            DB.close();
            println("[DB] SQLite WAL checkpointed and database vacuumed.");
            return 0;
        }
        if drv == "postgres" {
            DB.statement("VACUUM;");
            DB.close();
            println("[DB] PostgreSQL VACUUM complete.");
            return 0;
        }
        println("[DB] Automatic VACUUM is not needed for '{drv}' (use OPTIMIZE TABLE manually).");
        DB.close();
        return 0;
    }
}

public class ConfigCheckCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;

    fn new () {
        self.name = "config:check";
        self.usage = "config:check";
        self.description = "Validate all framework config namespaces and value bounds";
    }

    public fn handle(self, _cli_args: list) -> int {
        let _ = self;
        let res = validate_all_config();
        let checked = res["checked"];
        if res["ok"] == true {
            println("[Config] Validated {checked} namespace(s) — ok.");
            return 0;
        }
        for err in res["errors"] {
            let e = string(err);
            println("[Config FAIL] {e}");
        }
        return 1;
    }
}

public class StorageLinkCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;
    private let kernel;

    fn new (kernel) {
        self.name = "storage:link";
        self.usage = "storage:link";
        self.description = "Create public/storage link to storage/app/public";
        self.kernel = kernel;
    }

    public fn handle(self, _cli_args: list) -> int {
        let root = self.kernel.resolve_root();
        let src = "{root}storage/app/public";
        let dst = "{root}public/storage";
        if not is_dir(src) {
            let mk = dir_create(src);
            if mk.is_err() {
                let err = string(mk.unwrap_err());
                println("[Storage Error] Failed creating '{src}': {err}");
                return 1;
            }
        }
        if file_exists(dst) {
            println("[Storage] The '{dst}' link already exists.");
            return 0;
        }
        let is_win = env("OS").unwrap_or("").lower().contains("windows")
        or (env("USERPROFILE").is_some() and env("SystemRoot").is_some());
        let res = if is_win {
            let win_dst = dst.replace("/", "\\");
            let win_src = src.replace("/", "\\");
            exec_capture("cmd", ["/c", "mklink", "/J", win_dst, win_src], null)
        } else {
            exec_capture("ln", ["-s", "../storage/app/public", dst], null)
        };
        if res.is_err() or int(res.unwrap()["code"]) != 0 {
            println("[Storage Error] Could not link '{dst}' -> '{src}'.");
            return 1;
        }
        println("[Storage] Linked '{dst}' -> '{src}'.");
        return 0;
    }
}
