// framework/Console/Commands/MakeCommand.matrix
// Scaffolding commands (make:controller, make:model, make:middleware,
// make:provider, make:test, make:migration).

import "MigrationRegistry";

fn valid_scaffold_name(name: string) -> bool {
    let allowed = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789_";
    let s = string(name);
    if s.len() == 0 {
        return false;
    }
    let i = 0;
    while i < s.len() {
        if not allowed.contains(s.slice(i, i + 1)) {
            return false;
        }
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return not string("0123456789").contains(s.slice(0, 1));
}

fn write_scaffold(file_path: string, content: string, label: string) -> int {
    ensure_parent_dirs(file_path);
    let res = write_file(file_path, content);
    if res.is_err() {
        let err = string(res.unwrap_err());
        println("[Magic Error] {err}");
        return 1;
    }
    println("[Magic] {label} created successfully: {file_path}");
    return 0;
}

fn make_controller(root: string, name: string) -> int {
    if not valid_scaffold_name(name) {
        println(
            "[Magic Error] Invalid controller name '{name}' — use letters, digits and underscores only (e.g. PostController)."
        );
        return 1;
    }
    let clean = name;
    if not clean.ends_with("Controller") {
        clean = "{clean}Controller";
    }
    let base_view = clean.slice(0, clean.len() - 10).lower();
    let file_path = "{root}app/Controllers/{clean}.matrix";
    let content = "// {file_path}\nimport \"framework/Http/Controller\";\n\npublic class {clean} extends Controller {\n    fn new() {\n        let _ = self;\n    }\n\n    public fn index(self, req: any) -> Response {\n        return self.render(req, \"{base_view}.index\", {\"title\": \"{clean}\"});\n    }\n\n    public fn show(self, req: any) -> Response {\n        let id = req.int(\"id\");\n        return self.ok({\"id\": id});\n    }\n\n    public fn store(self, req: any) -> Response {\n        let data = req.validate_or_fail({\"title\": \"required|min:2\"});\n        return self.created(data);\n    }\n}\n";
    return write_scaffold(file_path, content, "Controller");
}

fn make_model(kernel, root: string, name: string, with_mig: bool) -> int {
    if not valid_scaffold_name(name) {
        println(
            "[Magic Error] Invalid model name '{name}' — use letters, digits and underscores only (e.g. Post)."
        );
        return 1;
    }
    let file_path = "{root}app/Models/{name}.matrix";
    let lower_n = string(name).lower();
    let table_name = "{lower_n}s";
    let content = "// {file_path}\nimport \"framework/Database/ModelRepository\";\n\npublic let {name} = define_model(\"{table_name}\", {\n    \"fillable\": [],\n    \"hidden\": [],\n    \"casts\": {}\n});\n\npublic let {name}Model = {name};\n";
    let code = write_scaffold(file_path, content, "Model");
    if code == 0 and with_mig {
        return make_migration(kernel, "create_{table_name}_table");
    }
    return code;
}

fn make_middleware(root: string, name: string) -> int {
    if not valid_scaffold_name(name) {
        println("[Magic Error] Invalid middleware name '{name}'.");
        return 1;
    }
    let clean = name;
    if not clean.ends_with("Middleware") {
        clean = "{clean}Middleware";
    }
    let file_path = "{root}app/Middleware/{clean}.matrix";
    let content = "// {file_path}\npublic class {clean} {\n    fn new() {\n        let _ = self;\n    }\n\n    public fn handle(self, req: any, next: any = null) -> any {\n        let _ = self;\n        if next != null {\n            return next(req);\n        }\n        return null;\n    }\n}\n";
    return write_scaffold(file_path, content, "Middleware");
}

fn make_provider(root: string, name: string) -> int {
    if not valid_scaffold_name(name) {
        println("[Magic Error] Invalid provider name '{name}'.");
        return 1;
    }
    let clean = name;
    if not clean.ends_with("Provider") {
        clean = "{clean}Provider";
    }
    let file_path = "{root}app/Providers/{clean}.matrix";
    let content = "// {file_path}\nimport \"framework/Foundation/ServiceProvider\";\n\npublic class {clean} extends ServiceProvider {\n    fn new() {\n        let _ = self;\n    }\n\n    public fn register(self, _container: any) {\n        let _ = self;\n    }\n\n    public fn boot(self, _container: any) {\n        let _ = self;\n    }\n}\n";
    return write_scaffold(file_path, content, "Provider");
}

fn make_test(root: string, name: string) -> int {
    if not valid_scaffold_name(name) {
        println("[Magic Error] Invalid test name '{name}'.");
        return 1;
    }
    let clean = name.lower();
    if not clean.starts_with("test_") {
        clean = "test_{clean}";
    }
    let file_path = "{root}tests/{clean}.matrix";
    let content = "// {file_path}\nimport \"framework/Web\";\n\nfn main() {\n    let app = web();\n    app.get(\"/ping\", req => {\"pong\": true});\n    app.test_client().get(\"/ping\").assert_ok().assert_json_path(\"pong\", true);\n    println(\"=== All {clean} Tests Passed! ===\");\n}\n";
    return write_scaffold(file_path, content, "Test suite");
}

fn ensure_suffix(name: string, suffix: string) -> string {
    if name.ends_with(suffix) {
        return name;
    }
    return "{name}{suffix}";
}

fn make_request(root: string, name: string) -> int {
    if not valid_scaffold_name(name) {
        println("[Magic Error] Invalid request name '{name}'.");
        return 1;
    }
    let clean = ensure_suffix(name, "Request");
    let file_path = "{root}app/Requests/{clean}.matrix";
    let content = "// {file_path}\npublic class {clean} {\n    fn new () {\n        let _ = self;\n    }\n\n    public fn rules(self) -> map {\n        let _ = self;\n        return {\n            \"name\": \"required|min:2\"\n        };\n    }\n\n    public fn validate(self, req: any) -> map {\n        return req.validate_or_fail(self.rules());\n    }\n}\n";
    return write_scaffold(file_path, content, "Request");
}

fn make_resource(root: string, name: string) -> int {
    if not valid_scaffold_name(name) {
        println("[Magic Error] Invalid resource name '{name}'.");
        return 1;
    }
    let clean = ensure_suffix(name, "Resource");
    let file_path = "{root}app/Resources/{clean}.matrix";
    let content = "// {file_path}\nimport \"framework/Http/Resource\";\n\npublic fn format_{clean}(row) {\n    return {\n        \"id\": row[\"id\"]\n    };\n}\n\npublic class {clean} {\n    public static fn item(model) {\n        let _ = self;\n        return resource_item(model, format_{clean});\n    }\n\n    public static fn collection(items) {\n        let _ = self;\n        return resource_collection(items, format_{clean});\n    }\n}\n";
    return write_scaffold(file_path, content, "Resource");
}

fn make_command(root: string, name: string) -> int {
    if not valid_scaffold_name(name) {
        println("[Magic Error] Invalid command name '{name}'.");
        return 1;
    }
    let clean = ensure_suffix(name, "Command");
    let slug = clean.slice(0, clean.len() - 7).lower();
    let file_path = "{root}app/Console/{clean}.matrix";
    let content = "// {file_path}\npublic class {clean} {\n    public let name;\n    public let usage;\n    public let description;\n\n    fn new () {\n        self.name = \"app:{slug}\";\n        self.usage = \"app:{slug}\";\n        self.description = \"Custom {clean}\";\n    }\n\n    public fn handle(self, _cli_args) -> int {\n        println(\"[{clean}] Running app:{slug}...\");\n        return 0;\n    }\n}\n";
    return write_scaffold(file_path, content, "Command");
}

fn make_seeder(root: string, name: string) -> int {
    if not valid_scaffold_name(name) {
        println("[Magic Error] Invalid seeder name '{name}'.");
        return 1;
    }
    let clean = ensure_suffix(name, "Seeder");
    let file_path = "{root}database/seeders/{clean}.matrix";
    let content = "// {file_path}\nimport \"framework/Database/DB\";\n\npublic class {clean} {\n    fn new () {\n        let _ = self;\n    }\n\n    public fn run(self) {\n        let _ = self;\n        // DB.table(\"items\").insert({\"name\": \"sample\"});\n    }\n}\n";
    return write_scaffold(file_path, content, "Seeder");
}

fn make_migration(kernel, name: string, fn_mode: bool = false) -> int {
    let allowed = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789_";
    let sn = string(name);
    let ok = sn.len() > 0;
    let i = 0;
    while i < sn.len() {
        if not allowed.contains(sn.slice(i, i + 1)) {
            ok = false;
        }
        i = i + 1;
    }
    if not ok {
        println(
            "[Magic Error] Invalid migration name '{sn}' — use lowercase words separated by underscores (e.g. create_posts_table)."
        );
        return 1;
    }
    let ts = format_migration_timestamp(int(unix_time()));
    let stem = "{ts}_{name}";
    let mdir = kernel.migrations_dir();
    let file_path = "{mdir}/{stem}.matrix";

    let content = "";
    if fn_mode {
        content = "// database/migrations/{stem}.matrix\nimport \"framework/Database/Schema\";\n\npublic fn up() {\n    Schema.create(\"sample\", table => {\n        table.id();\n        table.timestamps();\n    });\n}\n\npublic fn down() {\n    Schema.dropIfExists(\"sample\");\n}\n";
    } else {
        let class_words = [];
        for w in name.split("_") {
            let fw = w.trim();
            if fw != "" {
                let head = fw.slice(0, 1).upper();
                if fw.len() > 1 {
                    head = head + fw.slice(1, fw.len());
                }
                class_words.push(head);
            }
        }
        let cls_tail = class_words.join("");
        let class_name = "Migration{cls_tail}";
        content = "// database/migrations/{stem}.matrix\nimport \"framework/Database/Schema\";\nimport \"framework/Database/Migration\";\n\npublic class {class_name} extends Migration {\n    fn new (name: string = \"{stem}\") {\n        self.name = name;\n        self.checksum = \"\";\n    }\n\n    public fn up(self) {\n        let _ = self;\n        Schema.create(\"sample\", table => {\n            table.id();\n            table.timestamps();\n        });\n    }\n\n    public fn down(self) {\n        let _ = self;\n        Schema.dropIfExists(\"sample\");\n    }\n}\n";
    }
    let code = write_scaffold(file_path, content, "Migration");
    if code == 0 {
        let _ = kernel.sync_migration_registry(true, true);
        println("[Magic] Registered automatically — run `spid3r magic migrate` to apply.");
    }
    return code;
}

public class MakeCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;
    private let missing_msg;
    private let kernel;

    fn new (
        kernel,
        cmd_name: string,
        usage: string,
        description: string,
        missing_msg: string
    ) {
        self.kernel = kernel;
        self.name = cmd_name;
        self.usage = usage;
        self.description = description;
        self.missing_msg = missing_msg;
    }

    public fn handle(self, cli_args: list) -> int {
        if cli_args.len() < 2 {
            let u = self.usage;
            let msg = self.missing_msg;
            println("Error: Not enough arguments ({msg}). Usage: {u}");
            return 1;
        }
        let target = string(cli_args[1]);
        let root = self.kernel.resolve_root();
        match (self.name) {
            "make:controller" => {
                return make_controller(root, target);
            }
            ,
            "make:model" => {
                let with_mig = cli_args.len() > 2 and string(cli_args[2]) == "-m";
                return make_model(self.kernel, root, target, with_mig);
            }
            ,
            "make:middleware" => {
                return make_middleware(root, target);
            }
            ,
            "make:provider" => {
                return make_provider(root, target);
            }
            ,
            "make:test" => {
                return make_test(root, target);
            }
            ,
            "make:migration" => {
                let fn_mode = false;
                for a in cli_args {
                    if string(a) == "--fn" {
                        fn_mode = true;
                    }
                }
                return make_migration(self.kernel, target, fn_mode);
            }
            ,
            "make:request" => {
                return make_request(root, target);
            }
            ,
            "make:resource" => {
                return make_resource(root, target);
            }
            ,
            "make:command" => {
                return make_command(root, target);
            }
            ,
            "make:seeder" => {
                return make_seeder(root, target);
            }
            ,
            _ => {
                return 1;
            }
        }
    }
}

// framework/Console/Commands/MigrateCommands.matrix
// Database migration CLI command classes registered into ConsoleKernel.

import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Database/Migration";
import "MigrationRegistry";

fn parse_migrate_flags(cli_args) {
    let pretend = false;
    let step = false;
    let extra = [];
    for a in cli_args {
        let s = string(a);
        if s == "--pretend" and not pretend {
            pretend = true;
            extra.push("--pretend");
        } elif s == "--step" and not step {
            step = true;
            extra.push("--step");
        }
    }
    return {
        "pretend": pretend, "step": step, "extra": extra
    };
}

public class MigrateCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;
    private let kernel;

    fn new (kernel) {
        self.name = "migrate";
        self.usage = "migrate [--pretend] [--step]";
        self.description = "Run outstanding database migrations";
        self.kernel = kernel;
    }

    public fn handle(self, cli_args: list) -> int {
        let flags = parse_migrate_flags(cli_args);
        let mem = self.kernel.migrations();
        if mem.len() > 0 {
            let runner = new MigrationRunner();
            runner.migrate(mem, flags["pretend"] == true, flags["step"] == true);
            DB.close();
            return 0;
        }
        return run_migration_action(
            "migrate",
            self.kernel.migrations_dir(),
            self.kernel.registry_path(),
            flags["extra"]
        );
    }
}

    public let name;

    private let kernel;

        );
    }

    }

// framework/Console/Commands/MigrationRegistry.matrix
// Auto-discovery, timestamp formatting, and storage-backed migration runner
// registry supporting both Migration subclasses and up/down function modules.

import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Console/TestRunner";

public const DEFAULT_REGISTRY_PATH = "storage/framework/migrations.matrix";

public fn ensure_parent_dirs(file_path: string) -> string {
    let parts = string(file_path).replace("\\", "/").split("/");
    let cur = "";
    let i = 0;
    while i < parts.len() - 1 {
        let seg = parts[i];
        if seg != "" {
            if cur == "" {
                cur = seg;
            } else {
                cur = "{cur}/{seg}";
            }
            if cur != "." and not file_exists(cur) {
                dir_create(cur);
            }
        }
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return cur;
}

fn pad2_num(n: int) -> string {
    if n < 10 {
        return "0{n}";
    }
    return "{n}";
}

// UTC timestamp formatted as YYYY_MM_DD_HHMMSS for Laravel-style filenames.
public fn format_migration_timestamp(ts: int) -> string {
    let secs = ts % 86_400;
    let hh = secs / 3_600;
    let mm = (secs % 3_600) / 60;
    let ss = secs % 60;
    let days = ts / 86_400;
    let z = days + 719468;
    let era = z / 146097;
    let doe = z - era * 146097;
    let yoe = (doe - doe / 1460 + doe / 36524 - doe / 146096) / 365;
    let y = yoe + era * 400;
    let doy = doe - (365 * yoe + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100);
    let mp = (5 * doy + 2) / 153;
    let d = doy - (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + 1;
    let m = if mp < 10 {
        mp + 3
    } else {
        mp - 9
    };
    if m <= 2 {
        y = y + 1;
    }
    let ms = pad2_num(m);
    let ds = pad2_num(d);
    let hs = pad2_num(hh);
    let mins = pad2_num(mm);
    let secs_s = pad2_num(ss);
    return "{y}_{ms}_{ds}_{hs}{mins}{secs_s}";
}

public fn scan_migration_files(migrations_dir: string) -> list {
    let out = [];
    let res = list_dir(migrations_dir);
    if res.is_err() {
        return out;
    }
    for name in res.unwrap() {
        let n = string(name);
        if n.ends_with(".matrix") and n != "registry.matrix" {
            out.push(n);
        }
    }
    let i = 1;
    while i < out.len() {
        let cur = out[i];
        let j = i - 1;
        while j >= 0 and out[j] > cur {
            out[j + 1] = out[j];
            j = j - 1;
        }
        out[j + 1] = cur;
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return out;
}

public fn extract_migration_class(content: string) -> string {
    if not content.contains("extends Migration") {
        return "";
    }
    let parts = content.split("public class ");
    if parts.len() < 2 {
        return "";
    }
    let words = parts[1].trim().split(" ");
    if words.len() == 0 {
        return "";
    }
    return words[0].trim();
}

public fn is_function_migration(content: string) -> bool {
    if content.contains("extends Migration") {
        return false;
    }
    return content.contains("public fn up") and content.contains("public fn down");
}

fn normalize_import_prefix(migrations_dir: string) -> string {
    let p = string(migrations_dir).replace("\\", "/").trim();
    while p.ends_with("/") and p.len() > 1 {
        p = p.slice(0, p.len() - 1);
    }
    if p.starts_with("./") {
        p = p.slice(2, p.len());
    }
    return p;
}

public fn sync_registry(
    migrations_dir: string = "database/migrations",
    target_path: string = "storage/framework/migrations.matrix",
    verbose: bool = false,
    force: bool = false
) -> int {
    let files = scan_migration_files(migrations_dir);
    let import_prefix = normalize_import_prefix(migrations_dir);

    let known = 0;
    let stale = 0;
    let digest_parts = [];
    let entries = [];
    for f in files {
        let res = read_file("{migrations_dir}/{f}");
        if res.is_err() {
            continue;
        }
        let raw = string(res.unwrap());
        let csum = sha256(raw);
        let cls = extract_migration_class(raw);
        let fn_mode = is_function_migration(raw);
        if cls == "" and not fn_mode {
            continue;
        }
        let stem = f.slice(0, f.len() - 7);
        digest_parts.push("{stem}:{csum}");
        entries.push({
            "stem": stem,
            "cls": cls,
            "fn_mode": fn_mode,
            "checksum": csum
        });
    }
    let expected_digest = sha256(digest_parts.join("|"));

    let existing_digest = "";
    let has_runner = false;
    if file_exists(target_path) {
        let res = read_file(target_path);
        if res.is_ok() {
            let content = string(res.unwrap());
            has_runner = content.contains("fn main()") and content.contains("MigrationRunner");
            for f in files {
                let mod_name = f.slice(0, f.len() - 7);
                if content.contains("\"{import_prefix}/{mod_name}\"") {
                    known = known + 1;
                }
            }
            let prefix_tok = "import \"{import_prefix}/";
            for line in content.split("\n") {
                let t = line.trim();
                if t.starts_with("// sync-digest:") {
                    existing_digest = t.slice(15, t.len()).trim();
                } elif t.starts_with(prefix_tok) {
                    let ref_name = t.replace(prefix_tok, "").replace("\";", "");
                    if not file_exists("{migrations_dir}/{ref_name}.matrix") {
                        stale = stale + 1;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    let new_count = files.len() - known;
    if not force and has_runner and new_count <= 0 and stale == 0 and existing_digest == expected_digest and file_exists(
        target_path
    ) {
        return 0;
    }

    let imports = [];
    let calls = [];
    let idx = 0;
    for ent in entries {
        let stem = string(ent["stem"]);
        let csum = string(ent["checksum"]);
        if ent["fn_mode"] == true {
            imports.push(
                "import {\n    up as up_{idx}, down as down_{idx}\n} from \"{import_prefix}/{stem}\";"
            );
            calls.push(
                "    kernel.register_migration(new FunctionMigration(\"{stem}\", up_{idx}, down_{idx}, \"{csum}\"));"
            );
        } else {
            let cls = string(ent["cls"]);
            imports.push("import \"{import_prefix}/{stem}\";");
            calls.push("    let m_{idx} = new {cls}(\"{stem}\");");
            calls.push("    m_{idx}.checksum = \"{csum}\";");
            calls.push("    kernel.register_migration(m_{idx});");
        }
        idx = idx + 1;
    }

    let imp_block = imports.join("\n");
    let call_block = calls.join("\n");
    let header = "// {target_path}\n// AUTO-GENERATED by Spid3rWeb — do not edit by hand.\n// sync-digest: {expected_digest}";
    let main_body = "\nfn main() {\n    let cli_args = args();\n    let action = \"migrate\";\n    let pretend = false;\n    let step = false;\n    for a in cli_args {\n        let s = string(a);\n        if s == \"--pretend\" {\n            pretend = true;\n        } elif s == \"--step\" {\n            step = true;\n        } else {\n            action = s;\n        }\n    }\n    let holder = new RegistryHolder();\n    register_all_migrations(holder);\n    let runner = new MigrationRunner();\n    if action == \"rollback\" {\n        runner.rollback(holder.items);\n    } elif action == \"reset\" {\n        runner.reset(holder.items);\n    } elif action == \"fresh\" {\n        runner.fresh(holder.items, pretend, step);\n    } elif action == \"wipe\" {\n        runner.wipe();\n    } elif action == \"status\" {\n        runner.status(holder.items);\n    } else {\n        runner.migrate(holder.items, pretend, step);\n    }\n    DB.close();\n    return 0;\n}\n";
    let content = "{header}\n\nimport \"framework/Database/DB\";\nimport \"framework/Database/Migration\";\n{imp_block}\n\npublic fn register_all_migrations(kernel) {\n{call_block}\n}\n\nclass RegistryHolder {\n    public let items;\n    fn new () {\n        self.items = [];\n    }\n    public fn register_migration(self, m) {\n        self.items.push(m);\n    }\n}{main_body}";

    ensure_parent_dirs(target_path);
    let w = write_file_atomic(target_path, content);
    if w.is_err() {
        let err = string(w.unwrap_err());
        println("[Magic Error] Failed to write migration registry '{target_path}': {err}");
        return -1;
    }
    if verbose {
        let count = entries.len();
        println("[Magic] Migration registry synced — {count} migration(s) registered.");
    }
    return new_count;
}

public fn run_migration_action(
    action: string,
    migrations_dir: string = "database/migrations",
    target_path: string = "storage/framework/migrations.matrix",
    extra_args: any = null
) -> int {
    let synced = sync_registry(migrations_dir, target_path, false, false);
    if synced < 0 {
        return 1;
    }
    if env("SPID3R_PATH").unwrap_or("").trim() == "" {
        env_set("SPID3R_PATH", ".");
    }
    let bin = find_spid3r_binary();
    let run_args = ["run", target_path, action];
    if extra_args != null {
        for a in extra_args {
            run_args.push(string(a));
        }
    }
    let res = exec_capture(bin, run_args, null);
    if res.is_err() {
        let err = string(res.unwrap_err());
        println("[Magic Error] Failed to execute '{target_path}': {err}");
        return 1;
    }
    let out = res.unwrap();
    let stdout_str = string(out["stdout"]);
    let stderr_str = string(out["stderr"]);
    if stdout_str != "" {
        print(stdout_str);
    }
    if stderr_str != "" {
        print(stderr_str);
    }
    return int(out["code"]);
}

fn is_legacy_numeric_name(filename: string) -> bool {
    if filename.len() < 5 or not filename.ends_with(".matrix") {
        return false;
    }
    if filename.slice(3, 4) != "_" {
        return false;
    }
    let digits = "0123456789";
    let i = 0;
    while i < 3 {
        if not digits.contains(filename.slice(i, i + 1)) {
            return false;
        }
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return true;
}

public fn rename_legacy_migrations(
    migrations_dir: string = "database/migrations",
    target_path: string = "storage/framework/migrations.matrix"
) -> int {
    let files = scan_migration_files(migrations_dir);
    let base_ts = int(unix_time());
    let renamed = 0;

    for f in files {
        if not is_legacy_numeric_name(f) {
            continue;
        }
        let old_stem = f.slice(0, f.len() - 7);
        let tail = old_stem.slice(4, old_stem.len());
        let stamp = format_migration_timestamp(base_ts + renamed);
        let new_stem = "{stamp}_{tail}";
        let old_path = "{migrations_dir}/{f}";
        let new_path = "{migrations_dir}/{new_stem}.matrix";
        let r = read_file(old_path);
        if r.is_err() {
            continue;
        }
        let body = string(r.unwrap()).replace(old_stem, new_stem);
        if write_file(new_path, body).is_err() {
            println("[Magic Error] Failed writing '{new_path}'.");
            return 1;
        }
        file_delete(old_path);
        try {
            DB.statement(
                "UPDATE migrations SET migration = ? WHERE migration = ?",
                [new_stem,
                old_stem]
            );
        } catch (_e) {
        }
        println("[Migrate] Renamed {old_stem} -> {new_stem}");
        renamed = renamed + 1;
    }

    if renamed == 0 {
        println("[Migrate] No legacy numeric migrations found.");
    } else {
        let _ = sync_registry(migrations_dir, target_path, true, true);
    }
    DB.close();
    return 0;
}

// framework/Console/Commands/ServeCommand.matrix
// Development / TLS HTTP server CLI command with optional --watch reload loop.

import "framework/Foundation/Application";
import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Console/TestRunner";
import "WatchHelper";

public class ServeCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;
    private let kernel;

    fn new (kernel) {
        self.name = "serve";
        self.usage = "serve [--host H] [--port N] [--watch] [--tls]";
        self.description = "Start HTTP/HTTPS server";
        self.kernel = kernel;
    }

    public fn handle(self, cli_args: list) -> int {
        let port = "8000";
        let host = "";
        let dry_run = false;
        let use_tls = false;
        let use_watch = false;
        let cert_path = "";
        let key_path = "";
        let child_extra = [];
        let i = 1;
        while i < cli_args.len() {
            let p_arg = string(cli_args[i]);
            if p_arg == "--watch" {
                use_watch = true;
            } elif p_arg.starts_with("--port=") {
                port = p_arg.replace("--port=", "");
                child_extra.push(p_arg);
            } elif p_arg == "--port" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
                i = i + 1;
                port = string(cli_args[i]);
                child_extra.push(p_arg);
                child_extra.push(port);
            } elif p_arg.starts_with("--host=") {
                host = p_arg.replace("--host=", "");
                child_extra.push(p_arg);
            } elif p_arg == "--host" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
                i = i + 1;
                host = string(cli_args[i]);
                child_extra.push(p_arg);
                child_extra.push(host);
            } elif p_arg == "--tls" {
                use_tls = true;
                child_extra.push(p_arg);
            } elif p_arg.starts_with("--cert=") {
                cert_path = p_arg.replace("--cert=", "");
                use_tls = true;
                child_extra.push(p_arg);
            } elif p_arg == "--cert" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
                i = i + 1;
                cert_path = string(cli_args[i]);
                use_tls = true;
                child_extra.push(p_arg);
                child_extra.push(cert_path);
            } elif p_arg.starts_with("--key=") {
                key_path = p_arg.replace("--key=", "");
                use_tls = true;
                child_extra.push(p_arg);
            } elif p_arg == "--key" and i + 1 < cli_args.len() {
                i = i + 1;
                key_path = string(cli_args[i]);
                use_tls = true;
                child_extra.push(p_arg);
                child_extra.push(key_path);
            } elif p_arg == "--dry-run" or p_arg == "--check" {
                dry_run = true;
                child_extra.push(p_arg);
            } else {
                port = p_arg;
                child_extra.push(p_arg);
            }
            i = i + 1;
        }

        let scheme = "http";
        if use_tls {
            scheme = "https";
            if cert_path == "" or key_path == "" {
                println("Error: --tls requires both --cert <path> and --key <path>.");
                DB.close();
                return 1;
            }
        }
        let app = self.kernel.get_app() ?? new Application();
        try {
            app.check_production_ready();
        } catch (e) {
            let err = string(e);
            println("[Magic Error] {err}");
            DB.close();
            return 1;
        }

        let is_watch_child = env("SPID3R_WATCH_CHILD").unwrap_or("").trim() == "1";
        if use_watch and not is_watch_child and not dry_run {
            DB.close();
            let bin = find_spid3r_binary();
            let magic_entry = self.kernel.resolve_root() + "magic";
            let spawn_args = [magic_entry, "serve"];
            for ca in child_extra {
                spawn_args.push(ca);
            }
            println(
                "[Watch] Watching app/, bootstrap/, config/, framework/, routes/, resources/views/..."
            );
            while true {
                let sp = spawn_process(bin, spawn_args, {
                    "env": {
                        "SPID3R_WATCH_CHILD": "1"
                    }
                });
                if sp.is_err() {
                    let serr = string(sp.unwrap_err());
                    println("[Watch Error] {serr}");
                    return 1;
                }
                let exit_code = int(sp.unwrap());
                if exit_code != 42 {
                    return exit_code;
                }
            }
        }

        let watch_state = {
            "changed": false
        };
        if is_watch_child and not dry_run {
            start_watch_reload_thread(self.kernel.resolve_root(), 750, watch_state);
        }

        let app_env = app.env;
        let pnum = int(port);
        let disp_host = if host != "" {
            host
        } else {
            "127.0.0.1"
        };
        println("Starting Spid3rWeb ({app_env}) server: {scheme}://{disp_host}:{pnum}");
        println("Press Ctrl+C to stop the server.");
        if dry_run {
            DB.close();
            return 0;
        }
        if use_tls {
            app.listen_tls(pnum, cert_path, key_path, host);
        } else {
            app.listen(pnum, host);
        }
        DB.close();
        if watch_state["changed"] == true {
            return 42;
        }
        return 0;
    }
}

// framework/Console/Commands/UtilityCommands.matrix
// Core developer CLI commands: test, route:list, db:seed, queue:work,
// cache:clear, and key:generate.

import "framework/Http/Router";
import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Queue/Queue";
import "framework/Cache/Cache";
import "framework/Console/TestRunner";

public class TestCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;
    private let kernel;

    fn new (kernel) {
        self.name = "test";
        self.usage = "test [options]";
        self.description = "Run the Spid3rWeb automated test suite";
        self.kernel = kernel;
    }

    public fn handle(self, cli_args: list) -> int {
        if env("SPID3R_MAGIC_TEST_GUARD").is_some() {
            return 0;
        }
        env_set("SPID3R_MAGIC_TEST_GUARD", "1");
        let runner = new TestRunner(self.kernel.resolve_root());
        return runner.run(cli_args);
    }
}

public class RouteListCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;

    fn new () {
        self.name = "route:list";
        self.usage = "route:list";
        self.description = "List all registered application routes";
    }

    public fn handle(self, _cli_args: list) -> int {
        let _ = self;
        let routes = Route.routes();
        println("\n+--------+------------------------------------+--------------------------------+");
        println("| Method | URI                                | Action / Name                  |");
        println("+--------+------------------------------------+--------------------------------+");

        for r in routes {
            let m_pad = r.method;
            while m_pad.len() < 6 {
                m_pad = "{m_pad} ";
            }
            let path_pad = r.path;
            while path_pad.len() < 34 {
                path_pad = "{path_pad} ";
            }
            let action_str = "Closure";
            if type_of(r.handler) == "string" {
                action_str = r.handler;
            } elif r.route_name != "" {
                let rn = r.route_name;
                action_str = "Closure [{rn}]";
            }
            let a_pad = action_str;
            while a_pad.len() < 30 {
                a_pad = "{a_pad} ";
            }
            println("| {m_pad} | {path_pad} | {a_pad} |");
        }
        println("+--------+------------------------------------+--------------------------------+\n");
        return 0;
    }
}

public class DbSeedCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;
    private let kernel;

    fn new (kernel) {
        self.name = "db:seed";
        self.usage = "db:seed";
        self.description = "Seed the database with records";
        self.kernel = kernel;
    }

    public fn handle(self, _cli_args: list) -> int {
        println("[Database Seeder] Seeding database...");
        let count = 0;
        for seeder in self.kernel.seeders() {
            seeder();
            count = count + 1;
        }
        println("[Database Seeder] Complete. Executed {count} seeders.");
        DB.close();
        return 0;
    }
}

public class QueueWorkCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;

    fn new () {
        self.name = "queue:work";
        self.usage = "queue:work [--once|--daemon] [--sleep=MS]";
        self.description = "Process queued jobs";
    }

    public fn handle(self, cli_args: list) -> int {
        let _ = self;
        let once = false;
        let daemon = false;
        let sleep_ms_val = 500;
        let queue = "";
        let i = 0;
        while i < cli_args.len() {
            let a = string(cli_args[i]);
            if a == "--once" {
                once = true;
            } elif a == "--daemon" {
                daemon = true;
            } elif a.starts_with("--sleep=") {
                sleep_ms_val = int(a.slice(8, a.len()));
            } elif a.starts_with("--queue=") {
                queue = a.slice(8, a.len());
            }
            i = i + 1;
        }

        let label = queue;
        if label == "" {
            label = "default";
        }
        let pending = Queue.size(queue);
        println("[Queue] worker on queue '{label}' — {pending} job(s) pending");

        let processed = 0;
        if once {
            if Queue.run_next(queue) {
                processed = processed + 1;
            }
        } elif daemon {
            println("[Queue] daemon mode active (poll {sleep_ms_val} ms, Ctrl+C to stop)");
            processed = Queue.work_daemon(sleep_ms_val, queue);
        } else {
            processed = Queue.work(0, queue);
        }

        println("[Queue] processed {processed} job(s) — done");
        DB.close();
        return 0;
    }
}

public class CacheClearCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;

    fn new () {
        self.name = "cache:clear";
        self.usage = "cache:clear";
        self.description = "Flush the application cache store";
    }

    public fn handle(self, _cli_args: list) -> int {
        let _ = self;
        Cache.flush();
        println("[Magic] Application cache cleared.");
        return 0;
    }
}

public class KeyGenerateCommand {
    public let name;
    public let usage;
    public let description;
    private let kernel;

    fn new (kernel) {
        self.name = "key:generate";
        self.usage = "key:generate";
        self.description = "Generate a random 256-bit APP_KEY in .env";
        self.kernel = kernel;
    }

    public fn handle(self, _cli_args: list) -> int {
        let hex_str = hex_encode(random_bytes(32).unwrap());
        let new_key = "hex:{hex_str}";
        let root = self.kernel.resolve_root();
        let env_path = "{root}.env";
        let content = "";
        if file_exists(env_path) {
            let r = read_file(env_path);
            if r.is_ok() {
                content = string(r.unwrap());
            }
        }
        if content.contains("APP_KEY=") {
            let out_lines = [];
            for line in content.split("\n") {
                if line.trim().starts_with("APP_KEY=") {
                    out_lines.push("APP_KEY={new_key}");
                } else {
                    out_lines.push(line);
                }
            }
            content = out_lines.join("\n");
        } else {
            if content != "" and not content.ends_with("\n") {
                content = "{content}\n";
            }
            content = "{content}APP_KEY={new_key}\n";
        }
        let w = write_file_atomic(env_path, content);
        if w.is_err() {
            let err = string(w.unwrap_err());
            println("[Magic Error] {err}");
            return 1;
        }
        println("[Magic] Application key set successfully in {env_path}");
        return 0;
    }
}

// framework/Console/Commands/WatchHelper.matrix
// File mtime snapshot and change detector for `spid3r magic serve --watch`.

fn collect_watch_files(dir: string, snap) {
    if not file_exists(dir) {
        return;
    }
    let ls = list_dir(dir);
    if ls.is_err() {
        return;
    }
    for raw in ls.unwrap() {
        let name = string(raw);
        if name == "." or name == ".." or name == ".git" {
            continue;
        }
        let full = "{dir}/{name}";
        if is_dir(full) {
            collect_watch_files(full, snap);
        } elif name.ends_with(".matrix") or name.ends_with(".bd.html") {
            let mt = file_mtime(full);
            if mt.is_ok() {
                snap[full] = int(mt.unwrap());
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Capture `path -> mtime` across watched application source directories.
public fn snapshot_watch_mtimes(root: string = "", extra_dirs: any = null) -> map {
    let snap = {
    };
    let dirs = extra_dirs ?? [
    "app",
    "bootstrap",
    "config",
    "framework",
    "routes",
    "resources/views"
    ];
    for d in dirs {
        collect_watch_files("{root}{d}", snap);
    }
    let srv = "{root}server.matrix";
    if file_exists(srv) {
        let mt = file_mtime(srv);
        if mt.is_ok() {
            snap[srv] = int(mt.unwrap());
        }
    }
    return snap;
}

/// Return modified, added, or removed paths between two snapshots.
public fn detect_watch_changes(prev_snap: map, next_snap: map) -> list {
    let changed = [];
    for (p, mt) in next_snap {
        if not prev_snap.has(p) or int(prev_snap[p]) != int(mt) {
            changed.push(p);
        }
    }
    for (p, _mt) in prev_snap {
        if not next_snap.has(p) {
            changed.push(p);
        }
    }
    return changed;
}

/// Poll project sources on a background task and request cooperative shutdown on change.
public fn start_watch_reload_thread(
    root: string = "",
    interval_ms: int = 750,
    state: any = null
) {
    let watch_root = root;
    let poll_ms = if interval_ms > 100 {
        interval_ms
    } else {
        750
    };
    spawn fn() {
        let prev = snapshot_watch_mtimes(watch_root);
        while not shutdown_requested() {
            sleep_ms(poll_ms);
            let cur = snapshot_watch_mtimes(watch_root);
            let diffs = detect_watch_changes(prev, cur);
            if diffs.len() > 0 {
                let first_path = string(diffs[0]);
                println("[Watch] Detected change in {first_path} — reloading server...");
                if state != null {
                    state["changed"] = true;
                }
                request_shutdown();
                return 0;
            }
            prev = cur;
        }
        return 0;
    };
}

// framework/Database/Connection.matrix
// SQL Escaping, Identifier Guardrail, Auth Error Diagnostics & SQLite Driver

import "framework/Foundation/Config";
import "framework/Database/Driver";

// Table/column identifiers are interpolated into SQL unquoted. They must only
// ever come from code constants — this guard makes a mistake loud instead of
// injectable. Allows letters, digits, underscore and dot (qualified names).
public fn sql_check_identifier(name: string) -> string {
    let s = string(name).trim();
    if s.len() == 0 {
        throw "SQL identifier must not be empty";
    }
    let allowed = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789_.";
    for tok in s.split(" ") {
        let t = tok.trim();
        if t.len() == 0 {
            throw "SQL identifier contains illegal whitespace: " + s;
        }
        if t.lower() == "as" {
            continue;
        }
        for ch in t.split("") {
            if not allowed.contains(ch) {
                throw "SQL identifier contains illegal character: " + s;
            }
        }
    }
    return s;
}

public fn sql_escape_string(value: string, driver: string) -> string {
    let s = string(value);
    if driver == "postgres" {
        return s.replace("'", "''");
    }
    let out = s.replace("\\", "\\\\");
    out = out.replace("'", "''");
    out = out.replace("\n", "\\n");
    out = out.replace("\r", "\\r");
    out = out.replace(chr(0), "\\0");
    out = out.replace(chr(26), "\\Z");
    return out;
}

public fn sql_quote_value(value: any, driver: string) -> string {
    if value == null {
        return "NULL";
    }
    let t = type_of(value);
    if t == "int" or t == "float" {
        return string(value);
    }
    if t == "bool" {
        if driver == "postgres" {
            return if value {
                "TRUE"
            } else {
                "FALSE"
            };
        }
        return if value {
            "1"
        } else {
            "0"
        };
    }
    if t == "bytes" {
        throw "bytes values are not supported by the SQL driver escaping layer";
    }
    return "'" + sql_escape_string(string(value), driver) + "'";
}

public fn sql_interpolate(sql: string, params: any, driver: string) -> string {
    if params == null or params.len() == 0 {
        return sql;
    }

    let out = "";
    let in_str = false;
    let pi = 0;
    let i = 0;
    let n = sql.len();
    while i < n {
        let ch = sql.slice(i, i + 1);
        if in_str {
            out = out + ch;
            if driver == "mysql" and ch == "\\" and (i + 1) < n {
                out = out + sql.slice(i + 1, i + 2);
                i = i + 2;
            } elif ch == "'" {
                if (i + 1) < n and sql.slice(i + 1, i + 2) == "'" {
                    out = out + "'";
                    i = i + 2;
                } else {
                    in_str = false;
                    i = i + 1;
                }
            } else {
                i = i + 1;
            }
        } elif ch == "'" {
            in_str = true;
            out = out + ch;
            i = i + 1;
        } elif ch == "?" {
            if pi < params.len() {
                out = out + sql_quote_value(params[pi], driver);
                pi = pi + 1;
            } else {
                throw "sql_interpolate: placeholder/parameter count mismatch (more ? than params)";
            }
            i = i + 1;
        } else {
            out = out + ch;
            i = i + 1;
        }
    }

    if pi != params.len() {
        let p_len = params.len();
        throw "sql_interpolate: placeholder/parameter count mismatch ({p_len} params, {pi} placeholders)";
    }
    return out;
}

public fn is_transient_db_error(msg: string) -> bool {
    let m = string(msg).lower();
    return m.contains("broken pipe")
    or m.contains("connection reset")
    or m.contains("connection closed")
    or m.contains("gone away")
    or m.contains("unexpected eof")
    or m.contains("early eof")
    or m.contains("server closed")
    or m.contains("lost connection")
    or m.contains("not connected")
    or m.contains("connection aborted");
}

public fn enhance_db_auth_error(driver: string, err_msg: string) -> string {
    let raw = string(err_msg);
    let lower = raw.lower();
    if driver == "postgres" and (
        lower.contains("scram") or lower.contains("sasl") or lower.contains("auth")
    ) {
        return "{raw} (Spid3r PostgreSQL client supports md5/trust auth only; configure pg_hba.conf / ALTER USER with password_encryption='md5')";
    }
    if (driver == "mysql" or driver == "mariadb") and (
        lower.contains("caching_sha2") or lower.contains("plugin") or lower.contains("auth")
    ) {
        return "{raw} (Spid3r MySQL client requires mysql_native_password; ALTER USER ... IDENTIFIED WITH mysql_native_password BY '...')";
    }
    return raw;
}

public class SqliteConnection implements Driver {
    public let handle;
    public let conn_info;
    public let in_transaction;

    fn new (path: string) {
        let _ = self;
        self.conn_info = path;
        self.in_transaction = false;
        if path != ":memory:" and (path.contains("/") or path.contains("\\")) {
            let parts = path.replace("\\", "/").split("/");
            parts.pop();
            let parent = parts.join("/");
            if parent != "" and not is_dir(parent) {
                let _ = dir_create(parent);
            }
        }
        self.handle = db_open(path);
        let fk_val = if Config.get("database.connections.sqlite.foreign_key_constraints", true) == true {
            "ON"
        } else {
            "OFF"
        };
        let jm = string(Config.get("database.connections.sqlite.journal_mode", "WAL")).trim().upper();
        let sync_mode = string(Config.get("database.connections.sqlite.synchronous", "NORMAL")).trim().upper();
        let timeout_ms = int(Config.get("database.connections.sqlite.busy_timeout_ms", 5_000));
        self.handle.exec(
            "PRAGMA foreign_keys = {fk_val}; PRAGMA journal_mode = {jm}; PRAGMA synchronous = {sync_mode}; PRAGMA busy_timeout = {timeout_ms};"
        );
    }

    public fn driver_name(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "sqlite";
    }

    public fn quote_identifier(self, ident: string) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return quote_sql_identifier(ident, "sqlite");
    }

    public fn placeholder(self, _index: int) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "?";
    }

    public fn limit_sql(self, limit: int, offset: int) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return format_limit_sql("sqlite", limit, offset);
    }

    public fn begin_sql(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "BEGIN IMMEDIATE";
    }

    public fn commit_sql(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "COMMIT";
    }

    public fn rollback_sql(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "ROLLBACK";
    }

    public fn set_in_transaction(self, active: bool) {
        self.in_transaction = active;
    }

    public fn ping(self) -> bool {
        try {
            let rows = self.handle.query("SELECT 1 AS ok", []);
            return rows.len() > 0;
        } catch (_e) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    public fn query(self, sql: string, params: any = null) -> list {
        if params == null {
            return self.handle.query(sql, []);
        }
        return self.handle.query(sql, params);
    }

    public fn run(self, sql: string, params: any = null) -> any {
        if params == null {
            return self.handle.run(sql, []);
        }
        return self.handle.run(sql, params);
    }

    public fn exec(self, sql: string) -> any {
        return self.handle.exec(sql);
    }

    public fn statement(self, sql: string, params: any = null) -> any {
        if params == null or params.len() == 0 {
            return self.handle.exec(sql);
        }
        return self.handle.run(sql, params);
    }

    public fn close(self) {
        try {
            self.handle.exec("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE);");
        } catch (_e) {
        }
        self.handle.close();
    }
}

// framework/Database/ConnectionPool.matrix
// Mutex-Guarded Connection Pool with Health Ping & Replacement

/// Mutex-guarded idle connection pool with `ping()` health checks and stale connection replacement.
public class ConnectionPool {
    private let factory;
    public let max_size;
    private let idle;
    private let pool_lock;
    private let stats_map;

    fn new (factory, max_size: int = 4) {
        self.factory = factory;
        self.max_size = if max_size > 0 {
            max_size
        } else {
            1
        };
        self.idle = [];
        self.pool_lock = mutex(true);
        self.stats_map = {
            "created": 0,
            "reconnected": 0,
            "acquired": 0,
            "released": 0
        };
    }

    public fn acquire(self) -> any {
        let conn = null;
        let _g = self.pool_lock.lock();
        if self.idle.len() > 0 {
            conn = self.idle.pop();
        }
        self.stats_map["acquired"] = int(self.stats_map["acquired"]) + 1;
        self.pool_lock.unlock();

        if conn != null {
            let healthy = false;
            try {
                healthy = conn.ping();
            } catch (_e) {
                healthy = false;
            }
            if healthy {
                return conn;
            }
            try {
                conn.close();
            } catch (_e2) {
            }
            let _g2 = self.pool_lock.lock();
            self.stats_map["reconnected"] = int(self.stats_map["reconnected"]) + 1;
            self.pool_lock.unlock();
        }

        let factory_fn = self.factory;
        let fresh = factory_fn();
        let _g3 = self.pool_lock.lock();
        self.stats_map["created"] = int(self.stats_map["created"]) + 1;
        self.pool_lock.unlock();
        return fresh;
    }

    public fn release(self, conn: any) {
        if conn == null {
            return;
        }
        let close_overflow = false;
        let _g = self.pool_lock.lock();
        self.stats_map["released"] = int(self.stats_map["released"]) + 1;
        if self.idle.len() < self.max_size {
            self.idle.push(conn);
        } else {
            close_overflow = true;
        }
        self.pool_lock.unlock();
        if close_overflow {
            try {
                conn.close();
            } catch (_e) {
            }
        }
    }

    public fn stats(self) -> map {
        let _g = self.pool_lock.lock();
        let out = {
            "max_size": self.max_size,
            "idle": self.idle.len(),
            "created": self.stats_map["created"],
            "reconnected": self.stats_map["reconnected"],
            "acquired": self.stats_map["acquired"],
            "released": self.stats_map["released"]
        };
        self.pool_lock.unlock();
        return out;
    }

    public fn close_all(self) {
        let _g = self.pool_lock.lock();
        let conns = self.idle;
        self.idle = [];
        self.pool_lock.unlock();
        for c in conns {
            try {
                c.close();
            } catch (_e) {
            }
        }
    }
}

// framework/Database/DatabaseManager.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Connection & Driver Manager — resolves driver/config from env,
// lazily opens connections, and hands out QueryBuilder instances.

import "Driver";
import "Connection";
import "ServerConnections";
import "ConnectionPool";
import "QueryBuilder";
import "framework/Foundation/Env";
import "framework/Foundation/Config";

fn trim_trailing_slash(p: string) -> string {
    let s = string(p).trim().replace("\\", "/");
    while s.len() > 1 and s.ends_with("/") {
        s = s.slice(0, s.len() - 1);
    }
    return s;
}

fn db_env_or(key: string, fallback: string) -> string {
    let v = env(key);
    if v.is_some() {
        let val = string(v.unwrap());
        if val != "" {
            return val;
        }
    }
    return fallback;
}

fn resolve_db_driver() -> string {
    let raw = "";
    let env_driver = env("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER");
    if env_driver.is_some() {
        raw = string(env_driver.unwrap()).trim().lower();
    }
    if raw == "postgres" or raw == "postgresql" or raw == "pg" {
        return "postgres";
    }
    if raw == "mysql" {
        return "mysql";
    }
    if raw == "mariadb" {
        return "mariadb";
    }
    return "sqlite";
}

/// Resolve the active SQLite database file path, separating runtime state under `app.paths.state_dir`.
public fn resolve_sqlite_path() -> string {
    let env_path = env("SPID3R_DB_PATH").unwrap_or("").trim();
    if env_path != "" {
        return env_path;
    }
    let env_state = env("SPID3R_STATE_DIR").unwrap_or("").trim();
    if env_state != "" {
        let base = trim_trailing_slash(env_state);
        return "{base}/database/database.sqlite";
    }
    let cfg_state = trim_trailing_slash(
        string(Config.get("app.paths.state_dir", "storage"))
    );
    if cfg_state == "" {
        cfg_state = "storage";
    }
    let preferred = "{cfg_state}/database/database.sqlite";
    if file_exists(preferred) {
        return preferred;
    }
    if file_exists("database/database.sqlite") {
        return "database/database.sqlite";
    }
    return preferred;
}

/// Central database connection manager resolving drivers from env and coordinating transactions/pools.
public class DatabaseManager {
    public let db_handle;
    public let db_path;
    public let driver;
    public let conn_config;
    public let tx_depth;
    private let tx_lock;
    private let pool;

    fn new () {
        // Re-load .env here (idempotent: OS env wins) so every manager
        // instance — including the global singleton at import time — sees
        // the file's values for driver/connection resolution.
        let _env_reload = load_env_file(".env");
        self.driver = resolve_db_driver();
        self.db_path = "";
        self.conn_config = {
        };
        if self.driver == "sqlite" {
            self.db_path = resolve_sqlite_path();
        } else {
            self.conn_config = self.resolve_server_config();
        }
        self.db_handle = null;
        self.tx_depth = 0;
        self.tx_lock = mutex(true);
        self.pool = null;
    }

    // Server connection settings from SPID3R_PG_* / SPID3R_MYSQL_* env vars.
    fn resolve_server_config(self) -> map {
        let prefix = "SPID3R_PG";
        let default_port = "5432";
        if self.driver == "mysql" or self.driver == "mariadb" {
            prefix = "SPID3R_MYSQL";
            default_port = "3306";
        }
        let host = db_env_or("{prefix}_HOST", "127.0.0.1");
        let port = db_env_or("{prefix}_PORT", default_port);
        return {
            "host": host,
            "port": port,
            "addr": "{host}:{port}",
            "user": db_env_or("{prefix}_USER", "spid3r"),
            "password": db_env_or("{prefix}_PASSWORD", ""),
            "database": db_env_or("{prefix}_DATABASE", "spid3r")
        };
    }

    public fn driver_name(self) -> string {
        return self.driver;
    }

    public fn create_connection(self) -> any {
        if self.driver == "postgres" {
            return new PgConnection(
                self.conn_config["addr"],
                self.conn_config["user"],
                self.conn_config["password"],
                self.conn_config["database"]
            );
        }
        if self.driver == "mysql" or self.driver == "mariadb" {
            return new MySqlConnection(
                self.conn_config["addr"],
                self.conn_config["user"],
                self.conn_config["password"],
                self.conn_config["database"]
            );
        }
        return new SqliteConnection(self.db_path);
    }

    private fn ensure_pool(self) {
        if self.pool == null {
            let mgr = self;
            let default_sz = if self.driver == "sqlite" {
                1
            } else {
                4
            };
            let max_sz = int(
                Config.get("database.connections." + self.driver + ".pool_size", default_sz)
            );
            self.pool = new ConnectionPool(fn() {
                return mgr.create_connection();
            }, max_sz);
        }
        return self.pool;
    }

    public fn pool_stats(self) -> map {
        return self.ensure_pool().stats();
    }

    public fn with_connection(self, callback: any) -> any {
        let p = self.ensure_pool();
        let conn = p.acquire();
        try {
            return callback(conn);
        } finally {
            p.release(conn);
        }
    }

    public fn ping(self) -> bool {
        try {
            return self.connection().ping();
        } catch (_e) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    // Lazily open the connection for the active driver. Every driver wrapper
    // exposes the same contract, so QueryBuilder/Auth code is driver-agnostic.
    public fn connection(self) -> any {
        if self.db_handle == null {
            self.db_handle = self.create_connection();
        }
        return self.db_handle;
    }

    public fn select(self, sql: string, params: any = null) -> list {
        if params == null {
            params = [];
        }
        let conn = self.connection();
        return conn.query(sql, params);
    }

    public fn statement(self, sql: string, params: any = null) -> any {
        if params == null {
            params = [];
        }
        let conn = self.connection();
        return conn.statement(sql, params);
    }

    public fn table(self, table_name: string) -> QueryBuilder {
        return new QueryBuilder(table_name, self);
    }

    // Run callback() inside a transaction. Nested calls execute inline
    // (they run in the scope of the outer transaction — no savepoints).
    // On error the transaction is rolled back and the original exception
    // is re-thrown; a failed ROLLBACK never masks that original error.
    // Guarded by tx_lock when starting a top-level transaction so concurrent
    // tasks never interleave BEGIN/COMMIT/ROLLBACK on the shared connection.
    public fn transaction(self, callback: any) -> any {
        if self.tx_depth > 0 {
            return callback();
        }
        let _g = self.tx_lock.lock();
        self.tx_depth = 1;
        let conn = null;
        try {
            conn = self.connection();
            conn.set_in_transaction(true);
            conn.exec(conn.begin_sql());
        } catch (begin_err) {
            if conn != null {
                conn.set_in_transaction(false);
            }
            self.tx_depth = 0;
            self.tx_lock.unlock();
            throw begin_err;
        }
        try {
            let result = callback();
            conn.exec(conn.commit_sql());
            conn.set_in_transaction(false);
            self.tx_depth = 0;
            self.tx_lock.unlock();
            return result;
        } catch (e) {
            try {
                conn.exec(conn.rollback_sql());
            } catch (_rb_err) {
                // Rollback failure must not mask the original error.
            }
            conn.set_in_transaction(false);
            self.tx_depth = 0;
            self.tx_lock.unlock();
            throw e;
        }
    }

    public fn checkpoint(self) {
        if self.db_handle != null and self.driver == "sqlite" {
            self.db_handle.exec("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE);");
        }
    }

    public fn cleanup_artifacts(self) {
        if self.driver != "sqlite" {
            return;
        }
        let candidates = [
        self.db_path,
        "storage/database/database.sqlite",
        "database/database.sqlite"
        ];
        for p in candidates {
            let shm_path = p + "-shm";
            let wal_path = p + "-wal";
            if file_exists(shm_path) {
                file_delete(shm_path);
            }
            if file_exists(wal_path) {
                file_delete(wal_path);
            }
        }
    }

    public fn close(self) {
        if self.pool != null {
            self.pool.close_all();
            self.pool = null;
        }
        if self.db_handle != null {
            if self.driver == "sqlite" {
                try {
                    self.db_handle.exec("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE);");
                } catch (_e) {
                }
                self.db_handle.handle.close();
            } else {
                self.db_handle.close();
            }
            self.db_handle = null;
        }
        self.cleanup_artifacts();
    }
}

// framework/Database/DB.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Database Facade â€” global DB singleton.

// Global DB Facade Singleton

// framework/Database/Driver.matrix
// Database Driver Interface & Portable SQL Dialect Helpers

/// Uniform database driver contract implemented by SQLite, PostgreSQL, and MySQL connections.
public interface Driver {
    fn driver_name(self) -> string;
    fn quote_identifier(self, ident: string) -> string;
    fn placeholder(self, index: int) -> string;
    fn limit_sql(self, limit: int, offset: int) -> string;
    fn begin_sql(self) -> string;
    fn commit_sql(self) -> string;
    fn rollback_sql(self) -> string;
    fn set_in_transaction(self, active: bool);
    fn ping(self) -> bool;
    fn query(self, sql: string, params);
    fn run(self, sql: string, params);
    fn exec(self, sql: string);
    fn statement(self, sql: string, params);
    fn close(self);
}

public fn quote_sql_identifier(ident: string, driver_name: string) -> string {
    let s = string(ident).trim();
    if s == "" {
        throw "quote_sql_identifier: identifier must not be empty";
    }
    let q = if driver_name == "mysql" or driver_name == "mariadb" {
        "`"
    } else {
        "\""
    };
    let out = [];
    for tok in s.split(" ") {
        let t = tok.trim();
        if t == "" {
            continue;
        }
        if t.lower() == "as" {
            out.push("AS");
            continue;
        }
        let segs = t.split(".");
        let qsegs = [];
        for seg in segs {
            let clean = seg.replace(q, "");
            if clean == "*" {
                qsegs.push("*");
            } else {
                qsegs.push("{q}{clean}{q}");
            }
        }
        out.push(qsegs.join("."));
    }
    return out.join(" ");
}

public fn format_limit_sql(driver_name: string, limit_val: int, offset_val: int) -> string {
    if limit_val >= 0 {
        let clause = " LIMIT {limit_val}";
        if offset_val > 0 {
            clause = "{clause} OFFSET {offset_val}";
        }
        return clause;
    }
    if offset_val > 0 {
        if driver_name == "sqlite" {
            return " LIMIT -1 OFFSET {offset_val}";
        }
        return " OFFSET {offset_val}";
    }
    return "";
}

// framework/Database/Migration.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Transactional Database Migration Runner

import "DB";
import {
    sql_check_identifier
}
from "Connection";

/// Base class for schema migrations defining reversible `up()` and `down()` steps.
public class Migration {
    public let name;
    public let checksum;

    fn new (name: string = "") {
        self.name = name;
        self.checksum = "";
    }

    public fn up(self) {
        let _ = self;
    }
    public fn down(self) {
        let _ = self;
    }
}

/// Wrapper adapting Laravel-style module function pairs (`up()` / `down()`) as migrations.
public class FunctionMigration extends Migration {
    private let up_fn;
    private let down_fn;

    fn new (name: string, up_fn: any, down_fn: any, checksum: string = "") {
        self.name = name;
        self.checksum = checksum;
        self.up_fn = up_fn;
        self.down_fn = down_fn;
    }

    public fn up(self) {
        let h = self.up_fn;
        if h != null {
            h();
        }
    }

    public fn down(self) {
        let h = self.down_fn;
        if h != null {
            h();
        }
    }
}

/// Tracks and executes database migration batches atomically in the `migrations` table.
public class MigrationRunner {
    fn new () {
        self.ensure_migrations_table();
    }

    public fn ensure_migrations_table(self) {
        let _ = self;
        let dialect = DB.driver_name();
        if dialect == "postgres" {
            DB.statement(
                "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS migrations (
                id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                migration TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                batch INTEGER NOT NULL,
                applied_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                checksum TEXT DEFAULT ''
            );"
            );
            try {
                DB.statement("ALTER TABLE migrations ADD COLUMN checksum TEXT DEFAULT '';");
            } catch (_e) {
            }
        } elif dialect == "mysql" or dialect == "mariadb" {
            DB.statement(
                "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS migrations (
                id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
                migration VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                batch INT NOT NULL,
                applied_at VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
                checksum VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT ''
            );"
            );
            try {
                DB.statement("ALTER TABLE migrations ADD COLUMN checksum VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT '';");
            } catch (_e) {
            }
        } else {
            DB.statement(
                "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS migrations (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                migration TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                batch INTEGER NOT NULL,
                applied_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                checksum TEXT DEFAULT ''
            );"
            );
            try {
                DB.statement("ALTER TABLE migrations ADD COLUMN checksum TEXT DEFAULT '';");
            } catch (_e) {
            }
        }
    }

    private fn supports_transactional_ddl(self) -> bool {
        let _ = self;
        let d = DB.driver_name();
        return d == "sqlite" or d == "postgres";
    }

    private fn run_migration_unit(self, work_fn: any) {
        if self.supports_transactional_ddl() {
            DB.transaction(work_fn);
        } else {
            work_fn();
        }
    }

    public fn get_applied(self) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        let rows = DB.select("SELECT migration, batch FROM migrations ORDER BY id ASC");
        let applied_map = {
        };
        for r in rows {
            applied_map[string(r["migration"])] = int(r["batch"]);
        }
        return applied_map;
    }

    public fn get_checksums(self) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        let rows = DB.select("SELECT migration, checksum FROM migrations ORDER BY id ASC");
        let csums = {
        };
        for r in rows {
            let cs = if r.has("checksum") and r["checksum"] != null {
                string(r["checksum"]).trim()
            } else {
                ""
            };
            csums[string(r["migration"])] = cs;
        }
        return csums;
    }

    public fn get_next_batch(self) -> int {
        let _ = self;
        let rows = DB.select("SELECT max(batch) as max_b FROM migrations");
        if rows.len() > 0 and rows[0]["max_b"] != null {
            let val = rows[0]["max_b"];
            let t = type_of(val);
            if t == "int" {
                return int(val) + 1;
            }
            if t == "string" and val != "" {
                return int(val) + 1;
            }
        }
        return 1;
    }

    public fn migrate(self, migrations_list: list, pretend: bool = false, step: bool = false) -> int {
        let applied = self.get_applied();
        let stored_checksums = self.get_checksums();
        let next_batch = self.get_next_batch();
        let ran_count = 0;

        for m in migrations_list {
            let m_name = m.name;
            let cur_sum = string(m.checksum ?? "").trim();
            if applied.has(m_name) {
                let prev_sum = if stored_checksums.has(m_name) {
                    stored_checksums[m_name]
                } else {
                    ""
                };
                if cur_sum != "" and prev_sum != "" and cur_sum != prev_sum {
                    println(
                        "[Migrate WARN] Checksum mismatch for '{m_name}' (edited after applying)."
                    );
                }
                continue;
            }
            if pretend {
                println("[Pretend] Would apply: {m_name} (batch {next_batch})");
                ran_count = ran_count + 1;
                if step {
                    next_batch = next_batch + 1;
                }
                continue;
            }
            println("[Migrate] Running: {m_name}");
            let mig_ref = m;
            let batch_num = next_batch;
            self.run_migration_unit(fn() {
                mig_ref.up();
                let now = string(unix_time());
                DB.statement(
                    "INSERT INTO migrations (migration, batch, applied_at, checksum) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                    [m_name,
                    batch_num,
                    now,
                    cur_sum]
                );
            });
            println("[Migrate] Done:    {m_name}");
            ran_count = ran_count + 1;
            if step {
                next_batch = next_batch + 1;
            }
        }

        if ran_count == 0 {
            println("[Migrate] Nothing to migrate. Database is up to date.");
        }
        return ran_count;
    }

    public fn rollback(self, migrations_list: list) -> int {
        let rows = DB.select("SELECT max(batch) as max_b FROM migrations");
        if rows.len() == 0 or rows[0]["max_b"] == null {
            println("[Rollback] Nothing to rollback.");
            return 0;
        }
        let val = rows[0]["max_b"];
        if type_of(val) == "bytes" or val == null {
            println("[Rollback] Nothing to rollback.");
            return 0;
        }
        let last_batch = int(val);
        let batch_rows = DB.select(
            "SELECT migration FROM migrations WHERE batch = ? ORDER BY id DESC",
            [last_batch]
        );

        let rolled_count = 0;
        for r in batch_rows {
            let target_name = string(r["migration"]);
            for m in migrations_list {
                if m.name == target_name {
                    println("[Rollback] Rolling back: {m.name}");
                    let m_name = m.name;
                    let mig_ref = m;
                    self.run_migration_unit(fn() {
                        mig_ref.down();
                        DB.statement("DELETE FROM migrations WHERE migration = ?", [m_name]);
                    });
                    println("[Rollback] Reverted:     {m.name}");
                    rolled_count = rolled_count + 1;
                }
            }
        }
        return rolled_count;
    }

    public fn reset(self, migrations_list: list) -> int {
        let total = 0;
        while true {
            let n = self.rollback(migrations_list);
            if n <= 0 {
                break;
            }
            total = total + n;
        }
        return total;
    }

    public fn wipe(self) -> int {
        let _ = self;
        let dialect = DB.driver_name();
        let dropped = 0;
        if dialect == "sqlite" {
            let rows = DB.select(
                "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%' ORDER BY name DESC"
            );
            DB.statement("PRAGMA foreign_keys = OFF;");
            for r in rows {
                let tbl = sql_check_identifier(string(r["name"]));
                DB.statement("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS \"{tbl}\";");
                dropped = dropped + 1;
            }
            DB.statement("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;");
            return dropped;
        }
        if dialect == "postgres" {
            let rows = DB.select(
                "SELECT tablename AS name FROM pg_tables WHERE schemaname = 'public' ORDER BY tablename DESC"
            );
            for r in rows {
                let tbl = sql_check_identifier(string(r["name"]));
                DB.statement("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS \"{tbl}\" CASCADE;");
                dropped = dropped + 1;
            }
            return dropped;
        }
        let rows = DB.select(
            "SELECT table_name AS name FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema = DATABASE() ORDER BY table_name DESC"
        );
        DB.statement("SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 0;");
        for r in rows {
            let tbl = sql_check_identifier(string(r["name"]));
            DB.statement("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `{tbl}`;");
            dropped = dropped + 1;
        }
        DB.statement("SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 1;");
        return dropped;
    }

    public fn fresh(self, migrations_list: list, pretend: bool = false, step: bool = false) -> int {
        if pretend {
            for m in migrations_list {
                let m_name = m.name;
                println("[Pretend] Would wipe schema and apply: {m_name}");
            }
            return migrations_list.len();
        }
        let dropped = self.wipe();
        println("[Migrate] Dropped {dropped} table(s); re-running migrations...");
        self.ensure_migrations_table();
        return self.migrate(migrations_list, false, step);
    }

    public fn status(self, migrations_list: list) {
        let applied = self.get_applied();
        let checksums = self.get_checksums();
        println("\n+------------------------------------------------+---------------+");
        println("| Migration                                      | Status        |");
        println("+------------------------------------------------+---------------+");

        for m in migrations_list {
            let status = "No";
            if applied.has(m.name) {
                let batch_num = applied[m.name];
                let cur_sum = string(m.checksum ?? "").trim();
                let prev_sum = if checksums.has(m.name) {
                    checksums[m.name]
                } else {
                    ""
                };
                if cur_sum != "" and prev_sum != "" and cur_sum != prev_sum {
                    status = "Yes ({batch_num}) [MOD]";
                } else {
                    status = "Yes ({batch_num})";
                }
            }
            let name_padded = m.name;
            while name_padded.len() < 46 {
                name_padded = name_padded + " ";
            }
            let st_padded = status;
            while st_padded.len() < 13 {
                st_padded = st_padded + " ";
            }
            println("| {name_padded} | {st_padded} |");
        }
        println("+------------------------------------------------+---------------+\n");
    }
}
