// framework/Database/Model.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Active Record Model Base Class & Eager Loading Facade

import "DB";
import "ModelHelpers";
import "framework/Events/Event";

/// Active Record base class providing attributes, casts, hidden columns, dirty tracking, and persistence.
public class Model {
    public let table;
    public let primary_key;
    public let fillable;
    public let hidden;
    public let casts;
    public let attributes;
    public let original;
    public let exists;
    public let uses_timestamps;

    fn new (table_name: string = "models", fillable_list = [], attrs = {
    }) {
        self.table = table_name;
        self.primary_key = "id";
        self.fillable = fillable_list;
        if self.fillable == null {
            self.fillable = [];
        }
        self.hidden = [];
        self.casts = {
        };
        self.attributes = {
        };
        self.original = {
        };
        self.exists = false;
        self.uses_timestamps = true;

        if attrs != null and type_of(attrs) == "map" {
            for (k, v) in attrs {
                self.attributes[k] = v;
            }
            if self.attributes.has(self.primary_key) and self.attributes[self.primary_key] != null {
                self.exists = true;
                self.sync_original();
            }
        }
    }

    public fn sync_original(self) -> Model {
        self.original = {
        };
        for (k, v) in self.attributes {
            self.original[k] = v;
        }
        return self;
    }

    public fn is_dirty(self, key: string = "") -> bool {
        if key != "" {
            let cur = null;
            if self.attributes.has(key) {
                cur = self.attributes[key];
            }
            let orig = null;
            if self.original.has(key) {
                orig = self.original[key];
            }
            return string(cur) != string(orig);
        }
        for (k, v) in self.attributes {
            if not self.original.has(k) {
                return true;
            }
            if string(v) != string(self.original[k]) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    public fn get(self, key: string, default_val: any = null) -> any {
        if self.attributes.has(key) {
            let raw = self.attributes[key];
            if self.casts != null and self.casts.has(key) {
                return cast_attribute_value(string(self.casts[key]), raw);
            }
            return raw;
        }
        return default_val;
    }

    public fn set(self, key: string, value: any) -> Model {
        self.attributes[key] = value;
        return self;
    }

    public fn query(self) -> QueryBuilder {
        return DB.table(self.table);
    }

    private fn fire(self, phase: string) -> list {
        let tbl = self.table;
        return Event.dispatch("model.{tbl}.{phase}", self);
    }

    private fn cancelled(self, results: list) -> bool {
        let _ = self;
        for r in results {
            if r == false {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private fn prepare_db_value(self, key: string, val) {
        if self.casts != null and self.casts.has(key) {
            return serialize_attribute_for_db(string(self.casts[key]), val);
        }
        return val;
    }

    private fn collect_dirty_updates(self, data_to_update, has_orig: bool) {
        for (k, v) in self.attributes {
            if k != self.primary_key {
                if self.fillable.len() == 0 or self.fillable.contains(k) or k == "created_at" or k == "updated_at" {
                    if not has_orig or self.is_dirty(k) {
                        data_to_update[k] = self.prepare_db_value(k, v);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public fn save(self) -> any {
        let now = string(unix_time());

        if self.exists {
            let data_to_update = {
            };
            let has_orig = self.original.len() > 0;
            self.collect_dirty_updates(data_to_update, has_orig);
            if self.uses_timestamps {
                data_to_update["updated_at"] = now;
                self.attributes["updated_at"] = now;
            }
            if self.cancelled(self.fire("updating")) {
                return false;
            }
            // Re-collect any attributes mutated by the `updating` listener.
            self.collect_dirty_updates(data_to_update, has_orig);

            if data_to_update.len() > 0 {
                let pk_val = self.attributes[self.primary_key];
                self.query().where(self.primary_key, "=", pk_val).update(data_to_update);
            }
            self.sync_original();
            self.fire("updated");
            return self;
        }

        if self.uses_timestamps {
            if not self.attributes.has("created_at") {
                self.attributes["created_at"] = now;
            }
            if not self.attributes.has("updated_at") {
                self.attributes["updated_at"] = now;
            }
        }
        if self.cancelled(self.fire("creating")) {
            return false;
        }

        let data_to_insert = {
        };
        for (k, v) in self.attributes {
            if self.fillable.len() == 0 or self.fillable.contains(k) or k == "created_at" or k == "updated_at" {
                data_to_insert[k] = self.prepare_db_value(k, v);
            }
        }

        let new_id = self.query().insert(data_to_insert);
        if new_id == null or int(new_id) == 0 {
            let tbl = self.table;
            throw "Model insert failed for table {tbl}";
        }
        self.attributes[self.primary_key] = new_id;
        self.exists = true;
        self.sync_original();
        self.fire("created");
        return self;
    }

    public fn update(self, new_attributes: map) -> any {
        for (k, v) in new_attributes {
            if self.fillable.len() == 0 or self.fillable.contains(k) {
                self.attributes[k] = v;
            }
        }
        return self.save();
    }

    public fn delete(self) -> bool {
        if self.exists {
            if self.cancelled(self.fire("deleting")) {
                return false;
            }
            let pk_val = self.attributes[self.primary_key];
            self.query().where(self.primary_key, "=", pk_val).delete();
            self.exists = false;
            self.fire("deleted");
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    public fn to_map(self) -> map {
        let hidden_set = {
        };
        if self.hidden != null {
            for h in self.hidden {
                hidden_set[string(h)] = true;
            }
        }
        let out = {
        };
        for (k, _v) in self.attributes {
            if not hidden_set.has(k) {
                out[k] = self.get(k);
            }
        }
        return out;
    }

    public fn hasMany(self, table: string, foreign_key: string) -> QueryBuilder {
        return DB.table(table).where(foreign_key, "=", self.attributes[self.primary_key]);
    }

    public fn belongsTo(self, table: string, foreign_key: string, owner_key: string = "id") -> any {
        return DB.table(table).where(owner_key, "=", self.attributes[foreign_key]).first();
    }

    public fn reload(self) -> Model {
        if not self.exists {
            return self;
        }
        let pk_val = self.attributes[self.primary_key];
        let rows = self.query().where(self.primary_key, "=", pk_val).limit(1).get();
        if rows.len() > 0 {
            let fresh = {
            };
            let row = rows[0];
            for (k, v) in row {
                fresh[k] = v;
            }
            self.attributes = fresh;
            self.exists = true;
            self.sync_original();
        } else {
            self.exists = false;
        }
        return self;
    }

    public fn touch(self) -> Model {
        if not self.exists {
            return self;
        }
        let now = string(unix_time());
        self.attributes["updated_at"] = now;
        let pk_val = self.attributes[self.primary_key];
        self.query().where(self.primary_key, "=", pk_val).update({
            "updated_at": now
        });
        self.sync_original();
        return self;
    }
}

public fn load_has_many(
    models: list,
    table: string,
    foreign_key: string,
    as_key: string,
    owner_key: string = "id"
) -> list {
    return eager_load_has_many(models, table, foreign_key, as_key, owner_key);
}

public fn load_belongs_to(
    models: list,
    table: string,
    fk_column: string,
    as_key: string,
    owner_key: string = "id"
) -> list {
    return eager_load_belongs_to(models, table, fk_column, as_key, owner_key);
}

// framework/Database/ModelHelpers.matrix
// Attribute cast/serialization and eager-loading helpers extracted from Model.matrix.

import "DB";

public fn cast_attribute_value(cast_type: string, raw_val: any) -> any {
    if raw_val == null {
        return null;
    }
    let ct = cast_type.lower();
    if ct == "int" or ct == "integer" {
        try {
            return int(raw_val);
        } catch (_e) {
            return 0;
        }
    }
    if ct == "float" or ct == "numeric" or ct == "double" {
        try {
            return float(raw_val);
        } catch (_e) {
            return 0.0;
        }
    }
    if ct == "bool" or ct == "boolean" {
        if type_of(raw_val) == "bool" {
            return raw_val;
        }
        let s = string(raw_val).trim().lower();
        return s == "1" or s == "true" or s == "yes";
    }
    if ct == "string" {
        return string(raw_val);
    }
    if ct == "json" or ct == "array" or ct == "map" {
        let t = type_of(raw_val);
        if t == "map" or t == "list" {
            return raw_val;
        }
        let parsed = json_parse(string(raw_val));
        if type_of(parsed) == "result" {
            if parsed.is_ok() {
                return parsed.unwrap();
            }
            return raw_val;
        }
        return parsed;
    }
    return raw_val;
}

public fn serialize_attribute_for_db(cast_type: string, val: any) -> any {
    if val == null {
        return null;
    }
    let ct = cast_type.lower();
    if ct == "json" or ct == "array" or ct == "map" {
        let t = type_of(val);
        if t == "map" or t == "list" {
            return json_write(val);
        }
    }
    if ct == "bool" or ct == "boolean" {
        if val == true or string(val) == "true" or string(val) == "1" {
            return 1;
        }
        return 0;
    }
    return val;
}

public fn _elem_get(m: any, key: string) -> any {
    if type_of(m) == "map" {
        if m.has(key) {
            return m[key];
        }
        return null;
    }
    return m.get(key);
}

public fn _elem_set(m: any, key: string, value: any) {
    if type_of(m) == "map" {
        m[key] = value;
    } else {
        m.set(key, value);
    }
}

fn collect_unique_keys(models, col: string) {
    let key_list = [];
    let seen = {
    };
    let i = 0;
    while i < len(models) {
        let k = _elem_get(models[i], col);
        if k != null {
            let ks = string(k);
            if not seen.has(ks) {
                seen[ks] = true;
                key_list.push(k);
            }
        }
        i += 1;
    }
    return key_list;
}

public fn eager_load_has_many(
    models: list,
    table: string,
    foreign_key: string,
    as_key: string,
    owner_key: string = "id"
) -> list {
    let key_list = collect_unique_keys(models, owner_key);
    let grouped = {
    };
    if key_list.len() > 0 {
        let rows = DB.table(table).whereIn(foreign_key, key_list).get();
        let j = 0;
        while j < len(rows) {
            let fk = string(rows[j][foreign_key]);
            if not grouped.has(fk) {
                grouped[fk] = [];
            }
            grouped[fk].push(rows[j]);
            j += 1;
        }
    }

    let i = 0;
    while i < len(models) {
        let k = _elem_get(models[i], owner_key);
        if k != null and grouped.has(string(k)) {
            _elem_set(models[i], as_key, grouped[string(k)]);
        } else {
            _elem_set(models[i], as_key, []);
        }
        i += 1;
    }
    return models;
}

public fn eager_load_belongs_to(
    models: list,
    table: string,
    fk_column: string,
    as_key: string,
    owner_key: string = "id"
) -> list {
    let key_list = collect_unique_keys(models, fk_column);
    let parents = {
    };
    if key_list.len() > 0 {
        let rows = DB.table(table).whereIn(owner_key, key_list).get();
        let j = 0;
        while j < len(rows) {
            parents[string(rows[j][owner_key])] = rows[j];
            j += 1;
        }
    }

    let i = 0;
    while i < len(models) {
        let k = _elem_get(models[i], fk_column);
        if k != null and parents.has(string(k)) {
            _elem_set(models[i], as_key, parents[string(k)]);
        } else {
            _elem_set(models[i], as_key, null);
        }
        i += 1;
    }
    return models;
}

// framework/Database/ModelRepository.matrix
// Zero-Boilerplate Declarative ModelRepository & `define_model()` Factory

import "DB";
import "Model";

/// Zero-boilerplate table repository configured via options map (`fillable`, `hidden`, `casts`).
public class ModelRepository {
    public let table;
    public let primary_key;
    public let fillable;
    public let hidden;
    public let casts;
    public let uses_timestamps;

    fn new (table_name: string, opts = {
    }) {
        self.table = table_name;
        self.primary_key = "id";
        self.fillable = [];
        self.hidden = [];
        self.casts = {
        };
        self.uses_timestamps = true;

        if opts != null and type_of(opts) == "map" {
            if opts.has("primary_key") {
                self.primary_key = string(opts["primary_key"]);
            }
            if opts.has("fillable") and type_of(opts["fillable"]) == "list" {
                self.fillable = opts["fillable"];
            }
            if opts.has("hidden") and type_of(opts["hidden"]) == "list" {
                self.hidden = opts["hidden"];
            }
            if opts.has("casts") and type_of(opts["casts"]) == "map" {
                self.casts = opts["casts"];
            }
            if opts.has("timestamps") {
                self.uses_timestamps = opts["timestamps"] == true;
            }
        }
    }

    public fn new_instance(self, attrs: any = {
    }) -> Model {
        let m = new Model(self.table, self.fillable, attrs);
        m.primary_key = self.primary_key;
        m.hidden = self.hidden;
        m.casts = self.casts;
        m.uses_timestamps = self.uses_timestamps;
        return m;
    }

    public fn hydrate(self, row: any) -> any {
        if row == null {
            return null;
        }
        let m = self.new_instance(row);
        m.exists = true;
        m.sync_original();
        return m;
    }

    public fn hydrate_many(self, rows: any) -> list {
        let out = [];
        if rows == null {
            return out;
        }
        for r in rows {
            out.push(self.hydrate(r));
        }
        return out;
    }

    public fn format_row(self, row: any) -> any {
        if row == null {
            return null;
        }
        return self.hydrate(row).to_map();
    }

    public fn format_rows(self, rows: any) -> list {
        let out = [];
        if rows == null {
            return out;
        }
        for r in rows {
            out.push(self.format_row(r));
        }
        return out;
    }

    public fn query(self) -> QueryBuilder {
        return DB.table(self.table);
    }

    public fn all(self) -> list {
        return self.hydrate_many(self.query().get());
    }

    public fn find(self, id: any) -> any {
        return self.hydrate(self.query().where(self.primary_key, "=", id).first());
    }

    public fn find_or_fail(self, id: any, message: string = "") -> Model {
        return self.hydrate(self.query().find_or_fail(id, message));
    }

    public fn first(self) -> any {
        return self.hydrate(self.query().first());
    }

    public fn first_or_fail(self, message: string = "") -> Model {
        return self.hydrate(self.query().first_or_fail(message));
    }

    public fn create(self, attrs: map) -> any {
        return self.new_instance(attrs).save();
    }

    public fn update_or_create(self, search_attrs: map, update_attrs: map = {
    }) -> any {
        let q = self.query();
        for (k, v) in search_attrs {
            q.where(k, "=", v);
        }
        let existing_row = q.first();
        if existing_row != null {
            return self.hydrate(existing_row).update(update_attrs);
        }
        let merged = {
        };
        for (k, v) in search_attrs {
            merged[k] = v;
        }
        for (k, v) in update_attrs {
            merged[k] = v;
        }
        return self.create(merged);
    }

    public fn destroy(self, id: any) -> bool {
        let m = self.find(id);
        if m == null {
            return false;
        }
        return m.delete();
    }

    public fn where(self, column: string, op: any, val: any = null) -> QueryBuilder {
        return self.query().where(column, op, val);
    }

    public fn whereIn(self, column: string, values_list: any) -> QueryBuilder {
        return self.query().whereIn(column, values_list);
    }

    public fn orderBy(self, column: string, dir: string = "ASC") -> QueryBuilder {
        return self.query().orderBy(column, dir);
    }

    public fn latest(self, column: string = "id") -> QueryBuilder {
        return self.query().latest(column);
    }

    public fn oldest(self, column: string = "id") -> QueryBuilder {
        return self.query().oldest(column);
    }

    public fn paginate(self, page: int = 1, per_page: int = 15) -> map {
        let res = self.query().paginate(page, per_page);
        res["data"] = self.format_rows(res["data"]);
        return res;
    }

    public fn count(self) -> int {
        return self.query().count();
    }

    public fn exists(self) -> bool {
        return self.query().exists();
    }
}

public fn define_model(table_name: string, opts: map = {
}) -> ModelRepository {
    return new ModelRepository(table_name, opts);
}

// framework/Database/QueryBuilder.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Fluent SQL Query Builder — table-agnostic, injection-hardened.

import "Connection";
import "QueryCompiler";
import "framework/Http/HttpException";

/// Fluent, parameterized SQL query builder across SQLite, PostgreSQL, MySQL, and MariaDB.
public class QueryBuilder {
    public let table_name;
    public let db_manager;
    public let select_cols;
    public let wheres;
    public let joins;
    public let order_col;
    public let order_dir;
    public let limit_count;
    public let offset_count;

    fn new (table: string, manager = null) {
        self.table_name = sql_check_identifier(table);
        self.db_manager = manager;
        self.select_cols = ["*"];
        self.wheres = [];
        self.joins = [];
        self.order_col = "";
        self.order_dir = "ASC";
        self.limit_count = -1;
        self.offset_count = 0;
    }

    public fn select(self, cols: any) -> QueryBuilder {
        if type_of(cols) == "list" {
            let safe = [];
            for c in cols {
                safe.push(sql_check_identifier(c));
            }
            self.select_cols = safe;
        } else {
            self.select_cols = [sql_check_identifier(cols)];
        }
        return self;
    }

    public fn where(self, column: string, op: any, val: any = null) -> QueryBuilder {
        sql_check_identifier(column);
        if val == null {
            val = op;
            op = "=";
        }
        self.wheres.push({
            "col": column,
            "op": check_sql_op(op),
            "val": val,
            "bool": "AND"
        });
        return self;
    }

    public fn orWhere(self, column: string, op: any, val: any = null) -> QueryBuilder {
        sql_check_identifier(column);
        if val == null {
            val = op;
            op = "=";
        }
        self.wheres.push({
            "col": column,
            "op": check_sql_op(op),
            "val": val,
            "bool": "OR"
        });
        return self;
    }

    public fn whereIn(self, column: string, values_list: any) -> QueryBuilder {
        sql_check_identifier(column);
        if values_list == null {
            values_list = [];
        }
        self.wheres.push({
            "col": column, "op": "IN", "val": values_list, "bool": "AND"
        });
        return self;
    }

    public fn whereNotIn(self, column: string, values_list: any) -> QueryBuilder {
        sql_check_identifier(column);
        if values_list == null {
            values_list = [];
        }
        self.wheres.push({
            "col": column,
            "op": "NOT IN",
            "val": values_list,
            "bool": "AND"
        });
        return self;
    }

    public fn whereNull(self, column: string) -> QueryBuilder {
        sql_check_identifier(column);
        self.wheres.push({
            "col": column, "op": "IS NULL", "val": null, "bool": "AND"
        });
        return self;
    }

    public fn whereNotNull(self, column: string) -> QueryBuilder {
        sql_check_identifier(column);
        self.wheres.push({
            "col": column, "op": "IS NOT NULL", "val": null, "bool": "AND"
        });
        return self;
    }

    public fn whereGroup(self, bool_op: string, callback: any) -> QueryBuilder {
        let sub_qb = new QueryBuilder(self.table_name, self.db_manager);
        callback(sub_qb);
        let b = string(bool_op).upper();
        if b != "OR" {
            b = "AND";
        }
        self.wheres.push({
            "col": "",
            "op": "GROUP",
            "val": sub_qb.wheres,
            "bool": b
        });
        return self;
    }

    public fn orderBy(self, column: string, dir: string = "ASC") -> QueryBuilder {
        sql_check_identifier(column);
        let d = string(dir).upper();
        if d != "ASC" and d != "DESC" {
            d = "ASC";
        }
        self.order_col = column;
        self.order_dir = d;
        return self;
    }

    public fn latest(self, column: string = "id") -> QueryBuilder {
        return self.orderBy(column, "DESC");
    }

    public fn oldest(self, column: string = "id") -> QueryBuilder {
        return self.orderBy(column, "ASC");
    }

    public fn limit(self, count: int) -> QueryBuilder {
        self.limit_count = count;
        return self;
    }

    public fn offset(self, count: int) -> QueryBuilder {
        self.offset_count = count;
        return self;
    }

    private fn add_join(
        self,
        join_type: string,
        table: string,
        first_col: string,
        op: string,
        second_col
    ) {
        sql_check_identifier(table);
        sql_check_identifier(first_col);
        if second_col != null {
            sql_check_identifier(second_col);
        }
        if second_col == null {
            second_col = op;
            op = "=";
        }
        self.joins.push({
            "type": join_type,
            "table": table,
            "first": first_col,
            "op": op,
            "second": second_col
        });
        return self;
    }

    public fn join(self, table: string, first: string, op: string, second: any = null) -> QueryBuilder {
        return self.add_join("INNER", table, first, op, second);
    }

    public fn innerJoin(self, table: string, first: string, op: string, second: any = null) -> QueryBuilder {
        return self.add_join("INNER", table, first, op, second);
    }

    public fn leftJoin(self, table: string, first: string, op: string, second: any = null) -> QueryBuilder {
        return self.add_join("LEFT", table, first, op, second);
    }

    public fn rightJoin(self, table: string, first: string, op: string, second: any = null) -> QueryBuilder {
        return self.add_join("RIGHT", table, first, op, second);
    }

    public fn crossJoin(self, table: string) -> QueryBuilder {
        sql_check_identifier(table);
        self.joins.push({
            "type": "CROSS",
            "table": table,
            "first": "",
            "op": "",
            "second": ""
        });
        return self;
    }

    public fn toSql(self) -> string {
        let compiled = self.build_select_sql();
        return compiled["sql"];
    }

    public fn getBindings(self) -> list {
        let compiled = self.build_select_sql();
        return compiled["params"];
    }

    private fn build_select_sql_with(self, limit_override: int, offset_override: int) {
        let drv = "";
        if self.db_manager != null {
            drv = self.db_manager.driver_name();
        }
        return compile_select_sql(
            self.table_name,
            self.select_cols,
            self.joins,
            self.wheres,
            self.order_col,
            self.order_dir,
            limit_override,
            offset_override,
            drv
        );
    }

    public fn build_select_sql(self) -> map {
        return self.build_select_sql_with(self.limit_count, self.offset_count);
    }

    private fn get_connection(self) {
        return self.db_manager.connection();
    }

    public fn get(self) -> list {
        let compiled = self.build_select_sql();
        return self.get_connection().query(compiled["sql"], compiled["params"]);
    }

    public fn first(self) -> any {
        let compiled = self.build_select_sql_with(1, self.offset_count);
        let rows = self.get_connection().query(compiled["sql"], compiled["params"]);
        if rows.len() > 0 {
            return rows[0];
        }
        return null;
    }

    public fn first_or_fail(self, message: string = "") -> map {
        let row = self.first();
        if row == null {
            let msg = message;
            if msg == "" {
                let tbl = self.table_name;
                msg = "Record not found in {tbl}.";
            }
            throw not_found(msg);
        }
        return row;
    }

    public fn find(self, id: any) -> any {
        return self.where("id", "=", id).first();
    }

    public fn find_or_fail(self, id: any, message: string = "") -> map {
        let row = self.find(id);
        if row == null {
            let msg = message;
            if msg == "" {
                let tbl = self.table_name;
                msg = "Record #{id} not found in {tbl}.";
            }
            throw not_found(msg);
        }
        return row;
    }

    public fn count(self) -> int {
        let head = compile_head(
            "SELECT count(*) as c",
            self.table_name,
            self.joins,
            self.wheres
        );
        let rows = self.get_connection().query(head["sql"], head["params"]);
        if rows.len() > 0 and rows[0].has("c") {
            return int(rows[0]["c"]);
        }
        return 0;
    }

    public fn exists(self) -> bool {
        let head = compile_head("SELECT 1", self.table_name, self.joins, self.wheres);
        let head_sql = head["sql"];
        let rows = self.get_connection().query("{head_sql} LIMIT 1", head["params"]);
        return rows.len() > 0;
    }

    public fn value(self, column: string) -> any {
        sql_check_identifier(column);
        let row = self.first();
        if row != null and row.has(column) {
            return row[column];
        }
        return null;
    }

    public fn pluck(self, column: string) -> list {
        sql_check_identifier(column);
        let rows = self.get();
        let out = [];
        for r in rows {
            if r.has(column) {
                out.push(r[column]);
            }
        }
        return out;
    }

    private fn adjust_column(self, column: string, by: int, sign: string) {
        sql_check_identifier(column);
        let params = [by];
        let where_sql = compile_where_clause(self.wheres, params);
        let tbl = self.table_name;
        let sql = "UPDATE {tbl} SET {column} = {column} {sign} ?{where_sql}";
        let res = self.get_connection().run(sql, params);
        if res.has("changes") {
            return int(res["changes"]);
        }
        return 0;
    }

    public fn increment(self, column: string, by: int = 1) -> int {
        return self.adjust_column(column, by, "+");
    }

    public fn decrement(self, column: string, by: int = 1) -> int {
        return self.adjust_column(column, by, "-");
    }

    public fn paginate(self, page: int = 1, per_page: int = 15) -> map {
        if page < 1 {
            page = 1;
        }
        if per_page < 1 {
            per_page = 1;
        }
        if per_page > 100 {
            per_page = 100;
        }

        let conn = self.get_connection();
        let head = compile_head(
            "SELECT count(*) as c",
            self.table_name,
            self.joins,
            self.wheres
        );
        let count_rows = conn.query(head["sql"], head["params"]);
        let total = 0;
        if count_rows.len() > 0 and count_rows[0].has("c") {
            total = int(count_rows[0]["c"]);
        }

        let last_page = int((total + per_page - 1) / per_page);
        if last_page < 1 {
            last_page = 1;
        }

        let offset = (page - 1) * per_page;
        let compiled = self.build_select_sql_with(per_page, offset);
        let rows = conn.query(compiled["sql"], compiled["params"]);

        let from = offset + 1;
        let to = offset + rows.len();
        if rows.len() == 0 {
            from = 0;
            to = 0;
        }

        return {
            "data": rows,
            "total": total,
            "page": page,
            "per_page": per_page,
            "last_page": last_page,
            "from": from,
            "to": to,
            "has_more": page < last_page
        };
    }

    public fn insert(self, data: map) -> int {
        let compiled = compile_insert_sql(self.table_name, data);
        let res = self.get_connection().run(compiled["sql"], compiled["params"]);
        if res.has("last_id") {
            return int(res["last_id"]);
        }
        return 0;
    }

    public fn update(self, data: map) -> int {
        let compiled = compile_update_sql(self.table_name, data, self.wheres);
        let res = self.get_connection().run(compiled["sql"], compiled["params"]);
        if res.has("changes") {
            return int(res["changes"]);
        }
        return 0;
    }

    public fn delete(self) -> int {
        let params = [];
        let where_sql = compile_where_clause(self.wheres, params);
        let tbl = self.table_name;
        let res = self.get_connection().run("DELETE FROM {tbl}{where_sql}", params);
        if res.has("changes") {
            return int(res["changes"]);
        }
        return 0;
    }
}

public fn sql_escape_like(value: any) -> string {
    let s = string(value);
    let out = s.replace("\\", "\\\\");
    out = out.replace("%", "\\%");
    out = out.replace("_", "\\_");
    return out;
}

// framework/Database/QueryCompiler.matrix
// Stateless SQL compilation helpers extracted from QueryBuilder.matrix.

import "Driver";
import "Connection";

// Allowed SQL comparison operators — unknown operators fall back to "=".
public fn check_sql_op(op: string) -> string {
    let allowed = ["=", "!=", "<>", "<", ">", "<=", ">=", "LIKE", "NOT LIKE", "IN", "NOT IN"];
    let candidate = string(op).trim().upper();
    for a in allowed {
        if candidate == a {
            return candidate;
        }
    }
    return "=";
}

public fn compile_condition(w: map, params: list) -> string {
    let op = w["op"];
    if op == "GROUP" {
        let sub_wheres = w["val"];
        if sub_wheres == null or sub_wheres.len() == 0 {
            return "1 = 1";
        }
        let inner = "";
        let first = true;
        for sw in sub_wheres {
            if not first {
                let b = sw["bool"];
                inner = "{inner} {b} ";
            }
            inner = inner + compile_condition(sw, params);
            first = false;
        }
        return "({inner})";
    }
    let col = w["col"];
    if op == "IN" or op == "NOT IN" {
        let vals = w["val"];
        if vals == null or vals.len() == 0 {
            if op == "IN" {
                return "1 = 0";
            }
            return "1 = 1";
        }
        let placeholders = [];
        for v in vals {
            placeholders.push("?");
            params.push(v);
        }
        let ph = placeholders.join(", ");
        return "{col} {op} ({ph})";
    }
    if op == "IS NULL" or op == "IS NOT NULL" {
        return "{col} {op}";
    }
    params.push(w["val"]);
    return "{col} {op} ?";
}

public fn compile_where_clause(wheres: list, params: list) -> string {
    if wheres.len() == 0 {
        return "";
    }
    let sql = " WHERE ";
    let first = true;
    for w in wheres {
        if not first {
            let b = w["bool"];
            sql = "{sql} {b} ";
        }
        sql = sql + compile_condition(w, params);
        first = false;
    }
    return sql;
}

public fn compile_head(head_sql: string, table_name: string, joins: list, wheres: list) -> map {
    let sql = "{head_sql} FROM {table_name}";
    let params = [];

    if joins.len() > 0 {
        for j in joins {
            let j_type = j["type"];
            let j_table = j["table"];
            if j_type == "CROSS" {
                sql = "{sql} CROSS JOIN {j_table}";
            } else {
                let j_first = j["first"];
                let j_op = j["op"];
                let j_sec = j["second"];
                sql = "{sql} {j_type} JOIN {j_table} ON {j_first} {j_op} {j_sec}";
            }
        }
    }

    sql = sql + compile_where_clause(wheres, params);
    return {
        "sql": sql, "params": params
    };
}

public fn compile_select_sql(
    table_name: string,
    select_cols: list,
    joins: list,
    wheres: list,
    order_col: string,
    order_dir: string,
    limit_override: int,
    offset_override: int,
    driver_name: string
) -> map {
    let cols_sql = select_cols.join(", ");
    let head = compile_head("SELECT {cols_sql}", table_name, joins, wheres);
    let sql = head["sql"];
    let params = head["params"];

    if order_col != "" {
        sql = "{sql} ORDER BY {order_col} {order_dir}";
    }

    sql = sql + format_limit_sql(driver_name, limit_override, offset_override);

    return {
        "sql": sql, "params": params
    };
}

public fn compile_insert_sql(table_name: string, data: map) -> map {
    let keys = [];
    let placeholders = [];
    let params = [];
    for (k, v) in data {
        keys.push(sql_check_identifier(k));
        placeholders.push("?");
        params.push(v);
    }
    let key_sql = keys.join(", ");
    let ph_sql = placeholders.join(", ");
    return {
        "sql": "INSERT INTO {table_name} ({key_sql}) VALUES ({ph_sql})",
        "params": params
    };
}

public fn compile_update_sql(table_name: string, data: map, wheres: list) -> map {
    let set_clauses = [];
    let params = [];
    for (k, v) in data {
        let col = sql_check_identifier(k);
        set_clauses.push("{col} = ?");
        params.push(v);
    }
    let set_sql = set_clauses.join(", ");
    let where_sql = compile_where_clause(wheres, params);
    return {
        "sql": "UPDATE {table_name} SET {set_sql}{where_sql}",
        "params": params
    };
}

// framework/Database/Schema.matrix
// Spid3rWeb DDL Schema Builder & Blueprint (multi-dialect: sqlite, postgres, mysql/mariadb)

import "DB";
import {
    sql_check_identifier
}
from "Connection";

public class ColumnDefinition {
    public let name;
    public let kind;  // id | string | text | integer | float | boolean
    public let len;  // varchar length for string columns
    public let is_primary;
    public let is_unique;
    public let is_nullable;
    public let default_value;
    public let precision;
    public let scale;

    fn new (name: string, kind: string, len: int = 255) {
        self.name = name;
        self.kind = kind;
        self.len = len;
        self.is_primary = false;
        self.is_unique = false;
        self.is_nullable = false;
        self.default_value = null;
        self.precision = 12;
        self.scale = 2;
    }

    public fn unique(self) -> ColumnDefinition {
        self.is_unique = true;
        return self;
    }

    public fn nullable(self) -> ColumnDefinition {
        self.is_nullable = true;
        return self;
    }

    public fn default(self, val: any) -> ColumnDefinition {
        self.default_value = val;
        return self;
    }

    // Per-dialect base type mapping
    fn base_sql(self, dialect: string) -> string {
        if self.kind == "id" {
            if dialect == "postgres" {
                return "SERIAL PRIMARY KEY";
            }
            if dialect == "mysql" {
                return "INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY";
            }
            return "INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT";
        }
        if self.kind == "string" {
            let col_len = self.len;
            if dialect == "postgres" or dialect == "mysql" {
                return "VARCHAR({col_len})";
            }
            return "TEXT";
        }
        if self.kind == "text" {
            return "TEXT";
        }
        if self.kind == "integer" {
            if dialect == "mysql" {
                return "INT";
            }
            return "INTEGER";
        }
        if self.kind == "bigint" {
            if dialect == "postgres" {
                return "BIGINT";
            }
            if dialect == "mysql" {
                return "BIGINT";
            }
            return "INTEGER";  // SQLite INTEGER is 64-bit already
        }
        if self.kind == "decimal" {
            // Exact numeric where the engine supports it. SQLite has no
            // DECIMAL: it maps to INTEGER minor units by convention (the
            // application stores money as integer minor units anyway).
            if dialect == "sqlite" {
                return "INTEGER";
            }
            let prec = self.precision;
            let sc = self.scale;
            return "NUMERIC({prec},{sc})";
        }
        if self.kind == "float" {
            if dialect == "postgres" {
                return "DOUBLE PRECISION";
            }
            if dialect == "mysql" {
                return "DOUBLE";
            }
            return "REAL";
        }
        if self.kind == "boolean" {
            // The runtime SQL binder does not accept bool parameters, so
            // flags are stored as 0/1 integers on EVERY dialect (including
            // PostgreSQL/MySQL). This keeps inserts/updates portable.
            if dialect == "mysql" {
                return "TINYINT(1)";
            }
            return "INTEGER";
        }
        return "TEXT";
    }

    public fn to_sql(self, dialect: string = "sqlite") -> string {
        let parts = [self.name, self.base_sql(dialect)];
        if self.is_unique {
            parts.push("UNIQUE");
        }
        if not self.is_nullable and not self.is_primary {
            parts.push("NOT NULL");
        }
        if self.default_value != null {
            let def_val = self.default_value;
            if type_of(def_val) == "string" {
                parts.push("DEFAULT '{def_val}'");
            } elif type_of(def_val) == "bool" {
                // Boolean columns are integers on every dialect.
                if def_val {
                    parts.push("DEFAULT 1");
                } else {
                    parts.push("DEFAULT 0");
                }
            } else {
                parts.push("DEFAULT {def_val}");
            }
        }
        return parts.join(" ");
    }
}

/// Fluent DDL table blueprint collecting columns, foreign keys, unique constraints, and indexes.
public class Blueprint {
    public let columns;
    // Table-level clauses (foreign keys, composite uniques) appended after
    // the column definitions in CREATE TABLE.
    public let constraints;
    // Secondary indexes emitted after CREATE TABLE by SchemaBuilder.create.
    public let indexes;

    fn new () {
        self.columns = [];
        self.constraints = [];
        self.indexes = [];
    }

    public fn id(self, name: string = "id") -> ColumnDefinition {
        let col = new ColumnDefinition(name, "id");
        self.columns.push(col);
        return col;
    }

    public fn string(self, name: string, len: int = 255) -> ColumnDefinition {
        let col = new ColumnDefinition(name, "string", len);
        self.columns.push(col);
        return col;
    }

    public fn text(self, name: string) -> ColumnDefinition {
        let col = new ColumnDefinition(name, "text");
        self.columns.push(col);
        return col;
    }

    public fn integer(self, name: string) -> ColumnDefinition {
        let col = new ColumnDefinition(name, "integer");
        self.columns.push(col);
        return col;
    }

    // 64-bit integers: money in minor units, counters, totals.
    public fn bigint(self, name: string) -> ColumnDefinition {
        let col = new ColumnDefinition(name, "bigint");
        self.columns.push(col);
        return col;
    }

    // Exact numeric on PostgreSQL/MySQL (NUMERIC/DECIMAL). SQLite maps to
    // INTEGER — apps store money as integer minor units, never floats.
    public fn decimal(
        self,
        name: string,
        precision: int = 12,
        scale: int = 2
    ) -> ColumnDefinition {
        let col = new ColumnDefinition(name, "decimal");
        col.precision = precision;
        col.scale = scale;
        self.columns.push(col);
        return col;
    }

    // FOREIGN KEY (col) REFERENCES ref_table(ref_col) ON DELETE/UPDATE ...
    // Identifiers are validated with the shared guard before interpolation.
    public fn foreign(
        self,
        column: string,
        ref_table: string,
        ref_column: string,
        on_delete: string = "RESTRICT",
        on_update: string = "CASCADE"
    ) -> Blueprint {
        let c = sql_check_identifier(column);
        let t = sql_check_identifier(ref_table);
        let r = sql_check_identifier(ref_column);
        // Actions are fixed vocabulary — never pass user input here.
        let allowed_delete = ["CASCADE", "RESTRICT", "SET NULL", "NO ACTION"];
        let allowed_update = ["CASCADE", "RESTRICT", "SET NULL", "NO ACTION"];
        let od = string(on_delete).upper();
        let ou = string(on_update).upper();
        if not allowed_delete.contains(od) {
            od = "RESTRICT";
        }
        if not allowed_update.contains(ou) {
            ou = "CASCADE";
        }
        self.constraints.push(
            "FOREIGN KEY (" + c + ") REFERENCES " + t + "(" + r + ") ON DELETE " + od + " ON UPDATE " + ou
        );
        return self;
    }

    // Composite UNIQUE constraint (single-column uniqueness is .unique()
    // on a ColumnDefinition).
    public fn unique_columns(self, cols: list) -> Blueprint {
        let safe = [];
        for c in cols {
            safe.push(sql_check_identifier(string(c)));
        }
        self.constraints.push("UNIQUE (" + safe.join(", ") + ")");
        return self;
    }

    // Secondary index; `name` must be unique within the schema. Emitted
    // after CREATE TABLE by SchemaBuilder.create (portable: MySQL has no
    // CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS, so duplicates are tolerated by name).
    public fn index(self, name: string, cols: list, is_unique: bool = false) -> Blueprint {
        let safe = [];
        for c in cols {
            safe.push(sql_check_identifier(string(c)));
        }
        self.indexes.push({
            "name": sql_check_identifier(name),
            "columns": safe,
            "unique": is_unique
        });
        return self;
    }

    public fn float(self, name: string) -> ColumnDefinition {
        let col = new ColumnDefinition(name, "float");
        self.columns.push(col);
        return col;
    }

    public fn boolean(self, name: string) -> ColumnDefinition {
        let col = new ColumnDefinition(name, "boolean");
        self.columns.push(col);
        return col;
    }

    // Timestamps stay TEXT/VARCHAR across dialects because the Active Record
    // layer stores unix_time() string values in created_at / updated_at.
    public fn timestamps(self) {
        let c1 = new ColumnDefinition("created_at", "text");
        c1.nullable();
        self.columns.push(c1);

        let c2 = new ColumnDefinition("updated_at", "text");
        c2.nullable();
        self.columns.push(c2);
    }

    public fn to_sql(self, table_name: string, dialect: string = "sqlite") -> string {
        let col_defs = [];
        for col in self.columns {
            col_defs.push(col.to_sql(dialect));
        }
        for clause in self.constraints {
            col_defs.push(clause);
        }
        return "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS " + table_name + " (\n  " + col_defs.join(",\n  ") + "\n);";
    }
}

/// Multi-dialect DDL schema facade (`create`, `table`, `dropIfExists`).
public class SchemaBuilder {
    fn new () {
        let _ = self;
    }

    fn dialect(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        let d = DB.driver_name();
        // MariaDB uses MySQL DDL
        if d == "mariadb" {
            return "mysql";
        }
        return d;
    }

    // Table/column identifiers are interpolated into DDL — validate them
    // with the shared identifier guard so user-influenced names can never
    // inject SQL.
    fn check_identifier(self, name: string) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return sql_check_identifier(name);
    }

    public fn create(self, table_name: string, callback: any) -> bool {
        let blueprint = new Blueprint();
        callback(blueprint);
        let safe_name = self.check_identifier(table_name);
        let dialect = self.dialect();
        let sql = blueprint.to_sql(safe_name, dialect);
        DB.statement(sql);
        // Secondary indexes: IF NOT EXISTS where supported; MySQL needs a
        // plain CREATE INDEX with duplicate-name errors tolerated.
        let i = 0;
        while i < blueprint.indexes.len() {
            let idx = blueprint.indexes[i];
            let kind = "INDEX ";
            if idx["unique"] {
                kind = "UNIQUE INDEX ";
            }
            let guard = "IF NOT EXISTS ";
            if dialect == "mysql" {
                guard = "";
            }
            let index_sql = "CREATE " + kind + guard + idx["name"] + " ON "
            + safe_name + " (" + idx["columns"].join(", ") + ");";
            try {
                DB.statement(index_sql);
            } catch (e) {
                let msg = string(e).lower();
                if dialect == "mysql" and (
                    msg.contains("already exists") or msg.contains("duplicate key")
                ) {
                    // Idempotent re-run on MySQL.
                } else {
                    throw e;
                }
            }
            i += 1;
        }
        return true;
    }

    // ALTER TABLE helper: add new columns to an existing table.
    // Emits one ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN per column in the blueprint.
    // Note: Column.to_sql() already renders "name TYPE", so no extra
    // name prefix here. Duplicate-column errors are tolerated so the
    // call is idempotent.
    public fn table(self, table_name: string, callback: any) -> bool {
        let blueprint = new Blueprint();
        callback(blueprint);
        let safe_name = self.check_identifier(table_name);
        for col in blueprint.columns {
            let sql = "ALTER TABLE " + safe_name + " ADD COLUMN "
            + col.to_sql(self.dialect()) + ";";
            try {
                DB.statement(sql);
            } catch (e) {
                // Only tolerate "column already exists" — anything else
                // (syntax, locking, permissions) is a real failure and
                // must not be silently recorded as applied.
                let msg = string(e).lower();
                if msg.contains("duplicate column") or msg.contains("already exists") or msg.contains("42701") {
                    // Column already exists — treat as applied.
                } else {
                    throw e;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    public fn dropIfExists(self, table_name: string) -> bool {
        let sql = "DROP TABLE IF EXISTS " + self.check_identifier(table_name) + ";";
        DB.statement(sql);
        return true;
    }
}

public let Schema = new SchemaBuilder();

// framework/Database/ServerConnections.matrix
// PostgreSQL & MySQL/MariaDB Drivers with Auth Diagnostics & 1-Shot Reconnect

import "framework/Database/Driver";
import "framework/Database/Connection";

public class PgConnection implements Driver {
    public let handle;
    public let conn_info;
    public let in_transaction;
    private let addr;
    private let user;
    private let password;
    private let database;

    fn new (addr: string, user: string, password: string, database: string) {
        self.addr = addr;
        self.user = user;
        self.password = password;
        self.database = database;
        self.conn_info = addr;
        self.in_transaction = false;
        self.handle = null;
        self.connect();
    }

    private fn connect(self) {
        try {
            self.handle = pg_connect(self.addr, self.user, self.password, self.database);
        } catch (e) {
            let msg = enhance_db_auth_error("postgres", string(e));
            throw "pg connect failed: {msg}";
        }
        let r = pg_exec(self.handle, "SET standard_conforming_strings = on");
        if r.is_err() {
            let err = enhance_db_auth_error("postgres", string(r.unwrap_err()));
            throw "pg session setup failed: {err}";
        }
        pg_exec(self.handle, "SET statement_timeout = 30000");
        pg_exec(self.handle, "SET lock_timeout = 10000");
    }

    public fn reconnect(self) {
        try {
            if self.handle != null {
                pg_close(self.handle);
            }
        } catch (_e) {
        }
        self.connect();
    }

    public fn driver_name(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "postgres";
    }

    public fn quote_identifier(self, ident: string) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return quote_sql_identifier(ident, "postgres");
    }

    public fn placeholder(self, index: int) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "$" + string(index);
    }

    public fn limit_sql(self, limit: int, offset: int) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return format_limit_sql("postgres", limit, offset);
    }

    public fn begin_sql(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "BEGIN";
    }

    public fn commit_sql(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "COMMIT";
    }

    public fn rollback_sql(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "ROLLBACK";
    }

    public fn set_in_transaction(self, active: bool) {
        self.in_transaction = active;
    }

    public fn ping(self) -> bool {
        try {
            let res = pg_query(self.handle, "SELECT 1 AS ok");
            return res.is_ok();
        } catch (_e) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    private fn exec_query_once(self, final_sql: string) {
        let res = pg_query(self.handle, final_sql);
        if res.is_err() {
            let err = string(res.unwrap_err());
            if not self.in_transaction and is_transient_db_error(err) {
                self.reconnect();
                let res2 = pg_query(self.handle, final_sql);
                if res2.is_ok() {
                    return res2.unwrap();
                }
                err = string(res2.unwrap_err());
            }
            throw "pg_query failed: {err}";
        }
        return res.unwrap();
    }

    public fn query(self, sql: string, params: any = null) -> list {
        let final_sql = sql_interpolate(sql, params, "postgres");
        return self.exec_query_once(final_sql);
    }

    public fn run(self, sql: string, params: any = null) -> map {
        let final_sql = sql_interpolate(sql, params, "postgres");
        let upper = final_sql.trim().upper();

        if upper.starts_with("INSERT") {
            let res = pg_query(self.handle, final_sql + " RETURNING id");
            if res.is_ok() {
                let rows = res.unwrap();
                let lid = 0;
                if rows.len() > 0 and rows[0].has("id") and rows[0]["id"] != null {
                    lid = int(rows[0]["id"]);
                }
                return {
                    "last_id": lid, "changes": 1
                };
            }
            if not self.in_transaction and is_transient_db_error(string(res.unwrap_err())) {
                self.reconnect();
                let res_retry = pg_query(self.handle, final_sql + " RETURNING id");
                if res_retry.is_ok() {
                    let rows = res_retry.unwrap();
                    let lid = 0;
                    if rows.len() > 0 and rows[0].has("id") and rows[0]["id"] != null {
                        lid = int(rows[0]["id"]);
                    }
                    return {
                        "last_id": lid, "changes": 1
                    };
                }
            }
            let r2 = pg_exec(self.handle, final_sql);
            if r2.is_err() {
                let err = string(r2.unwrap_err());
                throw "pg_insert failed: {err}";
            }
            return {
                "last_id": 0, "changes": 1
            };
        }

        if upper.starts_with("UPDATE") or upper.starts_with("DELETE") {
            let res = pg_query(self.handle, final_sql + " RETURNING 1");
            if res.is_ok() {
                let rows = res.unwrap();
                return {
                    "last_id": 0, "changes": rows.len()
                };
            }
            let r2 = self.exec(final_sql);
            if r2.is_err() {
                let err = string(r2.unwrap_err());
                throw "pg_statement failed: {err}";
            }
            return {
                "last_id": 0, "changes": 0
            };
        }

        let r3 = self.exec(final_sql);
        if r3.is_err() {
            let err = string(r3.unwrap_err());
            throw "pg_statement failed: {err}";
        }
        return {
            "last_id": 0, "changes": 0
        };
    }

    public fn exec(self, sql: string) -> any {
        let res = pg_exec(self.handle, sql);
        if res.is_err() {
            let err = string(res.unwrap_err());
            if not self.in_transaction and is_transient_db_error(err) {
                self.reconnect();
                let res2 = pg_exec(self.handle, sql);
                if res2.is_ok() {
                    return res2;
                }
                err = string(res2.unwrap_err());
            }
            throw "pg_exec failed: {err}";
        }
        return res;
    }

    public fn statement(self, sql: string, params: any = null) -> any {
        return self.exec(sql_interpolate(sql, params, "postgres"));
    }

    public fn close(self) {
        if self.handle != null {
            pg_close(self.handle);
        }
    }
}

public class MySqlConnection implements Driver {
    public let handle;
    public let conn_info;
    public let in_transaction;
    private let addr;
    private let user;
    private let password;
    private let database;

    fn new (addr: string, user: string, password: string, database: string) {
        self.addr = addr;
        self.user = user;
        self.password = password;
        self.database = database;
        self.conn_info = addr;
        self.in_transaction = false;
        self.handle = null;
        self.connect();
    }

    private fn connect(self) {
        try {
            self.handle = mysql_connect(
                self.addr,
                self.user,
                self.password,
                self.database
            );
        } catch (e) {
            let msg = enhance_db_auth_error("mysql", string(e));
            throw "mysql connect failed: {msg}";
        }
        let mode_sql = "SET SESSION sql_mode = REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(COALESCE(@@SESSION.sql_mode, ''), 'NO_BACKSLASH_ESCAPES,', ''), ',NO_BACKSLASH_ESCAPES', ''), 'NO_BACKSLASH_ESCAPES', '')";
        let r = mysql_exec(self.handle, mode_sql);
        if r.is_err() {
            let err = enhance_db_auth_error("mysql", string(r.unwrap_err()));
            throw "mysql session setup failed: {err}";
        }
        mysql_exec(self.handle, "SET SESSION max_execution_time = 30000");
    }

    public fn reconnect(self) {
        try {
            if self.handle != null {
                mysql_close(self.handle);
            }
        } catch (_e) {
        }
        self.connect();
    }

    public fn driver_name(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "mysql";
    }

    public fn quote_identifier(self, ident: string) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return quote_sql_identifier(ident, "mysql");
    }

    public fn placeholder(self, _index: int) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "?";
    }

    public fn limit_sql(self, limit: int, offset: int) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return format_limit_sql("mysql", limit, offset);
    }

    public fn begin_sql(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "BEGIN";
    }

    public fn commit_sql(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "COMMIT";
    }

    public fn rollback_sql(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        return "ROLLBACK";
    }

    public fn set_in_transaction(self, active: bool) {
        self.in_transaction = active;
    }

    public fn ping(self) -> bool {
        try {
            let res = mysql_query(self.handle, "SELECT 1 AS ok");
            return res.is_ok();
        } catch (_e) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    public fn query(self, sql: string, params: any = null) -> list {
        let final_sql = sql_interpolate(sql, params, "mysql");
        let res = mysql_query(self.handle, final_sql);
        if res.is_err() {
            let err = string(res.unwrap_err());
            if not self.in_transaction and is_transient_db_error(err) {
                self.reconnect();
                let res2 = mysql_query(self.handle, final_sql);
                if res2.is_ok() {
                    return res2.unwrap();
                }
                err = string(res2.unwrap_err());
            }
            throw "mysql_query failed: {err}";
        }
        return res.unwrap();
    }

    public fn run(self, sql: string, params: any = null) -> map {
        let final_sql = sql_interpolate(sql, params, "mysql");
        let upper = final_sql.trim().upper();
        let res = self.exec(final_sql);

        if upper.starts_with("INSERT") {
            let lid = 0;
            let res2 = mysql_query(self.handle, "SELECT LAST_INSERT_ID() AS id");
            if res2.is_ok() {
                let rows = res2.unwrap();
                if rows.len() > 0 and rows[0].has("id") and rows[0]["id"] != null {
                    lid = int(rows[0]["id"]);
                }
            }
            return {
                "last_id": lid, "changes": 1
            };
        }

        if upper.starts_with("UPDATE") or upper.starts_with("DELETE") {
            let changes = 0;
            if res.is_ok() {
                let val = res.unwrap();
                if val != null {
                    changes = int(val);
                }
            }
            return {
                "last_id": 0, "changes": changes
            };
        }

        return {
            "last_id": 0, "changes": 0
        };
    }

    public fn exec(self, sql: string) -> any {
        let res = mysql_exec(self.handle, sql);
        if res.is_err() {
            let err = string(res.unwrap_err());
            if not self.in_transaction and is_transient_db_error(err) {
                self.reconnect();
                let res2 = mysql_exec(self.handle, sql);
                if res2.is_ok() {
                    return res2;
                }
                err = string(res2.unwrap_err());
            }
            throw "mysql_exec failed: {err}";
        }
        return res;
    }

    public fn statement(self, sql: string, params: any = null) -> any {
        return self.exec(sql_interpolate(sql, params, "mysql"));
    }

    public fn close(self) {
        if self.handle != null {
            mysql_close(self.handle);
        }
    }
}

// framework/Events/Event.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Event Dispatcher
//
// A synchronous, in-process publish/subscribe bus. Decouples side effects
// from core actions — a controller dispatches "user.registered" and never
// needs to know that a welcome-mailer or an analytics sink is listening.
//
// API (via the global `Event` facade, or `new EventDispatcher()` in tests):
//   Event.listen(name, handler)         handler(payload, event_name) -> any
//   Event.once(name, handler)           fires a single time, then unregisters
//   Event.dispatch(name, payload)       fire all matching listeners; returns
//                                       a list of their return values
//   Event.has_listeners(name)           exact or wildcard match
//   Event.count_listeners(name)         how many listeners would fire
//   Event.forget(name)                  remove all listeners for that exact
//                                       name or wildcard pattern
//   Event.subscribe(subscriber)         class-based grouping: the subscriber
//                                       must expose subscribe(dispatcher)
//   Event.flush()                       remove everything
//
// Wildcards: a pattern ending in "*" matches any event with that prefix —
//   Event.listen("user.*", handler) fires for "user.created", "user.deleted".
// Only trailing "*" is supported; mid-pattern globs are intentionally not.
//
// Semantics:
//   - Handlers receive exactly two arguments: (payload, event_name).
//   - Exact listeners fire first (registration order), then wildcard
//     listeners (pattern registration order, then their registration order).
//   - Listeners run synchronously on the dispatching task. Errors propagate
//     to the caller — wrap dispatch in try/catch when fire-and-forget is
//     wanted. A throwing listener stops later listeners: this is deliberate,
//     silent failure is a bug factory.
//   - Dispatch is safe from any task; there is no shared mutable state
//     beyond the listener registry itself.

/// Synchronous event pub/sub bus supporting exact names, trailing `*` wildcards, `once`, and subscribers.
public class EventDispatcher {
    private let listeners;  // name-or-pattern -> list of map("id", int, "handler", fn, "once", bool)
    private let next_id;  // monotonically increasing registration id

    fn new () {
        self.listeners = {
        };
        self.next_id = 1;
    }

    public fn listen(self, name: string, handler: any) -> EventDispatcher {
        self.register(name, handler, false);
        return self;
    }

    public fn once(self, name: string, handler: any) -> EventDispatcher {
        self.register(name, handler, true);
        return self;
    }

    fn register(self, name: string, handler: any, once_flag: bool) {
        if not self.listeners.has(name) {
            self.listeners[name] = [];
        }
        self.listeners[name].push({
            "id": self.next_id, "handler": handler, "once": once_flag
        });
        self.next_id += 1;
    }

    // Does a registered pattern cover this event name? Exact keys match
    // trivially; patterns must end in "*".
    fn pattern_matches(self, pattern: string, name: string) -> bool {
        let _ = self;
        if pattern == name {
            return true;
        }
        let n = len(pattern);
        if n > 0 and pattern.slice(n - 1, n) == "*" {
            return name.starts_with(pattern.slice(0, n - 1));
        }
        return false;
    }

    // Collect the listeners that would fire, in firing order.
    fn matching(self, name: string) {
        let matched = [];
        if self.listeners.has(name) {
            for h in self.listeners[name] {
                matched.push(h);
            }
        }
        for (pattern, pat_list) in self.listeners {
            if pattern != name and self.pattern_matches(pattern, name) {
                for h in pat_list {
                    matched.push(h);
                }
            }
        }
        return matched;
    }

    // Fire all matching listeners. Returns a list with one entry per
    // listener: its return value (null when the handler returns nothing).
    public fn dispatch(self, name: string, payload: any = null) -> list {
        let matched = self.matching(name);
        let results = [];
        let spent = [];  // ids of once-listeners that fired
        for h in matched {
            let f = h["handler"];
            results.push(f(payload, name));
            if h["once"] {
                spent.push(h["id"]);
            }
        }
        for id in spent {
            self.remove_id(id);
        }
        return results;
    }

    fn remove_id(self, id: int) {
        for (key, entries) in self.listeners {
            let kept = [];
            for h in entries {
                if h["id"] != id {
                    kept.push(h);
                }
            }
            if kept.len() == 0 {
                self.listeners.remove(key);
            } else {
                self.listeners[key] = kept;
            }
        }
    }

    public fn has_listeners(self, name: string) -> bool {
        if self.listeners.has(name) {
            return true;
        }
        for (pattern, _entries) in self.listeners {
            if pattern != name and self.pattern_matches(pattern, name) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    public fn count_listeners(self, name: string) -> int {
        return self.matching(name).len();
    }

    // Remove every listener registered under exactly this name or pattern.
    public fn forget(self, name: string) -> EventDispatcher {
        if self.listeners.has(name) {
            self.listeners.remove(name);
        }
        return self;
    }

    // Class-based listener grouping. The subscriber object must expose a
    // `subscribe(dispatcher)` method that registers its own listeners.
    // Bind the sink object to a local before closing over it:
    //   class UserSubscriber {
    //       public let log;
    //       fn new() { self.log = list(); }
    //       public fn subscribe(self, events) {
    //           let sink = self.log;
    //           events.listen("user.created", fn(p, n) { sink.push(p); return null; });
    //       }
    //   }
    //   Event.subscribe(new UserSubscriber());
    public fn subscribe(self, subscriber: any) -> EventDispatcher {
        subscriber.subscribe(self);
        return self;
    }

    public fn flush(self) -> EventDispatcher {
        self.listeners = {
        };
        self.next_id = 1;
        return self;
    }
}

// Global event facade singleton.
public let Event = new EventDispatcher();
