// framework/Filesystem/Storage.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Filesystem Subsystem & Facade
//
//   import "framework/Filesystem/Storage";
//   Storage.disk("public").put("receipts/a.txt", "hello");
//   Storage.disk("public").url("receipts/a.txt");   // "/uploads/receipts/a.txt"
//   Storage.disk("local").get("secrets.txt");
//
// Disks are declared in config/filesystems.matrix. Only the "local" driver
// ships in this build; roots are relative to the project root.
//
// Security: every relative path is normalized and validated — absolute
// paths, ".." traversal and Windows drive/UNC prefixes are rejected with a
// thrown error before any file operation touches the disk.

import "framework/Foundation/Config";
import "framework/Http/StaticFiles";

/// Path-guarded local filesystem disk with atomic writes, streaming, quotas, and extension filtering.
public class LocalDisk {
    public let name;
    public let root;
    public let visibility;
    public let url_prefix;
    public let max_bytes;
    public let blocked_extensions;

    fn new (
        name: string,
        root: string,
        visibility: string,
        url_prefix: string,
        max_bytes: int = 0,
        blocked_extensions = null
    ) {
        self.name = name;
        self.root = root;
        self.visibility = visibility;
        self.url_prefix = url_prefix;
        self.max_bytes = max_bytes;
        self.blocked_extensions = blocked_extensions ?? [];
    }

    fn guard(self, rel: string) -> string {
        let p = string(rel).replace("\\", "/").trim();
        if p == "" {
            throw "storage: empty path on disk '" + self.name + "'";
        }
        if p.starts_with("/") {
            throw "storage: absolute paths are not allowed: " + p;
        }
        if p.contains(":") {
            throw "storage: path contains ':' (drive/UNC prefix?): " + p;
        }
        let parts = p.split("/");
        let clean = [];
        for seg in parts {
            let s = string(seg).trim();
            if s == "" or s == "." {
                continue;
            }
            if s == ".." {
                throw "storage: path traversal is not allowed: " + rel;
            }
            clean.push(s);
        }
        if clean.len() == 0 {
            throw "storage: empty normalized path on disk '" + self.name + "'";
        }
        return clean.join("/");
    }

    fn check_extension_allowed(self, rel: string) {
        if self.blocked_extensions == null or self.blocked_extensions.len() == 0 {
            return;
        }
        let clean = string(rel).replace("\\", "/").trim();
        let segs = clean.split("/");
        let base = segs[segs.len() - 1];
        let dots = base.split(".");
        if dots.len() < 2 {
            return;
        }
        let ext = dots[dots.len() - 1].trim().lower();
        for blocked in self.blocked_extensions {
            if string(blocked).trim().lower() == ext {
                throw "storage: extension '.{ext}' is blocked on disk '{self.name}'";
            }
        }
    }

    fn full(self, rel: string) -> string {
        return self.root + "/" + self.guard(rel);
    }

    fn ensure_root(self) {
        if not file_exists(self.root) {
            let parts = self.root.split("/");
            let dir = parts[0];
            if dir != "" and not file_exists(dir) {
                dir_create(dir);
            }
            let i = 1;
            while i < parts.len() {
                dir = dir + "/" + parts[i];
                if not file_exists(dir) {
                    dir_create(dir);
                }
                i = i + 1;
            }
        }
    }

    fn ensure_parent(self, file_path: string) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        let parts = string(file_path).split("/");
        if parts.len() < 2 {
            return file_path;
        }
        let dir = parts[0];
        if not file_exists(dir) {
            dir_create(dir);
        }
        let i = 1;
        while i < parts.len() - 1 {
            dir = dir + "/" + parts[i];
            if not file_exists(dir) {
                dir_create(dir);
            }
            i = i + 1;
        }
        return file_path;
    }

    public fn exists(self, rel: string) -> bool {
        return file_exists(self.full(rel));
    }

    public fn put(self, rel: string, contents: string, atomic: bool = true) -> bool {
        let path = self.full(rel);
        self.check_extension_allowed(rel);
        let text = string(contents);
        if self.max_bytes > 0 and len(text) > self.max_bytes {
            let limit = self.max_bytes;
            throw "storage: payload exceeds disk quota ({limit} bytes) on disk '{self.name}'";
        }
        self.ensure_root();
        self.ensure_parent(path);
        let res = if atomic {
            write_file_atomic(path, text)
        } else {
            write_file(path, text)
        };
        return res.is_ok();
    }

    public fn put_stream(self, rel: string, source: any) -> bool {
        let path = self.full(rel);
        self.check_extension_allowed(rel);
        self.ensure_root();
        self.ensure_parent(path);
        let rnd = hex_encode(random_bytes(8).unwrap());
        let tmp_path = "{path}.tmp.{rnd}";
        let fh_res = file_open(tmp_path, "wb");
        if fh_res.is_err() {
            return false;
        }
        let fh = fh_res.unwrap();
        let written = 0;
        let ok = false;
        try {
            let st = type_of(source);
            if st == "function" {
                while true {
                    let chunk = source();
                    if chunk == null or len(chunk) == 0 {
                        break;
                    }
                    written = written + len(chunk);
                    if self.max_bytes > 0 and written > self.max_bytes {
                        let limit = self.max_bytes;
                        throw "storage: streamed payload exceeds disk quota ({limit} bytes)";
                    }
                    let w = file_write(fh, chunk);
                    if w.is_err() {
                        throw string(w.unwrap_err());
                    }
                }
            } elif st == "list" {
                for chunk in source {
                    if chunk == null or len(chunk) == 0 {
                        continue;
                    }
                    written = written + len(chunk);
                    if self.max_bytes > 0 and written > self.max_bytes {
                        let limit = self.max_bytes;
                        throw "storage: streamed payload exceeds disk quota ({limit} bytes)";
                    }
                    let w = file_write(fh, chunk);
                    if w.is_err() {
                        throw string(w.unwrap_err());
                    }
                }
            } elif source != null and len(source) > 0 {
                written = len(source);
                if self.max_bytes > 0 and written > self.max_bytes {
                    let limit = self.max_bytes;
                    throw "storage: streamed payload exceeds disk quota ({limit} bytes)";
                }
                let w = file_write(fh, source);
                if w.is_err() {
                    throw string(w.unwrap_err());
                }
            }
            file_flush(fh);
            ok = true;
        } finally {
            file_close(fh);
            if not ok and file_exists(tmp_path) {
                file_delete(tmp_path);
            }
        }
        let mv = rename(tmp_path, path);
        if mv.is_err() {
            if file_exists(tmp_path) {
                file_delete(tmp_path);
            }
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    public fn get_stream(self, rel: string, chunk_size: int = 65_536) -> any {
        let path = self.full(rel);
        if not file_exists(path) or not is_file(path) {
            return null;
        }
        return make_file_stream(path, chunk_size);
    }

    public fn append(self, rel: string, contents: string) -> bool {
        let path = self.full(rel);
        self.check_extension_allowed(rel);
        self.ensure_root();
        self.ensure_parent(path);
        let open_res = file_open(path, "a");
        if open_res.is_err() {
            return false;
        }
        let fh = open_res.unwrap();
        let ok = false;
        try {
            let w = file_write(fh, string(contents));
            file_flush(fh);
            ok = w.is_ok();
        } finally {
            file_close(fh);
        }
        return ok;
    }

    public fn get(self, rel: string) -> any {
        let path = self.full(rel);
        if not file_exists(path) {
            return null;
        }
        let res = read_file(path);
        if res.is_err() {
            return null;
        }
        return res.unwrap();
    }

    public fn delete(self, rel: string) -> bool {
        let path = self.full(rel);
        if not file_exists(path) {
            return false;
        }
        return file_delete(path).is_ok();
    }

    public fn path(self, rel: string) -> string {
        return self.full(rel);
    }

    public fn url(self, rel: string) -> string {
        if self.url_prefix == "" {
            return "";
        }
        return self.url_prefix + "/" + self.guard(rel);
    }
}

/// Filesystem storage manager resolving configured disks (`local`, `public`) and temp uploads.
public class StorageManager {
    public fn disk(self, name: string = "") -> LocalDisk {
        let _ = self;
        let disk_name = string(name);
        if disk_name == "" {
            disk_name = string(Config.get("filesystems.default", "local"));
        }
        let cfg = Config.get("filesystems.disks." + disk_name, null);
        if cfg == null or type_of(cfg) != "map" {
            throw "storage: unknown disk '" + disk_name + "' (check config/filesystems.matrix)";
        }
        let driver = "local";
        if cfg.has("driver") {
            driver = string(cfg["driver"]);
        }
        if driver != "local" {
            throw "storage: driver '" + driver + "' is not supported in this build (local only)";
        }
        let root = if cfg.has("root") and cfg["root"] != null {
            string(cfg["root"])
        } else {
            "storage/app"
        };
        let vis = if cfg.has("visibility") and cfg["visibility"] != null {
            string(cfg["visibility"])
        } else {
            "private"
        };
        let url = if cfg.has("url") and cfg["url"] != null {
            string(cfg["url"])
        } else {
            ""
        };
        let max_bytes = if cfg.has("max_bytes") and cfg["max_bytes"] != null {
            int(cfg["max_bytes"])
        } else {
            0
        };
        let blocked = if cfg.has("blocked_extensions") and cfg["blocked_extensions"] != null {
            cfg["blocked_extensions"]
        } else {
            []
        };
        return new LocalDisk(disk_name, root, vis, url, max_bytes, blocked);
    }

    public fn temp_path(self, prefix: string = "tmp_") -> string {
        let _ = self;
        let ts = int(unix_time());
        let rnd = hex_encode(random_bytes(8).unwrap());
        return "_tmp/{prefix}{ts}_{rnd}.tmp";
    }

    public fn prune_temp(self, max_age_seconds: int = 3_600) -> int {
        let d = self.disk("local");
        let dir_path = d.root + "/_tmp";
        if not file_exists(dir_path) {
            return 0;
        }
        let listing = list_dir(dir_path);
        if listing.is_err() {
            return 0;
        }
        let cutoff = int(unix_time()) - max_age_seconds;
        let removed = 0;
        for name in listing.unwrap() {
            let p = dir_path + "/" + string(name);
            if not is_file(p) {
                continue;
            }
            let mt = file_mtime(p);
            if mt.is_ok() and int(mt.unwrap()) <= cutoff {
                if file_delete(p).is_ok() {
                    removed = removed + 1;
                }
            }
        }
        return removed;
    }
}

public let Storage = new StorageManager();

// framework/Foundation/Application.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Application Kernel & HTTP Request Coordinator

import "framework/Http/Request";
import "framework/Http/Response";
import "framework/Http/Router";
import "framework/Http/Middleware";
import "framework/Http/StaticFiles";
import "framework/Http/Csrf";
import "framework/Http/ErrorRenderer";
import "framework/Http/HttpException";
import "framework/Auth/Auth";
import "framework/Foundation/Container";
import "framework/Foundation/Config";
import "framework/Foundation/RuntimeVersion";
import "framework/Foundation/Server";
import "framework/Events/Event";
import "framework/Testing/TestClient";

/// Core Spid3rWeb HTTP application kernel coordinating routing, middleware, CSRF, static files, and providers.
public class Application {
    public let router;
    public let global_middleware;
    public let base_path;
    public let name;
    public let env;
    public let debug;
    public let middleware_registry;
    public let container;
    public let providers;
    private let booted;

    fn new (base_path: string = ".") {
        self.base_path = base_path;
        self.router = Route;
        self.global_middleware = [new CorsMiddleware()];
        self.name = "Spid3rWeb";
        self.env = resolve_app_env();
        self.debug = resolve_app_debug();
        self.container = new ServiceContainer();
        self.providers = [];
        self.booted = false;
        let me = self;
        self.container.instance("app", me);
        self.container.singleton("router", fn(_c) {
            return me.router;
        });
        self.container.singleton("events", fn(_c) {
            return Event;
        });
        self.middleware_registry = {
        };
        self.middleware_registry["auth"] = fn(_args) {
            return new AuthMiddleware();
        };
        self.middleware_registry["guest"] = fn(_args) {
            return new GuestMiddleware();
        };
        self.middleware_registry["cors"] = fn(_args) {
            return new CorsMiddleware();
        };
        self.middleware_registry["logger"] = fn(_args) {
            return new LoggerMiddleware();
        };
        self.middleware_registry["etag"] = fn(_args) {
            return new EtagMiddleware();
        };
        self.middleware_registry["request_id"] = fn(_args) {
            return new RequestIdMiddleware();
        };
        self.middleware_registry["throttle"] = fn(args) {
            let max_attempts = 60;
            let decay_seconds = 60;
            if args.len() > 0 {
                max_attempts = int(args[0]);
            }
            if args.len() > 1 {
                decay_seconds = int(args[1]);
            }
            return new ThrottleMiddleware(max_attempts, decay_seconds);
        };
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Express / Fastify-Style Direct Routing Helpers
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    public fn get(self, path: string, handler: any) -> PendingRoute {
        return self.router.get(path, handler);
    }

    public fn post(self, path: string, handler: any) -> PendingRoute {
        return self.router.post(path, handler);
    }

    public fn put(self, path: string, handler: any) -> PendingRoute {
        return self.router.put(path, handler);
    }

    public fn patch(self, path: string, handler: any) -> PendingRoute {
        return self.router.patch(path, handler);
    }

    public fn delete(self, path: string, handler: any) -> PendingRoute {
        return self.router.delete(path, handler);
    }

    public fn options(self, path: string, handler: any) -> PendingRoute {
        return self.router.options(path, handler);
    }

    public fn any(self, path: string, handler: any) -> PendingRoute {
        return self.router.any(path, handler);
    }

    public fn match_any(self, methods: list, path: string, handler: any) -> PendingRoute {
        return self.router.match_any(methods, path, handler);
    }

    public fn group(self, options: map, callback: any) -> Router {
        return self.router.group(options, callback);
    }

    public fn resource(self, path: string, controller: any) -> Router {
        return self.router.resource(path, controller);
    }

    public fn bind(self, param: string, resolver: any) -> Application {
        self.router.bind(param, resolver);
        return self;
    }

    public fn fallback(self, handler: any) -> Router {
        return self.router.fallback(handler);
    }

    public fn mount(self, prefix: string, sub_router: Router) -> Application {
        self.router.mount(prefix, sub_router);
        return self;
    }

    public fn test_client(self) -> TestClient {
        return new TestClient(self);
    }

    public fn middleware(self, name: string, factory: any) -> Application {
        self.middleware_registry[string(name).trim().lower()] = factory;
        return self;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Service Provider Lifecycle
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    public fn register(self, provider: any) -> Application {
        provider.register(self.container);
        self.providers.push(provider);
        return self;
    }

    public fn boot(self) -> Application {
        if self.booted {
            return self;
        }
        // NOTE: `for x in list` iterates over COPIES in Spid3r — boot by index.
        let i = 0;
        let count = self.providers.len();
        while i < count {
            self.providers[i].boot(self.container);
            i += 1;
        }
        self.booted = true;
        Event.dispatch("app.booted", self);
        return self;
    }

    public fn use(self, mw: any) -> Application {
        self.global_middleware.push(mw);
        return self;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // HTTP Request Handler
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    public fn handle_request(self, raw_req: any) -> map {
        self.boot();
        let req = new Request();
        req.app = self;
        req.res = new Response();
        try {
            if raw_req != null {
                req.init_from_raw(raw_req);
            }
            req.res.set_https(req.is_secure());
        } catch (parse_err) {
            if is_http_exception(parse_err) {
                return self.finalize_http_response(
                    req,
                    ErrorRenderer.http_error(req, parse_err)
                );
            }
            return ErrorRenderer.server_error(req, parse_err);
        }

        let auth = new AuthManager();
        req.auth = auth;
        if req.session_id != "" {
            auth.authenticate_token(req.session_id);
        }
        if auth.check() {
            req.user_data = auth.user();
        }

        if req.method == "POST" or req.method == "PUT" or req.method == "PATCH" or req.method == "DELETE" {
            let origin = req.header("origin", "");
            if origin != "" and not is_same_origin(req, origin) {
                let plain = response("403 Forbidden — cross-origin request rejected", 403);
                plain.header("content-type", "text/plain");
                return plain.to_http();
            }
            if not csrf_valid(req) {
                return ErrorRenderer.csrf_timeout(req);
            }
        }

        if (req.method == "GET" or req.method == "HEAD") and is_static_prefix(req.path) {
            let static_res = try_serve_static(req.path, req, self.debug);
            if static_res != null {
                return static_res.to_http();
            }
            if is_unsafe_path(req.path) {
                return ErrorRenderer.not_found(req);
            }
        }

        let global_pipeline = new Pipeline();
        let final_response = null;
        try {
            final_response = global_pipeline.send(req).through(self.global_middleware).then(fn(r) {
                let match_result = self.router.match_route(r.method, r.path);
                if match_result == null {
                    if (r.method == "GET" or r.method == "HEAD") and is_root_static_file(r.path) {
                        let root_static = try_serve_static(r.path, r, self.debug);
                        if root_static != null {
                            return root_static;
                        }
                    }
                    if r.path == "/favicon.ico" {
                        let icon = response("", 204);
                        icon.header("content-type", "image/x-icon");
                        return icon;
                    }
                    let allowed = self.router.allowed_methods(r.path);
                    if allowed.len() > 0 {
                        return text_405(allowed.join(", "));
                    }
                    if self.router.fallback_handler != null {
                        let fb_res = self.router.fallback_handler(r);
                        return self.coerce_handler_output(fb_res);
                    }
                    return ErrorRenderer.not_found(r);
                }
                let route_entry = match_result["route"];
                r.params = match_result["params"];
                r.bound_params = self.router.resolve_bindings(r.params);
                let mw_instances = self.resolve_middleware(route_entry.middleware_list);
                let route_pipeline = new Pipeline();
                route_pipeline.send(r).through(mw_instances);
                return route_pipeline.then(fn(inner_req) {
                    try {
                        let raw_out = self.router.dispatch(route_entry.handler, inner_req);
                        return self.coerce_handler_output(raw_out);
                    } catch (e) {
                        if is_http_exception(e) {
                            return ErrorRenderer.http_error(inner_req, e);
                        }
                        return ErrorRenderer.server_error(inner_req, e);
                    }
                });
            });
        } catch (e) {
            if is_http_exception(e) {
                return ErrorRenderer.http_error(req, e);
            }
            return ErrorRenderer.server_error(req, e);
        }

        if final_response == null {
            let empty = response("", 200);
            empty.header("content-type", "text/plain");
            return self.finalize_http_response(req, empty.to_http());
        }

        return self.finalize_http_response(req, self.normalize_response(final_response));
    }

    fn coerce_handler_output(self, res: any) -> any {
        let _ = self;
        if res == null {
            let empty = response("", 200);
            empty.header("content-type", "text/plain");
            return empty;
        }
        let t = type_of(res);
        if t == "instance" {
            return res;
        }
        if t == "map" {
            if res.has("status") and res.has("headers") and res.has("body") {
                return res;
            }
            return json(res, 200);
        }
        if t == "list" {
            return json(res, 200);
        }
        if t == "string" {
            return html(res, 200);
        }
        let txt = response(string(res), 200);
        txt.header("content-type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8");
        return txt;
    }

    fn normalize_response(self, res) {
        let coerced = self.coerce_handler_output(res);
        let out = null;
        if type_of(coerced) == "map" and coerced.has("status") and coerced.has("headers") {
            out = coerced;
        } else {
            out = coerced.to_http();
        }
        if out.has("headers") and out["headers"] != null {
            let hdrs = out["headers"];
            let ht = type_of(hdrs);
            let has_acao = false;
            let ct = "";
            if ht == "map" {
                for (hk, hv) in hdrs {
                    let lk = string(hk).lower();
                    if lk == "access-control-allow-origin" {
                        has_acao = true;
                    } elif lk == "content-type" {
                        ct = string(hv);
                    }
                }
            } elif ht == "list" {
                for entry in hdrs {
                    if type_of(entry) == "map" and entry.has("name") and entry.has("value") {
                        let lk = string(entry["name"]).lower();
                        if lk == "access-control-allow-origin" {
                            has_acao = true;
                        } elif lk == "content-type" {
                            ct = string(entry["value"]);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            if (ht == "map" or ht == "list") and not has_acao and ct.contains("application/json") {
                append_http_header(out, "access-control-allow-origin", "*");
                append_http_header(
                    out,
                    "access-control-allow-methods",
                    "GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, PATCH, OPTIONS"
                );
                append_http_header(
                    out,
                    "access-control-allow-headers",
                    "content-type, authorization, x-requested-with"
                );
            }
        }
        return out;
    }

    fn finalize_http_response(self, req: Request, http_map: map) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        let secure_req = req.is_secure();
        if req.session_store != null and req.session_store.is_dirty() {
            req.session_store.commit_to_http(http_map, secure_req);
        }
        if is_production() and secure_req and Config.get("security.hsts.auto_production_tls", true) == true {
            if type_of(http_map) == "map" and http_map.has("headers") {
                let hdrs = http_map["headers"];
                let has_hsts = false;
                if type_of(hdrs) == "map" {
                    for (hk, _hv) in hdrs {
                        if string(hk).lower() == "strict-transport-security" {
                            has_hsts = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                } elif type_of(hdrs) == "list" {
                    for entry in hdrs {
                        if type_of(entry) == "map" and entry.has("name") {
                            if string(entry["name"]).lower() == "strict-transport-security" {
                                has_hsts = true;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                if not has_hsts {
                    append_http_header(
                        http_map,
                        "strict-transport-security",
                        hsts_header_value()
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        return http_map;
    }

    private fn resolve_middleware(self, mw_names) {
        let resolved = [];
        for name in mw_names {
            if type_of(name) == "function" or type_of(name) == "instance" {
                resolved.push(name);
                continue;
            }
            let raw = string(name).trim().lower();
            if raw == "" {
                continue;
            }
            let base = raw;
            let args = [];
            let colon = raw.index_of(":");
            if colon > 0 {
                base = raw.slice(0, colon).trim();
                let arg_str = raw.slice(colon + 1, raw.len()).trim();
                if arg_str != "" {
                    for a in arg_str.split(",") {
                        args.push(a.trim());
                    }
                }
            }
            if self.middleware_registry.has(base) {
                let factory = self.middleware_registry[base];
                resolved.push(factory(args));
            } else {
                throw "Unknown middleware alias '{raw}'. Register it via app.middleware(\"{base}\", factory).";
            }
        }
        return resolved;
    }

    public fn check_production_ready(self) {
        let _ = self;
        assert_app_key_configured();
        assert_min_runtime_version();
    }

    public fn listen(self, port: int = 8000, host: string = "", server_opts: any = null) {
        listen_http(self, port, host, server_opts);
    }

    public fn listen_tls(
        self,
        port: int,
        cert_path: string,
        key_path: string,
        host: string = "",
        server_opts: any = null
    ) {
        listen_https(self, port, cert_path, key_path, host, server_opts);
    }
}

// framework/Config/Config.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Configuration Repository
//
// Central dot-notation access to every config/*.matrix file:
//
//   Config.get("app.name")                        -> "Spid3rWeb Developer Platform"
//   Config.get("session.lifetime", 3600)          -> value or fallback
//   Config.get("logging.channels.mail.path")
//
// Conventions (mirroring config/cache & config/database):
//   - Mutable settings are resolved from environment variables at READ time
//     via "*_env" keys (e.g. "driver_env": "SPID3R_CACHE_DRIVER"), so the
//     same codebase runs anywhere without code edits.
//   - `Config` is a process-wide singleton (same pattern as `Auth`/`Cache`).
//
// Importing this module loads and registers every bundled config file.

import "config/app";
import "config/cache";
import "config/database";
import "config/auth";
import "config/session";
import "config/filesystems";
import "config/logging";
import "config/mail";
import "config/queue";
import "config/services";
import "config/security";
import "config/upload";
import "config/http";

/// Hierarchical dot-notation repository over `config/*.matrix` modules.
public class ConfigRepository {
    public let items;

    fn new () {
        self.items = {
        };
        // Register every bundled config file. Application config files can
        // register their own via Config.load("name", some_map).
        self.load("app", config_app);
        self.load("cache", config_cache);
        self.load("database", config_database);
        self.load("auth", config_auth);
        self.load("session", config_session);
        self.load("filesystems", config_filesystems);
        self.load("logging", config_logging);
        self.load("mail", config_mail);
        self.load("queue", config_queue);
        self.load("services", config_services);
        self.load("security", config_security);
        self.load("upload", config_upload);
        self.load("http", config_http);
    }

    // Register (or replace) a config namespace at runtime.
    public fn load(self, name: string, data: map) -> bool {
        self.items[name] = data;
        return true;
    }

    // Dot-notation read: "logging.channels.mail.path".
    // Returns `fallback` when any path segment is missing.
    public fn get(self, key: string, fallback: any = null) -> any {
        let node = self.items;
        let parts = string(key).split(".");
        for p in parts {
            if type_of(node) != "map" {
                return fallback;
            }
            if not node.has(p) {
                return fallback;
            }
            node = node[p];
        }
        return node;
    }

    // True when the dot-path resolves to an existing value.
    public fn has(self, key: string) -> bool {
        let node = self.items;
        let parts = string(key).split(".");
        for p in parts {
            if type_of(node) != "map" or not node.has(p) {
                return false;
            }
            node = node[p];
        }
        return true;
    }

    // Dot-notation write: Config.set("app.maintenance", true).
    // Creates intermediate maps as needed. Returns false for scalar
    // collisions along the path.
    public fn set(self, key: string, value: any) -> bool {
        let parts = string(key).split(".");
        let last = parts[parts.len() - 1];
        let node = self.items;
        let i = 0;
        while i < parts.len() - 1 {
            let p = parts[i];
            if not node.has(p) or type_of(node[p]) != "map" {
                node[p] = {
                };
            }
            node = node[p];
            i = i + 1;
        }
        node[last] = value;
        return true;
    }

    // Every registered namespace: ["app", "auth", ...]
    public fn namespaces(self) -> list {
        return self.items.keys();
    }
}

public let Config = new ConfigRepository();

let _ephemeral_app_key = {
    "value": "", "warned": false
};

public fn resolve_app_env() -> string {
    let s = env("APP_ENV").unwrap_or("").trim().lower();
    if s != "" {
        return s;
    }
    let cfg = string(Config.get("app.env", "production")).trim().lower();
    if cfg != "" {
        return cfg;
    }
    return "production";
}

public fn is_production() -> bool {
    return resolve_app_env() == "production";
}

public fn resolve_app_debug() -> bool {
    let s = env("APP_DEBUG").unwrap_or("").trim().lower();
    if s != "" {
        return s == "1" or s == "true" or s == "yes" or s == "on";
    }
    let e = resolve_app_env();
    return e == "development" or e == "local";
}

public fn is_insecure_app_key(key: string) -> bool {
    let trimmed = string(key).trim();
    if trimmed == "" or trimmed.len() < 16 {
        return true;
    }
    let lower = trimmed.lower();
    return lower == "spid3rweb-default-cookie-secret-key"
    or lower == "spid3rweb-default-jwt-secret-key-change-in-prod"
    or lower == "changeme"
    or lower == "secret"
    or lower == "your-app-key-here";
}

public fn configured_app_key() -> string {
    for env_name in ["APP_KEY", "SPID3R_APP_KEY"] {
        let ev = env(env_name);
        if ev.is_some() {
            let s = string(ev.unwrap()).trim();
            if s != "" {
                return s;
            }
        }
    }
    return string(Config.get("app.key", "")).trim();
}

public fn resolve_app_key() -> string {
    let key = configured_app_key();
    if key != "" {
        if is_production() and is_insecure_app_key(key) {
            throw "Security error: APP_KEY (or SPID3R_APP_KEY) must be at least 16 characters and not a default placeholder in production. Run 'spid3r magic key:generate'.";
        }
        return key;
    }
    if is_production() {
        throw "Security error: APP_KEY (or SPID3R_APP_KEY) is required in production. Run 'spid3r magic key:generate' or set APP_KEY in the environment.";
    }
    if _ephemeral_app_key["value"] == "" {
        _ephemeral_app_key["value"] = "ephemeral:" + hex_encode(random_bytes(32).unwrap());
        if not _ephemeral_app_key["warned"] {
            _ephemeral_app_key["warned"] = true;
            eprint(
                "[Spid3rWeb] Warning: APP_KEY is not set; using an ephemeral per-process key (non-production only).\n"
            );
        }
    }
    return _ephemeral_app_key["value"];
}

public fn assert_app_key_configured() {
    if is_production() {
        let _ = resolve_app_key();
    }
}

// framework/Foundation/ConfigValidator.matrix
// Runtime configuration validation across all bundled `config/*.matrix` namespaces.

import "framework/Foundation/Config";

const REQUIRED_NAMESPACES = [
"app",
"auth",
"cache",
"database",
"filesystems",
"http",
"logging",
"mail",
"queue",
"security",
"services",
"session",
"upload"
];

fn in_list(val: string, allowed) -> bool {
    for item in allowed {
        if string(item) == val {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

/// Validate all framework configuration namespaces and value bounds.
public fn validate_all_config() -> map {
    let errors = [];
    let checked = 0;

    for ns in REQUIRED_NAMESPACES {
        checked = checked + 1;
        if not Config.has(ns) or type_of(Config.get(ns, null)) != "map" {
            errors.push("missing or non-map namespace '{ns}'");
        }
    }

    let app_env = string(Config.get("app.env", "")).trim().lower();
    if app_env == "" {
        errors.push("app.env must not be empty");
    }
    if string(Config.get("app.paths.public", "")).trim() == "" {
        errors.push("app.paths.public must not be empty");
    }
    if string(Config.get("app.paths.views", "")).trim() == "" {
        errors.push("app.paths.views must not be empty");
    }
    if string(Config.get("app.paths.state_dir", "")).trim() == "" {
        errors.push("app.paths.state_dir must not be empty");
    }

    let hash_drv = string(Config.get("auth.hashing.driver", "")).trim().lower();
    if not in_list(hash_drv, ["argon2id", "argon2i", "argon2d", "argon2", "bcrypt"]) {
        errors.push("auth.hashing.driver '{hash_drv}' must be argon2id|argon2|bcrypt");
    }
    let bcrypt_rounds = int(Config.get("auth.hashing.bcrypt.rounds", 0));
    if bcrypt_rounds < 4 or bcrypt_rounds > 31 {
        errors.push("auth.hashing.bcrypt.rounds must be between 4 and 31");
    }

    let cache_drv = string(Config.get("cache.default", "")).trim().lower();
    if not in_list(cache_drv, ["memory", "file", "database"]) {
        errors.push("cache.default '{cache_drv}' must be memory|file|database");
    }

    let db_drv = string(Config.get("database.default", "")).trim().lower();
    if not in_list(db_drv, ["sqlite", "postgres", "mysql", "mariadb"]) {
        errors.push("database.default '{db_drv}' must be sqlite|postgres|mysql|mariadb");
    }

    if int(Config.get("upload.max_bytes", 0)) <= 0 {
        errors.push("upload.max_bytes must be > 0");
    }
    if int(Config.get("upload.max_files", 0)) <= 0 {
        errors.push("upload.max_files must be > 0");
    }
    if int(Config.get("http.drain_ms", -1)) < 0 {
        errors.push("http.drain_ms must be >= 0");
    }
    if int(Config.get("security.hsts.max_age", -1)) < 0 {
        errors.push("security.hsts.max_age must be >= 0");
    }

    let log_lvl = string(Config.get("logging.level", "")).trim().lower();
    if not in_list(log_lvl, ["debug", "info", "warn", "error"]) {
        errors.push("logging.level '{log_lvl}' must be debug|info|warn|error");
    }

    let queue_def = string(Config.get("queue.default", "")).trim().lower();
    if not in_list(queue_def, ["database", "sync"]) {
        errors.push("queue.default '{queue_def}' must be database|sync");
    }

    if not Config.has("filesystems.disks.local") or not Config.has("filesystems.disks.public") {
        errors.push("filesystems.disks must define both 'local' and 'public'");
    }

    return {
        "ok": errors.len() == 0,
        "errors": errors,
        "checked": checked
    };
}

// framework/Container/Container.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Dependency Injection Container
//
// A lightweight service container: named bindings resolved lazily through a
// factory, optionally memoized as singletons. This is the backbone the rest
// of the framework wires long-lived services through (cache, db, mail, ...).
//
// API:
//   Container.bind(key, factory)        factory(container) -> value, fresh every resolve
//   Container.singleton(key, factory)   factory(container) -> value, resolved once
//   Container.instance(key, value)      store a pre-built value (implicitly singleton)
//   Container.resolve(key) / .make(key) resolve a binding (throws when unbound)
//   Container.fresh(key)                re-run the factory, bypassing the singleton memo
//   Container.bound(key)                does this key have a binding or instance?
//   Container.resolved(key)             is a singleton already memoized?
//   Container.alias(alias, key)         alias resolves through to key (chains allowed)
//   Container.extend(key, wrapper)      wrapper(container, previous_factory) -> value
//   Container.forget(key)               drop the binding and any memoized instance
//   Container.flush()                   drop everything
//
// Factories receive the container itself, so nested dependencies resolve
// inside the factory:
//   Container.singleton("db", fn(c) { return new DatabaseManager(); });
//
// Design notes:
//   - Keys are plain strings; there is no class reflection in Spid3r, so the
//     container is a named-service registry rather than an auto-wirer.
//   - Alias chains are followed up to 32 hops; a cyclic alias configuration
//     surfaces as "nothing is bound" instead of hanging.
//   - `extend` wraps the previous factory, preserving the shared flag; it
//     invalidates any memoized singleton for that key.

/// Named dependency injection service container with bindings, singletons, aliases, and decorators.
public class ServiceContainer {
    private let bindings;  // key -> map("factory", fn, "shared", bool)
    private let instances;  // key -> memoized singleton value
    private let aliases;  // alias -> target key

    fn new () {
        self.bindings = {
        };
        self.instances = {
        };
        self.aliases = {
        };
    }

    // Follow an alias chain to its final key. A cycle is cut off after 32
    // hops; resolve() then reports the key as unbound, which is the honest
    // answer for a misconfigured alias loop.
    fn alias_for(self, key: string) -> string {
        let current = key;
        let steps = 0;
        while self.aliases.has(current) and steps < 32 {
            current = self.aliases[current];
            steps += 1;
        }
        return current;
    }

    public fn bind(self, key: string, factory: any) -> ServiceContainer {
        self.bindings[key] = {
            "factory": factory, "shared": false
        };
        return self;
    }

    public fn singleton(self, key: string, factory: any) -> ServiceContainer {
        self.bindings[key] = {
            "factory": factory, "shared": true
        };
        return self;
    }

    // Store a pre-built value. Returns the value for ergonomic chaining:
    //   let store = Container.instance("store", new MemoryCacheStore());
    public fn instance(self, key: string, value: any) -> any {
        self.instances[key] = value;
        return value;
    }

    public fn alias(self, alias: string, key: string) -> ServiceContainer {
        self.aliases[alias] = key;
        return self;
    }

    public fn bound(self, key: string) -> bool {
        let k = self.alias_for(key);
        return self.bindings.has(k) or self.instances.has(k);
    }

    public fn resolved(self, key: string) -> bool {
        let k = self.alias_for(key);
        return self.instances.has(k);
    }

    public fn resolve(self, key: string) -> any {
        let k = self.alias_for(key);
        if self.instances.has(k) {
            return self.instances[k];
        }
        if not self.bindings.has(k) {
            throw "Container: nothing is bound for '" + k + "'";
        }
        let b = self.bindings[k];
        let f = b["factory"];
        let obj = f(self);
        if b["shared"] {
            self.instances[k] = obj;
        }
        return obj;
    }

    // Laravel-style alias for resolve().
    public fn make(self, key: string) -> any {
        return self.resolve(key);
    }

    // Re-run the factory without touching the singleton memo.
    public fn fresh(self, key: string) -> any {
        let k = self.alias_for(key);
        if not self.bindings.has(k) {
            throw "Container: nothing is bound for '" + k + "'";
        }
        let b = self.bindings[k];
        let f = b["factory"];
        return f(self);
    }

    // Decorate an existing binding. The wrapper receives the container and
    // the previous factory; whatever it returns becomes the new factory.
    public fn extend(self, key: string, wrapper: any) -> ServiceContainer {
        let k = self.alias_for(key);
        if not self.bindings.has(k) {
            throw "Container: cannot extend an unbound key '" + k + "'";
        }
        let b = self.bindings[k];
        let previous = b["factory"];
        b["factory"] = fn(c) {
            return wrapper(c, previous);
        };
        // A changed factory invalidates any memoized singleton.
        if self.instances.has(k) {
            self.instances.remove(k);
        }
        return self;
    }

    public fn forget(self, key: string) -> ServiceContainer {
        let k = self.alias_for(key);
        if self.bindings.has(k) {
            self.bindings.remove(k);
        }
        if self.instances.has(k) {
            self.instances.remove(k);
        }
        return self;
    }

    public fn flush(self) -> ServiceContainer {
        self.bindings = {
        };
        self.instances = {
        };
        self.aliases = {
        };
        return self;
    }
}

// Global container facade singleton — the application-wide service registry.
// Import this module and use `Container.xxx(...)` directly; tests and tools
// that need isolation can `new ServiceContainer()` instead.
public let Container = new ServiceContainer();

// framework/Env.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Environment File Loader (.env)
//
// Loads KEY=VALUE pairs from a .env file at project root. Real OS environment
// variables ALWAYS win — .env only fills in values that are unset/empty.
// Called automatically before the DB singleton is constructed (see DB.matrix),
// so driver selection and connection settings can be controlled per-project
// without exporting variables manually.
//
// Supported syntax:
//   KEY=value
//   KEY="quoted value"        (quotes are stripped)
//   export KEY=value          (shell-style prefix is stripped)
//   # comment                 (blank lines and # comments are skipped)

/// Load KEY=VALUE pairs from `.env` without overwriting existing OS environment variables.
public fn load_env_file(path: string = ".env") -> bool {
    let exists = false;
    try {
        exists = file_exists(path);
    } catch (_e) {
        // Under security.basedir, .env may sit outside the app directory allowlist;
        // production environments pass config via OS environment variables instead.
        return false;
    }
    if not exists {
        return false;
    }
    let res = null;
    try {
        res = read_file(path);
    } catch (_e) {
        return false;
    }
    if res == null or res.is_err() {
        return false;
    }
    let content = res.unwrap();

    for raw_line in content.split("\n") {
        let line = raw_line.trim();
        if line.len() == 0 or line.starts_with("#") {
            continue;
        }
        // Optional shell-style "export " prefix
        if line.starts_with("export ") {
            line = line.slice(7, line.len()).trim();
        }

        let eq = line.index_of("=");
        if eq <= 0 {
            continue;
        }
        let key = line.slice(0, eq).trim();
        let mut_val = line.slice(eq + 1, line.len()).trim();

        // Strip one pair of surrounding quotes
        if mut_val.len() >= 2 {
            if (mut_val.starts_with("\"") and mut_val.ends_with("\"")) or (
                mut_val.starts_with("'") and mut_val.ends_with("'")
            ) {
                mut_val = mut_val.slice(1, mut_val.len() - 1);
            }
        }

        if key.len() == 0 {
            continue;
        }

        // Precedence: real OS environment always wins over .env
        let existing = env(key);
        if existing.is_some() and string(existing.unwrap()) != "" {
            continue;
        }
        env_set(key, mut_val);
    }
    return true;
}

FILE: framework/Foundation/RuntimeVersion.matrix
================================================
// framework/Foundation/RuntimeVersion.matrix
// Framework & minimum Spid3r runtime version policy and boot verification.

/// Current Spid3rWeb framework release version.
public const SPID3RWEB_VERSION = "1.1.1";

/// Minimum required Spid3r language runtime version.
public const MIN_SPID3R_VERSION = "1.1.1";

/// Parse the first X.Y.Z semver triple found in runtime or manifest text.
public fn parse_semver(ver_text: string) -> list {
    let normalized = string(ver_text)
    .replace("\r", " ")
    .replace("\n", " ")
    .replace("\t", " ")
    .trim();
    let nums = [];
    for tok in normalized.split(" ") {
        let t = tok.trim();
        if t.starts_with("v") and t.len() > 1 {
            t = t.slice(1, t.len());
        }
        let segs = t.split(".");
        if segs.len() >= 3 {
            try {
                let maj = int(segs[0]);
                let min = int(segs[1]);
                let pat_str = segs[2].split("-")[0].split("+")[0];
                let pat = int(pat_str);
                nums = [maj, min, pat];
                break;
            } catch (_e) {
            }
        }
    }
    return nums;
}

/// Return true when a parsed [major, minor, patch] triple meets the minimum version.
public fn version_at_least(nums: list, req_maj: int, req_min: int, req_pat: int) -> bool {
    if nums.len() < 3 {
        return false;
    }
    if int(nums[0]) != req_maj {
        return int(nums[0]) > req_maj;
    }
    if int(nums[1]) != req_min {
        return int(nums[1]) > req_min;
    }
    return int(nums[2]) >= req_pat;
}

fn resolve_runtime_bin(explicit_bin: string = "") -> string {
    if explicit_bin.trim() != "" {
        return explicit_bin.trim();
    }
    for env_key in ["SPID3R_BIN", "SPID3R_EXE"] {
        let v = env(env_key).unwrap_or("").trim();
        if v != "" {
            return v;
        }
    }
    try {
        let cur = exe_path();
        if cur.is_ok() {
            let p = string(cur.unwrap()).trim();
            let base = p.replace("\\", "/").split("/").pop().lower();
            if base == "spid3r" or base == "spid3r.exe" or base == "spid3r-self" {
                return p;
            }
        }
    } catch (_e) {
    }
    return "spid3r";
}

/// Verify the active Spid3r runtime meets MIN_SPID3R_VERSION when queryable.
public fn check_min_runtime_version(spid3r_bin: string = "") -> map {
    let bin = resolve_runtime_bin(spid3r_bin);
    let parsed = [];
    let raw_out = "";
    try {
        let res = exec_capture(bin, ["--version"], null);
        if res.is_ok() {
            let info = res.unwrap();
            if int(info["code"]) == 0 {
                raw_out = string(info["stdout"]).trim();
                parsed = parse_semver(raw_out);
            }
        }
    } catch (_e) {
        return {
            "checked": false,
            "ok": true,
            "version": "",
            "required": MIN_SPID3R_VERSION
        };
    }
    if parsed.len() < 3 {
        return {
            "checked": false,
            "ok": true,
            "version": raw_out,
            "required": MIN_SPID3R_VERSION
        };
    }
    let ver_str = string(parsed[0]) + "." + string(parsed[1]) + "." + string(parsed[2]);
    let ok = version_at_least(parsed, 1, 1, 1);
    return {
        "checked": true,
        "ok": ok,
        "version": ver_str,
        "required": MIN_SPID3R_VERSION
    };
}

/// Fail closed at boot if an installed Spid3r runtime is older than MIN_SPID3R_VERSION.
public fn assert_min_runtime_version(spid3r_bin: string = "") {
    let check = check_min_runtime_version(spid3r_bin);
    if check["checked"] == true and check["ok"] != true {
        let found = string(check["version"]);
        let req = string(check["required"]);
        throw "Runtime version error: Spid3r {found} is older than required minimum {req}.";
    }
}

// framework/Foundation/Server.matrix
// HTTP & TLS TCP listener bootstrap extracted from Application.matrix.

import "http";
import "framework/Foundation/Config";

public fn resolve_bind_host(host: string) -> string {
    let bind_host = string(host).trim();
    if bind_host != "" {
        return bind_host;
    }
    let env_host = env("SPID3R_HOST").unwrap_or("").trim();
    if env_host != "" {
        return env_host;
    }
    return "127.0.0.1";
}

public fn build_server_opts(server_opts: any, is_tls: bool) -> map {
    let drain_ms = int(Config.get("http.drain_ms", 2_000));
    let opts = {
        "access_log": false, "keep_alive": true, "drain_ms": drain_ms
    };
    let max_body = int(Config.get("http.max_body_bytes", 0));
    if max_body > 0 {
        opts["max_body_bytes"] = max_body;
    }
    let max_hdrs = int(Config.get("http.max_headers", 0));
    if max_hdrs > 0 {
        opts["max_headers"] = max_hdrs;
    }
    let timeout_ms = int(Config.get("http.timeout_ms", 0));
    if timeout_ms > 0 {
        opts["timeout_ms"] = timeout_ms;
    }
    let keepalive_ms = int(Config.get("http.keepalive_ms", 0));
    if keepalive_ms > 0 {
        opts["idle_timeout_ms"] = keepalive_ms;
    }
    if is_tls {
        opts["tls"] = true;
    }
    if server_opts != null and type_of(server_opts) == "map" {
        for (k, v) in server_opts {
            opts[string(k)] = v;
        }
    }
    return opts;
}

/// Bind and run an HTTP/1.1 TCP listener for `app` with configured limits and graceful drain.
public fn listen_http(app: any, port: int = 8000, host: string = "", server_opts: any = null) {
    app.check_production_ready();
    let bind_host = resolve_bind_host(host);
    app.boot();
    let r = tcp_listen("{bind_host}:{port}");
    if r.is_err() {
        throw r.unwrap_err();
    }
    let listener = r.unwrap();
    let real_addr = tcp_local_addr(listener).unwrap();
    let app_ref = app;
    let opts = build_server_opts(server_opts, false);
    println("[Spid3rWeb] Server ({app.env}) running on http://{real_addr}");
    println("[Spid3rWeb] Press Ctrl+C to stop the server\n");

    let _count = serve_listener(listener, fn(raw_req) {
        return app_ref.handle_request(raw_req);
    }, opts, -1);
}

/// Bind and run a native TLS HTTPS listener for `app` using `cert_path` and `key_path`.
public fn listen_https(
    app: any,
    port: int,
    cert_path: string,
    key_path: string,
    host: string = "",
    server_opts: any = null
) {
    app.check_production_ready();
    let bind_host = resolve_bind_host(host);
    app.boot();
    let r = tls_listen(cert_path, key_path, "{bind_host}:{port}");
    if r.is_err() {
        throw r.unwrap_err();
    }
    let listener = r.unwrap();
    let real_addr = tcp_local_addr(listener).unwrap();
    let app_ref = app;
    let opts = build_server_opts(server_opts, true);
    println("[Spid3rWeb] HTTPS server running on https://{real_addr}");
    println("[Spid3rWeb] Press Ctrl+C to stop the server\n");

    let _count = serve_listener(listener, fn(raw_req) {
        return app_ref.handle_request(raw_req);
    }, opts, -1);
}

// framework/Providers/ServiceProvider.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Service Provider Base Class
//
// Service providers are the framework's organized bootstrap points. Each
// provider has two phases, run once per process:
//
//   register(container) â€” bind services into the DI Container. MUST be
//     side-effect free: no database, no cache, no I/O. Only factories.
//     This phase runs at Application.register(provider) time.
//
//   boot(container) â€” initialize the provider after ALL providers have
//     registered. Safe to touch the database, config, cache, routes, and
//     the Event bus. Runs at Application.boot() (called automatically by
//     Application.listen(), and lazily on the first handled request).
//
// Every provider MUST extend this class â€” Application calls both methods
// directly, and this base guarantees they exist:
//
//   import "framework/Foundation/ServiceProvider";
//   import "framework/Foundation/Container";
//
//   class MailServiceProvider extends ServiceProvider {
//       public fn register(self, container) {
//           container.singleton("mailer", fn(c) {
//               return new Mailer(Config.get("mail.driver", "log"));
//           });
//       }
//       public fn boot(self, container) {
//           Event.listen("user.registered", fn(payload, name) {
//               container.make("mailer").send(...);
//           });
//       }
//   }
//
//   let app = new Application();
//   app.register(new MailServiceProvider());
//   app.listen();   // boots all registered providers, then serves

/// Abstract lifecycle base class for modular service registration (`register`) and initialization (`boot`).
public class ServiceProvider {
    // Bind services into the container. Override to add bindings.
    public fn register(self, _container: any) {
        let _ = self;
        return null;
    }

    // Post-registration initialization. Override for event listeners,
    // route registration, or anything needing other services.
    public fn boot(self, _container: any) {
        let _ = self;
        return null;
    }
}

// framework/Http/Controller.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Base Controller — the "C" of the MVC triad.
// Every application controller extends this class and inherits fluent
// response/view helpers plus shared view context (auth state, CSRF token).

import "framework/Http/Response";
import "framework/View/BD";
import "framework/Auth/Auth";

/// Base MVC controller providing JSON/view helpers, validation shortcuts, and shared template context.
public class Controller {
    fn new () {
        let _ = self;
    }

    public fn json(self, data: any, status: int = 200) -> Response {
        let _ = self;
        return json(data, status);
    }

    public fn ok(self, data: any) -> Response {
        let _ = self;
        return json(data, 200);
    }

    public fn created(self, data: any) -> Response {
        let _ = self;
        return json(data, 201);
    }

    public fn no_content(self) -> Response {
        let _ = self;
        return response("", 204);
    }

    public fn view(self, view_name: string, data: any = null) -> Response {
        let _ = self;
        return view(view_name, data ?? {
        });
    }

    // Preferred way to render a page: shared data (auth state, user, csrf_token)
    // is merged automatically. Controllers just write:
    //   return self.render(req, "posts.index", {"posts": posts});
    public fn render(self, req: any, view_name: string, data: any = null) -> Response {
        return self.view(view_name, self.shared(data ?? {
        }, req));
    }

    // Raw body response with an explicit content type — for XML feeds,
    // plain text, downloads.
    public fn respond(self, body: string, content_type: string, status: int = 200) -> Response {
        let _ = self;
        let res = response(body, status);
        res.header("content-type", content_type);
        return res;
    }

    public fn redirect(self, url: string, status: int = 302) -> Response {
        let _ = self;
        return redirect(url, status);
    }

    public fn back(self, req: any, fallback: string = "/") -> Response {
        let _ = self;
        let referer = "";
        if req != null {
            referer = string(req.header("referer", fallback));
        }
        if referer == "" {
            referer = fallback;
        }
        return redirect(referer, 302);
    }

    public fn validate(self, req: any, rules: map) -> any {
        let _ = self;
        return req.validate(rules);
    }

    public fn validate_or_fail(self, req: any, rules: map) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        return req.validate_or_fail(rules);
    }

    // Prefer the request-scoped AuthManager (safe under concurrent requests).
    public fn auth(self, req: any = null) -> any {
        let _ = self;
        return req ?. auth ?? Auth;
    }

    // Merges caller data with auth-state variables (`is_auth`, `user`, `user_name`,
    // `csrf_token`, `is_admin`).
    public fn shared(self, data: any = null, req: any = null) -> map {
        let out = data ?? {
        };
        let auth = self.auth(req);
        out["is_auth"] = auth.check();

        let user_name = "";
        let user = auth.user();
        out["user"] = user;
        if user != null and user.has("name") {
            user_name = string(user["name"]);
        }
        out["user_name"] = user_name;
        out["csrf_token"] = auth.csrf();

        out["is_admin"] = false;
        if user != null and user.has("is_admin") and user["is_admin"] != null {
            let adm = string(user["is_admin"]).lower();
            if adm == "1" or adm == "true" {
                out["is_admin"] = true;
            }
        }
        return out;
    }
}

// framework/Http/CookieJar.matrix
// RFC 6265 Cookie Validation, Secure-Flag Resolution & Jar Management

import "framework/Foundation/Config";

const COOKIE_NAME_CHARS = "!#$%&'*+-.^_`|~0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz";

fn has_cookie_ctl(value: string) -> bool {
    let i = 0;
    while i < 32 {
        if value.contains(chr(i)) {
            return true;
        }
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return value.contains(chr(127));
}

public fn is_valid_cookie_name(name: string) -> bool {
    let s = string(name);
    if s.len() == 0 {
        return false;
    }
    for ch in s.split("") {
        if not COOKIE_NAME_CHARS.contains(ch) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

public fn is_valid_cookie_value(value: string) -> bool {
    let s = string(value);
    if has_cookie_ctl(s) or s.contains(";") {
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

public fn is_valid_cookie_path(path: string) -> bool {
    let s = string(path);
    if s == "" or not s.starts_with("/") {
        return false;
    }
    return not has_cookie_ctl(s) and not s.contains(";");
}

// Single canonical Secure flag resolver:
// explicit arg > HTTPS request context > config session.secure > SPID3R_COOKIE_SECURE=1 > production.
public fn resolve_cookie_secure(explicit: any = null, is_https: bool = false) -> bool {
    if explicit != null {
        return explicit == true or explicit == 1 or string(explicit) == "1" or string(explicit) == "true";
    }
    if is_https {
        return true;
    }
    if Config.get("session.secure", false) == true {
        return true;
    }
    if env("SPID3R_COOKIE_SECURE").unwrap_or("0").trim() == "1" {
        return true;
    }
    return is_production();
}

public fn format_cookie_header(
    name: string,
    value: string,
    path: string = "/",
    max_age: int = 86_400,
    secure = null,
    is_https: bool = false
) -> string {
    let cname = string(name);
    let cval = string(value);
    let cpath = string(path);
    if not is_valid_cookie_name(cname) {
        throw "CookieJar: invalid cookie name '{cname}' (RFC 6265 token required)";
    }
    if not is_valid_cookie_value(cval) {
        throw "CookieJar: illegal character (CTL or ';') in cookie value for '{cname}'";
    }
    if not is_valid_cookie_path(cpath) {
        throw "CookieJar: invalid cookie path '{cpath}'";
    }
    let secure_flag = resolve_cookie_secure(secure, is_https);
    let cookie_str = "{cname}={cval}; Path={cpath}; Max-Age={max_age}";
    if Config.get("session.httponly", true) == true {
        cookie_str = "{cookie_str}; HttpOnly";
    }
    let same_site = Config.get("session.same_site", "Lax");
    cookie_str = "{cookie_str}; SameSite={same_site}";
    if secure_flag {
        cookie_str = "{cookie_str}; Secure";
    }
    return cookie_str;
}

/// RFC 6265 cookie jar validating names/values/paths and unifying Secure/SameSite/HttpOnly defaults.
public class CookieJar {
    private let items;
    private let is_https;

    fn new (is_https: bool = false) {
        self.items = [];
        self.is_https = is_https;
    }

    public fn set_https(self, flag: bool) -> CookieJar {
        self.is_https = flag;
        return self;
    }

    public fn queue(
        self,
        name: string,
        value: string,
        path: string = "/",
        max_age: int = 86_400,
        secure: any = null
    ) -> string {
        let hdr = format_cookie_header(name, value, path, max_age, secure, self.is_https);
        self.items.push(hdr);
        return hdr;
    }

    public fn queue_signed(
        self,
        name: string,
        value: string,
        path: string = "/",
        max_age: int = 86_400,
        secure: any = null
    ) -> string {
        let sig = hmac_sha256(resolve_app_key(), "{name}={value}");
        return self.queue(name, "{value}.{sig}", path, max_age, secure);
    }

    public fn forget(self, name: string, path: string = "/") -> string {
        return self.queue(name, "", path, 0, null);
    }

    public fn all(self) -> list {
        return self.items;
    }

    public fn count(self) -> int {
        return self.items.len();
    }

    public fn clear(self) {
        self.items = [];
    }
}

// framework/Http/Csrf.matrix
// Same-Origin & per-session CSRF token validation helpers.

// Compare an Origin header against the request Host, normalizing scheme
// and default ports. Returns false for anything malformed or foreign.
/// Layer 1 CSRF defense: verify an Origin header matches the request Host and scheme port.
public fn is_same_origin(req: any, origin: string) -> bool {
    let o = string(origin).trim().lower();
    let default_port = "";
    if o.starts_with("https://") {
        default_port = ":443";
        o = o.slice(8, o.len());
    } elif o.starts_with("http://") {
        default_port = ":80";
        o = o.slice(7, o.len());
    } else {
        return false;
    }
    if o.contains("/") {
        o = o.split("/")[0];
    }
    if default_port != "" and o.ends_with(default_port) {
        o = o.slice(0, o.len() - default_port.len());
    }
    let host = string(req.host()).trim().lower();
    if host == "" {
        return false;
    }
    if default_port != "" and host.ends_with(default_port) {
        host = host.slice(0, host.len() - default_port.len());
    }
    return host == o;
}

// Layer 2: cookie-authenticated form submissions without an Origin header must
// present the per-session CSRF token issued at login. Bearer-token API calls,
// logout, /api/* paths, and legacy sessions without tokens are exempt.
/// Layer 2 CSRF defense: require per-session `_csrf` token on cookie-authenticated mutations.
public fn csrf_valid(req: any) -> bool {
    let m = req.method;
    if m != "POST" and m != "PUT" and m != "PATCH" and m != "DELETE" {
        return true;
    }
    if req.header("origin", "") != "" {
        return true;
    }
    if req.session_source != "cookie" {
        return true;
    }
    if req.path == "/logout" or req.path.starts_with("/api/") {
        return true;
    }
    let expected = req.auth ?. csrf_token ?? "";
    if expected == "" {
        return true;
    }
    let provided = req.input("_csrf", "");
    return string(provided) == expected;
}

// framework/Http/ErrorRenderer.matrix
// Spid3rWeb HTTP Error Pages — JSON-aware 404/500 rendering.
// Extracted from Application.matrix so the kernel stays focused on
// request coordination; output is byte-identical to the original.

import "framework/Http/Response";
import "framework/View/BD";
import "framework/Http/HttpException";

/// Renders JSON-aware HTTP error responses (404, 419 CSRF, 4xx, 500) without leaking stack traces.
public class ErrorRenderer {
    // Typed HTTP exceptions (HttpException.matrix): correct status, JSON
    // for API clients, a status-aware HTML card otherwise. The client never
    // sees stack traces; only the message and optional field errors.
    public static fn http_error(req: any, e: map) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        let status = int(e["status"]);
        let message = string(e["message"]);
        if req.path.starts_with("/api/") or req.header("accept", "").contains("application/json") {
            let body = {
                "error": message, "status": status
            };
            if e.has("errors") and e["errors"] != null {
                body["errors"] = e["errors"];
            }
            return json(body, status).to_http();
        }
        let errors_html = "";
        if e.has("errors") and e["errors"] != null {
            errors_html = errors_html + "<ul class='errors'>";
            if type_of(e["errors"]) == "map" {
                for (field, val) in e["errors"] {
                    let fk = html_escape(string(field));
                    let fv = html_escape(string(val));
                    errors_html = "{errors_html}<li><strong>{fk}</strong>: {fv}</li>";
                }
            } else {
                let ev = html_escape(string(e["errors"]));
                errors_html = "{errors_html}<li>{ev}</li>";
            }
            errors_html = errors_html + "</ul>";
        }
        let msg_esc = html_escape(message);
        let html_content = "<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang='en'>
<head><meta charset='utf-8'><title>{status} - Spid3rWeb</title>
<style>
  body { font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif; background: #0f172a; color: #f8fafc; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; height: 100vh; margin: 0; }
  .card { background: #1e293b; padding: 2.5rem 3rem; border-radius: 1rem; border: 1px solid #334155; text-align: center; max-width: 500px; box-shadow: 0 20px 25px -5px rgba(0,0,0,0.5); }
  h1 { font-size: 4rem; margin: 0; color: #38bdf8; font-weight: 800; }
  p { color: #94a3b8; font-size: 1.1rem; margin: 1rem 0 2rem; }
  a { background: #38bdf8; color: #0f172a; text-decoration: none; padding: 0.75rem 1.5rem; border-radius: 0.5rem; font-weight: 600; }
  .errors { text-align: left; color: #fca5a5; margin: 0 0 1.5rem; padding-left: 1.25rem; }
</style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class='card'>
    <h1>{status}</h1>
    <p>{msg_esc}</p>
    {errors_html}
    <a href='/'>Return to Home</a>
  </div>
</body>
</html>"

        ;
        return html(html_content, status).to_http();
    }

    // 404: JSON payload for /api/* paths, dark-theme card page otherwise.
    public static fn not_found(req: any) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        if req.path.starts_with("/api/") {
            return json({
                "error": "Not Found",
                "message": "The requested endpoint was not found."
            }, 404).to_http();
        }
        let m_esc = html_escape(req.method);
        let p_esc = html_escape(req.path);
        let html_content = "<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang='en'>
<head><meta charset='utf-8'><title>404 Not Found - Spid3rWeb</title>
<style>
  body { font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif; background: #0f172a; color: #f8fafc; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; height: 100vh; margin: 0; }
  .card { background: #1e293b; padding: 2.5rem 3rem; border-radius: 1rem; border: 1px solid #334155; text-align: center; max-width: 500px; box-shadow: 0 20px 25px -5px rgba(0,0,0,0.5); }
  h1 { font-size: 4rem; margin: 0; color: #38bdf8; font-weight: 800; }
  p { color: #94a3b8; font-size: 1.1rem; margin: 1rem 0 2rem; }
  a { background: #38bdf8; color: #0f172a; text-decoration: none; padding: 0.75rem 1.5rem; border-radius: 0.5rem; font-weight: 600; }
</style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class='card'>
    <h1>404</h1>
    <h2>Route Not Found</h2>
    <p>No route defined matching <code>{m_esc} {p_esc}</code></p>
    <a href='/'>Return to Home</a>
  </div>
</body>
</html>"

        ;
        return html(html_content, 404).to_http();
    }

    // 419: CSRF token missing/invalid on a cookie-session form post.
    public static fn csrf_timeout(req: any) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        if req.path.starts_with("/api/") {
            return json({
                "error": "CSRF Token Mismatch",
                "message": "Missing or invalid CSRF token."
            }, 419).to_http();
        }
        return html(
            "<!DOCTYPE html><html lang='en'><head><meta charset='utf-8'><title>419 Page Expired - Spid3rWeb</title></head><body><h1>419 — Page Expired</h1><p>Your form session token was missing or invalid. Please go back, refresh the page, and try again.</p></body></html>",
            419
        ).to_http();
    }

    // 500: full exception detail stays server-side (println log); clients
    // get a generic message so driver errors can never leak SQL fragments
    // or schema details.
    public static fn server_error(req: any, exception: any) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        let err_str = string(exception);
        println("[Spid3rWeb] 500 error on {req.method} {req.path}: {err_str}");
        if req.path.starts_with("/api/") {
            return json({
                "error": "Internal Server Error",
                "message": "An unexpected error occurred."
            }, 500).to_http();
        }
        let m_esc = html_escape(req.method);
        let p_esc = html_escape(req.path);
        let html_content = "<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang='en'>
<head><meta charset='utf-8'><title>500 Error - Spid3rWeb</title>
<style>
  body { font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif; background: #0b0f17; color: #f1f5f9; padding: 3rem; }
  .box { background: #1e293b; border-left: 6px solid #ef4444; border-radius: 8px; padding: 2rem; }
  h1 { color: #ef4444; font-size: 1.8rem; margin: 0 0 1rem; }
  p { color: #94a3b8; }
</style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class='box'>
    <h1>Spid3rWeb Internal Server Error</h1>
    <p><strong>Route:</strong> <code>{m_esc} {p_esc}</code></p>
    <p>Something went wrong while processing your request. Check the server logs for details.</p>
    <a href='/' style='color:#38bdf8;'>Return to Home</a>
  </div>
</body>
</html>"

        ;
        return html(html_content, 500).to_http();
    }
}

// framework/HttpException.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Typed HTTP Exceptions
//
// Handlers, middleware, and resources can abort request processing with a
// meaningful status code instead of a generic 500:
//
//   import "framework/Http/HttpException";
//
//   let post = DB.table("posts").find(id);
//   if post == null {
//       throw not_found("Post not found.");
//   }
//   throw validation_failed(map("email", "The email has already been taken."));
//
// Application.handle_request catches these centrally and renders them
// through ErrorRenderer.http_error: JSON payloads for /api/* paths and
// Accept: application/json clients, HTML error cards otherwise. Anything
// thrown that is NOT an http exception still becomes a 500 with a generic
// message â€” internal details never leak.
//
// Exceptions are plain maps carrying a marker key (Spid3r's type_of cannot
// tell class instances apart), built exclusively by the helpers below.

// The one true constructor â€” helpers feed it; is_http_exception() checks it.
public fn make_http_exception(status: int, message: string, errors: any = null) -> map {
    let e = {
        "__spid3rweb_exception": true,
        "status": status,
        "message": message
    };
    if errors != null {
        e["errors"] = errors;
    }
    return e;
}

public fn is_http_exception(e: any) -> bool {
    return type_of(e) == "map" and e.has("__spid3rweb_exception") and e["__spid3rweb_exception"] == true;
}

// Immediately abort request processing with an HTTP status code and message.
/// Throw a typed HTTP exception map with status code and optional validation error map.
public fn abort_http(status: int, message: string = "", errors: any = null) {
    let msg = message;
    if msg == "" {
        msg = match (status) {
            400 => "Bad Request.",
            401 => "Authentication required.",
            403 => "This action is unauthorized.",
            404 => "Not Found.",
            413 => "Payload Too Large.",
            415 => "Unsupported Media Type.",
            422 => "The given data was invalid.",
            429 => "Too Many Requests.",
            _ => "HTTP Error {status}"
        };
    }
    throw make_http_exception(status, msg, errors);
}

// 400 â€” malformed request syntax.
public fn bad_request(message: string = "Bad Request.") -> map {
    return make_http_exception(400, message);
}

// 401 — unauthenticated (who are you?).
public fn unauthorized(message: string = "Authentication required.") -> map {
    return make_http_exception(401, message);
}

// 403 — authenticated but not allowed (I know who you are; no).
public fn forbidden(message: string = "This action is unauthorized.") -> map {
    return make_http_exception(403, message);
}

// 404 — resource does not exist.
public fn not_found(message: string = "Not Found.") -> map {
    return make_http_exception(404, message);
}

// 422 — request parsed but semantically invalid; `errors` maps field names
// to their messages (validated with the framework Validator or by hand).
public fn validation_failed(errors: any, message: string = "The given data was invalid.") -> map {
    return make_http_exception(422, message, errors);
}

// 429 — rate limit exceeded.
public fn too_many_requests(message: string = "Too Many Requests.") -> map {
    return make_http_exception(429, message);
}
