// framework/Middleware.matrix
// Spid3rWeb True Onion Middleware Pipeline & Built-in Guards
//
// Supports THREE middleware styles seamlessly:
//   1. Closure / Function Onion Middleware (Express / Hono / Koa style):
//      `app.use((req, next) => { let res = next(req); res.header("x-foo", "bar"); return res; });`
//   2. Class Onion Middleware (2-argument `handle(self, req, next)`):
//      Calls `let res = next(req);` to invoke downstream pipeline and mutate/inspect response.
//   3. Legacy Pre-Filter Class Middleware (1-argument `handle(self, req)`):
//      Returns `null` to proceed to the next middleware, or returns a `Response`
//      to short-circuit the pipeline.

import "framework/Http/Response";
import "framework/Auth/Auth";
import "framework/Cache/Cache";
import "framework/Foundation/Config";

public class Pipeline {
    public let passable;
    public let pipes;

    fn new () {
        self.passable = null;
        self.pipes = [];
    }

    public fn send(self, passable: any) -> Pipeline {
        self.passable = passable;
        return self;
    }

    public fn through(self, pipes_list: list) -> Pipeline {
        self.pipes = pipes_list;
        return self;
    }

    // Ensure any handler/middleware return value is wrapped in a Response object
    // when after-middleware needs `.header(...)` or `.status_code`.
    fn ensure_response_obj(self, val: any) -> any {
        let _ = self;
        if val == null {
            return null;
        }
        let t = type_of(val);
        if t == "instance" {
            return val;
        }
        if t == "map" {
            if val.has("status") and val.has("headers") and val.has("body") {
                let r = new Response(string(val["body"]), int(val["status"]));
                if type_of(val["headers"]) == "list" {
                    r.headers = {
                    };
                    for entry in val["headers"] {
                        if type_of(entry) == "map" and entry.has("name") and entry.has("value") {
                            let hn = string(entry["name"]).lower();
                            let hv = string(entry["value"]);
                            if hn == "set-cookie" {
                                r.cookies.push(hv);
                            } else {
                                r.headers[hn] = hv;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    r.headers = val["headers"];
                }
                return r;
            }
            return json(val, 200);
        }
        if t == "list" {
            return json(val, 200);
        }
        if t == "string" {
            return html(val, 200);
        }
        return response(string(val), 200);
    }

    fn run_step(self, index: int, current_req, destination) {
        if self.pipes == null or index >= self.pipes.len() {
            return destination(current_req);
        }

        let pipe = self.pipes[index];
        let me = self;
        let next_called = {
            "flag": false, "res": null
        };

        let next_fn = fn(next_req = null) {
            next_called["flag"] = true;
            let r_to_pass = current_req;
            if next_req != null {
                r_to_pass = next_req;
            }
            let down_res = me.run_step(index + 1, r_to_pass, destination);
            let wrapped = me.ensure_response_obj(down_res);
            next_called["res"] = wrapped;
            return wrapped;
        };

        // Attach `req.next` so even 1-arg middleware can call `req.next()` if desired
        let res = null;
        let pt = type_of(pipe);

        if pt == "function" {
            // Try 2-arg `(req, next) => ...` first, fallback to 1-arg `req => ...`
            res = pipe(current_req, next_fn);
        } else {
            // Instance with `.handle` â€” try 2-arg `.handle(req, next)` if supported,
            // otherwise fallback to 1-arg `.handle(req)`.
            let handled_2arg = false;
            try {
                res = pipe.handle(current_req, next_fn);
                handled_2arg = true;
            } catch (err) {
                let err_msg = string(err);
                if err_msg.contains("expected 2 arguments, got 3") or err_msg.contains("expected 1 arguments, got 2") or err_msg.contains("arity") or err_msg.contains("arguments") {
                    handled_2arg = false;
                } else {
                    throw err;
                }
            }
            if not handled_2arg {
                res = pipe.handle(current_req);
            }
        }

        // If the middleware returned a non-null response directly, use it
        if res != null {
            return res;
        }
        // If the middleware called `next(req)` and returned null, return the downstream response
        if next_called["flag"] {
            return next_called["res"];
        }
        // Legacy 1-arg middleware returned `null` without calling `next` -> proceed to next step!
        return self.run_step(index + 1, current_req, destination);
    }

    public fn then(self, destination: any) -> any {
        return self.run_step(0, self.passable, destination);
    }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Built-in Framework Middleware
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Attach Cross-Origin Resource Sharing headers and short-circuit `OPTIONS` preflight requests.
public class CorsMiddleware {
    public let allow_origin;
    public let allow_methods;
    public let allow_headers;

    fn new (
        origin: string = "*",
        methods: string = "GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, PATCH, OPTIONS",
        headers: string = "content-type, authorization, x-requested-with, x-csrf-token"
    ) {
        self.allow_origin = origin;
        self.allow_methods = methods;
        self.allow_headers = headers;
    }

    public fn handle(self, req: any, next: any = null) -> any {
        if req.method == "OPTIONS" {
            let res = response("", 204);
            res.header("access-control-allow-origin", self.allow_origin);
            res.header("access-control-allow-methods", self.allow_methods);
            res.header("access-control-allow-headers", self.allow_headers);
            return res;
        }
        if next != null {
            let res = next(req);
            if res != null and type_of(res) == "instance" {
                // Attach CORS headers on API / JSON responses unless already set
                if req.path.starts_with("/api/") or (
                    res.headers.has("content-type") and string(res.headers["content-type"]).contains("application/json")
                ) {
                    if not res.headers.has("access-control-allow-origin") {
                        res.header("access-control-allow-origin", self.allow_origin);
                        res.header("access-control-allow-methods", self.allow_methods);
                        res.header("access-control-allow-headers", self.allow_headers);
                    }
                }
            }
            return res;
        }
        return null;
    }
}

public class LoggerMiddleware {
    fn new () {
        let _ = self;
    }

    public fn handle(self, req: any, next: any = null) -> any {
        let _ = self;
        let start = now_ms();
        if next != null {
            let res = next(req);
            let elapsed = now_ms() - start;
            let status = 200;
            if res != null and type_of(res) == "instance" {
                status = res.status_code;
            }
            println("[Spid3rWeb] {req.method} {req.path} -> {status} ({elapsed}ms)");
            return res;
        }
        println("[Spid3rWeb] {req.method} {req.path}");
        return null;
    }
}

/// Require an authenticated session or Bearer JWT token (returns 401 JSON or redirects `/login`).
public class AuthMiddleware {
    fn new () {
        let _ = self;
    }

    public fn handle(self, req: any, next: any = null) -> any {
        let _ = self;
        // Prefer the request-scoped auth state (set by Application); fall
        // back to the global facade for direct/legacy invocation.
        let auth = null;
        if req.auth != null {
            auth = req.auth;
        } else {
            auth = Auth;
        }

        // Authenticate session if token present
        if req.session_id != "" and not auth.check() {
            auth.authenticate_token(req.session_id);
        }

        if not auth.check() {
            if req.path.starts_with("/api/") or req.header("accept", "").contains("application/json") {
                return json({
                    "error": "Unauthorized", "message": "Authentication required."
                }, 401);
            }
            return redirect("/login");
        }
        if next != null {
            return next(req);
        }
        return null;
    }
}

// GuestMiddleware â€” the inverse of AuthMiddleware: for routes that only make
// sense while logged out (login/register pages). An authenticated visitor is
// redirected away instead of seeing the form again. Alias: "guest".
public class GuestMiddleware {
    public let redirect_to;

    fn new (redirect_to: string = "/") {
        self.redirect_to = redirect_to;
    }

    public fn handle(self, req: any, next: any = null) -> any {
        let auth = null;
        if req.auth != null {
            auth = req.auth;
        } else {
            auth = Auth;
        }
        if auth.check() {
            return redirect(self.redirect_to);
        }
        if next != null {
            return next(req);
        }
        return null;
    }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ThrottleMiddleware â€” Cache-backed per-client rate limiting
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Usage: Route.get("/x", h).middleware("throttle:5,60")
//   â†’ at most 5 requests per 60 seconds per client (IP or session token).
// Exceeding the limit returns 429 (JSON for API clients). The counter lives
// in the configured Cache store; the memory driver resets on process restart.
/// Cache-backed per-client rate limiter emitting `X-RateLimit-*` and `429 Too Many Requests`.
public class ThrottleMiddleware {
    public let max_attempts;
    public let decay_seconds;

    fn new (max_attempts: int = 60, decay_seconds: int = 60) {
        self.max_attempts = max_attempts;
        self.decay_seconds = decay_seconds;
    }

    public fn handle(self, req: any, next: any = null) -> any {
        // Key by session token when present (stable per client), else by IP.
        let identity = req.client_ip();
        if req.session_id != "" {
            identity = req.session_id;
        }
        let key = "throttle:" + identity + ":" + req.method + ":" + req.path;

        // Fixed-window counter: add() seeds the TTL on the first hit so the
        // window always starts at the first request.
        if Cache.missing(key) {
            Cache.add(key, 0, self.decay_seconds);
        }
        let hits = Cache.increment(key);

        if hits > self.max_attempts {
            let retry = self.decay_seconds;
            if req.path.starts_with("/api/") or req.header("accept", "").contains("application/json") {
                return json({
                    "error": "Too Many Requests",
                    "message": "Rate limit exceeded. Try again in {retry} seconds."
                }, 429);
            }
            let res = response(
                "<!DOCTYPE html><html lang='en'><head><meta charset='utf-8'><title>429 Too Many Requests - Spid3rWeb</title></head><body><h1>429 â€” Too Many Requests</h1><p>Rate limit exceeded. Please try again in a few moments.</p></body></html>",
                429
            );
            res.header("retry-after", string(retry));
            return res;
        }
        if next != null {
            let res = next(req);
            if res != null and type_of(res) == "instance" {
                let remaining = self.max_attempts - hits;
                if remaining < 0 {
                    remaining = 0;
                }
                res.header("x-ratelimit-limit", string(self.max_attempts));
                res.header("x-ratelimit-remaining", string(remaining));
            }
            return res;
        }
        return null;
    }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// EtagMiddleware â€” Automatic ETag & 304 Not Modified for GET/HEAD 200 responses
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
public class EtagMiddleware {
    fn new () {
        let _ = self;
    }

    public fn handle(self, req: any, next: any = null) -> any {
        let _ = self;
        if next == null {
            return null;
        }
        let res = next(req);
        if res == null or type_of(res) != "instance" {
            return res;
        }
        if (req.method == "GET" or req.method == "HEAD") and res.status_code == 200 and res.body != "" {
            let etag = "\"" + sha256(res.body).slice(0, 16) + "\"";
            res.header("etag", etag);
            let inm = req.header("if-none-match", "");
            if inm != "" and inm == etag {
                res.status(304);
                res.body = "";
            }
        }
        return res;
    }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// RequestIdMiddleware â€” Attaches X-Request-Id to req and response headers
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
public class RequestIdMiddleware {
    fn new () {
        let _ = self;
    }

    public fn handle(self, req: any, next: any = null) -> any {
        let _ = self;
        let req_id = req.header("x-request-id", "");
        if req_id == "" {
            req_id = "req_" + hex_encode(random_bytes(8).unwrap());
        }
        req.headers["x-request-id"] = req_id;
        if next != null {
            let res = next(req);
            if res != null and type_of(res) == "instance" {
                res.header("x-request-id", req_id);
            }
            return res;
        }
        return null;
    }
}

// framework/Http/Request.matrix
// Spid3rWeb HTTP Request Representation (query/form/JSON/multipart parsing,
// method spoofing, cookies, file uploads, session & auth accessors, and
// one-line validation).

import "http";
import "framework/Http/Validator";
import "framework/Http/RequestParser";
import "framework/Http/HttpException";
import "framework/Foundation/Config";
import "framework/Session/Session";

/// Parsed HTTP request context with query/form/JSON/multipart inputs, cookies, session, and auth state.
public class Request {
    public let method;
    public let original_method;
    public let path;
    public let params;
    public let bound_params;
    public let query_params;
    public let headers;
    public let cookies;
    public let files;
    public let body;
    public let data;
    public let session_id;
    public let auth;
    public let user_data;
    public let app;
    public let res;
    public let session_store;
    public let session_source;
    public let raw;

    fn new (raw_req = null) {
        self.params = {
        };
        self.bound_params = {
        };
        self.query_params = {
        };
        self.headers = {
        };
        self.cookies = {
        };
        self.files = {
        };
        self.data = {
        };
        self.body = "";
        self.method = "GET";
        self.original_method = "GET";
        self.path = "/";
        self.session_id = "";
        self.session_source = "";
        self.auth = null;
        self.user_data = null;
        self.app = null;
        self.res = null;
        self.session_store = null;
        self.raw = null;

        if raw_req != null {
            self.init_from_raw(raw_req);
        }
    }

    public fn init_from_raw(self, raw: map) {
        self.raw = raw;
        if raw.has("method") {
            self.method = string(raw["method"]).upper();
            self.original_method = self.method;
        }
        if raw.has("path") {
            self.path = string(raw["path"]);
        }
        if raw.has("params") and raw["params"] != null {
            self.params = raw["params"];
        }
        if raw.has("headers") and raw["headers"] != null {
            for (k, v) in raw["headers"] {
                self.headers[string(k).lower()] = string(v);
            }
        }

        if self.headers.has("cookie") {
            parse_cookies_into(string(self.headers["cookie"]), self.cookies);
        }

        if self.path.contains("?") {
            let parts = self.path.split("?");
            self.path = parts[0];
            if parts.len() > 1 {
                parse_query_into(parts[1], self.query_params, self.data);
            }
        }

        if raw.has("data") and raw["data"] != null and type_of(raw["data"]) == "map" {
            for (k, v) in raw["data"] {
                self.data[string(k)] = v;
            }
        }

        if raw.has("query") and raw["query"] != null and type_of(raw["query"]) == "map" {
            for (k, v) in raw["query"] {
                let sk = string(k);
                let sv = string(v);
                self.query_params[sk] = sv;
                if not self.data.has(sk) {
                    self.data[sk] = sv;
                }
            }
        }

        if raw.has("raw_query") and raw["raw_query"] != null {
            let rq = string(raw["raw_query"]);
            if rq != "" {
                parse_query_into(rq, self.query_params, self.data);
            }
        }

        let parsed_as_json = false;
        let parsed_json = http_body_json(raw);
        if parsed_json.is_ok() {
            let val = parsed_json.unwrap();
            if type_of(val) == "map" {
                for (k, v) in val {
                    self.data[string(k)] = v;
                }
                parsed_as_json = true;
            }
        }

        if raw.has("body") and raw["body"] != null {
            self.body = string(raw["body"]);
            if not parsed_as_json and self.body != "" {
                let ct = "";
                if self.headers.has("content-type") {
                    ct = string(self.headers["content-type"]).lower();
                }
                if ct.contains("application/x-www-form-urlencoded") or (ct == "" and self.body.contains("=")) {
                    parse_form_into(self.body, self.data);
                } elif ct.contains("multipart/form-data") {
                    parse_multipart_into(
                        self.body,
                        string(self.headers["content-type"]),
                        self.data,
                        self.files
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        if self.method == "POST" {
            let spoof = "";
            if self.headers.has("x-http-method-override") {
                spoof = string(self.headers["x-http-method-override"]).trim().upper();
            } elif self.data.has("_method") {
                spoof = string(self.data["_method"]).trim().upper();
            } elif self.query_params.has("_method") {
                spoof = string(self.query_params["_method"]).trim().upper();
            }
            if spoof == "PUT" or spoof == "PATCH" or spoof == "DELETE" {
                self.method = spoof;
            }
        }

        self.session_id = self.extract_session_token();
    }

    fn extract_session_token(self) -> string {
        if self.headers.has("authorization") {
            let auth = self.headers["authorization"];
            if auth.starts_with("Bearer ") {
                self.session_source = "bearer";
                return auth.replace("Bearer ", "").trim();
            }
        }
        let cookie_name = Config.get("session.cookie", "spid3r_session");
        if self.cookies.has(cookie_name) {
            self.session_source = "cookie";
            return string(self.cookies[cookie_name]);
        }
        return "";
    }

    public fn peer_ip(self) -> string {
        let peer = resolve_peer_ip(self.raw);
        if peer != "" {
            return peer;
        }
        return "unknown";
    }

    public fn client_ip(self) -> string {
        return resolve_client_ip(self.headers, self.raw);
    }

    public fn ip(self) -> string {
        return self.client_ip();
    }

    public fn scheme(self) -> string {
        return resolve_request_scheme(self.headers, self.raw);
    }

    public fn is_secure(self) -> bool {
        return self.scheme() == "https";
    }

    public fn host(self) -> string {
        return resolve_request_host(self.headers, self.raw);
    }

    public fn bearer(self) -> string {
        if self.headers.has("authorization") {
            let auth = string(self.headers["authorization"]);
            if auth.starts_with("Bearer ") {
                return auth.slice(7, auth.len()).trim();
            }
        }
        return "";
    }

    public fn user(self) -> any {
        return self.user_data ?? self.auth ?. current_user;
    }

    public fn session(self) -> SessionStore {
        if self.session_store == null {
            let sid = self.cookie("spid3r_sid", "");
            self.session_store = new SessionStore(string(sid));
        }
        return self.session_store;
    }

    public fn is_json(self) -> bool {
        let ct = string(self.header("content-type", "")).lower();
        return ct.contains("application/json");
    }

    public fn wants_json(self) -> bool {
        let accept = string(self.header("accept", "")).lower();
        return self.path.starts_with("/api/") or accept.contains("application/json") or self.is_json();
    }

    public fn cookie(self, name: string, default_val: any = null) -> any {
        if self.cookies.has(name) {
            return self.cookies[name];
        }
        return default_val;
    }

    public fn signed_cookie(self, name: string, default_val: any = null) -> any {
        if not self.cookies.has(name) {
            return default_val;
        }
        return verify_signed_cookie_value(string(self.cookies[name]), name, default_val);
    }

    public fn file(self, key: string) -> any {
        if self.files.has(key) {
            return self.files[key];
        }
        return null;
    }

    public fn has_file(self, key: string) -> bool {
        return self.files.has(key) and self.files[key] != null;
    }

    public fn store_file(
        self,
        key: string,
        directory: string = "uploads",
        disk_name: string = "public"
    ) -> string {
        let f = self.file(key);
        if f == null {
            throw bad_request("No uploaded file found for field '{key}'.");
        }
        return store_uploaded_file(f, directory, disk_name);
    }

    public fn input(self, key: string, default_val: any = null) -> any {
        if self.data.has(key) {
            return self.data[key];
        }
        if self.query_params.has(key) {
            return self.query_params[key];
        }
        if self.params.has(key) {
            return self.params[key];
        }
        return default_val;
    }

    public fn string(self, key: string, default_val: string = "") -> string {
        let v = self.input(key, null);
        if v == null {
            return default_val;
        }
        return string(v);
    }

    public fn int(self, key: string, default_val: int = 0) -> int {
        let v = self.input(key, null);
        if v == null {
            return default_val;
        }
        try {
            return int(v);
        } catch (_e) {
            return default_val;
        }
    }

    public fn float(self, key: string, default_val: float = 0.0) -> float {
        let v = self.input(key, null);
        if v == null {
            return default_val;
        }
        try {
            return float(v);
        } catch (_e) {
            return default_val;
        }
    }

    public fn bool(self, key: string, default_val: bool = false) -> bool {
        let v = self.input(key, null);
        if v == null {
            return default_val;
        }
        if type_of(v) == "bool" {
            return v;
        }
        let s = string(v).trim().lower();
        if s == "true" or s == "1" or s == "yes" or s == "on" {
            return true;
        }
        if s == "false" or s == "0" or s == "no" or s == "off" {
            return false;
        }
        return default_val;
    }

    public fn only(self, keys: list) -> map {
        let out = {
        };
        for k in keys {
            let sk = string(k);
            if self.has(sk) {
                out[sk] = self.input(sk);
            }
        }
        return out;
    }

    public fn except(self, keys: list) -> map {
        let excluded = {
        };
        for k in keys {
            excluded[string(k)] = true;
        }
        let out = {
        };
        for (k, v) in self.data {
            if not excluded.has(k) {
                out[k] = v;
            }
        }
        return out;
    }

    public fn page(self, default_val: int = 1) -> int {
        let p = self.int("page", default_val);
        if p < 1 {
            return 1;
        }
        return p;
    }

    public fn per_page(self, default_val: int = 15, max_val: int = 100) -> int {
        let pp = self.int("per_page", default_val);
        if pp < 1 {
            return default_val;
        }
        if pp > max_val {
            return max_val;
        }
        return pp;
    }

    public fn has(self, key: string) -> bool {
        return self.data.has(key) or self.query_params.has(key) or self.params.has(key);
    }

    public fn all(self) -> map {
        return self.data;
    }

    public fn param(self, key: string, default_val: any = null) -> any {
        if self.params.has(key) {
            return self.params[key];
        }
        return default_val;
    }

    /// Retrieve a route parameter hydrated through `Route.bind()` / `app.bind()`.
    public fn bound(self, key: string, default_val: any = null) -> any {
        if self.bound_params.has(key) {
            return self.bound_params[key];
        }
        return default_val;
    }

    public fn query(self, key: string, default_val: any = null) -> any {
        if self.query_params.has(key) {
            return self.query_params[key];
        }
        return default_val;
    }

    public fn header(self, key: string, default_val: any = null) -> any {
        let lkey = string(key).lower();
        if self.headers.has(lkey) {
            return self.headers[lkey];
        }
        return default_val;
    }

    public fn validate(self, rules: map) -> Validator {
        let engine = new Validator();
        return engine.check(self, rules);
    }

    public fn validate_or_fail(self, rules: map) -> map {
        let v = self.validate(rules);
        if v.fails() {
            throw validation_failed(v.error_messages(), v.first());
        }
        return v.validated();
    }
}

FILE: framework/Http/RequestParser.matrix

// framework/Http/RequestParser.matrix
// Cookie, query string, form, multipart, client IP, and upload storage helpers.

import "framework/Foundation/Config";
import "framework/Filesystem/Storage";
import "framework/Http/HttpException";

public fn parse_cookies_into(cookie_header: string, cookies_map: map) {
    let pairs = cookie_header.split(";");
    for p in pairs {
        let trimmed = p.trim();
        if trimmed != "" {
            let eq = trimmed.index_of("=");
            if eq > 0 {
                let k = trimmed.slice(0, eq).trim();
                let v = trimmed.slice(eq + 1, trimmed.len()).trim();
                cookies_map[k] = url_decode(v);
            }
        }
    }
}

public fn parse_query_into(qs: string, query_params: map, data: map) {
    let clean_qs = qs.trim();
    if clean_qs.starts_with("?") {
        clean_qs = clean_qs.slice(1);
    }
    let pairs = clean_qs.split("&");
    for p in pairs {
        let p_trim = p.trim();
        if p_trim != "" {
            let eq = p_trim.index_of("=");
            if eq >= 0 {
                let k = url_decode(p_trim.slice(0, eq).trim());
                let v = url_decode(p_trim.slice(eq + 1));
                query_params[k] = v;
                if not data.has(k) {
                    data[k] = v;
                }
            } else {
                let k = url_decode(p_trim);
                query_params[k] = "";
                if not data.has(k) {
                    data[k] = "";
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

public fn parse_form_into(body_text: string, data: map) {
    let clean_body = body_text.trim();
    let pairs = clean_body.split("&");
    for p in pairs {
        let p_trim = p.trim();
        if p_trim != "" {
            let eq = p_trim.index_of("=");
            if eq >= 0 {
                let k = url_decode(p_trim.slice(0, eq).trim());
                let v = url_decode(p_trim.slice(eq + 1));
                data[k] = v;
            } else {
                let k = url_decode(p_trim);
                data[k] = "";
            }
        }
    }
}

public fn parse_multipart_into(body_text: string, content_type: string, data: map, files: map) {
    let b_idx = content_type.index_of("boundary=");
    if b_idx < 0 {
        return;
    }
    let boundary = content_type.slice(b_idx + 9, content_type.len()).trim();
    if boundary.starts_with("\"") and boundary.ends_with("\"") {
        boundary = boundary.slice(1, boundary.len() - 1);
    }
    let delim = "--{boundary}";

    let max_files = int(Config.get("upload.max_files", 10));
    let max_bytes = int(Config.get("upload.max_bytes", 5_242_880));
    let file_count = 0;

    let chunks = body_text.split(delim);
    for chunk in chunks {
        let c = chunk;
        if c.starts_with("\r\n") {
            c = c.slice(2, c.len());
        } elif c.starts_with("\n") {
            c = c.slice(1, c.len());
        }
        if c.len() == 0 or c == "--" {
            continue;
        }

        let sep = c.index_of("\r\n\r\n");
        let header_len = 4;
        if sep < 0 {
            sep = c.index_of("\n\n");
            header_len = 2;
            if sep < 0 {
                continue;
            }
        }
        let part_headers_raw = c.slice(0, sep);
        let part_headers = part_headers_raw.lower();
        let value = c.slice(sep + header_len, c.len());

        if value.ends_with("\r\n") {
            value = value.slice(0, value.len() - 2);
        } elif value.ends_with("\n") or value.ends_with("\r") {
            value = value.slice(0, value.len() - 1);
        }

        let n_idx = part_headers.index_of("name=\"");
        if n_idx < 0 {
            continue;
        }
        let rest = part_headers_raw.slice(n_idx + 6, part_headers_raw.len());
        let end_q = rest.index_of("\"");
        if end_q < 0 {
            continue;
        }
        let field = rest.slice(0, end_q);
        if field == "" {
            continue;
        }

        let fn_idx = part_headers.index_of("filename=\"");
        if fn_idx >= 0 {
            let fn_rest = part_headers_raw.slice(fn_idx + 10, part_headers_raw.len());
            let fn_end = fn_rest.index_of("\"");
            let filename = "";
            if fn_end >= 0 {
                let raw_fn = fn_rest.slice(0, fn_end).replace("\\", "/");
                let fn_segs = raw_fn.split("/");
                filename = fn_segs[fn_segs.len() - 1];
            }
            let part_ct = "application/octet-stream";
            let ct_idx = part_headers.index_of("content-type:");
            if ct_idx >= 0 {
                let ct_sub = part_headers_raw.slice(ct_idx + 13, part_headers_raw.len());
                let ct_lines = ct_sub.split("\n");
                if ct_lines.len() > 0 {
                    part_ct = ct_lines[0].trim();
                }
            }
            if filename != "" {
                file_count = file_count + 1;
                if max_files > 0 and file_count > max_files {
                    throw make_http_exception(413, "Too many uploaded files (max {max_files}).");
                }
                if max_bytes > 0 and value.len() > max_bytes {
                    throw make_http_exception(
                        413,
                        "Uploaded file '{filename}' exceeds max_bytes limit ({max_bytes} bytes)."
                    );
                }
                let ext = "";
                let dot_segs = filename.split(".");
                if dot_segs.len() > 1 {
                    let tail = dot_segs[dot_segs.len() - 1].lower();
                    ext = ".{tail}";
                }
                files[field] = {
                    "name": filename,
                    "type": part_ct,
                    "size": value.len(),
                    "content": value,
                    "ext": ext
                };
            }
            continue;
        }

        data[field] = value;
    }
}

public fn strip_ip_port(raw_addr: string) -> string {
    let s = string(raw_addr).trim();
    if s == "" {
        return "";
    }
    if s.starts_with("[") {
        let close_b = s.index_of("]");
        if close_b > 1 {
            return s.slice(1, close_b);
        }
    }
    let parts = s.split(":");
    if parts.len() == 2 {
        return parts[0].trim();
    }
    return s;
}

public fn resolve_peer_ip(raw: any) -> string {
    if raw != null and type_of(raw) == "map" {
        for k in ["remote", "peer_addr", "remote_addr", "client_addr", "ip"] {
            if raw.has(k) {
                let v = strip_ip_port(string(raw[k]));
                if v != "" {
                    return v;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return "";
}

fn cidr_prefix_match(ip: string, pattern: string) -> bool {
    if pattern == "" {
        return false;
    }
    if ip == pattern {
        return true;
    }
    if pattern.contains("/") {
        let slash = pattern.index_of("/");
        let net = pattern.slice(0, slash);
        let bits = -1;
        try {
            bits = int(pattern.slice(slash + 1, pattern.len()));
        } catch (_e) {
            return false;
        }
        let ip_octets = ip.split(".");
        let net_octets = net.split(".");
        if ip_octets.len() != 4 or net_octets.len() != 4 {
            return false;
        }
        let match_octets = 0;
        if bits == 8 {
            match_octets = 1;
        } elif bits == 16 {
            match_octets = 2;
        } elif bits == 24 {
            match_octets = 3;
        } elif bits == 32 {
            match_octets = 4;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
        let i = 0;
        while i < match_octets {
            if ip_octets[i] != net_octets[i] {
                return false;
            }
            i = i + 1;
        }
        return true;
    }
    if pattern.ends_with(".") and ip.starts_with(pattern) {
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

fn configured_trusted_proxies() {
    let proxies = [];
    let env_list = env("SPID3R_TRUSTED_PROXIES").unwrap_or("").trim();
    if env_list != "" {
        for item in env_list.split(",") {
            let t = item.trim();
            if t != "" {
                proxies.push(t);
            }
        }
    }
    let cfg_list = Config.get("http.trusted_proxies", null);
    if cfg_list != null and type_of(cfg_list) == "list" {
        for item in cfg_list {
            let t = string(item).trim();
            if t != "" {
                proxies.push(t);
            }
        }
    }
    return proxies;
}

public fn is_trusted_proxy(peer_ip: string) -> bool {
    let legacy = env("SPID3R_TRUST_PROXY").unwrap_or("").trim().lower();
    if legacy == "1" or legacy == "true" or legacy == "yes" {
        return true;
    }
    let proxies = configured_trusted_proxies();
    if proxies.len() == 0 {
        return false;
    }
    let peer = strip_ip_port(peer_ip).lower();
    for entry in proxies {
        let p = string(entry).trim().lower();
        if p == "*" {
            return true;
        }
        if peer == "" {
            continue;
        }
        if p == "loopback" {
            if peer == "127.0.0.1" or peer == "::1" or peer == "localhost" or peer.starts_with("127.") {
                return true;
            }
        }
        if cidr_prefix_match(peer, p) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

public fn resolve_client_ip(headers: map, raw: any) -> string {
    let peer = resolve_peer_ip(raw);
    if is_trusted_proxy(peer) {
        if headers.has("x-forwarded-for") {
            let parts = string(headers["x-forwarded-for"]).split(",");
            if parts.len() > 0 {
                let first = strip_ip_port(parts[0]);
                if first != "" {
                    return first;
                }
            }
        }
        if headers.has("x-real-ip") {
            let rip = strip_ip_port(string(headers["x-real-ip"]));
            if rip != "" {
                return rip;
            }
        }
    }
    if peer != "" {
        return peer;
    }
    return "unknown";
}

public fn resolve_request_scheme(headers: map, raw: any) -> string {
    if raw != null and type_of(raw) == "map" and raw.has("tls") and raw["tls"] == true {
        return "https";
    }
    let peer = resolve_peer_ip(raw);
    if is_trusted_proxy(peer) and headers.has("x-forwarded-proto") {
        let raw_proto = string(headers["x-forwarded-proto"]).split(",")[0].trim().lower();
        if raw_proto == "https" or raw_proto == "http" {
            return raw_proto;
        }
    }
    return "http";
}

public fn resolve_request_host(headers: map, raw: any) -> string {
    let peer = resolve_peer_ip(raw);
    if is_trusted_proxy(peer) and headers.has("x-forwarded-host") {
        let fh = string(headers["x-forwarded-host"]).split(",")[0].trim();
        if fh != "" {
            return fh;
        }
    }
    if headers.has("host") {
        return string(headers["host"]).trim();
    }
    return "";
}

public fn verify_signed_cookie_value(raw_val: string, name: string, default_val: any) -> any {
    let parts = raw_val.split(".");
    if parts.len() < 2 {
        return default_val;
    }
    let sig = parts[parts.len() - 1];
    if sig == "" or raw_val.len() <= sig.len() + 1 {
        return default_val;
    }
    let payload = raw_val.slice(0, raw_val.len() - sig.len() - 1);
    let expected = hmac_sha256(resolve_app_key(), "{name}={payload}");
    if secure_compare(sig, expected) {
        return payload;
    }
    return default_val;
}

fn list_has_lower(items, target: string) -> bool {
    if items == null or type_of(items) != "list" {
        return false;
    }
    for item in items {
        if string(item).trim().lower() == target {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

public fn validate_uploaded_file(f: map, opts: any = null) {
    let max_bytes = int(Config.get("upload.max_bytes", 5_242_880));
    let allowed_exts = Config.get("upload.allowed_extensions", null);
    let blocked_exts = Config.get("upload.blocked_extensions", null);
    let allowed_mimes = Config.get("upload.allowed_mimes", null);
    if opts != null and type_of(opts) == "map" {
        if opts.has("max_bytes") {
            max_bytes = int(opts["max_bytes"]);
        }
        if opts.has("allowed_extensions") {
            allowed_exts = opts["allowed_extensions"];
        }
        if opts.has("blocked_extensions") {
            blocked_exts = opts["blocked_extensions"];
        }
        if opts.has("allowed_mimes") {
            allowed_mimes = opts["allowed_mimes"];
        }
    }

    let size = int(f["size"]);
    if max_bytes > 0 and size > max_bytes {
        throw make_http_exception(413, "Uploaded file exceeds max_bytes limit ({max_bytes} bytes).");
    }

    let raw_ext = "";
    if f.has("ext") {
        raw_ext = string(f["ext"]).trim().lower();
        if raw_ext.starts_with(".") {
            raw_ext = raw_ext.slice(1, raw_ext.len());
        }
    }
    if raw_ext != "" and list_has_lower(blocked_exts, raw_ext) {
        throw make_http_exception(415, "File extension '.{raw_ext}' is blocked.");
    }
    if allowed_exts != null and type_of(allowed_exts) == "list" and allowed_exts.len() > 0 {
        if raw_ext == "" or not list_has_lower(allowed_exts, raw_ext) {
            throw make_http_exception(415, "File extension '.{raw_ext}' is not allowed.");
        }
    }
    if allowed_mimes != null and type_of(allowed_mimes) == "list" and allowed_mimes.len() > 0 {
        let mime = "";
        if f.has("type") {
            mime = string(f["type"]).split(";")[0].trim().lower();
        }
        if mime != "" and not list_has_lower(allowed_mimes, mime) {
            throw make_http_exception(415, "Uploaded media type '{mime}' is not allowed.");
        }
    }
}

public fn store_uploaded_file(f: map, directory: string, disk_name: string) -> string {
    validate_uploaded_file(f);
    let rnd = hex_encode(random_bytes(16).unwrap());
    let ext = "";
    if f.has("ext") {
        ext = string(f["ext"]).trim().lower();
        if ext != "" and not ext.starts_with(".") {
            ext = ".{ext}";
        }
    }
    let safe_name = "{rnd}{ext}";
    let rel_path = directory;
    if rel_path != "" and not rel_path.ends_with("/") {
        rel_path = "{rel_path}/";
    }
    rel_path = "{rel_path}{safe_name}";
    Storage.disk(disk_name).put(rel_path, string(f["content"]));
    return rel_path;
}

// framework/Resource.matrix
// Spid3rWeb REST Resource Layer
//
// Consistent JSON envelopes for API responses. Transform models or row maps
// into API shapes with a transformer function (so API structure never leaks
// database columns like password hashes or internal flags):
//
//   import "framework/Http/Resource";
//
//   let user_transformer = fn(u) {
//       return map("id", u["id"], "name", u["name"], "email", u["email"]);
//   };
//
//   return json(resource_item(user, user_transformer));            // single
//   return json(resource_collection(users, user_transformer));     // list
//   return json(resource_paginated(page, user_transformer));       // paginate() result
//
// Envelopes:
//   resource_item        â†’  {"data": {...}}
//   resource_collection  â†’  {"data": [...], "meta": {"count": n}}
//   resource_paginated   â†’  {"data": [...], "meta": {"count", "total",
//                            "page", "per_page", "last_page", "has_more",
//                            "from", "to"}}
//
// Transformers always receive a PLAIN MAP: Model instances are unwrapped
// via to_map() first, so `u["field"]` works uniformly. Null input produces
// {"data": null} (never an error) â€” the natural shape for miss-or-404 flows.

import "framework/Database/Model";

// Normalize one record to a plain map (instances â†’ to_map()).
fn _resource_plain(item) {
    if item == null {
        return null;
    }
    if type_of(item) == "map" {
        return item;
    }
    return item.to_map();
}

// Single record: {"data": transformed}
/// Wrap a single model or map in a `{"data": ...}` API resource envelope.
public fn resource_item(item: any, transformer: any = null) -> map {
    let plain = _resource_plain(item);
    if transformer != null and plain != null {
        return {
            "data": transformer(plain)
        };
    }
    return {
        "data": plain
    };
}

// List of records: {"data": [...], "meta": {"count": n}}
/// Transform a list of records into `{"data": [...], "meta": {"count": n}}`.
public fn resource_collection(items: list, transformer: any = null) -> map {
    let out = [];
    let i = 0;
    while i < len(items) {
        let plain = _resource_plain(items[i]);
        if transformer != null {
            out.push(transformer(plain));
        } else {
            out.push(plain);
        }
        i += 1;
    }
    return {
        "data": out, "meta": {
            "count": len(out)
        }
    };
}

// QueryBuilder.paginate() result â†’ full pagination envelope. The page map's
// "data" rows are transformed; every other key becomes "meta".
/// Transform a `QueryBuilder.paginate()` result into a standardized API envelope with pagination meta.
public fn resource_paginated(page_result: map, transformer: any = null) -> map {
    let rows = page_result["data"];
    let out = [];
    let i = 0;
    while i < len(rows) {
        let plain = _resource_plain(rows[i]);
        if transformer != null {
            out.push(transformer(plain));
        } else {
            out.push(plain);
        }
        i += 1;
    }
    let meta = {
        "count": len(out),
        "total": page_result["total"],
        "page": page_result["page"],
        "per_page": page_result["per_page"],
        "last_page": page_result["last_page"],
        "from": page_result["from"],
        "to": page_result["to"],
        "has_more": page_result["has_more"]
    };
    return {
        "data": out, "meta": meta
    };
}

// framework/Http/Response.matrix
// Spid3rWeb HTTP Response Builder & Helpers

import "http";
import "framework/Foundation/Config";
import "framework/Http/CookieJar";

fn init_hsts_enabled() -> bool {
    let hsts = env("SPID3R_HSTS");
    return hsts.is_some() and string(hsts.unwrap()).trim().lower() == "1";
}

let HSTS_ENABLED = init_hsts_enabled();

fn has_header_ctl(value: string) -> bool {
    return value.contains("\r") or value.contains("\n") or value.contains(chr(0));
}

public fn hsts_header_value() -> string {
    let max_age = int(Config.get("security.hsts.max_age", 31_536_000));
    let val = "max-age={max_age}";
    if Config.get("security.hsts.include_subdomains", true) == true {
        val = "{val}; includeSubDomains";
    }
    if Config.get("security.hsts.preload", false) == true {
        val = "{val}; preload";
    }
    return val;
}

public fn default_security_headers() -> map {
    let headers = {
        "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
        "x-content-type-options": "nosniff",
        "x-frame-options": "SAMEORIGIN",
        "referrer-policy": "strict-origin-when-cross-origin",
        "x-permitted-cross-domain-policies": "none",
        "cross-origin-opener-policy": "same-origin",
        "permissions-policy": "camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=()",
        "content-security-policy": "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data:; font-src 'self'; form-action 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'; object-src 'none'"
    };
    let cfg_headers = Config.get("security.headers", null);
    if cfg_headers != null and type_of(cfg_headers) == "map" {
        for (k, v) in cfg_headers {
            headers[string(k).lower()] = string(v);
        }
    }
    return headers;
}

// Append a header to a raw HTTP response map without overwriting existing
// headers of the same name (required for multiple Set-Cookie headers).
public fn append_http_header(http_map: map, name: string, value: string) {
    let k = string(name).lower();
    let v = string(value);
    if has_header_ctl(k) or has_header_ctl(v) {
        throw "append_http_header: illegal character (CR/LF/NUL) in header '{k}'";
    }
    if not http_map.has("headers") or http_map["headers"] == null {
        http_map["headers"] = {
            k: v
        };
        return;
    }
    let h = http_map["headers"];
    let ht = type_of(h);
    if ht == "map" {
        let dup = false;
        for (hk, _hv) in h {
            if string(hk).lower() == k {
                dup = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        if dup {
            let out = [];
            for (hk, hv) in h {
                out.push({
                    "name": string(hk), "value": string(hv)
                });
            }
            out.push({
                "name": k, "value": v
            });
            http_map["headers"] = out;
        } else {
            h[k] = v;
            http_map["headers"] = h;
        }
        return;
    }
    if ht == "list" {
        h.push({
            "name": k, "value": v
        });
        http_map["headers"] = h;
    }
}

/// Fluent HTTP response builder with security headers, CSP overrides, signed cookies, and streaming.
public class Response {
    public let status_code;
    public let headers;
    public let cookies;
    public let body;
    private let stream_source;
    private let cookie_jar;

    fn new (body = "", status_code: int = 200) {
        self.body = string(body);
        self.status_code = status_code;
        self.stream_source = null;
        self.cookie_jar = new CookieJar();
        self.cookies = self.cookie_jar.all();
        // Sensible security headers from config/security.matrix. CORS headers are
        // intentionally NOT global defaults: blanket access-control-allow-*
        // should only be set by CorsMiddleware / API routing, never on
        // cookie-authenticated HTML responses.
        self.headers = default_security_headers();
        if HSTS_ENABLED or Config.get("security.hsts.enabled", false) == true {
            self.headers["strict-transport-security"] = hsts_header_value();
        }
    }

    public fn set_https(self, flag: bool) -> Response {
        self.cookie_jar.set_https(flag);
        return self;
    }

    public fn csp(self, policy: string) -> Response {
        return self.header("content-security-policy", string(policy));
    }

    public fn security_headers(self, overrides: map) -> Response {
        if overrides != null and type_of(overrides) == "map" {
            self.withHeaders(overrides);
        }
        return self;
    }

    // Header names and values must never carry CRLF — a single \r or \n
    // enables HTTP response splitting. Values are rejected, not sanitized,
    // so misuse fails loudly instead of silently corrupting headers.
    private fn is_header_safe(self, value: string) -> bool {
        let _ = self;
        return not has_header_ctl(value);
    }

    public fn header(self, key: string, value: string) -> Response {
        let k = string(key).lower();
        if not self.is_header_safe(string(value)) {
            throw "Response.header: illegal character (CR/LF/NUL) in header value for '{k}'";
        }
        self.headers[k] = string(value);
        return self;
    }

    public fn withHeaders(self, new_headers: map) -> Response {
        for (k, v) in new_headers {
            self.header(k, string(v));
        }
        return self;
    }

    public fn cookie(
        self,
        name: string,
        value: string,
        path: string = "/",
        max_age: int = 86_400,
        secure: any = null
    ) -> Response {
        self.cookie_jar.queue(name, value, path, max_age, secure);
        self.cookies = self.cookie_jar.all();
        return self;
    }

    public fn signed_cookie(
        self,
        name: string,
        value: string,
        path: string = "/",
        max_age: int = 86_400,
        secure: any = null
    ) -> Response {
        self.cookie_jar.queue_signed(name, value, path, max_age, secure);
        self.cookies = self.cookie_jar.all();
        return self;
    }

    public fn clear_cookie(self, name: string, path: string = "/") -> Response {
        self.cookie_jar.forget(name, path);
        self.cookies = self.cookie_jar.all();
        return self;
    }

    public fn status(self, code: int) -> Response {
        self.status_code = code;
        return self;
    }

    // Express-style fluent methods on `res` (`res.status(201).json(data)`)
    public fn json(self, data: any, status: int = 0) -> Response {
        if status > 0 {
            self.status_code = status;
        }
        self.body = json_write(data);
        self.header("content-type", "application/json; charset=utf-8");
        return self;
    }

    public fn html(self, content: string, status: int = 0) -> Response {
        if status > 0 {
            self.status_code = status;
        }
        self.body = string(content);
        self.header("content-type", "text/html; charset=utf-8");
        return self;
    }

    public fn text(self, content: string, status: int = 0) -> Response {
        if status > 0 {
            self.status_code = status;
        }
        self.body = string(content);
        self.header("content-type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8");
        return self;
    }

    public fn send(self, content: any, status: int = 0) -> Response {
        if status > 0 {
            self.status_code = status;
        }
        let t = type_of(content);
        if t == "map" or t == "list" {
            return self.json(content);
        }
        return self.html(string(content));
    }

    public fn redirect(self, url: string, status: int = 302) -> Response {
        self.status_code = status;
        self.body = "";
        self.header("location", url);
        return self;
    }

    public fn stream(
        self,
        source: any,
        content_type: string = "application/octet-stream",
        status: int = 0
    ) -> Response {
        if status > 0 {
            self.status_code = status;
        }
        self.body = "";
        self.stream_source = source;
        self.header("content-type", content_type);
        return self;
    }

    public fn sse(self, source: any, status: int = 0) -> Response {
        if status > 0 {
            self.status_code = status;
        }
        self.body = "";
        self.stream_source = source;
        self.header("content-type", "text/event-stream; charset=utf-8");
        self.header("cache-control", "no-cache");
        self.header("connection", "keep-alive");
        return self;
    }

    private fn build_http_headers(self) {
        let cookie_count = self.cookies.len();
        if self.headers.has("set-cookie") {
            cookie_count = cookie_count + 1;
        }
        if cookie_count <= 1 {
            let out = {
            };
            for (k, v) in self.headers {
                out[k] = v;
            }
            if self.cookies.len() == 1 {
                out["set-cookie"] = self.cookies[0];
            }
            return out;
        }
        let out = [];
        for (k, v) in self.headers {
            out.push({
                "name": k, "value": string(v)
            });
        }
        for c in self.cookies {
            out.push({
                "name": "set-cookie", "value": c
            });
        }
        return out;
    }

    public fn to_http(self) -> map {
        let hdrs = self.build_http_headers();
        if self.stream_source != null {
            return http_stream(self.status_code, hdrs, self.stream_source);
        }
        return http_response(self.status_code, hdrs, self.body);
    }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Global Fluent Response Helpers
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

public fn response(content: any = "", status: int = 200) -> Response {
    return new Response(content, status);
}

public fn json(data: any, status: int = 200) -> Response {
    let res = new Response(json_write(data), status);
    res.header("content-type", "application/json; charset=utf-8");
    return res;
}

public fn html(content: string, status: int = 200) -> Response {
    let res = new Response(content, status);
    res.header("content-type", "text/html; charset=utf-8");
    return res;
}

public fn text(content: string, status: int = 200) -> Response {
    let res = new Response(content, status);
    res.header("content-type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8");
    return res;
}

public fn text_405(allow: string) -> Response {
    let res = new Response("405 Method Not Allowed", 405);
    res.header("content-type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8");
    res.header("allow", allow);
    return res;
}

public fn redirect(url: string, status: int = 302) -> Response {
    let res = new Response("", status);
    res.header("location", url);
    return res;
}

public fn stream(
    source: any,
    content_type: string = "application/octet-stream",
    status: int = 200
) -> Response {
    let res = new Response("", status);
    return res.stream(source, content_type);
}

public fn sse(source: any, status: int = 200) -> Response {
    let res = new Response("", status);
    return res.sse(source);
}

// framework/Http/RouteMatcher.matrix
// Segment/wildcard route matching and reverse URL building helpers.

public fn match_segments(p_parts: list, a_parts: list) -> any {
    let params = {
    };
    let i = 0;
    let n = p_parts.len();
    while i < n {
        let p_seg = p_parts[i];
        let a_seg = a_parts[i];

        if p_seg.starts_with(":") {
            let param_name = p_seg.slice(1, p_seg.len());
            params[param_name] = a_seg;
        } elif p_seg.starts_with("{") and p_seg.ends_with("}") {
            let param_name = p_seg.slice(1, p_seg.len() - 1).trim();
            params[param_name] = a_seg;
        } elif p_seg != a_seg {
            return null;
        }
        i += 1;
    }
    return params;
}

public fn match_wildcard_segments(p_parts: list, a_parts: list) -> any {
    let p_len = p_parts.len();
    let a_len = a_parts.len();
    if a_len < p_len - 1 {
        return null;
    }
    let params = {
    };
    let i = 0;
    while i < p_len {
        let p_seg = p_parts[i];
        if p_seg.starts_with("*") {
            let param_name = p_seg.slice(1, p_seg.len());
            if param_name == "" {
                param_name = "wildcard";
            }
            let rest = [];
            let j = i;
            while j < a_len {
                rest.push(a_parts[j]);
                j += 1;
            }
            params[param_name] = rest.join("/");
            return params;
        }
        if i >= a_len {
            return null;
        }
        let a_seg = a_parts[i];
        if p_seg.starts_with(":") {
            params[p_seg.slice(1, p_seg.len())] = a_seg;
        } elif p_seg.starts_with("{") and p_seg.ends_with("}") {
            params[p_seg.slice(1, p_seg.len() - 1).trim()] = a_seg;
        } elif p_seg != a_seg {
            return null;
        }
        i += 1;
    }
    return params;
}

public fn match_path(pattern: string, actual: string) -> any {
    if pattern == actual {
        return {
        };
    }
    let p_parts = pattern.split("/");
    let a_parts = actual.split("/");
    if p_parts.len() > 0 and p_parts[p_parts.len() - 1].starts_with("*") {
        return match_wildcard_segments(p_parts, a_parts);
    }
    if p_parts.len() != a_parts.len() {
        return null;
    }
    return match_segments(p_parts, a_parts);
}

public fn match_dynamic_bucket(bucket: map, a_parts: list) -> any {
    let a_len = a_parts.len();
    if a_len > 2 {
        let s12 = a_parts[1] + "/" + a_parts[2];
        if bucket["second_seg"].has(s12) {
            let c2 = bucket["second_seg"][s12];
            let k = 0;
            let kn = c2.len();
            while k < kn {
                let r2 = c2[k];
                let p2 = match_segments(r2.segments, a_parts);
                if p2 != null {
                    return {
                        "route": r2, "params": p2
                    };
                }
                k += 1;
            }
        }
    }
    if a_len > 1 {
        let s1 = a_parts[1];
        if bucket["first_seg"].has(s1) {
            let c1 = bucket["first_seg"][s1];
            let i = 0;
            let n = c1.len();
            while i < n {
                let route = c1[i];
                let params = match_segments(route.segments, a_parts);
                if params != null {
                    return {
                        "route": route, "params": params
                    };
                }
                i += 1;
            }
        }
    }
    let pf = bucket["param_first"];
    let j = 0;
    let jn = pf.len();
    while j < jn {
        let route_pf = pf[j];
        let params_pf = match_segments(route_pf.segments, a_parts);
        if params_pf != null {
            return {
                "route": route_pf, "params": params_pf
            };
        }
        j += 1;
    }
    return null;
}

public fn match_wildcard_list(w_list: list, a_parts: list) -> any {
    let i = 0;
    let n = w_list.len();
    while i < n {
        let route = w_list[i];
        let params = match_wildcard_segments(route.segments, a_parts);
        if params != null {
            return {
                "route": route, "params": params
            };
        }
        i += 1;
    }
    return null;
}

public fn build_route_url(entry: any, name: string, params: any) -> string {
    if params == null or type_of(params) != "map" {
        return entry.path;
    }

    let used_keys = {
    };
    let out_segs = [];
    for seg in entry.segments {
        if seg.starts_with(":") {
            let k = seg.slice(1, seg.len());
            if not params.has(k) {
                throw "Router: missing required parameter '{k}' for route '{name}'";
            }
            used_keys[k] = true;
            out_segs.push(string(params[k]));
        } elif seg.starts_with("{") and seg.ends_with("}") {
            let k = seg.slice(1, seg.len() - 1).trim();
            if not params.has(k) {
                throw "Router: missing required parameter '{k}' for route '{name}'";
            }
            used_keys[k] = true;
            out_segs.push(string(params[k]));
        } elif seg.starts_with("*") {
            let k = seg.slice(1, seg.len());
            if k == "" {
                k = "wildcard";
            }
            if params.has(k) {
                used_keys[k] = true;
                out_segs.push(string(params[k]));
            }
        } else {
            out_segs.push(seg);
        }
    }

    let built = out_segs.join("/");
    if built == "" {
        built = "/";
    }

    let qs_parts = [];
    for (k, v) in params {
        if not used_keys.has(k) and v != null {
            let sk = string(k);
            let sv = string(v);
            qs_parts.push("{sk}={sv}");
        }
    }
    if qs_parts.len() > 0 {
        built = built + "?" + qs_parts.join("&");
    }
    return built;
}

// framework/Http/Router.matrix
// Spid3rWeb High-Performance Pattern Router & Controller Dispatcher

import "framework/Http/RouteMatcher";

/// Registered route metadata binding an HTTP method, path pattern, handler, middleware, and name.
public class RouteEntry {
    public let method;
    public let path;
    public let handler;
    public let middleware_list;
    public let route_name;
    public let segments;
    public let is_static;
    public let has_wildcard;
    private let router_ref;

    fn new (method: string, path: string, handler, router_ref = null) {
        self.method = string(method).upper();
        self.path = path;
        self.handler = handler;
        self.middleware_list = [];
        self.route_name = "";
        self.router_ref = router_ref;
        self.segments = path.split("/");
        self.is_static = true;
        self.has_wildcard = false;

        let i = 0;
        let n = self.segments.len();
        while i < n {
            let seg = self.segments[i];
            if seg.starts_with(":") or (seg.starts_with("{") and seg.ends_with("}")) {
                self.is_static = false;
            } elif seg.starts_with("*") {
                self.is_static = false;
                self.has_wildcard = true;
            }
            i += 1;
        }
    }

    public fn middleware(self, mw: any) -> RouteEntry {
        if type_of(mw) == "list" {
            for m in mw {
                self.middleware_list.push(m);
            }
        } else {
            self.middleware_list.push(mw);
        }
        return self;
    }

    public fn name(self, n: string) -> RouteEntry {
        if self.router_ref != null {
            self.router_ref.register_named_route(self, n);
        } else {
            self.route_name = n;
        }
        return self;
    }
}

/// O(1) static and prefix-bucketed dynamic pattern router with groups, resource routes, and mounting.
public class Router {
    private let routes_list;
    private let static_routes;
    private let dynamic_by_len;
    private let wildcard_routes;
    private let named_routes;
    private let group_prefix;
    private let group_name_prefix;
    private let group_middleware;
    private let param_binders;
    public let fallback_handler;

    public fn register_named_route(self, entry: RouteEntry, n: string) {
        if self.group_name_prefix != "" {
            entry.route_name = "{self.group_name_prefix}{n}";
        } else {
            entry.route_name = n;
        }
        if entry.route_name != "" {
            self.named_routes[entry.route_name] = entry;
        }
    }

    fn new () {
        self.routes_list = [];
        self.static_routes = {
        };
        self.dynamic_by_len = {
        };
        self.wildcard_routes = {
        };
        self.named_routes = {
        };
        self.group_prefix = "";
        self.group_name_prefix = "";
        self.group_middleware = [];
        self.param_binders = {
        };
        self.fallback_handler = null;
    }

    public fn clear(self) -> Router {
        self.routes_list = [];
        self.static_routes = {
        };
        self.dynamic_by_len = {
        };
        self.wildcard_routes = {
        };
        self.named_routes = {
        };
        self.group_prefix = "";
        self.group_name_prefix = "";
        self.group_middleware = [];
        self.param_binders = {
        };
        self.fallback_handler = null;
        return self;
    }

    /// Register a route parameter resolver for model binding (`Route.bind("user", ...)`).
    public fn bind(self, param: string, resolver: any) -> Router {
        self.param_binders[string(param)] = resolver;
        return self;
    }

    /// Resolve any bound route parameters into their hydrated domain objects.
    public fn resolve_bindings(self, params: any) -> map {
        let bound = {
        };
        if params == null or type_of(params) != "map" {
            return bound;
        }
        for (k, v) in params {
            let pk = string(k);
            if self.param_binders.has(pk) {
                let resolver = self.param_binders[pk];
                bound[pk] = resolver(v);
            }
        }
        return bound;
    }

    public fn fallback(self, handler: any) -> Router {
        self.fallback_handler = handler;
        return self;
    }

    public fn match_any(self, methods: list, path: string, handler: any) -> RouteEntry {
        let entry = null;
        for m in methods {
            entry = self.add(m, path, handler);
        }
        return entry;
    }

    public fn any(self, path: string, handler: any) -> RouteEntry {
        return self.match_any(
            ["GET",
            "POST",
            "PUT",
            "PATCH",
            "DELETE",
            "OPTIONS"],
            path,
            handler
        );
    }

    public fn add(self, method: string, path: string, handler: any) -> RouteEntry {
        let m = string(method).upper();
        let full_path = self.group_prefix + path;
        if full_path.len() > 1 and full_path.ends_with("/") {
            full_path = full_path.slice(0, full_path.len() - 1);
        }
        if not full_path.starts_with("/") {
            full_path = "/{full_path}";
        }
        while full_path.contains("//") {
            full_path = full_path.replace("//", "/");
        }

        let entry = new RouteEntry(m, full_path, handler, self);
        for mw in self.group_middleware {
            entry.middleware(mw);
        }
        self.routes_list.push(entry);

        if entry.is_static {
            if not self.static_routes.has(m) {
                self.static_routes[m] = {
                };
            }
            if not self.static_routes[m].has(full_path) {
                self.static_routes[m][full_path] = entry;
            }
        } elif entry.has_wildcard {
            if not self.wildcard_routes.has(m) {
                self.wildcard_routes[m] = [];
            }
            self.wildcard_routes[m].push(entry);
        } else {
            if not self.dynamic_by_len.has(m) {
                self.dynamic_by_len[m] = {
                };
            }
            let seg_key = string(entry.segments.len());
            if not self.dynamic_by_len[m].has(seg_key) {
                self.dynamic_by_len[m][seg_key] = {
                    "all": [],
                    "first_seg": {
                    }
                    ,
                    "second_seg": {
                    }
                    ,
                    "param_first": []
                };
            }
            let bucket = self.dynamic_by_len[m][seg_key];
            bucket["all"].push(entry);

            let s1 = "";
            if entry.segments.len() > 1 {
                s1 = entry.segments[1];
            }
            if s1 == "" or s1.starts_with(":") or s1.starts_with("{") {
                bucket["param_first"].push(entry);
            } else {
                if not bucket["first_seg"].has(s1) {
                    bucket["first_seg"][s1] = [];
                }
                bucket["first_seg"][s1].push(entry);

                if entry.segments.len() > 2 {
                    let s2 = entry.segments[2];
                    if not s2.starts_with(":") and not s2.starts_with("{") {
                        let s12 = "{s1}/{s2}";
                        if not bucket["second_seg"].has(s12) {
                            bucket["second_seg"][s12] = [];
                        }
                        bucket["second_seg"][s12].push(entry);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return entry;
    }

    public fn get(self, path: string, handler: any) -> RouteEntry {
        return self.add("GET", path, handler);
    }

    public fn post(self, path: string, handler: any) -> RouteEntry {
        return self.add("POST", path, handler);
    }

    public fn put(self, path: string, handler: any) -> RouteEntry {
        return self.add("PUT", path, handler);
    }

    public fn delete(self, path: string, handler: any) -> RouteEntry {
        return self.add("DELETE", path, handler);
    }

    public fn patch(self, path: string, handler: any) -> RouteEntry {
        return self.add("PATCH", path, handler);
    }

    public fn options(self, path: string, handler: any) -> RouteEntry {
        return self.add("OPTIONS", path, handler);
    }

    // Register standard RESTful controller endpoints under `path`:
    // GET /, POST /, GET /:id, PUT /:id, PATCH /:id, DELETE /:id
    public fn resource(self, path: string, controller: any) -> Router {
        let base = path;
        if base.len() > 1 and base.ends_with("/") {
            base = base.slice(0, base.len() - 1);
        }
        let ctl = controller;
        self.get(base, fn(req) {
            return ctl.index(req);
        });
        self.post(base, fn(req) {
            return ctl.store(req);
        });
        self.get("{base}/:id", fn(req) {
            return ctl.show(req);
        });
        self.put("{base}/:id", fn(req) {
            return ctl.update(req);
        });
        self.patch("{base}/:id", fn(req) {
            return ctl.update(req);
        });
        self.delete("{base}/:id", fn(req) {
            return ctl.destroy(req);
        });
        return self;
    }

    public fn mount(self, prefix: string, sub_router: Router) -> Router {
        let sub_routes = sub_router.routes();
        let i = 0;
        while i < sub_routes.len() {
            let r = sub_routes[i];
            let entry = self.add(r.method, prefix + r.path, r.handler);
            for mw in r.middleware_list {
                entry.middleware(mw);
            }
            if r.route_name != "" {
                entry.name(r.route_name);
            }
            i += 1;
        }
        return self;
    }

    public fn group(self, options: map, callback: any) -> Router {
        let prev_prefix = self.group_prefix;
        let prev_name_prefix = self.group_name_prefix;
        let prev_mw = [];
        for m in self.group_middleware {
            prev_mw.push(m);
        }

        if options.has("prefix") {
            let pfx = string(options["prefix"]);
            self.group_prefix = "{self.group_prefix}{pfx}";
        }
        if options.has("name") {
            let npfx = string(options["name"]);
            self.group_name_prefix = "{self.group_name_prefix}{npfx}";
        }
        if options.has("middleware") {
            let mw = options["middleware"];
            if type_of(mw) == "list" {
                for m in mw {
                    self.group_middleware.push(m);
                }
            } else {
                self.group_middleware.push(mw);
            }
        }

        callback();

        self.group_prefix = prev_prefix;
        self.group_name_prefix = prev_name_prefix;
        self.group_middleware = prev_mw;
        return self;
    }

    public fn url(self, name: string, params: any = null) -> string {
        let entry = null;
        if self.named_routes.has(name) {
            entry = self.named_routes[name];
        } else {
            for r in self.routes_list {
                if r.route_name == name {
                    entry = r;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        if entry == null {
            throw "Router: named route '{name}' is not defined";
        }
        return build_route_url(entry, name, params);
    }

    public fn match_route(self, method: string, request_path: string) -> any {
        let m = string(method).upper();
        let target_path = request_path;
        if target_path.len() > 1 and target_path.ends_with("/") {
            target_path = target_path.slice(0, target_path.len() - 1);
        }

        let matched = self.match_route_exact(m, target_path);
        if matched == null and m == "HEAD" {
            matched = self.match_route_exact("GET", target_path);
        }
        return matched;
    }

    private fn match_route_exact(self, m: string, target_path: string) {
        if self.static_routes.has(m) {
            let m_statics = self.static_routes[m];
            if m_statics.has(target_path) {
                return {
                    "route": m_statics[target_path], "params": {
                    }
                };
            }
        }

        let a_parts = null;

        if self.dynamic_by_len.has(m) {
            a_parts = target_path.split("/");
            let seg_key = string(a_parts.len());
            let m_dyn = self.dynamic_by_len[m];
            if m_dyn.has(seg_key) {
                let hit = match_dynamic_bucket(m_dyn[seg_key], a_parts);
                if hit != null {
                    return hit;
                }
            }
        }

        if self.wildcard_routes.has(m) {
            if a_parts == null {
                a_parts = target_path.split("/");
            }
            return match_wildcard_list(self.wildcard_routes[m], a_parts);
        }

        return null;
    }

    public fn allowed_methods(self, request_path: string) -> list {
        let target_path = request_path;
        if target_path.len() > 1 and target_path.ends_with("/") {
            target_path = target_path.slice(0, target_path.len() - 1);
        }
        let found = [];
        for route in self.routes_list {
            if match_path(route.path, target_path) != null and not found.contains(route.method) {
                found.push(route.method);
            }
        }
        return found;
    }

    public fn dispatch(self, handler: any, req: any) -> any {
        let _ = self;
        try {
            return handler(req);
        } catch (e) {
            let msg = string(e).lower();
            if (
                msg.contains("argument") or msg.contains("arity") or msg.contains("parameter")
            ) and req.res != null {
                let out = handler(req, req.res);
                if out == null {
                    return req.res;
                }
                return out;
            }
            throw e;
        }
    }

    public fn routes(self) -> list {
        return self.routes_list;
    }
}

public let Route = new Router();

public fn route_url(name: string, params: any = null) -> string {
    return Route.url(name, params);
}
