// framework/Http/StaticFiles.matrix
// Static asset resolution, traversal guards, streaming, weak ETag, and MIME lookup.

import "framework/Http/Response";
import "framework/Foundation/Config";

public const MIME_TYPES = {
    "css": "text/css; charset=utf-8",
    "js": "application/javascript; charset=utf-8",
    "mjs": "application/javascript; charset=utf-8",
    "html": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
    "htm": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
    "json": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
    "txt": "text/plain; charset=utf-8",
    "png": "image/png",
    "jpg": "image/jpeg",
    "jpeg": "image/jpeg",
    "gif": "image/gif",
    "webp": "image/webp",
    "svg": "image/svg+xml",
    "ico": "image/x-icon",
    "woff": "font/woff",
    "woff2": "font/woff2",
    "ttf": "font/ttf",
    "otf": "font/otf",
    "pdf": "application/pdf",
    "map": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
    "xml": "application/xml; charset=utf-8",
    "webmanifest": "application/manifest+json",
    "mp4": "video/mp4",
    "webm": "video/webm",
    "mp3": "audio/mpeg",
    "wasm": "application/wasm",
    "zip": "application/zip"
};

const ASCII_LETTERS = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ";

// Reject parent-directory segments (".."), backslashes, NULs, encoded slashes,
// and leading Windows drive letters while permitting harmless dots/colons inside
// single filenames (e.g., "app..min.js", "v1:2.css").
/// Check whether a URL path attempts segment traversal (`..`), backslash escape, or Windows drive access.
public fn is_unsafe_path(path: string) -> bool {
    let raw = string(path);
    let lower = raw.lower();
    if raw.contains("\\") or raw.contains(chr(0))
    or lower.contains("%5c") or lower.contains("%00") or lower.contains("%2f") {
        return true;
    }
    let seg_idx = 0;
    for seg in raw.split("/") {
        if seg == "" {
            continue;
        }
        let seg_lower = seg.lower().replace("%2e", ".");
        if seg_lower == ".." {
            return true;
        }
        if seg_idx == 0 {
            let norm = seg.lower().replace("%3a", ":");
            if norm.len() == 2 and ASCII_LETTERS.contains(norm.slice(0, 1)) and norm.slice(1, 2) == ":" {
                return true;
            }
        }
        seg_idx = seg_idx + 1;
    }
    return false;
}

public fn content_type_for(path: string) -> string {
    let slash_parts = string(path).lower().split("/");
    let filename = slash_parts[slash_parts.len() - 1];
    let dot_parts = filename.split(".");
    if dot_parts.len() < 2 {
        return "application/octet-stream";
    }
    let ext = dot_parts[dot_parts.len() - 1];
    if MIME_TYPES.has(ext) {
        return MIME_TYPES[ext];
    }
    return "application/octet-stream";
}

public fn is_text_content_type(ct: string) -> bool {
    let lower = string(ct).lower();
    return lower.starts_with("text/")
    or lower.contains("application/json")
    or lower.contains("application/javascript")
    or lower.contains("application/xml")
    or lower.contains("application/manifest+json")
    or lower.contains("image/svg+xml");
}

// URL prefixes served as static assets from public/
public fn is_static_prefix(path: string) -> bool {
    let prefixes = [
    "/css/",
    "/js/",
    "/assets/",
    "/images/",
    "/fonts/",
    "/vendor/",
    "/uploads/"
    ];
    for p in prefixes {
        if path.starts_with(p) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

// Root-level static files commonly served from public/
public fn is_root_static_file(path: string) -> bool {
    return path == "/robots.txt" or path == "/sitemap.xml" or path == "/favicon.ico" or path == "/manifest.json" or path == "/site.webmanifest";
}

fn parse_nonneg_int(s: string) -> int {
    let t = string(s).trim();
    if t.len() == 0 {
        return -1;
    }
    let digits = "0123456789";
    let i = 0;
    while i < t.len() {
        if not digits.contains(t.slice(i, i + 1)) {
            return -1;
        }
        i = i + 1;
    }
    try {
        return int(t);
    } catch (_e) {
        return -1;
    }
}

/// Parse an HTTP single-byte `Range` header (`bytes=start-end`, `bytes=start-`, `bytes=-suffix`).
public fn parse_byte_range(header_val: string, total_size: int) -> map {
    let bad = {
        "valid": false, "start": 0, "end": -1
    };
    if total_size <= 0 {
        return bad;
    }
    let raw = string(header_val).trim().lower();
    if not raw.starts_with("bytes=") {
        return bad;
    }
    let spec = raw.slice(6, raw.len()).trim();
    if spec == "" or spec.contains(",") {
        return bad;
    }
    let parts = spec.split("-");
    if parts.len() != 2 {
        return bad;
    }
    let s_str = parts[0].trim();
    let e_str = parts[1].trim();
    if s_str == "" and e_str == "" {
        return bad;
    }
    if s_str == "" {
        let suf = parse_nonneg_int(e_str);
        if suf <= 0 {
            return bad;
        }
        let st = total_size - suf;
        if st < 0 {
            st = 0;
        }
        return {
            "valid": true, "start": st, "end": total_size - 1
        };
    }
    let st = parse_nonneg_int(s_str);
    if st < 0 or st >= total_size {
        return bad;
    }
    if e_str == "" {
        return {
            "valid": true, "start": st, "end": total_size - 1
        };
    }
    let en = parse_nonneg_int(e_str);
    if en < st {
        return bad;
    }
    if en >= total_size {
        en = total_size - 1;
    }
    return {
        "valid": true, "start": st, "end": en
    };
}

/// Lazy byte-range file stream compatible with `http_stream`.
public fn make_file_range_stream(
    file_path: string,
    start: int,
    length: int,
    chunk_size: int = 65_536
) -> any {
    let st = {
        "fh": null, "remaining": length, "done": length <= 0
    };
    return fn() {
        if st["done"] {
            return null;
        }
        if st["fh"] == null {
            let open_res = file_open(file_path, "rb");
            if open_res.is_err() {
                st["done"] = true;
                return null;
            }
            let fh = open_res.unwrap();
            if start > 0 {
                let sk = file_seek(fh, start, 0);
                if sk.is_err() {
                    file_close(fh);
                    st["done"] = true;
                    return null;
                }
            }
            st["fh"] = fh;
        }
        let rem = int(st["remaining"]);
        if rem <= 0 {
            file_close(st["fh"]);
            st["fh"] = null;
            st["done"] = true;
            return null;
        }
        let to_read = if rem < chunk_size {
            rem
        } else {
            chunk_size
        };
        let r = file_read(st["fh"], to_read);
        if r.is_err() {
            file_close(st["fh"]);
            st["fh"] = null;
            st["done"] = true;
            return null;
        }
        let chunk = r.unwrap();
        if chunk == null or len(chunk) == 0 {
            file_close(st["fh"]);
            st["fh"] = null;
            st["done"] = true;
            return null;
        }
        let got = len(chunk);
        st["remaining"] = rem - got;
        if int(st["remaining"]) <= 0 or got < to_read {
            file_close(st["fh"]);
            st["fh"] = null;
            st["done"] = true;
        }
        return chunk;
    };
}

// Lazy chunked binary file stream compatible with stdlib `http_stream`.
// Never opens the descriptor until the first body chunk is pulled (zero FD leak on HEAD).
public fn make_file_stream(file_path: string, chunk_size: int = 65_536) -> any {
    let st = {
        "fh": null, "done": false
    };
    return fn() {
        if st["done"] {
            return null;
        }
        if st["fh"] == null {
            let open_res = file_open(file_path, "rb");
            if open_res.is_err() {
                st["done"] = true;
                return null;
            }
            st["fh"] = open_res.unwrap();
        }
        let r = file_read(st["fh"], chunk_size);
        if r.is_err() {
            file_close(st["fh"]);
            st["fh"] = null;
            st["done"] = true;
            return null;
        }
        let chunk = r.unwrap();
        if chunk == null or len(chunk) == 0 {
            file_close(st["fh"]);
            st["fh"] = null;
            st["done"] = true;
            return null;
        }
        return chunk;
    };
}

public fn try_serve_static(path: string, req: any = null, debug: bool = false) -> any {
    if is_unsafe_path(path) {
        return null;
    }
    let clean = path;
    if clean.starts_with("/") {
        clean = clean.slice(1, clean.len());
    }

    let public_root = string(Config.get("app.paths.public", "public")).replace("\\", "/").trim();
    while public_root.ends_with("/") and public_root.len() > 1 {
        public_root = public_root.slice(0, public_root.len() - 1);
    }
    if public_root == "" {
        public_root = "public";
    }
    let p = "{public_root}/{clean}";
    if not file_exists(p) or not is_file(p) {
        return null;
    }

    let etag_val = "";
    let file_bytes = -1;
    let sz_res = file_size(p);
    let mt_res = file_mtime(p);
    if sz_res.is_ok() {
        file_bytes = int(sz_res.unwrap());
    }
    let range_hdr = if req != null {
        string(req.header("range", "")).trim()
    } else {
        ""
    };
    if sz_res.is_ok() and mt_res.is_ok() {
        let mt = mt_res.unwrap();
        etag_val = "W/\"{file_bytes}-{mt}\"";
        if req != null and range_hdr == "" {
            let inm = req.header("if-none-match", "");
            if inm != "" and inm == etag_val {
                let not_mod = response("", 304);
                not_mod.header("etag", etag_val);
                return not_mod;
            }
        }
    }

    let ct = content_type_for(p);
    let resp = null;
    if range_hdr != "" and file_bytes >= 0 {
        let parsed = parse_byte_range(range_hdr, file_bytes);
        if parsed["valid"] != true {
            let unsat = response("", 416);
            unsat.header("content-range", "bytes */{file_bytes}");
            unsat.header("accept-ranges", "bytes");
            return unsat;
        }
        let r_start = int(parsed["start"]);
        let r_end = int(parsed["end"]);
        let r_len = r_end - r_start + 1;
        resp = response("", 206);
        resp.stream(make_file_range_stream(p, r_start, r_len), ct, 206);
        resp.header("content-range", "bytes {r_start}-{r_end}/{file_bytes}");
    } elif req != null and req.method != "HEAD" and string(
        req.header("accept-encoding", "")
    ).lower().contains("gzip") and is_text_content_type(ct) and file_bytes >= 1_024 and file_bytes <= 2_097_152 {
        let res = read_file(p);
        if res.is_err() {
            return null;
        }
        let gz = gzip_compress(string(res.unwrap()));
        resp = response("", 200);
        resp.stream([gz], ct, 200);
        resp.header("content-encoding", "gzip");
        resp.header("vary", "Accept-Encoding");
    } elif file_bytes > 65_536 or not is_text_content_type(ct) {
        resp = response("", 200);
        resp.stream(make_file_stream(p), ct);
    } else {
        let res = read_file(p);
        if res.is_err() {
            return null;
        }
        resp = response(res.unwrap(), 200);
        resp.header("content-type", ct);
    }

    resp.header("accept-ranges", "bytes");
    if etag_val != "" {
        resp.header("etag", etag_val);
    }
    if debug {
        resp.header("cache-control", "no-cache");
    } else {
        resp.header("cache-control", "public, max-age=3600");
    }
    if clean.starts_with("uploads/") {
        resp.header("content-disposition", "attachment");
        resp.header("x-content-type-options", "nosniff");
        resp.header(
            "content-security-policy",
            string(Config.get("security.uploads_csp", "default-src 'none'; sandbox"))
        );
    }
    return resp;
}

// framework/Http/Validator.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Declarative Validation & Type-Coercion Engine.

import "framework/Http/ValidatorRules";

/// Declarative rule validator and type coercion engine (`required`, `int`, `email`, `unique`, etc.).
public class Validator {
    public let errors;
    public let failed;
    public let validated_data;

    fn new () {
        self.errors = {
        };
        self.failed = false;
        self.validated_data = {
        };
    }

    public fn add_error(self, field: string, message: string) {
        self.failed = true;
        if not self.errors.has(field) {
            self.errors[field] = [];
        }
        self.errors[field].push(message);
    }

    public fn fails(self) -> bool {
        return self.failed;
    }

    public fn passes(self) -> bool {
        return not self.failed;
    }

    public fn validated(self) -> map {
        return self.validated_data;
    }

    public fn error_messages(self) -> map {
        return self.errors;
    }

    public fn first_error(self, field: string) -> any {
        if self.errors.has(field) and self.errors[field].len() > 0 {
            return self.errors[field][0];
        }
        return "";
    }

    public fn first(self) -> string {
        for (_f, list_err) in self.errors {
            if list_err != null and list_err.len() > 0 {
                return string(list_err[0]);
            }
        }
        return "";
    }

    public fn all_errors(self) -> list {
        let all = [];
        for (_f, list_err) in self.errors {
            if list_err != null {
                for msg in list_err {
                    all.push(string(msg));
                }
            }
        }
        return all;
    }

    public fn error_summary(self, sep: string = " ") -> string {
        let all = self.all_errors();
        if all.len() == 0 {
            return "";
        }
        return all.join(sep);
    }

    public fn check(self, req: any, rules: map) -> Validator {
        let _ = self;
        let validator = new Validator();

        for (field, raw_rule) in rules {
            let field_rules = string(raw_rule).split("|");
            let val = req.input(field, null);
            let val_str = "";
            if val != null {
                val_str = string(val).trim();
            }

            let skip_if_empty = false;
            let coerce_to = "";
            for nr in field_rules {
                let nrt = nr.trim();
                if nrt == "nullable" {
                    skip_if_empty = true;
                } elif nrt == "integer" or nrt == "int" {
                    coerce_to = "int";
                } elif nrt == "numeric" or nrt == "float" {
                    if coerce_to == "" {
                        coerce_to = "float";
                    }
                } elif nrt == "boolean" or nrt == "bool" {
                    coerce_to = "bool";
                } elif nrt == "array" or nrt == "list" {
                    coerce_to = "list";
                } elif nrt == "map" {
                    coerce_to = "map";
                }
            }
            if skip_if_empty and (val == null or val_str == "") {
                validator.validated_data[field] = null;
                continue;
            }

            let field_had_error = false;
            for r in field_rules {
                let r_trimmed = r.trim();
                let rule_name = r_trimmed;
                let rule_arg = "";
                let colon_idx = r_trimmed.index_of(":");
                if colon_idx >= 0 {
                    rule_name = r_trimmed.slice(0, colon_idx).trim();
                    rule_arg = r_trimmed.slice(colon_idx + 1).trim();
                }
                if evaluate_rule(
                    req,
                    validator,
                    field,
                    val,
                    val_str,
                    coerce_to,
                    rule_name,
                    rule_arg
                ) {
                    field_had_error = true;
                }
            }

            if not field_had_error and val != null {
                if coerce_to == "int" and val_str != "" {
                    try {
                        validator.validated_data[field] = int(val_str);
                    } catch (_e) {
                        validator.validated_data[field] = val;
                    }
                } elif coerce_to == "float" and val_str != "" {
                    try {
                        validator.validated_data[field] = float(val_str);
                    } catch (_e) {
                        validator.validated_data[field] = val;
                    }
                } elif coerce_to == "bool" and val_str != "" {
                    let bl = val_str.lower();
                    validator.validated_data[field] = (bl == "true" or bl == "1" or bl == "yes");
                } elif type_of(val) == "string" {
                    validator.validated_data[field] = val_str;
                } else {
                    validator.validated_data[field] = val;
                }
            }
        }

        return validator;
    }
}

FILE: framework/Http/ValidatorRules.matrix
================================================
// framework/Http/ValidatorRules.matrix
// Field validation predicates and individual rule evaluators.

import "framework/Database/DB";

public fn is_valid_email(val_str: string) -> bool {
    if val_str.len() < 3 or val_str.len() > 254 {
        return false;
    }
    if val_str.contains(" ") or val_str.contains("\t") or val_str.contains("\r") or val_str.contains("\n") {
        return false;
    }
    let at_parts = val_str.split("@");
    if at_parts.len() != 2 {
        return false;
    }
    let user = at_parts[0];
    let domain = at_parts[1];
    if user.len() == 0 or user.len() > 64 {
        return false;
    }
    if user.starts_with(".") or user.ends_with(".") or user.contains("..") {
        return false;
    }
    if domain.len() == 0 or domain.len() > 255 {
        return false;
    }
    if domain.starts_with(".") or domain.ends_with(".") or domain.contains("..") or not domain.contains(".") {
        return false;
    }

    let allowed_user_chars = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789.!#$%&'*+-/=?^_`{|}~";
    for ch in user.split("") {
        if not allowed_user_chars.contains(ch) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    let dot_parts = domain.split(".");
    if dot_parts.len() < 2 {
        return false;
    }
    let allowed_domain_chars = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789-";
    for label in dot_parts {
        if label.len() == 0 or label.len() > 63 {
            return false;
        }
        if label.starts_with("-") or label.ends_with("-") {
            return false;
        }
        for ch in label.split("") {
            if not allowed_domain_chars.contains(ch) {
                return false;
            }
        }
    }

    let tld = dot_parts[dot_parts.len() - 1];
    if tld.len() < 2 {
        return false;
    }
    let digits = "0123456789";
    let all_digits = true;
    for ch in tld.split("") {
        if not digits.contains(ch) {
            all_digits = false;
        }
    }
    return not all_digits;
}

public fn is_valid_url(val_str: string) -> bool {
    if val_str.contains(" ") or val_str.contains("\t") or val_str.contains("\r") or val_str.contains("\n") {
        return false;
    }
    let rest = "";
    if val_str.starts_with("https://") {
        rest = val_str.slice(8, val_str.len());
    } elif val_str.starts_with("http://") {
        rest = val_str.slice(7, val_str.len());
    } else {
        return false;
    }
    if rest.len() == 0 or rest.starts_with("/") or rest.starts_with(".") {
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

public fn is_valid_uuid(val_str: string) -> bool {
    if val_str.len() != 36 {
        return false;
    }
    let parts = val_str.lower().split("-");
    if parts.len() != 5 {
        return false;
    }
    if parts[0].len() != 8 or parts[1].len() != 4 or parts[2].len() != 4 or parts[3].len() != 4 or parts[4].len() != 12 {
        return false;
    }
    let hex_chars = "0123456789abcdef-";
    for ch in val_str.lower().split("") {
        if not hex_chars.contains(ch) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

fn contains_only(s: string, allowed: string) -> bool {
    for ch in s.split("") {
        if not allowed.contains(ch) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

// Evaluate one validation rule. Returns true if a validation error was added.
public fn evaluate_rule(
    req,
    validator,
    field: string,
    val,
    val_str: string,
    coerce_to: string,
    rule_name: string,
    rule_arg: string
) -> bool {
    if rule_name == "required" {
        if val == null or (type_of(val) == "string" and val_str == "") or (type_of(val) == "list" and val.len() == 0) {
            validator.add_error(field, "The {field} field is required.");
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "min" {
        if val_str != "" and val_str.len() < int(rule_arg) {
            validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must be at least {rule_arg} characters.");
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "max" {
        if val_str != "" and val_str.len() > int(rule_arg) {
            validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must not exceed {rule_arg} characters.");
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "between" {
        if val_str != "" and rule_arg.contains(",") {
            let b_parts = rule_arg.split(",");
            let lo_s = b_parts[0].trim();
            let hi_s = b_parts[1].trim();
            let low = float(lo_s);
            let high = float(hi_s);
            if coerce_to == "int" or coerce_to == "float" {
                try {
                    let n = float(val_str);
                    if n < low or n > high {
                        validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must be between {lo_s} and {hi_s}.");
                        return true;
                    }
                } catch (_e) {
                    validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must be a number.");
                    return true;
                }
            } elif float(val_str.len()) < low or float(val_str.len()) > high {
                validator.add_error(
                    field,
                    "The {field} must be between {lo_s} and {hi_s} characters."
                );
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "min_value" or rule_name == "max_value" {
        if val_str != "" {
            let num = 0.0;
            try {
                num = float(val_str);
            } catch (_e) {
                validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must be a number.");
                return true;
            }
            if rule_name == "min_value" and num < float(rule_arg) {
                validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must be at least {rule_arg}.");
                return true;
            }
            if rule_name == "max_value" and num > float(rule_arg) {
                validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must not be greater than {rule_arg}.");
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "numeric" or rule_name == "float" {
        if val_str != "" {
            try {
                let _f = float(val_str);
            } catch (_e) {
                validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must be a number.");
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "integer" or rule_name == "int" {
        if val_str != "" {
            try {
                let _i = int(val_str);
            } catch (_e) {
                validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must be an integer.");
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "email" {
        if val_str != "" and not is_valid_email(val_str) {
            validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must be a valid email address.");
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "url" {
        if val_str != "" and not is_valid_url(val_str) {
            validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must be a valid URL.");
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "uuid" {
        if val_str != "" and not is_valid_uuid(val_str) {
            validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must be a valid UUID.");
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "regex" {
        if val_str != "" and rule_arg != "" and not regex_match(rule_arg, val_str) {
            validator.add_error(field, "The {field} format is invalid.");
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "array" or rule_name == "list" {
        if val != null and type_of(val) != "list" {
            validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must be a list.");
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "map" {
        if val != null and type_of(val) != "map" {
            validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must be a map.");
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "unique" or rule_name == "exists" {
        if val_str != "" and rule_arg != "" {
            let parts = rule_arg.split(",");
            let tbl = parts[0].trim();
            let col = field;
            if parts.len() > 1 and parts[1].trim() != "" {
                col = parts[1].trim();
            }
            let q = DB.table(tbl).where(col, "=", val_str);
            if rule_name == "unique" and parts.len() > 2 and parts[2].trim() != "" {
                q.where("id", "!=", parts[2].trim());
            }
            let hit = q.exists();
            if rule_name == "unique" and hit {
                validator.add_error(field, "The {field} has already been taken.");
                return true;
            }
            if rule_name == "exists" and not hit {
                validator.add_error(field, "The selected {field} does not exist.");
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "confirmed" {
        let conf_val = req.input("{field}_confirmation", null);
        let conf_str = "";
        if conf_val != null {
            conf_str = string(conf_val).trim();
        }
        if val_str != conf_str {
            validator.add_error(field, "The {field} confirmation does not match.");
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "same" {
        let same_val = req.input(rule_arg, null);
        let same_str = "";
        if same_val != null {
            same_str = string(same_val).trim();
        }
        if val_str != same_str {
            validator.add_error(field, "The {field} and {rule_arg} must match.");
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "in" {
        if val_str != "" {
            let matched = false;
            for a in rule_arg.split(",") {
                if a.trim() == val_str {
                    matched = true;
                }
            }
            if not matched {
                validator.add_error(field, "The selected {field} is invalid.");
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "boolean" or rule_name == "bool" {
        if val_str != "" {
            let bl = val_str.lower();
            if bl != "true" and bl != "false" and bl != "1" and bl != "0" and bl != "yes" and bl != "no" {
                validator.add_error(field, "The {field} field must be true or false.");
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "alpha" {
        if val_str != "" and not contains_only(
            val_str,
            "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ"
        ) {
            validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must only contain letters.");
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    if rule_name == "alphanumeric" or rule_name == "alpha_num" {
        if val_str != "" and not contains_only(
            val_str,
            "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789"
        ) {
            validator.add_error(field, "The {field} must only contain letters and numbers.");
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

// framework/Log/Log.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Logging Subsystem & Facade
//
//   Log.debug("cached product", map("id", 42));
//   Log.error("checkout failed", map("order", 7));
//   Log.write("warn", "...", ctx, "queue");   // explicit channel
//
// Channels live in config/logging.matrix. Drivers:
//   single â€” one fixed file (cfg "path")
//   daily  â€” per-UTC-day file: <path-without-.log>-YYYY-MM-DD.log
//
// Every line: [YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ] LEVEL: message {"ctx":"json"}
// Minimum level: channel "level" else logging.level, with the real
// environment variable SPID3R_LOG_LEVEL overriding both. Nothing is ever
// logged below the effective minimum.

import "framework/Foundation/Config";
import "framework/Foundation/Env";

/// Thread-safe structured logger supporting `single`, `daily`, and channel-based log retention pruning.
public class LogManager {
    private let levels;
    private let write_lock;

    fn new () {
        self.levels = {
            "debug": 100, "info": 200, "warn": 300, "error": 400
        };
        self.write_lock = mutex(true);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Facade API
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    public fn debug(self, message: string, context: any = null) -> bool {
        return self.write("debug", message, context, "");
    }
    public fn info(self, message: string, context: any = null) -> bool {
        return self.write("info", message, context, "");
    }
    public fn warn(self, message: string, context: any = null) -> bool {
        return self.write("warn", message, context, "");
    }
    public fn error(self, message: string, context: any = null) -> bool {
        return self.write("error", message, context, "");
    }

    // Core entry. Empty channel = the configured default channel.
    public fn write(
        self,
        level: string,
        message: string,
        context: any = null,
        channel: string = ""
    ) -> bool {
        let lvl = string(level).lower();
        if not self.levels.has(lvl) {
            lvl = "info";
        }

        let ch = channel;
        if ch == "" {
            let def = Config.get("logging.default", "single");
            ch = string(def);
        }

        // Minimum level: channel-specific -> global config -> SPID3R_LOG_LEVEL
        let min_name = Config.get("logging.level_env", "SPID3R_LOG_LEVEL");
        if env(min_name).is_some() and string(env(min_name).unwrap()) != "" {
            min_name = string(env(min_name).unwrap());
        } else {
            let chan_level = Config.get("logging.channels." + ch + ".level", null);
            if chan_level != null {
                min_name = string(chan_level);
            } else {
                min_name = string(Config.get("logging.level", "debug"));
            }
        }
        let min = 200;
        if self.levels.has(min_name) {
            min = int(self.levels[min_name]);
        }
        if int(self.levels[lvl]) < min {
            return false;  // filtered out
        }

        let cfg = self.channel_cfg(ch);
        let path = self.file_for(cfg);

        // Log-forging defense: strip CR/LF/NUL so an attacker-controlled
        // message can never inject fake log lines. Context is already safe
        // (json_write escapes newlines).
        let safe_msg = string(message);
        safe_msg = safe_msg.replace("\r", "\\r").replace("\n", "\\n");

        let upper_lvl = string(lvl).upper();
        let stamp_str = self.stamp(int(unix_time()));
        let line = "[{stamp_str}] {upper_lvl}: {safe_msg}";
        if context != null {
            let ctx_json = json_write(context);
            line = "{line} {ctx_json}";
        }
        line = "{line}\n";

        let _guard = self.write_lock.lock();
        let ok = false;
        let open_res = file_open(path, "a");
        if open_res.is_ok() {
            let fh = open_res.unwrap();
            try {
                file_lock(fh, true);
                let w = file_write(fh, line);
                file_flush(fh);
                ok = w.is_ok();
            } finally {
                file_unlock(fh);
                file_close(fh);
            }
        }
        self.write_lock.unlock();
        return ok;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Internals
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    fn channel_cfg(self, channel: string) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        let cfg = Config.get("logging.channels." + channel, null);
        if cfg == null or type_of(cfg) != "map" {
            // Unknown channel â€” fall back to the single-file channel config
            cfg = Config.get("logging.channels.single", {
                "driver": "single", "path": "storage/logs/app.log", "level": "debug"
            });
        }
        return cfg;
    }

    fn file_for(self, cfg) -> string {
        let path = "storage/logs/app.log";
        if cfg.has("path") {
            path = string(cfg["path"]);
        }
        let driver = "single";
        if cfg.has("driver") {
            driver = string(cfg["driver"]);
        }
        if driver == "daily" {
            let parts = self.date_parts(int(unix_time()));
            let base = path;
            if base.ends_with(".log") {
                base = base.slice(0, base.len() - 4);
            }
            let py = parts["y"];
            let pm = self.pad2(parts["m"]);
            let pd = self.pad2(parts["d"]);
            path = "{base}-{py}-{pm}-{pd}.log";
        }
        self.ensure_dir(path);
        return path;
    }

    // Create parent directories for a path like storage/logs/app.log
    fn ensure_dir(self, path: string) {
        let _ = self;
        let parts = string(path).split("/");
        if parts.len() < 2 {
            return;
        }
        let dir = parts[0];
        let i = 1;
        while i < parts.len() - 1 {
            dir = dir + "/" + parts[i];
            if not file_exists(dir) {
                dir_create(dir);
            }
            i = i + 1;
        }
    }

    // "2026-11-05T02:15:30Z" from a unix timestamp (UTC)
    fn stamp(self, ts: int) -> string {
        let p = self.date_parts(ts);
        let rem = ts - (ts / 86400) * 86400;
        let hh = rem / 3600;
        let mm = (rem - hh * 3600) / 60;
        let ss = rem - hh * 3600 - mm * 60;
        return string(p["y"]) + "-" + self.pad2(p["m"]) + "-" + self.pad2(p["d"])
        + "T" + self.pad2(hh) + ":" + self.pad2(mm) + ":" + self.pad2(ss) + "Z";
    }

    fn pad2(self, n: any) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        let s = string(int(n));
        if s.len() < 2 {
            return "0" + s;
        }
        return s;
    }

    // Civil date from days-since-epoch (Howard Hinnant's algorithm).
    // Safe with plain integer division: z is always positive here.
    fn date_parts(self, ts: int) -> map {
        let _ = self;
        let days = ts / 86_400;
        let z = days + 719468;
        let era = z / 146097;
        let doe = z - era * 146097;
        let yoe = (doe - doe / 1460 + doe / 36524 - doe / 146096) / 365;
        let y = yoe + era * 400;
        let doy = doe - (365 * yoe + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100);
        let mp = (5 * doy + 2) / 153;
        let d = doy - (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + 1;
        let m = 0;
        if mp < 10 {
            m = mp + 3;
        } else {
            m = mp - 9;
        }
        if m <= 2 {
            y = y + 1;
        }
        return {
            "y": y, "m": m, "d": d
        };
    }

    public fn prune(self, days: int = -1, dir_path: string = "storage/logs") -> int {
        let _ = self;
        let retention = days;
        if retention < 0 {
            let env_days = env("SPID3R_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS");
            if env_days.is_some() and string(env_days.unwrap()).trim() != "" {
                try {
                    retention = int(string(env_days.unwrap()).trim());
                } catch (_e) {
                    retention = -1;
                }
            }
        }
        if retention < 0 {
            retention = int(Config.get("logging.retention_days", 14));
        }
        if retention < 0 or not file_exists(dir_path) {
            return 0;
        }
        let listing = list_dir(dir_path);
        if listing.is_err() {
            return 0;
        }
        let cutoff = int(unix_time()) - retention * 86_400;
        let removed = 0;
        for name in listing.unwrap() {
            let n = string(name);
            if not n.ends_with(".log") {
                continue;
            }
            let p = "{dir_path}/{n}";
            if not is_file(p) {
                continue;
            }
            let mt = file_mtime(p);
            if mt.is_ok() and int(mt.unwrap()) <= cutoff {
                if file_delete(p).is_ok() {
                    removed = removed + 1;
                }
            }
        }
        return removed;
    }
}

public let Log = new LogManager();

FILE: framework/Mail/Mail.matrix

// framework/Mail/Mail.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Mail Subsystem & Facade
//
//   import "framework/Mail/Mail";
//   Mail.to("ada@example.com", "Welcome!", "Thanks for signing up.");
//   Mail.send(map("to","ada@example.com","subject","Hi","body","..."));
//
// Drivers (config/mail.matrix):
//   log  â€” renders the full message into the "mail" log channel
//          (storage/logs/mail.log-YYYY-MM-DD.log). Always available and
//          the default: perfect for development and staging.
//   smtp â€” intentionally fails fast at send time: the Spid3r runtime has
//          no outbound TCP API yet, so attempting SMTP would either hang
//          or lie. The error explains how to stay on the log driver.
//
// Security: the body is written verbatim; the envelope values (to/from)
// are logged as data, never concatenated into an SMTP session, so header
// injection via "\r\n" in addresses is inert with the log driver.

import "framework/Foundation/Config";
import "framework/Log/Log";
import "framework/Queue/Queue";

/// Mail delivery facade supporting channel logging, synchronous SMTP error guidance, and `queue()`.
public class Mailer {
    // Shorthand: Mail.to(to, subject, body)
    public fn to(self, address: string, subject: string, body: string) -> bool {
        return self.send({
            "to": address,
            "subject": subject,
            "body": body
        });
    }

    // Full send: map("to", ..., "subject", ..., "body", ...)
    // Optional keys: "from_address", "from_name" override config defaults.
    public fn send(self, message: any) -> bool {
        if message == null or type_of(message) != "map" or not message.has("to") {
            throw "mail: message requires a 'to' address";
        }
        let subject = "";
        if message.has("subject") {
            subject = string(message["subject"]);
        }
        let body = "";
        if message.has("body") {
            body = string(message["body"]);
        }

        // From: config default, overridable per message
        let from_address = "hello@spid3r.test";
        let from_name = "Spid3r";
        let from_cfg = Config.get("mail.from", null);
        if from_cfg != null and type_of(from_cfg) == "map" {
            if from_cfg.has("address") {
                from_address = string(from_cfg["address"]);
            }
            if from_cfg.has("name") {
                from_name = string(from_cfg["name"]);
            }
        }
        if message.has("from_address") {
            from_address = string(message["from_address"]);
        }
        if message.has("from_name") {
            from_name = string(message["from_name"]);
        }

        // Active mailer: env override -> config default
        let mailer_env = Config.get("mail.mailer_env", "SPID3R_MAIL_MAILER");
        let mailer = string(Config.get("mail.default", "log"));
        if env(mailer_env).is_some() and string(env(mailer_env).unwrap()) != "" {
            mailer = string(env(mailer_env).unwrap());
        }

        let cfg = Config.get("mail.mailers." + mailer, null);
        if cfg == null or type_of(cfg) != "map" {
            throw "mail: unknown mailer '" + mailer + "' (check config/mail.matrix)";
        }

        let driver = "log";
        if cfg.has("driver") {
            driver = string(cfg["driver"]);
        }

        if driver == "log" {
            let channel = "mail";
            if cfg.has("channel") {
                channel = string(cfg["channel"]);
            }
            Log.write("info", "[mail] delivered message", {
                "mailer": "log",
                "from": from_address,
                "from_name": from_name,
                "to": string(message["to"]),
                "subject": subject,
                "body": body
            }, channel);
            return true;
        }

        if driver == "smtp" {
            return self.send_smtp(
                cfg,
                from_address,
                from_name,
                string(message["to"]),
                subject,
                body
            );
        }

        throw "mail: unsupported mail driver '{driver}'";
    }

    fn smtp_expect(self, conn: any, expected_prefix: string, stage: string) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        let r = tcp_read(conn, 4096);
        if r.is_err() {
            let err = string(r.unwrap_err());
            throw "mail(smtp): read failed during {stage}: {err}";
        }
        let reply = string(r.unwrap());
        if not reply.starts_with(expected_prefix) {
            let trimmed = reply.trim();
            throw "mail(smtp): unexpected reply at {stage}: {trimmed}";
        }
        return reply;
    }

    fn smtp_cmd(self, conn, cmd: string, expected_prefix: string, stage: string) {
        let w = tcp_write(conn, "{cmd}\r\n");
        if w.is_err() {
            let err = string(w.unwrap_err());
            throw "mail(smtp): write failed during {stage}: {err}";
        }
        return self.smtp_expect(conn, expected_prefix, stage);
    }

    fn send_smtp(
        self,
        cfg,
        from_address: string,
        from_name: string,
        to_address: string,
        subject: string,
        body: string
    ) -> bool {
        let encryption = "";
        if cfg.has("encryption") and cfg["encryption"] != null {
            encryption = string(cfg["encryption"]).trim().lower();
        }
        if encryption == "tls" or encryption == "ssl" or encryption == "starttls" {
            throw "mail(smtp): outbound TLS/STARTTLS requires tls_connect (not yet exposed by Spid3r net stdlib) â€” use plain SMTP to a local relay/Mailpit or set SPID3R_MAIL_MAILER=log";
        }
        // Reject CRLF header injection in SMTP envelope/headers
        if from_address.contains("\r") or from_address.contains("\n") or to_address.contains("\r") or to_address.contains("\n") or subject.contains("\r") or subject.contains("\n") {
            throw "mail(smtp): illegal CR/LF in envelope or subject";
        }
        let host = "127.0.0.1";
        if cfg.has("host") and string(cfg["host"]).trim() != "" {
            host = string(cfg["host"]).trim();
        }
        let port = 1025;
        if cfg.has("port") and int(cfg["port"]) > 0 {
            port = int(cfg["port"]);
        }
        let timeout_ms = 10_000;
        if cfg.has("timeout_ms") and int(cfg["timeout_ms"]) > 0 {
            timeout_ms = int(cfg["timeout_ms"]);
        }
        let timeout_env = if cfg.has("timeout_env") and cfg["timeout_env"] != null {
            string(cfg["timeout_env"])
        } else {
            "SPID3R_MAIL_TIMEOUT_MS"
        };
        let env_t = env(timeout_env);
        if env_t.is_some() and string(env_t.unwrap()).trim() != "" {
            try {
                let parsed_t = int(string(env_t.unwrap()).trim());
                if parsed_t > 0 {
                    timeout_ms = parsed_t;
                }
            } catch (_e) {
            }
        }

        let cr = tcp_connect("{host}:{port}");
        if cr.is_err() {
            let err = string(cr.unwrap_err());
            throw "mail(smtp): failed to connect to {host}:{port}: {err}";
        }
        let conn = cr.unwrap();
        try {
            tcp_set_timeout(conn, timeout_ms);
            self.smtp_expect(conn, "220", "greeting");
            self.smtp_cmd(conn, "EHLO spid3rweb.local", "250", "EHLO");
            self.smtp_cmd(conn, "MAIL FROM:<{from_address}>", "250", "MAIL FROM");
            self.smtp_cmd(conn, "RCPT TO:<{to_address}>", "250", "RCPT TO");
            self.smtp_cmd(conn, "DATA", "354", "DATA");
            // Dot-stuff lines beginning with '.' per RFC 5321 Â§4.5.2
            let safe_body = body.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\n.", "\n..").replace("\n", "\r\n");
            let from_hdr = from_address;
            if from_name != "" and not from_name.contains("\r") and not from_name.contains("\n") {
                from_hdr = "{from_name} <{from_address}>";
            }
            let payload = "From: {from_hdr}\r\nTo: {to_address}\r\nSubject: {subject}\r\nContent-Type: text/plain; charset=utf-8\r\n\r\n{safe_body}\r\n.";
            self.smtp_cmd(conn, payload, "250", "DATA body");
            tcp_write(conn, "QUIT\r\n");
        } finally {
            tcp_close(conn);
        }
        return true;
    }

    public fn queue(self, message: any, queue_name: string = "mail", _delay_seconds: int = 0) -> bool {
        self.register_queue_handler("mail.send");
        return Queue.push("mail.send", message, queue_name);
    }

    public fn register_queue_handler(self, job_name: string = "mail.send") {
        let mailer = self;
        Queue.register(job_name, fn(payload) {
            return mailer.send(payload);
        });
    }
}

public let Mail = new Mailer();

// framework/Queue/Queue.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Queue Subsystem & Facade
//
//   import "framework/Queue/Queue";
//   Queue.push("mail.send", map("to", "ada@example.com", "subject", "Hi", "body", "..."));
//   Queue.size();                       // pending jobs on the active queue
//   Queue.work(1);                      // drain up to N jobs (worker loop)
//
// Handlers are registered in routes/queue.matrix:
//
//   Queue.job("mail.send", fn(data) { return Mail.send(data); });
//
// Drivers (config/queue.matrix):
//   database â€” rows in the `jobs` table; drained by `spid3r magic queue:work`
//   sync     â€” handlers execute immediately inside push()
//
// Failure policy: a handler exception releases the job for retry after
// retry_after seconds; after max_attempts failures the job is deleted and
// the error is written to the "queue" log channel.

import "framework/Foundation/Config";
import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Log/Log";

/// Background job queue dispatcher supporting atomic DB lease claiming (`retry_after` CAS) and sync mode.
public class QueueManager {
    private let handlers;

    fn new () {
        self.handlers = {
        };
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Handler registration (routes/queue.matrix)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    public fn job(self, name: string, handler: any) -> bool {
        self.handlers[name] = handler;
        return true;
    }

    public fn registered(self) -> list {
        return self.handlers.keys();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Configuration helpers
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    fn conn(self, override: string = "") -> map {
        let _ = self;
        // Precedence: explicit argument > SPID3R_QUEUE_CONNECTION > config default
        let active = string(Config.get("queue.default", "database"));
        let env_conn = env("SPID3R_QUEUE_CONNECTION").unwrap_or("");
        if env_conn != "" {
            active = env_conn;
        }
        if string(override) != "" {
            active = string(override);
        }
        let cfg = Config.get("queue.connections.{active}", null);
        if cfg == null or type_of(cfg) != "map" {
            throw "queue: unknown connection '{active}' (check config/queue.matrix)";
        }
        return cfg;
    }

    fn active_queue(self) -> string {
        let _ = self;
        let q_env = string(Config.get("queue.queue_env", "SPID3R_QUEUE"));
        let q_val = env(q_env).unwrap_or("");
        if q_val != "" {
            return q_val;
        }
        return "default";
    }

    fn table_name(self) -> string {
        let t = "jobs";
        let cfg = self.conn();
        if cfg.has("table") {
            t = string(cfg["table"]);
        }
        // SQL-identifier safety: config-sourced table names are interpolated
        // into raw SQL, so validate against [A-Za-z0-9_] before use.
        let allowed = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789_";
        let s = t.trim();
        if s.len() == 0 {
            return "jobs";
        }
        let i = 0;
        while i < s.len() {
            if not allowed.contains(s.slice(i, i + 1)) {
                throw "queue: invalid table identifier '" + s + "' (allowed: A-Z a-z 0-9 _)";
            }
            i = i + 1;
        }
        return s;
    }

    fn int_cfg(self, key: string, fallback: int) -> int {
        let cfg = self.conn();
        if cfg.has(key) {
            return int(cfg[key]);
        }
        return fallback;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Push
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Queue.push("handler.name", data, "queue", "connection") â€” queue and
    // connection arguments optional. With the "sync" connection the handler
    // runs inline during push; with "database" a row is persisted.
    public fn push(
        self,
        handler: string,
        data: any = null,
        queue: string = "",
        connection: string = ""
    ) -> bool {
        let cfg = self.conn(connection);
        let driver = "database";
        if cfg.has("driver") {
            driver = string(cfg["driver"]);
        }

        // The handler must be registered in THIS process only for the sync
        // driver, which runs it inline. With the database driver the worker
        // process resolves the handler at run time â€” a standalone producer
        // script must be able to push without importing the job registry.
        if driver == "sync" {
            if not self.handlers.has(handler) {
                throw "queue: handler not registered: {handler}";
            }
            return self.dispatch(handler, data);
        }

        let q = string(queue);
        if q == "" {
            q = self.active_queue();
            let cfg_q = "default";
            if cfg.has("queue") {
                cfg_q = string(cfg["queue"]);
            }
            if q == "default" {
                q = cfg_q;
            }
        }
        let now = int(unix_time());
        DB.table(self.table_name()).insert({
            "queue": q,
            "payload": json_write({
                "job": handler, "data": data
            }),
            "attempts": 0,
            "reserved_at": 0,
            "available_at": now,
            "created_at": now
        });
        Log.write("info", "[queue] pushed job", {
            "job": handler, "queue": q
        }, "queue");
        return true;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Worker
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Pending (available) jobs on a queue (unreserved or stale reservation).
    public fn size(self, queue: string = "") -> int {
        let q = string(queue);
        if q == "" {
            q = self.active_queue();
        }
        let now = int(unix_time());
        let cutoff = now - self.int_cfg("retry_after", 90);
        let tbl = self.table_name();
        let rows = DB.select(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM {tbl} WHERE queue = ? AND available_at <= ? AND (reserved_at = 0 OR reserved_at < ?)",
            [q,
            now,
            cutoff]
        );
        if rows.len() == 0 {
            return 0;
        }
        return int(rows[0]["n"]);
    }

    // Reserve the next available job using optimistic compare-and-swap on
    // `reserved_at` so both fresh (`reserved_at = 0`) and stale/crashed
    // (`reserved_at < cutoff`) jobs are safely reclaimed without races.
    public fn pop(self, queue: string = "") -> any {
        let q = string(queue);
        if q == "" {
            q = self.active_queue();
        }
        let now = int(unix_time());
        let retry_after = self.int_cfg("retry_after", 90);
        let cutoff = now - retry_after;
        let tbl = self.table_name();
        let rows = DB.select(
            "SELECT * FROM {tbl} WHERE queue = ? AND available_at <= ? AND (reserved_at = 0 OR reserved_at < ?) ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT 1",
            [q,
            now,
            cutoff]
        );
        if rows.len() == 0 {
            return null;
        }
        let job = rows[0];
        let prev_reserved = int(job["reserved_at"]);
        let next_attempts = int(job["attempts"]) + 1;
        let changes = DB.table(tbl)
        .where("id", job["id"])
        .where("reserved_at", prev_reserved)
        .update({
            "reserved_at": now, "attempts": next_attempts
        });
        if changes == 0 {
            return null;  // another worker won the race
        }
        job["attempts"] = next_attempts;
        job["reserved_at"] = now;
        return job;
    }

    // Run the next job. Returns true when a job ran, false when idle.
    public fn run_next(self, queue: string = "") -> bool {
        let job = self.pop(queue);
        if job == null {
            return false;
        }
        let max_attempts = self.int_cfg("max_attempts", 3);
        let job_id = job["id"];
        let job_attempts = job["attempts"];
        let tbl = self.table_name();
        if int(job_attempts) > max_attempts {
            DB.table(tbl).where("id", job_id).delete();
            Log.write("error", "[queue] job exceeded max_attempts via stale reservations and was discarded", {
                "id": job_id, "attempts": job_attempts
            }, "queue");
            return true;
        }
        let parsed = null;
        try {
            parsed = json_parse(string(job["payload"]));
        } catch (_e) {
            // Unparseable payload can never succeed â€” discard + log
            DB.table(tbl).where("id", job_id).delete();
            Log.write("error", "[queue] discarded job with corrupt payload", {
                "id": job_id
            }, "queue");
            return true;
        }
        if not parsed.has("job") {
            DB.table(tbl).where("id", job_id).delete();
            Log.write("error", "[queue] discarded job payload without a job name", {
                "id": job_id
            }, "queue");
            return true;
        }
        let handler = string(parsed["job"]);
        let data = null;
        if parsed.has("data") {
            data = parsed["data"];
        }

        try {
            self.dispatch(handler, data);
            DB.table(tbl).where("id", job_id).delete();
            Log.write("info", "[queue] job completed", {
                "job": handler, "id": job_id
            }, "queue");
            return true;
        } catch (e) {
            let max_attempts = self.int_cfg("max_attempts", 3);
            if int(job_attempts) >= max_attempts {
                DB.table(tbl).where("id", job_id).delete();
                Log.write("error", "[queue] job failed permanently", {
                    "job": handler,
                    "id": job_id,
                    "attempts": job_attempts,
                    "error": string(e)
                }, "queue");
            } else {
                let retry_after = self.int_cfg("retry_after", 90);
                DB.table(tbl).where("id", job_id).update({
                    "reserved_at": 0,
                    "available_at": int(unix_time()) + retry_after
                });
                Log.write("warn", "[queue] job failed, released for retry", {
                    "job": handler,
                    "id": job_id,
                    "attempts": job_attempts,
                    "error": string(e)
                }, "queue");
            }
            return true;
        }
    }

    // Drain up to max_jobs jobs (0 = until the queue is empty).
    // Returns the number of processed jobs.
    public fn work(self, max_jobs: int = 0, queue: string = "") -> int {
        let done = 0;
        while max_jobs == 0 or done < max_jobs {
            let ran = self.run_next(queue);
            if not ran {
                break;
            }
            done = done + 1;
        }
        return done;
    }

    // Long-running worker loop: drains jobs continuously and sleeps `poll_ms`
    // while idle, exiting cleanly when SIGINT/SIGTERM triggers shutdown_requested().
    public fn work_daemon(self, poll_ms: int = 500, queue: string = "") -> int {
        let interval = poll_ms;
        if interval < 10 {
            interval = 10;
        }
        let done = 0;
        while not shutdown_requested() {
            let ran = self.run_next(queue);
            if ran {
                done = done + 1;
            } else {
                sleep_ms(interval);
            }
        }
        return done;
    }

    // Invoke a registered handler.
    fn dispatch(self, handler: string, data) -> bool {
        if not self.handlers.has(handler) {
            throw "queue: handler not registered: " + handler;
        }
        let fn_handle = self.handlers[handler];
        return fn_handle(data);
    }
}

public let Queue = new QueueManager();

// framework/Session/Session.matrix
// Spid3rWeb General-Purpose Session & Flash Store
//
// Provides key-value session storage and one-request flash messages:
//   req.session().put("cart_count", 3);
//   req.session().flash("status", "Profile updated!");
//   let msg = req.session().get_flash("status", "");

import "framework/Cache/Cache";
import "framework/Foundation/Config";
import "framework/Http/CookieJar";
import "framework/Http/Response";

/// General-purpose key-value session and one-time flash store backed by Cache and HttpOnly cookies.
public class SessionStore {
    public let id;
    public let data;
    private let dirty;
    private let new_cookie;

    fn new (existing_id: string = "") {
        self.id = existing_id;
        self.data = {
        };
        self.dirty = false;
        self.new_cookie = false;

        if self.id != "" {
            let stored = Cache.get("session:" + self.id, null);
            if stored != null and type_of(stored) == "map" {
                self.data = stored;
            }
        }
    }

    fn ensure_id(self) {
        if self.id == "" {
            self.id = "sid_" + hex_encode(random_bytes(16).unwrap());
            self.new_cookie = true;
        }
    }

    public fn is_dirty(self) -> bool {
        return self.dirty;
    }

    public fn get(self, key: string, default_val: any = null) -> any {
        if self.data.has(key) {
            return self.data[key];
        }
        return default_val;
    }

    public fn put(self, key: string, value: any) -> SessionStore {
        self.ensure_id();
        self.data[key] = value;
        self.dirty = true;
        return self;
    }

    public fn has(self, key: string) -> bool {
        return self.data.has(key) and self.data[key] != null;
    }

    public fn forget(self, key: string) -> SessionStore {
        if self.data.has(key) {
            self.data.remove(key);
            self.dirty = true;
        }
        return self;
    }

    public fn all(self) -> map {
        return self.data;
    }

    public fn flush(self) -> SessionStore {
        self.data = {
        };
        if self.id != "" {
            Cache.forget("session:" + self.id);
        }
        self.dirty = true;
        return self;
    }

    // Store a flash value for the next request.
    public fn flash(self, key: string, value: any) -> SessionStore {
        self.ensure_id();
        let flash_bag = {
        };
        if self.data.has("__flash__") and type_of(self.data["__flash__"]) == "map" {
            flash_bag = self.data["__flash__"];
        }
        flash_bag[key] = value;
        self.data["__flash__"] = flash_bag;
        self.dirty = true;
        return self;
    }

    // Retrieve and consume a flash value (one-time read).
    public fn get_flash(self, key: string, default_val: any = null) -> any {
        if self.data.has("__flash__") and type_of(self.data["__flash__"]) == "map" {
            let flash_bag = self.data["__flash__"];
            if flash_bag.has(key) {
                let val = flash_bag[key];
                flash_bag.remove(key);
                self.data["__flash__"] = flash_bag;
                self.dirty = true;
                return val;
            }
        }
        return default_val;
    }

    public fn has_flash(self, key: string) -> bool {
        if self.data.has("__flash__") and type_of(self.data["__flash__"]) == "map" {
            return self.data["__flash__"].has(key);
        }
        return false;
    }

    public fn save(self) {
        if self.id != "" and self.dirty {
            let ttl = int(Config.get("session.lifetime", 86400));
            Cache.put("session:" + self.id, self.data, ttl);
            self.dirty = false;
        }
    }

    public fn commit_to_http(self, http_map: map, is_https: bool = false) {
        if not self.dirty {
            return;
        }
        self.save();
        if self.new_cookie and type_of(http_map) == "map" and http_map.has("headers") {
            let ttl = int(Config.get("session.lifetime", 86_400));
            let cookie_str = format_cookie_header(
                "spid3r_sid",
                self.id,
                "/",
                ttl,
                null,
                is_https
            );
            append_http_header(http_map, "set-cookie", cookie_str);
            self.new_cookie = false;
        }
    }
}

// framework/Testing/TestClient.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Fluent HTTP Integration Test Client & Assertion Wrapper
//
// Example:
//   let client = new TestClient(app);
//   client.acting_as({"id": 1, "name": "Ada"})
//         .post_json("/api/posts", {"title": "Spid3r MVC"})
//         .assert_created()
//         .assert_json_path("title", "Spid3r MVC");

import "framework/Auth/Jwt";

fn resolve_json_dot_path(root, dot_path: string) {
    if root == null or dot_path == "" {
        return root;
    }
    let parts = dot_path.split(".");
    let curr = root;
    for seg in parts {
        if curr == null {
            return null;
        }
        let t = type_of(curr);
        if t == "map" {
            if curr.has(seg) {
                curr = curr[seg];
            } else {
                return null;
            }
        } elif t == "list" {
            try {
                let idx = int(seg);
                if idx >= 0 and idx < curr.len() {
                    curr = curr[idx];
                } else {
                    return null;
                }
            } catch (_e) {
                return null;
            }
        } else {
            return null;
        }
    }
    return curr;
}

public class TestResponse {
    public let status;
    public let headers;
    public let body;
    public let body_bytes;
    public let stream_bytes;
    public let is_streamed;
    public let raw;

    fn new (raw_http) {
        self.raw = raw_http;
        self.status = int(raw_http["status"]);
        self.headers = raw_http["headers"];
        self.body_bytes = null;
        self.stream_bytes = 0;
        self.is_streamed = false;
        if raw_http.has("stream") and raw_http["stream"] != null {
            self.is_streamed = true;
            let src = raw_http["stream"];
            let st = type_of(src);
            let chunks = [];
            let text_parts = [];
            let total = 0;
            let has_binary = false;
            if st == "function" {
                while true {
                    let c = src();
                    if c == null or len(c) == 0 {
                        break;
                    }
                    total = total + len(c);
                    chunks.push(c);
                    if type_of(c) == "bytes" {
                        has_binary = true;
                    } else {
                        text_parts.push(string(c));
                    }
                }
            } elif st == "list" {
                for c in src {
                    if c != null and len(c) > 0 {
                        total = total + len(c);
                        chunks.push(c);
                        if type_of(c) == "bytes" {
                            has_binary = true;
                        } else {
                            text_parts.push(string(c));
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                total = len(src);
                chunks.push(src);
                if type_of(src) == "bytes" {
                    has_binary = true;
                } else {
                    text_parts.push(string(src));
                }
            }
            self.stream_bytes = total;
            self.body_bytes = bytes_concat(chunks);
            self.body = if has_binary and text_parts.len() == 0 {
                ""
            } else {
                text_parts.join("")
            };
        } elif raw_http["body"] == null {
            self.body = "";
        } else {
            self.body = string(raw_http["body"]);
        }
    }

    public fn json(self) -> any {
        let parsed = json_parse(self.body);
        if type_of(parsed) == "result" {
            if parsed.is_err() {
                throw "TestResponse.json(): response body is not valid JSON: " + self.body;
            }
            return parsed.unwrap();
        }
        return parsed;
    }

    public fn header_values(self, key: string) -> list {
        let lk = key.lower();
        let out = [];
        if self.headers == null {
            return out;
        }
        let ht = type_of(self.headers);
        if ht == "map" {
            for (hk, hv) in self.headers {
                if string(hk).lower() == lk {
                    out.push(string(hv));
                }
            }
            return out;
        }
        if ht == "list" {
            for entry in self.headers {
                if type_of(entry) == "map" and entry.has("name") and entry.has("value") {
                    if string(entry["name"]).lower() == lk {
                        out.push(string(entry["value"]));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return out;
    }

    public fn header(self, key: string, default_val: string = "") -> string {
        let vals = self.header_values(key);
        if vals.len() > 0 {
            return vals[0];
        }
        return default_val;
    }

    public fn assert_status(self, expected: int) -> TestResponse {
        if self.status != expected {
            throw "Assertion failed: expected HTTP status {expected}, got {self.status} (body: {self.body})";
        }
        return self;
    }

    public fn assert_ok(self) -> TestResponse {
        return self.assert_status(200);
    }

    public fn assert_created(self) -> TestResponse {
        return self.assert_status(201);
    }

    public fn assert_no_content(self) -> TestResponse {
        return self.assert_status(204);
    }

    public fn assert_not_found(self) -> TestResponse {
        return self.assert_status(404);
    }

    public fn assert_unauthorized(self) -> TestResponse {
        return self.assert_status(401);
    }

    public fn assert_forbidden(self) -> TestResponse {
        return self.assert_status(403);
    }

    public fn assert_unprocessable(self) -> TestResponse {
        return self.assert_status(422);
    }

    public fn assert_header(self, key: string, expected_val: string = "") -> TestResponse {
        let lk = key.lower();
        let vals = self.header_values(lk);
        if vals.len() == 0 {
            throw "Assertion failed: expected header '" + lk + "' to be present.";
        }
        if expected_val != "" {
            let matched = false;
            for v in vals {
                if v == expected_val {
                    matched = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if not matched {
                throw "Assertion failed: expected header '" + lk + "' to equal '" + expected_val + "', got '" + vals.join(", ") + "'";
            }
        }
        return self;
    }

    public fn assert_see(self, substring: string) -> TestResponse {
        if not self.body.contains(substring) {
            throw "Assertion failed: expected response body to contain '" + substring + "', got: " + self.body;
        }
        return self;
    }

    public fn assert_dont_see(self, substring: string) -> TestResponse {
        if self.body.contains(substring) {
            throw "Assertion failed: expected response body NOT to contain '" + substring + "'.";
        }
        return self;
    }

    public fn assert_json_path(self, dot_path: string, expected_val: any) -> TestResponse {
        let data = self.json();
        let actual = resolve_json_dot_path(data, dot_path);
        let exp_str = string(expected_val);
        let act_str = string(actual);
        if act_str != exp_str {
            throw "Assertion failed at JSON path '{dot_path}': expected '{exp_str}', got '{act_str}'";
        }
        return self;
    }
}

/// In-memory HTTP test client supporting `acting_as()` JWT auth, cookie jars, and fluent assertions.
public class TestClient {
    public let app;
    public let default_headers;
    public let cookies;

    fn new (app_instance) {
        self.app = app_instance;
        self.default_headers = {
        };
        self.cookies = {
        };
    }

    public fn with_header(self, key: string, value: string) -> TestClient {
        self.default_headers[key.lower()] = value;
        return self;
    }

    public fn with_headers(self, hdrs: map) -> TestClient {
        for (k, v) in hdrs {
            self.default_headers[string(k).lower()] = string(v);
        }
        return self;
    }

    public fn with_token(self, token: string) -> TestClient {
        self.default_headers["authorization"] = "Bearer " + token;
        return self;
    }

    public fn acting_as(self, user: map, ttl: int = 3600) -> TestClient {
        let token = Jwt.sign(user, ttl);
        return self.with_token(token);
    }

    public fn with_cookie(self, name: string, value: string) -> TestClient {
        self.cookies[name] = value;
        return self;
    }

    public fn request(self, method: string, path: string, body_str: string = "", extra_headers: map = {
    }) -> TestResponse {
        let merged_headers = {
        };
        for (dk, dv) in self.default_headers {
            merged_headers[dk] = dv;
        }
        if self.cookies.len() > 0 {
            let c_parts = [];
            for (ck, cv) in self.cookies {
                c_parts.push("{ck}={cv}");
            }
            merged_headers["cookie"] = c_parts.join("; ");
        }
        if extra_headers != null and type_of(extra_headers) == "map" {
            for (ek, ev) in extra_headers {
                merged_headers[string(ek).lower()] = string(ev);
            }
        }
        let raw_req = {
            "method": method.upper(),
            "path": path,
            "headers": merged_headers,
            "body": body_str
        };
        let raw_res = self.app.handle_request(raw_req);
        return new TestResponse(raw_res);
    }

    public fn get(self, path: string, headers: map = {
    }) -> TestResponse {
        return self.request("GET", path, "", headers);
    }

    public fn delete(self, path: string, headers: map = {
    }) -> TestResponse {
        return self.request("DELETE", path, "", headers);
    }

    public fn post(self, path: string, body_or_map: any = "", headers: map = {
    }) -> TestResponse {
        if type_of(body_or_map) == "map" {
            let pairs = [];
            for (k, v) in body_or_map {
                pairs.push("{k}={v}");
            }
            let h = {
                "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"
            };
            for (ek, ev) in headers {
                h[string(ek).lower()] = string(ev);
            }
            return self.request("POST", path, pairs.join("&"), h);
        }
        return self.request("POST", path, string(body_or_map), headers);
    }

    public fn post_json(self, path: string, payload: any = {
    }, headers: map = {
    }) -> TestResponse {
        let h = {
            "content-type": "application/json", "accept": "application/json"
        };
        for (ek, ev) in headers {
            h[string(ek).lower()] = string(ev);
        }
        return self.request("POST", path, json_write(payload), h);
    }

    public fn put_json(self, path: string, payload: any = {
    }, headers: map = {
    }) -> TestResponse {
        let h = {
            "content-type": "application/json", "accept": "application/json"
        };
        for (ek, ev) in headers {
            h[string(ek).lower()] = string(ev);
        }
        return self.request("PUT", path, json_write(payload), h);
    }

    public fn patch_json(self, path: string, payload: any = {
    }, headers: map = {
    }) -> TestResponse {
        let h = {
            "content-type": "application/json", "accept": "application/json"
        };
        for (ek, ev) in headers {
            h[string(ek).lower()] = string(ev);
        }
        return self.request("PATCH", path, json_write(payload), h);
    }
}
