// framework/View/BD.matrix
// SpiderBD v2 Template Engine

import "framework/Http/Response";
import {
    Config
}
from "framework/Foundation/Config";
import "TemplateEvaluator";
import "TemplateCompiler";

public fn html_escape(value: any) -> string {
    return tpl_html_escape(value);
}

/// SpiderBD v2 HTML template compiler, cache, and evaluator (`@if`, `@foreach`, `@csrf`, `@method`).
public class BD {
    public let views_path;
    public let template_cache;
    public let compiled_cache;
    public let shared_data;

    fn new (views_path: string = "resources/views") {
        self.views_path = views_path;
        self.template_cache = {
        };
        self.compiled_cache = {
        };
        self.shared_data = {
        };
    }

    public fn share(self, key: string, value: any) -> BD {
        self.shared_data[key] = value;
        return self;
    }

    public fn load_template(self, file_path: string) -> any {
        let use_cache = env("SPID3R_VIEW_CACHE").unwrap_or("").trim() == "1";
        if use_cache and self.template_cache.has(file_path) {
            return self.template_cache[file_path];
        }
        let res = read_file(file_path);
        if res.is_err() {
            return null;
        }
        let content = res.unwrap();
        if use_cache {
            self.template_cache[file_path] = content;
        }
        return content;
    }

    public fn resolve_view_path(self, view_name: string) -> string {
        let clean = view_name.replace(".", "/");
        let cfg_views = string(Config.get("app.paths.views", "resources/views")).replace("\\", "/").trim();
        while cfg_views.ends_with("/") and cfg_views.len() > 1 {
            cfg_views = cfg_views.slice(0, cfg_views.len() - 1);
        }
        if cfg_views == "" {
            cfg_views = "resources/views";
        }
        let candidates = [
        "{self.views_path}/{clean}.bd.html",
        "{self.views_path}/{clean}.html"
        ];
        if cfg_views != self.views_path {
            candidates.push("{cfg_views}/{clean}.bd.html");
            candidates.push("{cfg_views}/{clean}.html");
        }
        for p in candidates {
            if file_exists(p) {
                return p;
            }
        }
        return "{self.views_path}/{clean}.bd.html";
    }

    private fn merge_view_data(self, data) {
        let merged = {
        };
        for (sk, sv) in self.shared_data {
            merged[sk] = sv;
        }
        if data != null and type_of(data) == "map" {
            for (k, v) in data {
                merged[k] = v;
            }
        }
        return merged;
    }

    public fn render(self, view_name: string, data: any = null) -> string {
        let merged = self.merge_view_data(data);
        let file_path = self.resolve_view_path(view_name);
        let lines = compile_view_lines(self, file_path);
        if lines == null {
            let cur_dir = cwd().unwrap_or(".");
            throw "BD Error: View '{view_name}' not found at '{file_path}' (cwd: {cur_dir})";
        }
        let out_lines = [];
        self.eval_lines(lines, merged, out_lines);
        return evaluate_variables(out_lines.join("\n"), merged);
    }

    public fn render_string(self, template: string, data: any = null) -> string {
        let merged = self.merge_view_data(data);
        let lines = expand_form_directives(template).split("\n");
        let out_lines = [];
        self.eval_lines(lines, merged, out_lines);
        return evaluate_variables(out_lines.join("\n"), merged);
    }

    private fn eval_lines(self, lines, data, out) {
        let i = 0;
        while i < lines.len() {
            let raw = lines[i];
            let line = raw.trim();

            if line.starts_with("@if(") and line.ends_with(")") {
                let first_cond = extract_quoted_arg(line, "@if(");
                let branches = [];
                let current_lines = [];
                let current_cond = first_cond;

                i = i + 1;
                let depth = 0;
                while i < lines.len() {
                    let l2 = lines[i].trim();
                    if l2.starts_with("@if(") and l2.ends_with(")") {
                        depth = depth + 1;
                        current_lines.push(lines[i]);
                    } elif l2 == "@endif" {
                        if depth == 0 {
                            branches.push({
                                "cond": current_cond, "lines": current_lines
                            });
                            break;
                        }
                        depth = depth - 1;
                        current_lines.push(lines[i]);
                    } elif depth == 0 and l2.starts_with("@elif(") and l2.ends_with(")") {
                        branches.push({
                            "cond": current_cond, "lines": current_lines
                        });
                        current_cond = extract_quoted_arg(l2, "@elif(");
                        current_lines = [];
                    } elif depth == 0 and l2.starts_with("@elseif(") and l2.ends_with(")") {
                        branches.push({
                            "cond": current_cond, "lines": current_lines
                        });
                        current_cond = extract_quoted_arg(l2, "@elseif(");
                        current_lines = [];
                    } elif depth == 0 and l2 == "@else" {
                        branches.push({
                            "cond": current_cond, "lines": current_lines
                        });
                        current_cond = "__ELSE__";
                        current_lines = [];
                    } else {
                        current_lines.push(lines[i]);
                    }
                    i = i + 1;
                }
                i = i + 1;

                for b in branches {
                    let b_cond = string(b["cond"]);
                    if b_cond == "__ELSE__" or evaluate_truthiness(b_cond, data) {
                        self.eval_lines(b["lines"], data, out);
                        break;
                    }
                }
                continue;
            }

            if line.starts_with("@foreach(") and line.ends_with(")") {
                let expr = line.slice(9, line.len() - 1).trim();
                let loop_list_name = "";
                let loop_item_name = "";
                if expr.contains(" in ") {
                    let in_parts = expr.split(" in ");
                    if in_parts.len() == 2 {
                        loop_list_name = in_parts[1].trim();
                        loop_item_name = in_parts[0].trim();
                    }
                } else {
                    let parts = expr.split(" as ");
                    if parts.len() == 2 {
                        loop_list_name = parts[0].trim();
                        loop_item_name = parts[1].trim();
                    }
                }
                if loop_item_name.starts_with("$") {
                    loop_item_name = loop_item_name.slice(1, loop_item_name.len()).trim();
                }
                if loop_list_name.starts_with("$") {
                    loop_list_name = loop_list_name.slice(1, loop_list_name.len()).trim();
                }

                i = i + 1;
                let body_lines = [];
                let depth = 0;
                while i < lines.len() {
                    let l2 = lines[i].trim();
                    if l2.starts_with("@foreach(") and l2.ends_with(")") {
                        depth = depth + 1;
                        body_lines.push(lines[i]);
                    } elif l2 == "@endforeach" {
                        if depth == 0 {
                            break;
                        }
                        depth = depth - 1;
                        body_lines.push(lines[i]);
                    } else {
                        body_lines.push(lines[i]);
                    }
                    i = i + 1;
                }
                i = i + 1;

                let items = resolve_expression(loop_list_name, data);
                if items != null and type_of(items) == "list" {
                    let total_items = items.len();
                    let idx = 0;
                    while idx < total_items {
                        let item = items[idx];
                        let item_data = {
                        };
                        for (k, v) in data {
                            item_data[k] = v;
                        }
                        item_data[loop_item_name] = item;
                        item_data["loop"] = {
                            "index": idx,
                            "iteration": idx + 1,
                            "first": idx == 0,
                            "last": idx == total_items - 1,
                            "count": total_items
                        };
                        let body_out_lines = [];
                        self.eval_lines(body_lines, item_data, body_out_lines);
                        out.push(
                            evaluate_variables(body_out_lines.join("\n"), item_data)
                        );
                        idx += 1;
                    }
                }
                continue;
            }

            if line == "@else" or line == "@endif" or line == "@endforeach" or line.starts_with("@elif(") or line.starts_with("@elseif(") {
                i = i + 1;
                continue;
            }

            if raw.contains("@if(") and raw.contains("@endif") {
                out.push(expand_inline_conditionals(raw, data));
            } else {
                out.push(raw);
            }
            i = i + 1;
        }
    }
}

public let global_bd = new BD();

public fn view(view_name: string, data: any = null) -> Response {
    if data == null {
        data = {
        };
    }
    let rendered = global_bd.render(view_name, data);
    let res = response(rendered, 200);
    res.header("content-type", "text/html; charset=utf-8");
    return res;
}

public fn bd_view(view_name: string, data: any = null) -> Response {
    return view(view_name, data);
}

// framework/View/TemplateCompiler.matrix
// Layout inheritance, @section/@yield, @include, and form directive expansion
// extracted from BD.matrix.

import "TemplateEvaluator";

public fn extract_quoted_arg(line: string, prefix: string) -> string {
    let inner = line.slice(prefix.len(), line.len());
    if inner.ends_with(")") {
        inner = inner.slice(0, inner.len() - 1);
    }
    inner = inner.trim();
    if (inner.starts_with("'") and inner.ends_with("'")) or (
        inner.starts_with("\"") and inner.ends_with("\"")
    ) {
        return inner.slice(1, inner.len() - 1);
    }
    return inner;
}

public fn expand_form_directives(text: string) -> string {
    let out_lines = [];
    for raw_line in text.split("\n") {
        let line = raw_line;
        if line.contains("@csrf") {
            line = line.replace(
                "@csrf",
                "<input type=\"hidden\" name=\"_csrf\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">"
            );
        }
        while line.contains("@method(") {
            let m_start = line.index_of("@method(");
            let after_m = line.slice(m_start, line.len());
            let m_end = after_m.index_of(")");
            if m_end > 8 {
                let call_str = after_m.slice(0, m_end + 1);
                let verb = tpl_html_escape(extract_quoted_arg(call_str, "@method(").upper());
                line = line.replace(
                    call_str,
                    "<input type=\"hidden\" name=\"_method\" value=\"{verb}\">"
                );
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
        out_lines.push(line);
    }
    return out_lines.join("\n");
}

public fn parse_sections(template: string) -> map {
    let sections = {
    };
    let current_section = "";
    let current_content = [];
    let in_section = false;

    for raw_line in template.split("\n") {
        let line = raw_line.trim();
        if line.starts_with("@section(") and line.contains(",") and line.ends_with(")") {
            let inner = line.slice(9, line.len() - 1).trim();
            let comma_idx = inner.index_of(",");
            if comma_idx > 0 {
                let sec_name = inner.slice(0, comma_idx).trim().replace("'", "").replace("\"", "");
                let raw_val = inner.slice(comma_idx + 1, inner.len()).trim();
                let sec_val = raw_val;
                if (raw_val.starts_with("'") and raw_val.ends_with("'")) or (
                    raw_val.starts_with("\"") and raw_val.ends_with("\"")
                ) {
                    sec_val = raw_val.slice(1, raw_val.len() - 1);
                } else {
                    sec_val = "{{{raw_val}}}";
                }
                sections[sec_name] = sec_val;
            }
            continue;
        }
        if line.starts_with("@section(") and line.ends_with(")") {
            current_section = extract_quoted_arg(line, "@section(");
            current_content = [];
            in_section = true;
            continue;
        }
        if in_section and (line == "@endsection" or line == "@stop") {
            sections[current_section] = current_content.join("\n");
            in_section = false;
            current_section = "";
            continue;
        }
        if in_section {
            current_content.push(raw_line);
        }
    }
    return sections;
}

public fn apply_yields(layout: string, sections: map) -> string {
    let out_lines = [];
    for raw_line in layout.split("\n") {
        let line = raw_line;
        while line.contains("@yield(") {
            let start_idx = line.index_of("@yield(");
            let after = line.slice(start_idx, line.len());
            let rel_end = after.index_of(")");
            if start_idx >= 0 and rel_end > 0 {
                let yield_call = after.slice(0, rel_end + 1);
                let inner = yield_call.slice(7, yield_call.len() - 1).trim();
                let replacement = "";
                let comma_idx = inner.index_of(",");
                if comma_idx > 0 {
                    let sec_name = inner.slice(0, comma_idx).trim().replace("'", "").replace("\"", "");
                    let def_raw = inner.slice(comma_idx + 1, inner.len()).trim();
                    let def_val = def_raw;
                    if (def_raw.starts_with("'") and def_raw.ends_with("'")) or (
                        def_raw.starts_with("\"") and def_raw.ends_with("\"")
                    ) {
                        def_val = def_raw.slice(1, def_raw.len() - 1);
                    }
                    if sections.has(sec_name) {
                        replacement = sections[sec_name];
                    } else {
                        replacement = def_val;
                    }
                } else {
                    let sec_name = inner.trim().replace("'", "").replace("\"", "");
                    if sections.has(sec_name) {
                        replacement = sections[sec_name];
                    }
                }
                line = line.replace(yield_call, replacement);
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
        out_lines.push(line);
    }
    return out_lines.join("\n");
}

public fn apply_includes(bd: any, template: string, depth: int) -> string {
    if depth > 5 {
        return template;
    }
    let out_lines = [];
    for raw_line in template.split("\n") {
        let line = raw_line.trim();
        if line.starts_with("@include(") and line.ends_with(")") {
            let partial_name = extract_quoted_arg(line, "@include(");
            let p_file = bd.resolve_view_path(partial_name);
            let p_content = bd.load_template(p_file);
            if p_content != null {
                out_lines.push(apply_includes(bd, p_content, depth + 1));
            } else {
                out_lines.push("<!-- BD Include Error: {partial_name} -->");
            }
        } else {
            out_lines.push(raw_line);
        }
    }
    return out_lines.join("\n");
}

public fn compile_view_lines(bd: any, file_path: string) -> any {
    let cur_mtime = 0;
    let mt_res = file_mtime(file_path);
    if mt_res.is_ok() {
        cur_mtime = int(mt_res.unwrap());
    }
    if bd.compiled_cache.has(file_path) {
        let entry = bd.compiled_cache[file_path];
        if int(entry["mtime"]) == cur_mtime and cur_mtime > 0 {
            return entry["lines"];
        }
    }

    let template = bd.load_template(file_path);
    if template == null {
        return null;
    }

    let layout_name = "";
    for line in template.split("\n") {
        let s = line.trim();
        if s.starts_with("@extends(") and s.ends_with(")") {
            layout_name = extract_quoted_arg(s, "@extends(");
            break;
        }
    }

    let sections = parse_sections(template);
    let output = template;
    if layout_name != "" {
        let layout_file = bd.resolve_view_path(layout_name);
        let layout_res = bd.load_template(layout_file);
        if layout_res != null {
            output = apply_yields(layout_res, sections);
        }
    }

    output = apply_includes(bd, output, 0);
    output = expand_form_directives(output);

    let lines = output.split("\n");
    bd.compiled_cache[file_path] = {
        "mtime": cur_mtime, "lines": lines
    };
    return lines;
}



================================================
FILE: framework/View/TemplateEvaluator.matrix

// framework/View/TemplateEvaluator.matrix
// Runtime expression evaluation, property traversal, conditionals, and escaping
// extracted from BD.matrix.

public fn tpl_html_escape(value: any) -> string {
    if value == null {
        return "";
    }
    return string(value)
    .replace("&", "&amp;")
    .replace("<", "&lt;")
    .replace(">", "&gt;")
    .replace("\"", "&quot;")
    .replace("'", "&#039;");
}

public fn resolve_property(obj: any, prop: string) -> any {
    if obj == null {
        return null;
    }
    let t = type_of(obj);
    if t == "map" {
        if obj.has(prop) {
            return obj[prop];
        }
        return null;
    }
    if t == "instance" {
        try {
            let val = obj.get(prop);
            if val != null {
                return val;
            }
        } catch (_e1) {
        }
        try {
            let attrs = obj.attributes;
            if attrs != null and type_of(attrs) == "map" and attrs.has(prop) {
                return attrs[prop];
            }
        } catch (_e2) {
        }
        return null;
    }
    if t == "list" {
        try {
            let idx = int(prop);
            if idx >= 0 and idx < obj.len() {
                return obj[idx];
            }
        } catch (_e5) {
        }
        return null;
    }
    return null;
}

public fn resolve_dot_path(path: string, data: any) -> any {
    if path == "" or data == null {
        return null;
    }
    if type_of(data) == "map" and data.has(path) {
        return data[path];
    }
    let parts = path.split(".");
    let first = parts[0].trim();
    if type_of(data) != "map" or not data.has(first) {
        return null;
    }
    let curr = data[first];
    let i = 1;
    while i < parts.len() {
        let seg = parts[i].trim();
        if curr == null {
            return null;
        }
        curr = resolve_property(curr, seg);
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return curr;
}

public fn resolve_expression(raw_expr: string, data: any) -> any {
    let expr = raw_expr.trim();
    if expr.starts_with("$") {
        expr = expr.slice(1, expr.len()).trim();
    }
    if expr == "" {
        return null;
    }
    return resolve_dot_path(expr, data);
}

public fn resolve_condition_operand(raw_token: string, data: any) -> any {
    let tok = raw_token.trim();
    if tok == "" {
        return null;
    }
    if (tok.starts_with("'") and tok.ends_with("'")) or (
        tok.starts_with("\"") and tok.ends_with("\"")
    ) {
        return tok.slice(1, tok.len() - 1);
    }
    if tok == "true" {
        return true;
    }
    if tok == "false" {
        return false;
    }
    if tok == "null" or tok == "nil" {
        return null;
    }
    let first_ch = tok.slice(0, 1);
    if "0123456789-".contains(first_ch) {
        if tok.contains(".") {
            try {
                return float(tok);
            } catch (_e) {
            }
        } else {
            try {
                return int(tok);
            } catch (_e2) {
            }
        }
    }
    return resolve_expression(tok, data);
}

public fn compare_values(left: any, op: string, right: any) -> bool {
    if op == "==" {
        if left == null and right == null {
            return true;
        }
        if left == null or right == null {
            return false;
        }
        return string(left) == string(right);
    }
    if op == "!=" {
        if left == null and right == null {
            return false;
        }
        if left == null or right == null {
            return true;
        }
        return string(left) != string(right);
    }
    if left == null or right == null {
        return false;
    }
    try {
        let lf = float(string(left));
        let rf = float(string(right));
        if op == ">=" {
            return lf >= rf;
        }
        if op == "<=" {
            return lf <= rf;
        }
        if op == ">" {
            return lf > rf;
        }
        if op == "<" {
            return lf < rf;
        }
    } catch (_e) {
        let ls = string(left);
        let rs = string(right);
        if op == ">=" {
            return ls >= rs;
        }
        if op == "<=" {
            return ls <= rs;
        }
        if op == ">" {
            return ls > rs;
        }
        if op == "<" {
            return ls < rs;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

public fn evaluate_truthiness(cond_expr: string, data: any) -> bool {
    let effective = string(cond_expr).trim();
    if effective == "" {
        return false;
    }
    if effective.contains(" or ") {
        for part in effective.split(" or ") {
            if evaluate_truthiness(part, data) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    if effective.contains(" || ") {
        for part in effective.split(" || ") {
            if evaluate_truthiness(part, data) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    if effective.contains(" and ") {
        for part in effective.split(" and ") {
            if not evaluate_truthiness(part, data) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
    if effective.contains(" && ") {
        for part in effective.split(" && ") {
            if not evaluate_truthiness(part, data) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
    if effective.starts_with("not ") {
        return not evaluate_truthiness(
            effective.slice(4, effective.len()).trim(),
            data
        );
    }
    if effective.starts_with("!") and not effective.starts_with("!=") {
        return not evaluate_truthiness(
            effective.slice(1, effective.len()).trim(),
            data
        );
    }

    for op in ["==", "!=", ">=", "<=", ">", "<"] {
        let op_idx = effective.index_of(op);
        if op_idx > 0 {
            let lhs_val = resolve_condition_operand(
                effective.slice(0, op_idx).trim(),
                data
            );
            let rhs_val = resolve_condition_operand(
                effective.slice(op_idx + op.len(), effective.len()).trim(),
                data
            );
            return compare_values(lhs_val, op, rhs_val);
        }
    }

    let val = resolve_condition_operand(effective, data);
    if val == null {
        return false;
    }
    let t = type_of(val);
    if t == "bool" {
        return val == true;
    }
    if t == "string" {
        return val != "" and val != "0" and val != "false";
    }
    if t == "int" {
        return val > 0;
    }
    if t == "float" {
        return val > 0.0;
    }
    if t == "list" or t == "map" {
        return val.len() > 0;
    }
    return true;
}

public fn expand_inline_conditionals(text: string, data: any) -> string {
    let cur = text;
    let guard = 0;
    while cur.contains("@if(") and cur.contains("@endif") and guard < 10 {
        guard += 1;
        let start = cur.index_of("@if(");
        let after_if = cur.slice(start + 4, cur.len());
        let close_paren = after_if.index_of(")");
        let endif_rel = after_if.index_of("@endif");
        if close_paren < 0 or endif_rel < 0 or close_paren > endif_rel {
            break;
        }
        let cond_str = after_if.slice(0, close_paren).trim();
        let body_str = after_if.slice(close_paren + 1, endif_rel);
        let true_str = body_str;
        let false_str = "";
        let else_idx = body_str.index_of("@else");
        if else_idx >= 0 {
            true_str = body_str.slice(0, else_idx);
            false_str = body_str.slice(else_idx + 5, body_str.len());
        }
        let chosen = false_str;
        if evaluate_truthiness(cond_str, data) {
            chosen = true_str;
        }
        let prefix = cur.slice(0, start);
        let suffix = after_if.slice(endif_rel + 6, after_if.len());
        cur = "{prefix}{chosen}{suffix}";
    }
    return cur;
}

public fn evaluate_variables(text: string, data: any) -> string {
    let result = "";
    let remaining = text;

    while true {
        let raw_idx = remaining.index_of("{!!");
        let esc_idx = remaining.index_of("{{");
        if raw_idx < 0 and esc_idx < 0 {
            result = result + remaining;
            break;
        }
        let is_raw = raw_idx >= 0 and (esc_idx < 0 or raw_idx < esc_idx);
        let start_idx = esc_idx;
        let open_len = 2;
        let close_tag = "}}";
        if is_raw {
            start_idx = raw_idx;
            open_len = 3;
            close_tag = "!!}";
        }
        result = result + remaining.slice(0, start_idx);
        let after_open = remaining.slice(start_idx + open_len, remaining.len());
        let end_idx = after_open.index_of(close_tag);
        if end_idx < 0 {
            let open_str = "{{";
            if is_raw {
                open_str = "{!!";
            }
            result = result + open_str + after_open;
            break;
        }
        let expr = after_open.slice(0, end_idx).trim();
        remaining = after_open.slice(end_idx + close_tag.len(), after_open.len());

        let val = resolve_expression(expr, data);
        let s_val = "";
        if val != null {
            s_val = string(val);
        }
        if is_raw {
            result = result + s_val;
        } else {
            result = result + tpl_html_escape(s_val);
        }
    }

    return result;
}



================================================
FILE: framework/WebSocket/WsServer.matrix
================================================
// framework/WebSocket/WsServer.matrix
// Spid3rWeb WebSocket Server
//
// Live, bidirectional WebSocket connections: per-connection protocols AND
// cross-connection fan-out (broadcast, chat relays, notifications) — the
// runtime's independent write half makes concurrent send-while-reading
// safe (requires a runtime from 2026-11-06 or later; see ARCHITECTURE
// NOTES).
//
//   import "framework/WebSocket/WsServer";
//
//   let ws = new WsServer();
//   ws.on_connection(fn(h) {
//       // h is a WebSocket handle: ws_send / ws_recv / ws_close
//       while true {
//           let r = ws_recv(h);
//           if r.is_err() { break; }        // timeout / protocol error
//           let msg = r.unwrap();
//           if msg == null { break; }       // peer closed cleanly
//           ws_send(h, "echo: " + msg);
//       }
//   });
//   ws.listen("127.0.0.1:9001");
//
// Run it alongside the HTTP app (a second process or a spawned task):
//   spawn fn() { ws.listen("127.0.0.1:9001"); return 0; };
//
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
// ARCHITECTURE NOTES (read before extending)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
// - Handshake: the runtime native ws_accept() consumes the client's HTTP
//   Upgrade request and performs the RFC 6455 handshake itself (the runtime
//   has no SHA-1 native, so the framework cannot compute the accept key).
//   Consequence: the request path/headers are NOT visible to framework
//   code. One WsServer per port; route by port, not by path.
// - Cross-connection messaging (broadcast, chat relays, fan-out) works:
//   the runtime gives every plain TCP connection an independent write
//   half, so ws_send/ws_close never block behind another task's ws_recv,
//   and all frame writers serialize on the write-half mutex (frames never
//   interleave). Requires a runtime built AFTER the reader/writer split
//   (spid3r net.rs, 2026-11-06); on older runtimes broadcast will hang.
// - Authentication: use the first-message pattern — require the client to
//   send a credential (session token / signed ticket from the HTTP app)
//   as its first frame and close on anything else. `opts.timeout_ms`
//   bounds how long a silent connection may linger (enforced by the
//   underlying socket timeout, so a client that never authenticates is
//   reaped, not leaked).
// - Frames: text frames arrive as strings via ws_recv (binary as bytes,
//   pings auto-answered, fragments reassembled — all native-side). The
//   runtime caps a single frame at 8 MiB.
// - Concurrency: every connection runs in its own spawned task, exactly
//   like the HTTP server. The client registry is mutex-guarded and only
//   tracks liveness — broadcast() is safe from any task.

/// Concurrent RFC 6455 WebSocket server with client caps, Origin allowlists, frame bounds, and broadcast.
public class WsServer {
    // opts.timeout_ms     — per-connection socket timeout in ms (idle cap
    //                       for unauthenticated or dead connections)
    // opts.watch_signals  — process-global Ctrl+C/SIGTERM stops the accept
    //                       loop (true by default; disable when embedding
    //                       multiple servers in one process)
    public let opts;
    private let handler;
    private let listener;
    private let clients;  // mutex(map: handle-id -> handle)

    fn new () {
        self.opts = {
            "timeout_ms": 300_000,
            "watch_signals": true,
            "max_clients": 1_000,
            "max_message_bytes": 65_536,
            "allowed_origins": []
        };
        self.handler = null;
        self.listener = -1;
        self.clients = mutex({
        });
    }

    public fn is_origin_allowed(self, origin: string) -> bool {
        let allowed = null;
        if self.opts.has("allowed_origins") {
            allowed = self.opts["allowed_origins"];
        }
        if allowed == null or type_of(allowed) != "list" or allowed.len() == 0 {
            return true;
        }
        let target = string(origin).trim().lower();
        if target == "" {
            return false;
        }
        for entry in allowed {
            let pat = string(entry).trim().lower();
            if pat == "*" or pat == target {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    public fn require_origin(self, h: any, origin: string) -> bool {
        if self.is_origin_allowed(origin) {
            return true;
        }
        ws_close(h);
        return false;
    }

    public fn recv(self, h: any) -> any {
        let r = ws_recv(h);
        if r.is_err() {
            return r;
        }
        let msg = r.unwrap();
        let max_bytes = 65_536;
        if self.opts.has("max_message_bytes") and self.opts["max_message_bytes"] != null {
            max_bytes = int(self.opts["max_message_bytes"]);
        }
        if msg != null and max_bytes > 0 and len(msg) > max_bytes {
            ws_close(h);
            return err("websocket: message exceeds max_message_bytes ({max_bytes})");
        }
        return r;
    }

    // Register the per-connection handler: fn(handle). The handler owns the
    // connection loop; when it returns, the framework closes the socket.
    public fn on_connection(self, handler: any) -> WsServer {
        self.handler = handler;
        return self;
    }

    // Bind without serving. Returns the real address — matters when `addr`
    // uses port 0 ("127.0.0.1:0" picks a free port; tests rely on this).
    public fn bind(self, addr: string) -> string {
        let r = tcp_listen(addr);
        if r.is_err() {
            throw r.unwrap_err();
        }
        self.listener = r.unwrap();
        return tcp_local_addr(self.listener).unwrap();
    }

    // Accept loop. `max_connections < 0` serves forever; otherwise returns
    // after accepting that many connections (tests use this).
    public fn serve(self, max_connections: int) -> int {
        if self.handler == null {
            throw "WsServer: register a handler with on_connection() first";
        }
        if self.listener < 0 {
            throw "WsServer: call bind() first";
        }
        let timeout_ms = int(self.opts["timeout_ms"]);
        let watch = true;
        if self.opts.has("watch_signals") {
            watch = self.opts["watch_signals"];
        }
        let srv = self;
        let accepted = 0;
        while max_connections < 0 or accepted < max_connections {
            if watch and shutdown_requested() {
                tcp_close(self.listener);
                break;
            }
            // Bounded accept so shutdown stays deterministic on every
            // platform (same pattern as the stdlib HTTP server loop).
            let c = tcp_accept_timeout(self.listener, 50);
            if c == null {
                continue;
            }
            if c.is_err() {
                break;
            }
            let conn = c.unwrap();
            accepted += 1;
            spawn fn() {
                srv.handle_conn(conn, timeout_ms);
                return 0;
            };
        }
        if watch and shutdown_requested() {
            tcp_close(self.listener);
        }
        return accepted;
    }

    // Handshake + handler lifecycle for one connection.
    fn handle_conn(self, conn, timeout_ms) {
        let max_clients = 0;
        if self.opts.has("max_clients") and self.opts["max_clients"] != null {
            max_clients = int(self.opts["max_clients"]);
        }
        let pending_key = "pending:" + string(conn);
        let m_pre = self.clients.lock();
        if max_clients > 0 and m_pre.len() >= max_clients {
            self.clients.unlock();
            tcp_close(conn);
            return false;
        }
        m_pre[pending_key] = null;
        self.clients.unlock();

        // The socket timeout bounds the handshake AND any silent idle
        // period: a client that connects and never speaks is reaped here.
        tcp_set_timeout(conn, timeout_ms);
        let up = ws_accept(conn);
        if up.is_err() {
            let m_err = self.clients.lock();
            if m_err.has(pending_key) {
                m_err.remove(pending_key);
            }
            self.clients.unlock();
            // Not a WebSocket handshake (plain HTTP, garbage, probe) —
            // drop the connection without invoking the handler.
            tcp_close(conn);
            return false;
        }
        let h = up.unwrap();
        let m_up = self.clients.lock();
        if m_up.has(pending_key) {
            m_up.remove(pending_key);
        }
        m_up[string(h)] = h;
        self.clients.unlock();
        try {
            let f = self.handler;
            f(h);
        } finally {
            self.track(h, false);
            ws_close(h);
        }
        return true;
    }

    fn track(self, h, add: bool) {
        let m = self.clients.lock();
        if add {
            m[string(h)] = h;
        } elif m.has(string(h)) {
            m.remove(string(h));
        }
        self.clients.unlock();
    }

    // How many clients are currently connected (handshake completed,
    // handler still running). Liveness bookkeeping only.
    public fn client_count(self) -> int {
        let m = self.clients.lock();
        let n = 0;
        for (k, _v) in m {
            if not string(k).starts_with("pending:") {
                n = n + 1;
            }
        }
        self.clients.unlock();
        return n;
    }

    // Send `text` to every connected client. Requires a runtime with the
    // reader/writer socket split (2026-11-06 or later) — on older builds
    // this blocks on the first client parked in ws_recv. Returns how many
    // clients received the frame; dead sockets are pruned on failure.
    public fn broadcast(self, text: string) -> int {
        let m = self.clients.lock();
        let ids = [];
        for (k, _v) in m {
            let sk = string(k);
            if not sk.starts_with("pending:") {
                ids.push(sk);
            }
        }
        self.clients.unlock();

        let sent = 0;
        let i = 0;
        while i < len(ids) {
            let h = null;
            let m2 = self.clients.lock();
            if m2.has(ids[i]) {
                h = m2[ids[i]];
            }
            self.clients.unlock();
            if h != null {
                let r = ws_send(h, text);
                if r.is_err() {
                    self.track(h, false);
                } else {
                    sent += 1;
                }
            }
            i += 1;
        }
        return sent;
    }

    // Bind + serve forever (blocking — run it in a spawned task next to a
    // running HTTP app).
    public fn listen(self, addr: string) -> int {
        let real = self.bind(addr);
        println("[Spid3rWeb WS] WebSocket server listening on ws://" + real);
        return self.serve(-1);
    }

    public fn close(self) -> bool {
        if self.listener >= 0 {
            tcp_close(self.listener);
        }
        return true;
    }
}

                <li><code>app/</code> — controllers, models, middleware</li>
                <li><code>resources/views/</code> — SpiderBD templates</li>
                <li><code>routes/</code> — web, api and queue job registrations</li>
                <li><code>config/</code> — app, auth, session, mail, queue, logging, filesystems, services</li>
                <li><code>database/migrations/</code> — schema migrations</li>
                <li><code>docs/developer-docs.html</code> — full framework documentation</li>
            </ul>
        </footer>
    </main>
</body>
</html>

// routes/queue.matrix
// Queue job handler registrations.
//
// Handlers MUST be registered here before Queue.push("name", ...) works —
// the registry is process-local, so this file is imported by both entry
// points (`magic` and `server.matrix`). Add your own jobs to the function
// below; the worker resolves them by name at run time.

import "framework/Queue/Queue";
import "framework/Mail/Mail";
import "framework/Log/Log";

public fn register_queue_jobs() {
    // Log a message — handy for proving the pipeline works end-to-end.
    Queue.job("log.message", fn(data) {
        let msg = "(empty)";
        if data != null and type_of(data) == "map" and data.has("message") {
            msg = string(data["message"]);
        }
        Log.info("[job] log.message: " + msg, data);
        return true;
    });

    // Send an email via the Mail facade (log driver in development).
    Queue.job("mail.send", fn(data) {
        return Mail.send(data);
    });
}

// routes/web.matrix
// Spid3rWeb Web Application Route Registrations

import "framework/Http/Router";
import "app/Controllers/HomeController";

public fn register_web_routes() {
    let home = new HomeController();
    Route.get("/", fn(req) {
        return home.index(req);
    }).name("welcome");
    Route.get("/health", fn(req) {
        return home.health(req);
    }).name("health");
    Route.get("/info", fn(req) {
        return home.info(req);
    }).name("info");
}

// tests/test_cache.matrix
// Unit tests for the Spid3rWeb caching system (memory, file, database drivers)

import "framework/Cache/Cache";
import "framework/Cache/CacheStore";
import "framework/Database/DB";

// Run the standard operation battery against one store instance.
fn exercise_store(s, label: string) {
    // 1. Miss on empty
    assert(s.get("missing_key") == null, label + ": missing key returns null");
    assert(
        s.get("missing_key", "fallback") == "fallback",
        label + ": missing key returns default"
    );
    assert(s.has("missing_key") == false, label + ": has() false on empty");

    // 2. Put + get (string)
    s.put("greeting", "hello world", 60);
    assert(s.get("greeting") == "hello world", label + ": string round-trip");
    assert(s.has("greeting") == true, label + ": has() true after put");

    // 3. Structured values round-trip (JSON-based stores)
    s.put("profile", {
        "name": "ada", "age": 36, "tags": ["math", "code"]
    }, 60);
    let p = s.get("profile");
    assert(p["name"] == "ada", label + ": map value round-trip (name)");
    assert(int(p["age"]) == 36, label + ": map value round-trip (age)");
    assert(p["tags"].len() == 2, label + ": list inside map round-trip");

    s.put("numbers", [1, 2, 3], 60);
    assert(s.get("numbers").len() == 3, label + ": list value round-trip");

    // 4. Overwrite
    s.put("greeting", "updated", 60);
    assert(s.get("greeting") == "updated", label + ": put overwrites");

    // 5. TTL expiry (2 seconds in the past)
    s.put("shortlived", "gone soon", -1);
    assert(s.get("shortlived") == null, label + ": expired entry is a miss");
    assert(s.has("shortlived") == false, label + ": has() false after expiry");

    // 6. Forever entries (0 = never expires)
    s.put("forever_key", "eternal", 0);
    assert(s.get("forever_key") == "eternal", label + ": forever entry stored");

    // 7. Forget
    s.forget("greeting");
    assert(s.get("greeting") == null, label + ": forget removes key");

    // 8. add() semantics are facade-level; store-level put verified above

    // 9. Flush
    s.put("flush_a", 1, 60);
    s.put("flush_b", 2, 60);
    s.flush();
    assert(
        s.get("flush_a") == null and s.get("flush_b") == null,
        label + ": flush clears everything"
    );
}

fn test_memory_store() {
    exercise_store(new MemoryCacheStore(), "memory");
    println("  [PASS] test_memory_store");
}

fn test_file_store() {
    exercise_store(new FileCacheStore("storage/framework/cache_test"), "file");
    // Clean the test directory afterwards
    let s = new FileCacheStore("storage/framework/cache_test");
    s.flush();
    if file_exists("storage/framework/cache_test") {
        let _ = dir_remove("storage/framework/cache_test");
    }
    println("  [PASS] test_file_store");
}

fn test_database_store() {
    exercise_store(new DatabaseCacheStore(), "database");
    println("  [PASS] test_database_store");
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn test_facade_defaults_and_selection() {
    // Default driver resolution: memory (or file in production), unless env overrides it
    let app_env = env("APP_ENV").unwrap_or("production").trim().lower();
    let expected_default = if app_env == "production" {
        "file"
    } else {
        "memory"
    };
    let d = env("SPID3R_CACHE_DRIVER");
    if d.is_some() {
        let raw = string(d.unwrap()).trim().lower();
        if raw == "file" {
            expected_default = "file";
        } elif raw == "database" or raw == "db" {
            expected_default = "database";
        } elif raw == "memory" {
            expected_default = "memory";
        }
    }
    assert(Cache.driver_name() == expected_default, "default driver resolution");

    // Explicit store switching shares instances (lazy singleton per name)
    let m1 = Cache.store("memory");
    let m2 = Cache.store("memory");
    assert(m1 == m2, "store() reuses instances");

    // Facade delegates to default driver
    Cache.put("facade_key", "facade_value", 60);
    assert(Cache.get("facade_key") == "facade_value", "facade put/get");
    assert(Cache.has("facade_key"), "facade has");
    Cache.forget("facade_key");
    assert(Cache.missing("facade_key"), "facade forget");

    // Env override resolution (fresh manager)
    env_set("SPID3R_CACHE_DRIVER", "file");
    let c1 = new CacheManager();
    assert(c1.driver_name() == "file", "env driver: file");
    env_set("SPID3R_CACHE_DRIVER", "database");
    let c2 = new CacheManager();
    assert(c2.driver_name() == "database", "env driver: database");
    env_set("SPID3R_CACHE_DRIVER", "db");
    let c3 = new CacheManager();
    assert(c3.driver_name() == "database", "env driver: db alias");
    env_set("SPID3R_CACHE_DRIVER", "garbage");
    let c4 = new CacheManager();
    assert(c4.driver_name() == "memory", "unknown env driver falls back to memory");
    env_set("SPID3R_CACHE_DRIVER", "");

    println("  [PASS] test_facade_defaults_and_selection");
}

fn test_facade_remember_and_add() {
    let s = Cache.store();
    s.flush();

    let computer = fn() {
        // Closures can't mutate outer locals portably; use a sentinel map
        return {
            "computed": true, "stamp": string(unix_time())
        };
    };

    // remember: computes and stores on miss
    let v1 = Cache.remember("report", 60, computer);
    assert(v1["computed"] == true, "remember computes on miss");
    // remember: returns cached on hit (same object content)
    let v2 = Cache.remember("report", 60, computer);
    assert(v2["stamp"] == v1["stamp"], "remember returns cached on hit");

    // rememberForever
    let v3 = Cache.rememberForever("eternal_report", computer);
    assert(v3["computed"] == true, "rememberForever works");

    // add: succeeds on absent key, fails on present key
    s.flush();
    assert(Cache.add("once", "first", 60) == true, "add succeeds on absent key");
    assert(Cache.add("once", "second", 60) == false, "add refuses existing key");
    assert(Cache.get("once") == "first", "add does not overwrite");

    // increment / decrement
    s.flush();
    assert(Cache.increment("counter") == 1, "increment from zero");
    assert(Cache.increment("counter", 5) == 6, "increment by 5");
    assert(Cache.decrement("counter", 2) == 4, "decrement by 2");

    // Cleanup
    Cache.flush();
    println("  [PASS] test_facade_remember_and_add");
}

fn test_cache_across_engines_note() {
    // The database store rides the DB facade — driver-agnostic by design.
    // This assertion documents the contract; live per-engine coverage comes
    // from the multi-db suite.
    let s = new DatabaseCacheStore();
    assert(s.driver_name() == "database", "database store driver name");
    println("  [PASS] test_cache_across_engines_note");
}

fn main() {
    println("\n=== Running Cache Test Suite ===");
    test_memory_store();
    test_file_store();
    test_database_store();
    test_facade_defaults_and_selection();
    test_facade_remember_and_add();
    test_cache_across_engines_note();
    DB.close();
    println("=== All Cache Tests Passed! ===\n");
    return 0;
}

// tests/test_container.matrix
// Unit tests for the Spid3rWeb dependency injection container.

import "framework/Foundation/Container";

fn test_bind_fresh_resolution() {
    let c = new ServiceContainer();
    let counter = {
        "n": 0
    };
    c.bind("greeting", fn(_container) {
        counter["n"] = counter["n"] + 1;
        return {
            "hello": "world", "built_at": counter["n"]
        };
    });
    assert(c.bound("greeting"), "bound() true after bind");
    assert(not c.resolved("greeting"), "non-shared binding never memoizes");

    let a = c.resolve("greeting");
    let b = c.resolve("greeting");
    assert(a["hello"] == "world", "factory result returned");
    assert(a["built_at"] == 1 and b["built_at"] == 2, "bind resolves fresh each time");
    assert(a != b, "each resolve produces a distinct value");
    println("  [PASS] test_bind_fresh_resolution");
}

fn test_singleton_memoizes() {
    let c = new ServiceContainer();
    let builds = {
        "i": 0
    };
    c.singleton("conn", fn(_container) {
        builds["i"] = builds["i"] + 1;
        return {
            "build": builds["i"]
        };
    });
    assert(not c.resolved("conn"), "singleton not resolved before first make");
    let a = c.resolve("conn");
    assert(c.resolved("conn"), "singleton memoized after first resolve");
    assert(a["build"] == 1, "factory ran once");
    let b = c.resolve("conn");
    assert(
        a == b and a["build"] == 1,
        "singleton returns the same instance without re-running the factory"
    );

    // fresh() bypasses the memo but does not replace it
    let f = c.fresh("conn");
    assert(f["build"] == 2, "fresh builds a new value");
    assert(c.resolve("conn")["build"] == 1, "fresh did not disturb the memo");
    println("  [PASS] test_singleton_memoizes");
}

fn test_instance_and_make_alias() {
    let c = new ServiceContainer();
    let svc = {
        "kind": "prebuilt"
    };
    let returned = c.instance("svc", svc);
    assert(returned == svc, "instance() returns the stored value");
    assert(c.bound("svc") and c.resolved("svc"), "instance counts as bound+resolved");
    assert(c.make("svc") == svc, "make() is an alias of resolve()");
    assert(c.resolve("svc") == svc, "resolve returns the prebuilt instance");
    println("  [PASS] test_instance_and_make_alias");
}

fn test_factory_receives_container() {
    let c = new ServiceContainer();
    c.singleton("dep", fn(_container) {
        return {
            "n": 41
        };
    });
    c.bind("composite", fn(container) {
        let d = container.resolve("dep");
        return {
            "total": d["n"] + 1
        };
    });
    assert(
        c.resolve("composite")["total"] == 42,
        "factory receives the container for nested resolution"
    );
    println("  [PASS] test_factory_receives_container");
}

fn test_unbound_throws() {
    let c = new ServiceContainer();
    assert(not c.bound("ghost"), "unbound key reports not bound");
    let threw = false;
    try {
        c.resolve("ghost");
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw, "resolve on unbound key throws");
    threw = false;
    try {
        c.fresh("ghost");
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw, "fresh on unbound key throws");
    println("  [PASS] test_unbound_throws");
}

fn test_aliases_chain() {
    let c = new ServiceContainer();
    c.singleton("db.connection", fn(_container) {
        return {
            "engine": "sqlite"
        };
    });
    c.alias("db", "db.connection");
    c.alias("database", "db");
    assert(c.bound("database"), "alias resolves through the chain");
    assert(
        c.resolve("database")["engine"] == "sqlite",
        "aliased resolve reaches the target"
    );
    let a = c.resolve("db");
    assert(c.resolve("database") == a, "aliases share the memoized singleton");

    // extend through an alias
    c.extend("database", fn(container, previous) {
        let inner = previous(container);
        inner["wrapped"] = true;
        return inner;
    });
    assert(c.resolve("db")["wrapped"] == true, "extend works through an alias");
    println("  [PASS] test_aliases_chain");
}

fn test_extend_decorates() {
    let c = new ServiceContainer();
    c.bind("logger", fn(_container) {
        return {
            "lines": []
        };
    });
    c.extend("logger", fn(container, previous) {
        let l = previous(container);
        l["lines"].push("decorated");
        return l;
    });
    let out = c.resolve("logger");
    assert(
        out["lines"].len() == 1 and out["lines"][0] == "decorated",
        "extend wraps the previous factory"
    );

    // extend invalidates a memoized singleton
    let c2 = new ServiceContainer();
    c2.singleton("s", fn(_container) {
        return {
            "v": 1
        };
    });
    let first = c2.resolve("s");
    c2.extend("s", fn(container, previous) {
        let inner = previous(container);
        inner["v"] = inner["v"] + 100;
        return inner;
    });
    let second = c2.resolve("s");
    assert(second["v"] == 101, "extend rebuilds after memo invalidation");
    assert(first != second, "extend drops the stale memoized instance");
    println("  [PASS] test_extend_decorates");
}

fn test_extend_unbound_throws() {
    let c = new ServiceContainer();
    let threw = false;
    try {
        c.extend("nope", fn(container, previous) {
            return previous(container);
        });
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw, "extend on unbound key throws");
    println("  [PASS] test_extend_unbound_throws");
}

fn test_forget_and_flush() {
    let c = new ServiceContainer();
    c.singleton("a", fn(_container) {
        return {
            "v": 1
        };
    });
    c.bind("b", fn(_container) {
        return {
            "v": 2
        };
    });
    c.instance("c", {
        "v": 3
    });
    c.alias("aa", "a");

    let _memo = c.resolve("a");
    c.forget("a");
    assert(not c.bound("a"), "forget removes the binding");
    assert(not c.bound("aa"), "forget the target also empties the alias");
    assert(not c.resolved("a"), "forget removes the memoized instance");

    c.flush();
    assert(not c.bound("b") and not c.bound("c"), "flush removes every binding");
    let threw = false;
    try {
        c.resolve("c");
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw, "resolve after flush throws");
    println("  [PASS] test_forget_and_flush");
}

fn test_global_facade_isolation() {
    // The global Container must stay usable and independent of the local
    // instances exercised above.
    Container.flush();
    Container.singleton("global.svc", fn(_c) {
        return {
            "ok": true
        };
    });
    assert(Container.resolve("global.svc")["ok"] == true, "global facade resolves");
    assert(Container.bound("global.svc"), "global facade bound");
    Container.flush();
    assert(not Container.bound("global.svc"), "global facade flushes");
    println("  [PASS] test_global_facade_isolation");
}

fn main() {
    println("\n=== Running Container Test Suite ===");
    test_bind_fresh_resolution();
    test_singleton_memoizes();
    test_instance_and_make_alias();
    test_factory_receives_container();
    test_unbound_throws();
    test_aliases_chain();
    test_extend_decorates();
    test_extend_unbound_throws();
    test_forget_and_flush();
    test_global_facade_isolation();
    println("=== All Container Tests Passed! ===\n");
    return 0;
}

FILE: tests/test_core_systems.matrix

// tests/test_core_systems.matrix
// Unit tests for the core systems: Config, Log, Filesystem (Storage),
// Mail, Queue (sync + database) and the session/auth config wiring.

import "framework/Foundation/Config";
import "framework/Log/Log";
import "framework/Filesystem/Storage";
import "framework/Mail/Mail";
import "framework/Queue/Queue";
import "framework/Database/DB";
import "routes/queue";

fn test_config_repository() {
    // Dot-notation reads over every bundled namespace
    assert(Config.get("app.name") == "Spid3rWeb Developer Platform", "config: app.name");
    assert(Config.get("session.lifetime") == 86400, "config: session.lifetime default");
    assert(Config.get("auth.default") == "session", "config: auth default guard");
    assert(Config.get("logging.default") == "daily", "config: logging default channel");
    assert(Config.get("mail.default") == "log", "config: mail default driver");
    assert(Config.get("queue.default") == "database", "config: queue default connection");
    assert(Config.get("filesystems.default") == "local", "config: filesystems default disk");

    // Deep paths
    assert(
        Config.get("logging.channels.mail.path") == "storage/logs/mail.log",
        "config: deep channel path"
    );
    assert(Config.get("filesystems.disks.public.url") == "/uploads", "config: public disk url");

    // Fallbacks + has/set/load
    assert(
        Config.get("does.not.exist", "fallback") == "fallback",
        "config: fallback on missing"
    );
    assert(Config.has("queue.connections.sync.driver") == true, "config: has() true");
    assert(Config.has("queue.connections.nope.driver") == false, "config: has() false");
    Config.set("app.test_runtime_key", 7);
    assert(int(Config.get("app.test_runtime_key")) == 7, "config: set() then get()");
    assert(Config.namespaces().len() >= 8, "config: all namespaces registered");
    println("  [PASS] test_config_repository");
}

fn test_log_subsystem() {
    // Default channel writes a parseable daily log line
    let ok = Log.info("core-systems test message", {
        "suite": "core"
    });
    assert(ok == true, "log: info() returns true");

    // Stamp + date math sanity: known epochs format correctly.
    // 1762300800 = 2025-11-05T00:00:00Z; 1762281600 = 2025-11-04T18:40:00Z
    assert(Log.stamp(1762300800) == "2025-11-05T00:00:00Z", "log: UTC stamp midnight");
    assert(Log.stamp(1762281600) == "2025-11-04T18:40:00Z", "log: UTC stamp with time");

    // Explicit channel routing (queue channel file lives in storage/logs)
    assert(
        Log.write("error", "core-systems channel test", null, "queue") == true,
        "log: explicit channel write"
    );

    // Level filter: an unknown level name must not crash and maps to info
    assert(
        Log.write("bogus", "still logs as info", null) == true,
        "log: unknown level falls back to info"
    );

    // Daily rotation filename: the queue channel write lands in a
    // queue-YYYY-MM-DD.log file named by the UTC date.
    let today = Log.stamp(int(unix_time()));
    let day = today.slice(0, 10);
    assert(
        file_exists("storage/logs/queue-" + day + ".log") == true,
        "log: daily rotation uses UTC date filename"
    );

    // SPID3R_LOG_LEVEL pins the minimum level for every channel
    env_set("SPID3R_LOG_LEVEL", "error");
    assert(
        Log.info("filtered out by env level") == false,
        "log: SPID3R_LOG_LEVEL=error filters info"
    );
    assert(
        Log.error("still passes at error level") == true,
        "log: error passes at env level error"
    );
    env_set("SPID3R_LOG_LEVEL", "");
    assert(
        Log.info("passes again after clearing env") == true,
        "log: clearing env restores default"
    );
    println("  [PASS] test_log_subsystem");
}

fn test_storage_disk() {
    let d = Storage.disk("local");
    assert(d.name == "local", "storage: local disk selected");

    // put / exists / get / append / delete round-trip
    assert(d.put("core-tests/hello.txt", "hello spid3r") == true, "storage: put");
    assert(d.exists("core-tests/hello.txt") == true, "storage: exists after put");
    assert(d.get("core-tests/hello.txt") == "hello spid3r", "storage: get round-trip");
    assert(d.append("core-tests/hello.txt", " +more") == true, "storage: append");
    assert(d.get("core-tests/hello.txt") == "hello spid3r +more", "storage: append content");

    // Missing reads are null, missing deletes are false
    assert(d.get("core-tests/never-there.txt") == null, "storage: missing get is null");
    assert(d.delete("core-tests/never-there.txt") == false, "storage: missing delete is false");

    // Path traversal must throw
    let blocked = false;
    try {
        d.get("../outside.txt");
    } catch (_e) {
        blocked = true;
    }
    assert(blocked == true, "storage: ../ traversal rejected");

    blocked = false;
    try {
        d.put("/absolute/path.txt", "x");
    } catch (_e) {
        blocked = true;
    }
    assert(blocked == true, "storage: absolute path rejected");

    // Nested traversal via dot segments
    blocked = false;
    try {
        d.get("a/../../escape.txt");
    } catch (_e) {
        blocked = true;
    }
    assert(blocked == true, "storage: nested traversal rejected");

    // Unknown disk throws
    let bad_disk = false;
    try {
        Storage.disk("moon");
    } catch (_e) {
        bad_disk = true;
    }
    assert(bad_disk == true, "storage: unknown disk throws");

    // public disk URL mapping
    let pub = Storage.disk("public");
    assert(pub.url("img/logo.png") == "/uploads/img/logo.png", "storage: public url prefix");

    // private disks are not web-servable: empty URL prefix
    let priv = Storage.disk("local");
    assert(priv.url("secret.txt") == "", "storage: private disk url is empty");
    assert(priv.visibility == "private", "storage: local disk is private");

    // cleanup
    d.delete("core-tests/hello.txt");
    if file_exists("storage/app/core-tests") {
        let _ = dir_remove("storage/app/core-tests");
    }
    println("  [PASS] test_storage_disk");
}

fn test_mail_log_driver() {
    let ok = Mail.to("core-test@example.com", "Core systems suite", "hello from the test suite");
    assert(ok == true, "mail: log driver send returns true");

    // From address resolved from config
    assert(
        Config.get("mail.from.address") == "hello@spid3r.test",
        "mail: from address from config"
    );

    // The smtp driver must fail fast with a clear error (no outbound TCP)
    let threw = false;
    try {
        Mail.send({
            "to": "x@example.com", "subject": "s", "body": "b", "_driver": "smtp"
        });
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    // Note: _driver is not how selection works (mailer is config/env driven),
    // so without SPID3R_MAIL_MAILER=smtp this call legitimately succeeds.
    // The direct assertion below is what really verifies fail-fast:
    if env("SPID3R_MAIL_MAILER").is_some() and string(env("SPID3R_MAIL_MAILER").unwrap()) == "smtp" {
        assert(threw == true, "mail: smtp driver fails fast when selected");
    } else {
        assert(threw == false, "mail: log driver handles the send (smtp not selected)");
    }
    println("  [PASS] test_mail_log_driver");
}

fn test_queue_sync_and_database() {
    // ---- sync driver: handlers run immediately ----
    let conn_cfg = Config.get("queue.connections.sync");
    assert(conn_cfg["driver"] == "sync", "queue: sync connection present");

    // Registered framework handlers
    let names = Queue.registered();
    let has_mail = false;
    for n in names {
        if n == "mail.send" {
            has_mail = true;
        }
    }
    assert(has_mail == true, "queue: routes/queue.matrix handlers registered");

    // Unregistered handler must throw on a SYNC push (handler runs inline),
    // but a DATABASE push must succeed without the handler registered â€”
    // the worker process resolves handlers at run time, so standalone
    // producers can push jobs they cannot execute themselves.
    let threw = false;
    try {
        Queue.push("no.such.handler", {
        }, "", "sync");
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw == true, "queue: sync push with unknown handler throws");

    DB.table("jobs").where("queue", "=", "core-test").delete();  // deterministic start
    let pre = Queue.size("core-test");
    Queue.push("no.such.handler", {
        "x": 1
    }, "core-test");
    assert(
        int(Queue.size("core-test")) == int(pre) + 1,
        "queue: database push works without registered handler"
    );
    DB.table("jobs").where("queue", "=", "core-test").delete();  // clean up the unreachable job

    // ---- database driver: push, size, pop semantics ----
    DB.table("jobs").where("queue", "=", "core-test").delete();  // deterministic start
    let before = Queue.size("core-test");
    Queue.push("log.message", {
        "message": "queued from core systems test"
    }, "core-test");
    let after = Queue.size("core-test");
    assert(int(after) == int(before) + 1, "queue: database push increments size");

    let job = Queue.pop("core-test");
    assert(job != null, "queue: pop reserves a job");
    assert(int(job["attempts"]) == 1, "queue: pop increments attempts");
    // Second pop with everything reserved -> null
    assert(Queue.pop("core-test") == null, "queue: reserved job is not re-popped");

    // A reserved job belongs to the worker that popped it (invisible to
    // run_next until retry_after) â€” simulate that worker completing it:
    DB.table("jobs").where("id", "=", job["id"]).delete();

    // Fresh job processed end-to-end by run_next
    Queue.push("log.message", {
        "message": "second"
    }, "core-test");
    let ran = Queue.run_next("core-test");
    assert(ran == true, "queue: run_next processed the reserved job");
    assert(Queue.size("core-test") == 0, "queue: queue empty after run");

    // run_next on empty queue -> false
    assert(Queue.run_next("core-test") == false, "queue: run_next on empty returns false");

    // ---- sync driver: handlers run inline during push ----
    // (via the optional per-push connection override)
    let probe = {
        "hit": false
    };
    Queue.job("core.sync_probe", fn(_data) {
        probe["hit"] = true;
        return true;
    });
    assert(probe["hit"] == false, "queue: sync probe handler not yet run");
    Queue.push("core.sync_probe", {
    }, "core-test", "sync");
    assert(probe["hit"] == true, "queue: sync connection runs handler inline");
    assert(Queue.size("core-test") == int(before), "queue: sync push stores no row");

    // Built-in mail.send handler end-to-end through sync (log driver)
    assert(Queue.push("mail.send", {
        "to": "sync@example.com", "subject": "s", "body": "b"
    }, "", "sync") == true, "queue: mail.send handler runs via sync");

    // ---- failure policy: failing handler retries then discards ----
    Queue.job("core.always_fails", fn(_data) {
        throw "boom";
    });
    Queue.push("core.always_fails", {
    }, "core-test");
    // A failed attempt releases the job with available_at = now + retry_after,
    // which a real worker would wait out. Backdate it so this test exercises
    // the FULL retry -> max_attempts -> discard path immediately.
    let guard = 0;
    while guard < 8 {
        let rows = DB.select(
            "SELECT id FROM jobs WHERE queue = ? AND available_at > ?",
            ["core-test",
            int(unix_time())]
        );
        for r in rows {
            DB.table("jobs").where("id", "=", r["id"]).update({
                "available_at": int(unix_time()) - 1,
                "reserved_at": 0
            });
        }
        if Queue.size("core-test") == 0 {
            break;
        }
        let _ = Queue.run_next("core-test");
        guard = guard + 1;
    }
    // After max_attempts the job must be gone from the table entirely
    // (not just invisible until its retry window opens).
    let leftover = DB.select("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM jobs WHERE queue = ?", ["core-test"]);
    assert(int(leftover[0]["n"]) == 0, "queue: failing job discarded after max attempts");

    // Cleanup so the suite leaves no residue in the dev database
    DB.table("jobs").where("queue", "=", "core-test").delete();
    println("  [PASS] test_queue_sync_and_database");
}

fn test_session_auth_config_wiring() {
    // AuthManager consults config for the sessions table + lifetime
    assert(Config.get("session.table") == "sessions", "session: table config");
    assert(Config.get("session.cookie") == "spid3r_session", "session: cookie name config");
    assert(Config.get("session.httponly") == true, "session: httponly flag");
    assert(Config.get("auth.guards.session.table") == "users", "auth: guard table config");
    assert(
        Config.get("auth.guards.session.username_column") == "email",
        "auth: username column config"
    );
    assert(
        int(Config.get("auth.passwords.reset_expire")) == 3600,
        "auth: reset expiry config"
    );
    println("  [PASS] test_session_auth_config_wiring");
}

fn main() {
    println("\n=== Running Core Systems Test Suite ===");
    register_queue_jobs();  // load the handler registry (magic/server do this at boot)
    test_config_repository();
    test_log_subsystem();
    test_storage_disk();
    test_mail_log_driver();
    test_queue_sync_and_database();
    test_session_auth_config_wiring();
    DB.close();
    println("=== All Core Systems Tests Passed! ===\n");
    return 0;
}
