// tests/test_drivers.matrix
// Unit tests for the multi-driver database layer (escaping, interpolation,
// dialect DDL compilation, driver resolution). No live server required.

import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Database/DatabaseManager";
import "framework/Database/Schema";
import "framework/Database/Connection";

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 1. String escaping
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn test_pg_string_escaping() {
    assert(sql_escape_string("O'Brien", "postgres") == "O''Brien", "pg: single quote doubled");
    assert(
        sql_escape_string("back\\slash", "postgres") == "back\\slash",
        "pg: backslash literal (standard_conforming_strings)"
    );
    assert(sql_escape_string("plain", "postgres") == "plain", "pg: plain text unchanged");
    assert(sql_escape_string("", "postgres") == "", "pg: empty string unchanged");
    assert(sql_escape_string("a'b'c", "postgres") == "a''b''c", "pg: multiple quotes doubled");
    println("  [PASS] test_pg_string_escaping");
}

fn test_mysql_string_escaping() {
    assert(
        sql_escape_string("back\\slash", "mysql") == "back\\\\slash",
        "mysql: backslash doubled first"
    );
    // Quote doubling ('') â€” correct under BOTH default sql_mode and NO_BACKSLASH_ESCAPES
    assert(sql_escape_string("O'Brien", "mysql") == "O''Brien", "mysql: quote doubled");
    assert(
        sql_escape_string("say \"hi\"", "mysql") == "say \"hi\"",
        "mysql: double quote untouched (safe inside single-quoted literal)"
    );
    assert(
        sql_escape_string("line1\nline2", "mysql") == "line1\\nline2",
        "mysql: newline escaped"
    );
    assert(
        sql_escape_string("cr\rtest", "mysql") == "cr\\rtest",
        "mysql: carriage return escaped"
    );
    // MariaDB rides the same escaping (driver name normalized to mysql path)
    assert(
        sql_escape_string("O'Brien", "mariadb") == "O''Brien",
        "mariadb: same escaping as mysql"
    );
    println("  [PASS] test_mysql_string_escaping");
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 2. Value rendering
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn test_value_rendering() {
    assert(sql_quote_value(null, "postgres") == "NULL", "null -> NULL");
    assert(sql_quote_value(null, "mysql") == "NULL", "null -> NULL (mysql)");
    assert(sql_quote_value(42, "postgres") == "42", "int -> literal");
    assert(sql_quote_value(42, "mysql") == "42", "int -> literal (mysql)");
    assert(sql_quote_value(2.5, "postgres") == "2.5", "float -> literal");
    assert(sql_quote_value(true, "postgres") == "TRUE", "bool -> TRUE (pg)");
    assert(sql_quote_value(false, "postgres") == "FALSE", "bool -> FALSE (pg)");
    assert(sql_quote_value(true, "mysql") == "1", "bool -> 1 (mysql)");
    assert(sql_quote_value(false, "mysql") == "0", "bool -> 0 (mysql)");
    assert(sql_quote_value("hi", "postgres") == "'hi'", "string -> quoted");
    println("  [PASS] test_value_rendering");
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 3. Injection resistance (the critical security property)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn test_injection_resistance() {
    // Classic SQL injection payloads must remain inside the string literal
    let payload1 = "Robert'); DROP TABLE users;--";
    let pg_lit = sql_quote_value(payload1, "postgres");
    assert(
        pg_lit == "'Robert''); DROP TABLE users;--'",
        "pg: injection payload fully contained in literal"
    );

    let payload2 = "x'; DELETE FROM sessions WHERE '1'='1";
    let my_lit = sql_quote_value(payload2, "mysql");
    assert(
        my_lit == "'x''; DELETE FROM sessions WHERE ''1''=''1'",
        "mysql: injection payload fully contained in literal"
    );

    // Unicode / weird bytes stay escaped
    let payload3 = "emoji ðŸ•·ï¸ ' quote";
    let pg_lit2 = sql_quote_value(payload3, "postgres");
    assert(pg_lit2.starts_with("'emoji"), "pg: unicode payload kept in literal");
    assert(
        pg_lit2 == "'emoji ðŸ•·ï¸ '' quote'",
        "pg: interior quote escaped, unicode preserved"
    );

    println("  [PASS] test_injection_resistance");
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 4. Placeholder interpolation
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn test_interpolation() {
    // Single placeholder
    let s1 = sql_interpolate(
        "SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?",
        ["ada@example.com"],
        "postgres"
    );
    assert(
        s1 == "SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = 'ada@example.com'",
        "pg: single placeholder substituted"
    );

    // Multiple placeholders
    let s2 = sql_interpolate("INSERT INTO t (a, b) VALUES (?, ?)", [1, "two"], "mysql");
    assert(
        s2 == "INSERT INTO t (a, b) VALUES (1, 'two')",
        "mysql: multiple placeholders substituted"
    );

    // No params -> unchanged
    let s3 = sql_interpolate("SELECT 1", [], "postgres");
    assert(s3 == "SELECT 1", "empty params -> unchanged");

    // Null params list
    let s4 = sql_interpolate("SELECT 1", null, "postgres");
    assert(s4 == "SELECT 1", "null params -> unchanged");

    // Injection via placeholder
    let s5 = sql_interpolate(
        "SELECT * FROM t WHERE name = ?",
        ["x'; DROP TABLE t;--"],
        "postgres"
    );
    assert(
        s5 == "SELECT * FROM t WHERE name = 'x''; DROP TABLE t;--'",
        "pg: placeholder value escaped"
    );

    // WHERE with multiple conditions (QueryBuilder pattern)
    let s6 = sql_interpolate(
        "SELECT * FROM orders WHERE status = ? AND user_id = ? ORDER BY id DESC",
        ["published",
        7],
        "mysql"
    );
    assert(
        s6 == "SELECT * FROM orders WHERE status = 'published' AND user_id = 7 ORDER BY id DESC",
        "mysql: multi-condition interpolation"
    );

    println("  [PASS] test_interpolation");
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 5. Dialect DDL compilation
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn blueprint_dialect_sql(dialect: string) -> string {
    let bp = new Blueprint();
    bp.id();
    bp.string("title");
    bp.string("slug").unique();
    bp.text("content");
    bp.integer("views_count");
    bp.float("rating");
    bp.boolean("is_active");
    bp.timestamps();
    return bp.to_sql("probe_dialect", dialect);
}

fn test_dialect_ddl_sqlite() {
    let sql = blueprint_dialect_sql("sqlite");
    assert(sql.contains("id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT"), "sqlite: id column");
    assert(sql.contains("title TEXT NOT NULL"), "sqlite: string -> TEXT");
    assert(sql.contains("slug TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL"), "sqlite: unique modifier");
    assert(sql.contains("views_count INTEGER NOT NULL"), "sqlite: integer -> INTEGER");
    assert(sql.contains("rating REAL NOT NULL"), "sqlite: float -> REAL");
    assert(sql.contains("is_active INTEGER NOT NULL"), "sqlite: boolean -> INTEGER");
    assert(sql.contains("created_at TEXT"), "sqlite: timestamps present");
    assert(sql.starts_with("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS probe_dialect"), "sqlite: create stmt");
    println("  [PASS] test_dialect_ddl_sqlite");
}

fn test_dialect_ddl_postgres() {
    let sql = blueprint_dialect_sql("postgres");
    assert(sql.contains("id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY"), "pg: id -> SERIAL");
    assert(sql.contains("title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL"), "pg: string -> VARCHAR(255)");
    assert(sql.contains("views_count INTEGER NOT NULL"), "pg: integer -> INTEGER");
    assert(sql.contains("rating DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL"), "pg: float -> DOUBLE PRECISION");
    // Boolean columns are INTEGER on every dialect: the runtime SQL binder
    // cannot bind bool params, so 0/1 integers are the portable storage
    // (see framework/Database/Schema.matrix).
    assert(sql.contains("is_active INTEGER NOT NULL"), "pg: boolean -> INTEGER (portable 0/1)");
    assert(sql.starts_with("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS probe_dialect"), "pg: create stmt");
    println("  [PASS] test_dialect_ddl_postgres");
}

fn test_dialect_ddl_mysql() {
    let sql = blueprint_dialect_sql("mysql");
    assert(sql.contains("id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY"), "mysql: id -> AUTO_INCREMENT");
    assert(sql.contains("title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL"), "mysql: string -> VARCHAR(255)");
    assert(sql.contains("views_count INT NOT NULL"), "mysql: integer -> INT");
    assert(sql.contains("rating DOUBLE NOT NULL"), "mysql: float -> DOUBLE");
    assert(sql.contains("is_active TINYINT(1) NOT NULL"), "mysql: boolean -> TINYINT(1)");
    println("  [PASS] test_dialect_ddl_mysql");
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 6. Driver resolution
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn test_driver_resolution() {
    // If a project-root .env exists it legitimately supplies driver/connection
    // values (empty OS vars fall back to it), so the default-value assertions
    // below would not hold. Skip â€” .env behavior is covered by
    // test_env_file_loading / test_env_file_drives_driver.
    if file_exists(".env") {
        println("  [SKIP] test_driver_resolution (project .env present)");
        return;
    }

    // Hermeticity: clear any inherited connection env vars so default-value
    // assertions below test true defaults regardless of the host environment.
    let env_keys = [
    "SPID3R_PG_HOST",
    "SPID3R_PG_PORT",
    "SPID3R_PG_USER",
    "SPID3R_PG_PASSWORD",
    "SPID3R_PG_DATABASE",
    "SPID3R_MYSQL_HOST",
    "SPID3R_MYSQL_PORT",
    "SPID3R_MYSQL_USER",
    "SPID3R_MYSQL_PASSWORD",
    "SPID3R_MYSQL_DATABASE"
    ];
    for k in env_keys {
        env_set(k, "");
    }

    env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "postgres");
    let m1 = new DatabaseManager();
    assert(m1.driver_name() == "postgres", "postgres driver resolved");
    assert(m1.conn_config["addr"] == "127.0.0.1:5432", "pg default addr");
    assert(m1.conn_config["user"] == "spid3r", "pg default user");

    env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "postgresql");
    let m2 = new DatabaseManager();
    assert(m2.driver_name() == "postgres", "postgresql normalizes to postgres");

    env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "pg");
    let m3 = new DatabaseManager();
    assert(m3.driver_name() == "postgres", "pg alias resolves");

    env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "mysql");
    let m4 = new DatabaseManager();
    assert(m4.driver_name() == "mysql", "mysql driver resolved");
    assert(m4.conn_config["addr"] == "127.0.0.1:3306", "mysql default addr");

    env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "mariadb");
    let m5 = new DatabaseManager();
    assert(m5.driver_name() == "mariadb", "mariadb driver resolved");

    env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "sqlite");
    let m6 = new DatabaseManager();
    assert(m6.driver_name() == "sqlite", "sqlite driver resolved");

    env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "");
    let m7 = new DatabaseManager();
    assert(m7.driver_name() == "sqlite", "empty driver falls back to sqlite");

    env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "totally-unknown");
    let m8 = new DatabaseManager();
    assert(m8.driver_name() == "sqlite", "unknown driver falls back to sqlite");

    env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "sqlite");

    // Custom host/port
    env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "postgres");
    env_set("SPID3R_PG_HOST", "db.example.com");
    env_set("SPID3R_PG_PORT", "6543");
    let m9 = new DatabaseManager();
    assert(m9.conn_config["addr"] == "db.example.com:6543", "pg custom host/port");
    env_set("SPID3R_PG_HOST", "");
    env_set("SPID3R_PG_PORT", "");

    env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "sqlite");

    println("  [PASS] test_driver_resolution");
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 7. MariaDB schema dialect normalization

fn test_mariadb_schema_dialect() {
    // SchemaBuilder.dialect() maps mariadb -> mysql DDL
    let _sb = new SchemaBuilder();
    let bp = new Blueprint();
    bp.id();
    bp.string("name");
    // Call create against the global DB (sqlite default) â€” but compile SQL
    // directly to verify mariadb normalization without a server.
    let sql = bp.to_sql("t", "mysql");
    assert(sql.contains("INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY"), "mariadb compiles with mysql DDL");
    println("  [PASS] test_mariadb_schema_dialect");
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 7. Security hardening (post-audit F-1/F-5/F-6/F-7/F-8)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn test_quote_aware_interpolation() {
    // A `?` inside a string literal is NOT a placeholder (audit F-5)
    let s1 = sql_interpolate("SELECT 'what?col' AS v, ? AS w", [42], "postgres");
    assert(s1 == "SELECT 'what?col' AS v, 42 AS w", "pg: ? inside literal untouched");

    let s2 = sql_interpolate("SELECT 'a?b' AS v, ? AS w", ["x"], "mysql");
    assert(s2 == "SELECT 'a?b' AS v, 'x' AS w", "mysql: ? inside literal untouched");

    // Doubled quote inside literal keeps literal state
    let s3 = sql_interpolate("SELECT 'it''s ? ok' AS v, ? AS w", [1], "postgres");
    assert(s3 == "SELECT 'it''s ? ok' AS v, 1 AS w", "pg: doubled quote keeps literal open");

    // Placeholder count mismatch throws (audit F-5/F-6)
    let threw = false;
    try {
        sql_interpolate("SELECT * FROM t WHERE a = ? AND b = ?", [1], "postgres");
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw, "fewer params than placeholders throws");

    threw = false;
    try {
        sql_interpolate("SELECT * FROM t WHERE a = ?", [1, 2], "postgres");
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw, "more params than placeholders throws");
    println("  [PASS] test_quote_aware_interpolation");
}

fn test_bytes_rejected() {
    let threw = false;
    try {
        sql_quote_value("text".bytes(), "postgres");
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw, "bytes value throws instead of corrupting (F-7)");
    println("  [PASS] test_bytes_rejected");
}

fn test_identifier_guardrail() {
    // Valid identifiers pass through
    assert(sql_check_identifier("users") == "users", "plain identifier ok");
    assert(sql_check_identifier("orders.user_id") == "orders.user_id", "qualified identifier ok");
    assert(sql_check_identifier("col_1") == "col_1", "underscore ok");

    // Injection-shaped identifiers throw
    let threw = false;
    try {
        sql_check_identifier("users; DROP TABLE users");
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw, "semicolon identifier throws");
    threw = false;
    try {
        sql_check_identifier("id) FROM users--");
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw, "comment identifier throws");
    threw = false;
    try {
        sql_check_identifier("' OR '1'='1");
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw, "quote identifier throws");
    threw = false;
    try {
        sql_check_identifier("");
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw, "empty identifier throws");

    // QueryBuilder surfaces the guardrail
    threw = false;
    try {
        DB.table("users").orderBy("id; DROP TABLE users", "DESC");
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw, "orderBy injection throws");

    // orderBy direction whitelist
    let q = DB.table("users");
    q.orderBy("id", "DESC; DROP TABLE users");
    assert(q.order_dir == "ASC", "invalid order direction falls back to ASC");
    println("  [PASS] test_identifier_guardrail");
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn main() {
    println("\n=== Running Database Driver Test Suite ===");
    test_pg_string_escaping();
    test_mysql_string_escaping();
    test_value_rendering();
    test_injection_resistance();
    test_interpolation();
    test_dialect_ddl_sqlite();
    test_dialect_ddl_postgres();
    test_dialect_ddl_mysql();
    test_driver_resolution();
    test_mariadb_schema_dialect();
    test_quote_aware_interpolation();
    test_bytes_rejected();
    test_identifier_guardrail();
    DB.close();
    println("=== All Database Driver Tests Passed! ===\n");
    return 0;
}

// tests/test_env.matrix
// Unit tests for framework/Foundation/Env loader and .env-driven DB resolution.

import "framework/Foundation/Env";
import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Database/DatabaseManager";

fn test_env_file_loading() {
    let content = "# Database driver configuration\nSPID3R_TEST_PLAIN=hello\nSPID3R_TEST_QUOTED=\"quoted value\"\nSPID3R_TEST_SINGLE='single quoted'\nexport SPID3R_TEST_EXPORTED=exported\nSPID3R_TEST_EMPTY=\nSPID3R_TEST_EQ=a=b=c\n";
    let w = write_file(".env_test", content);
    assert(w.is_ok(), "test .env written");

    assert(load_env_file(".env_test"), "existing .env returns true");

    assert(env("SPID3R_TEST_PLAIN").unwrap() == "hello", "plain KEY=VALUE parsed");
    assert(env("SPID3R_TEST_QUOTED").unwrap() == "quoted value", "double quotes stripped");
    assert(env("SPID3R_TEST_SINGLE").unwrap() == "single quoted", "single quotes stripped");
    assert(env("SPID3R_TEST_EXPORTED").unwrap() == "exported", "export prefix stripped");
    assert(env("SPID3R_TEST_EMPTY").unwrap() == "", "empty value allowed");
    assert(env("SPID3R_TEST_EQ").unwrap() == "a=b=c", "only first = is the separator");

    // Precedence: existing non-empty OS env wins over .env
    env_set("SPID3R_TEST_PRECEDENCE", "from_os");
    let w2 = write_file(".env_test", "SPID3R_TEST_PRECEDENCE=from_file\n");
    assert(w2.is_ok(), "precedence .env written");
    load_env_file(".env_test");
    assert(env("SPID3R_TEST_PRECEDENCE").unwrap() == "from_os", "OS env wins over .env");

    file_delete(".env_test");
    println("  [PASS] test_env_file_loading");
}

fn test_env_file_drives_driver() {
    // A .env in the project root must control driver resolution.
    // A real user .env is backed up and restored on all paths.
    let had_env = file_exists(".env");
    let backup_content = "";
    if had_env {
        let r = read_file(".env");
        if r.is_ok() {
            backup_content = r.unwrap();
        }
    }

    let ok_driver = false;
    let ok_addr = false;
    let ok_fallback = false;
    try {
        let w = write_file(
            ".env",
            "SPID3R_DB_DRIVER=mysql\nSPID3R_MYSQL_HOST=db.test\nSPID3R_MYSQL_PORT=3400\n"
        );
        assert(w.is_ok(), "root .env written");

        env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "");
        env_set("SPID3R_MYSQL_HOST", "");
        env_set("SPID3R_MYSQL_PORT", "");

        let m = new DatabaseManager();
        ok_driver = m.driver_name() == "mysql";
        ok_addr = m.conn_config["addr"] == "db.test:3400";

        if had_env {
            write_file(".env", backup_content);
        } else {
            file_delete(".env");
        }
        env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "");
        env_set("SPID3R_MYSQL_HOST", "");
        env_set("SPID3R_MYSQL_PORT", "");
        let _m2 = new DatabaseManager();
        ok_fallback = true;
    } catch (_e) {
        if had_env {
            write_file(".env", backup_content);
        } elif file_exists(".env") {
            file_delete(".env");
        }
        env_set("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER", "");
        env_set("SPID3R_MYSQL_HOST", "");
        env_set("SPID3R_MYSQL_PORT", "");
        throw _e;
    }

    assert(ok_driver, ".env driver resolved");
    assert(ok_addr, ".env connection settings resolved");
    assert(ok_fallback, "state restored after test");
    println("  [PASS] test_env_file_drives_driver");
}

fn main() {
    println("\n=== Running Environment Loader Test Suite ===");
    test_env_file_loading();
    test_env_file_drives_driver();
    DB.close();
    println("=== All Environment Loader Tests Passed! ===\n");
    return 0;
}

// tests/test_events.matrix
// Unit tests for the Spid3rWeb event dispatcher.

import "framework/Events/Event";

fn test_listen_and_dispatch() {
    let d = new EventDispatcher();
    let sink = [];
    d.listen("user.created", fn(payload, _name) {
        sink.push(payload);
        return "ok";
    });
    assert(d.has_listeners("user.created"), "has_listeners after listen");
    assert(d.count_listeners("user.created") == 1, "one listener registered");

    let results = d.dispatch("user.created", {
        "id": 7
    });
    assert(results.len() == 1 and results[0] == "ok", "dispatch collects handler results");
    assert(sink.len() == 1 and sink[0]["id"] == 7, "handler received the payload");
    assert(not d.has_listeners("user.deleted"), "unrelated event has no listeners");
    println("  [PASS] test_listen_and_dispatch");
}

fn test_handler_receives_event_name() {
    let d = new EventDispatcher();
    let seen = [];
    d.listen("a.b", fn(_payload, name) {
        seen.push(name);
        return null;
    });
    d.dispatch("a.b", null);
    assert(seen[0] == "a.b", "handler receives the event name as second argument");
    println("  [PASS] test_handler_receives_event_name");
}

fn test_dispatch_order_and_payload_default() {
    let d = new EventDispatcher();
    let order = [];
    d.listen("evt", fn(_p, _n) {
        order.push("first"); return 1;
    });
    d.listen("evt", fn(_p, _n) {
        order.push("second"); return 2;
    });
    let results = d.dispatch("evt");
    assert(
        order[0] == "first" and order[1] == "second",
        "listeners fire in registration order"
    );
    assert(
        results.len() == 2 and results[0] == 1 and results[1] == 2,
        "results in firing order"
    );
    // dispatch without a payload defaults to null and still fires
    let got = [];
    d.listen("evt2", fn(p, _n) {
        got.push(p); return null;
    });
    d.dispatch("evt2");
    assert(got.len() == 1 and got[0] == null, "default payload is null");
    println("  [PASS] test_dispatch_order_and_payload_default");
}

fn test_wildcards() {
    let d = new EventDispatcher();
    let hits = [];
    d.listen("user.*", fn(_p, n) {
        hits.push("wild:" + n); return "w";
    });
    d.listen("user.created", fn(_p, _n) {
        hits.push("exact"); return "e";
    });

    assert(d.has_listeners("user.created"), "wildcard counts as listener for matching event");
    assert(not d.has_listeners("userx.created"), "prefix must end on a segment boundary");
    assert(not d.has_listeners("order.created"), "unrelated event does not match");
    assert(d.count_listeners("user.created") == 2, "exact + wildcard both counted");

    let results = d.dispatch("user.created", null);
    assert(results.len() == 2, "both listeners fired");
    assert(
        hits[0] == "exact" and hits[1] == "wild:user.created",
        "exact fires before wildcard; name passed through"
    );

    d.dispatch("user.deleted", null);
    assert(
        hits.len() == 3 and hits[2] == "wild:user.deleted",
        "wildcard fires for every matching event"
    );

    d.forget("user.*");
    assert(
        d.count_listeners("user.created") == 1,
        "forget removes the wildcard, exact listener remains"
    );
    d.forget("user.created");
    assert(
        not d.has_listeners("user.created"),
        "forget on the exact name removes the last listener"
    );
    println("  [PASS] test_wildcards");
}

fn test_once_fires_single_time() {
    let d = new EventDispatcher();
    let calls = [];
    d.once("boot", fn(p, _n) {
        calls.push(p); return "once";
    });
    assert(d.count_listeners("boot") == 1, "once registers one listener");
    let r1 = d.dispatch("boot", "x");
    assert(r1.len() == 1 and r1[0] == "once", "once listener fires the first time");
    assert(d.count_listeners("boot") == 0, "once listener unregisters after firing");
    let r2 = d.dispatch("boot", "y");
    assert(r2.len() == 0, "second dispatch has no listeners");
    assert(calls.len() == 1, "handler body ran exactly once");

    // once + wildcard
    let seen = [];
    d.once("job.*", fn(_p, n) {
        seen.push(n); return null;
    });
    d.dispatch("job.process", null);
    d.dispatch("job.finish", null);
    assert(
        seen.len() == 1 and seen[0] == "job.process",
        "wildcard once fires a single time overall"
    );
    println("  [PASS] test_once_fires_single_time");
}

fn test_forget_and_flush() {
    let d = new EventDispatcher();
    d.listen("x", fn(_p, _n) {
        return 1;
    });
    d.listen("x", fn(_p, _n) {
        return 2;
    });
    d.listen("y", fn(_p, _n) {
        return 3;
    });
    assert(d.count_listeners("x") == 2, "two listeners on x");
    d.forget("x");
    assert(not d.has_listeners("x"), "forget removes all listeners for the event");
    assert(d.has_listeners("y"), "forget leaves other events alone");
    d.flush();
    assert(not d.has_listeners("y"), "flush removes everything");
    assert(d.count_listeners("anything") == 0, "count is zero after flush");
    println("  [PASS] test_forget_and_flush");
}

// Class declarations are top-level in Spid3r; this subscriber backs the
// test_subscriber case below.
class OrderSubscriber {
    public let log;
    fn new () {
        self.log = [];
    }
    public fn subscribe(self, events) {
        let sink = self.log;
        events.listen("order.placed", fn(p, _n) {
            sink.push(p);
            return "sub";
        });
    }
}

fn test_subscriber() {
    let d = new EventDispatcher();
    let sub = new OrderSubscriber();
    d.subscribe(sub);
    let results = d.dispatch("order.placed", {
        "total": 42
    });
    assert(
        results.len() == 1 and results[0] == "sub",
        "subscriber-registered listener fires"
    );
    assert(
        sub.log.len() == 1 and sub.log[0]["total"] == 42,
        "subscriber sink received payload"
    );
    println("  [PASS] test_subscriber");
}

fn test_listener_error_propagates_and_stops() {
    let d = new EventDispatcher();
    let after = [];
    d.listen("boom", fn(_p, _n) {
        throw "listener exploded";
    });
    d.listen("boom", fn(_p, _n) {
        after.push("reached"); return null;
    });
    let threw = false;
    try {
        d.dispatch("boom", null);
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw, "a throwing listener propagates to the dispatch caller");
    assert(after.len() == 0, "a throwing listener stops later listeners");
    println("  [PASS] test_listener_error_propagates_and_stops");
}

fn test_global_facade() {
    Event.flush();
    let seen = [];
    Event.listen("facade.ping", fn(p, _n) {
        seen.push(p); return "pong";
    });
    let r = Event.dispatch("facade.ping", "ping");
    assert(r[0] == "pong" and seen[0] == "ping", "global facade dispatches");
    Event.flush();
    assert(not Event.has_listeners("facade.ping"), "global facade flushes");
    println("  [PASS] test_global_facade");
}

fn main() {
    println("\n=== Running Events Test Suite ===");
    test_listen_and_dispatch();
    test_handler_receives_event_name();
    test_dispatch_order_and_payload_default();
    test_wildcards();
    test_once_fires_single_time();
    test_forget_and_flush();
    test_subscriber();
    test_listener_error_propagates_and_stops();
    test_global_facade();
    println("=== All Events Tests Passed! ===\n");
    return 0;
}

FILE: tests/test_http_head.matrix

// tests/test_http_head.matrix
// In-process HTTP lifecycle tests for the fresh-install surface:
// route dispatch, HEADâ†’GET fallback, static file serving (incl. HEAD),
// 404/405 semantics and static path-traversal rejection.
// Boots a real Application and drives handle_request() directly.

import "framework/Foundation/Application";
import "framework/Http/Router";
import "routes/web";

let passed = 0;
let failed = 0;

fn check(name: string, cond) {
    if cond {
        passed = passed + 1;
        println("  [PASS] " + name);
    } else {
        failed = failed + 1;
        println("  [FAIL] " + name);
    }
}

fn status_of(app, method: string, path: string) -> int {
    let res = app.handle_request({
        "method": method,
        "path": path,
        "headers": {
        }
        ,
        "body": ""
    });
    // handle_request returns a map: { status, headers, body }
    return int(res["status"]);
}

fn main() {
    println("=== Running HTTP Lifecycle (fresh install) Test Suite ===");

    let app = new Application();
    register_web_routes();

    // --- Core dispatch ---
    check("GET / serves the welcome page (200)", status_of(app, "GET", "/") == 200);
    check("GET /health returns 200", status_of(app, "GET", "/health") == 200);
    check("GET /missing is a 404", status_of(app, "GET", "/missing") == 404);
    check("POST / is a 405 (route is GET-only)", status_of(app, "POST", "/") == 405);
    check("DELETE / is a 405", status_of(app, "DELETE", "/") == 405);

    // --- HEADâ†’GET fallback (Router.match_route) ---
    check("HEAD / is served by the GET handler (200)", status_of(app, "HEAD", "/") == 200);
    check("HEAD /missing is a 404", status_of(app, "HEAD", "/missing") == 404);

    // --- Static files ---
    check(
        "GET /css/welcome.css is served (200)",
        status_of(app, "GET", "/css/welcome.css") == 200
    );
    check(
        "HEAD /css/welcome.css is served (200)",
        status_of(app, "HEAD", "/css/welcome.css") == 200
    );

    // --- Static path traversal must never serve files outside public/ ---
    check("GET /css/../.env is rejected (404)", status_of(app, "GET", "/css/../.env") == 404);
    check(
        "GET /css/..%5c..%5c.env is rejected (404)",
        status_of(app, "GET", "/css/..%5c..%5c.env") == 404
    );
    check("GET /%2e%2e/.env is rejected (404)", status_of(app, "GET", "/%2e%2e/.env") == 404);
    check(
        "GET /css/welcome.css%00 is rejected (404)",
        status_of(app, "GET", "/css/welcome.css%00") == 404
    );
    check(
        "GET /C:/Users/x/.env is rejected (404)",
        status_of(app, "GET", "/C:/Users/x/.env") == 404
    );

    // --- Trailing slash + double slash normalization ---
    check("GET // normalizes to / (200)", status_of(app, "GET", "//") == 200);

    println("------------------------------------------------------------------");
    println(
        "HTTP Lifecycle Suite: {passed} passed, {failed} failed"
    );
    if failed > 0 {
        println("[SUITE FAILED]");
    } else {
        println("=== All HTTP Lifecycle Tests Passed! ===");
    }
}

FILE: tests/test_migrations_registry.matrix

// tests/test_migrations_registry.matrix
// Regression suite for Phase 3 (Structure):
//   - Auto-generated storage-backed migration registry (sorted, stem-named, incremental, pruning)
//   - Route.resource RESTful controller registration
//   - bootstrap/app.matrix create_application() + HomeController wiring

import "framework/Console/Commands/MigrationRegistry";
import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Http/Router";
import "framework/Foundation/Application";
import "framework/Http/Controller";
import "bootstrap/app";

class WidgetController extends Controller {
    fn new () {
        let _ = self;
    }

    public fn index(self, _req) {
        return self.ok({
            "action": "index"
        });
    }

    public fn show(self, req) {
        return self.ok({
            "action": "show", "id": req.param("id")
        });
    }

    public fn store(self, _req) {
        return self.created({
            "action": "store"
        });
    }

    public fn update(self, req) {
        return self.ok({
            "action": "update", "id": req.param("id")
        });
    }

    public fn destroy(self, req) {
        return self.ok({
            "action": "destroy", "id": req.param("id")
        });
    }
}

fn cleanup_temp_dir(dir: string) {
    if not file_exists(dir) {
        return;
    }
    let ls = list_dir(dir);
    if ls.is_ok() {
        for name in ls.unwrap() {
            let n = string(name);
            if n != "." and n != ".." {
                let p = "{dir}/{n}";
                if list_dir(p).is_ok() {
                    cleanup_temp_dir(p);
                } else {
                    file_delete(p);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    let _ = dir_remove(dir);
}

fn sample_migration_source(cls: string, stem: string) -> string {
    return "import \"framework/Database/Migration\";\npublic class {cls} extends Migration {\n    fn new(name: string = \"{stem}\") { self.name = name; self.checksum = \"\"; }\n    public fn up(self) { let _ = self; }\n    public fn down(self) { let _ = self; }\n}\n";
}

fn test_registry_sync_sorted_incremental_and_prune() {
    let base = "storage/framework/test_mig_tmp";
    let mdir = "{base}/migrations";
    let reg = "{base}/migrations.matrix";
    cleanup_temp_dir(base);
    ensure_parent_dirs("{mdir}/dummy.txt");
    dir_create(mdir);

    try {
        let f1 = "001_init_alpha";
        let f2 = "2026_11_20_063000_add_beta";
        assert(
            write_file("{mdir}/{f1}.matrix", sample_migration_source("InitAlpha", f1)).is_ok(),
            "wrote migration 1"
        );
        assert(
            write_file("{mdir}/{f2}.matrix", sample_migration_source("MigrationAddBeta", f2)).is_ok(),
            "wrote migration 2"
        );

        let n1 = sync_registry(mdir, reg, false, false);
        assert(n1 == 2, "initial sync detects 2 migrations");
        assert(file_exists(reg), "generated registry file exists");

        let body1 = string(read_file(reg).unwrap());
        let pos1 = body1.index_of("001_init_alpha");
        let pos2 = body1.index_of("2026_11_20_063000_add_beta");
        assert(pos1 >= 0 and pos2 > pos1, "imports sorted ascending by filename");
        assert(
            body1.contains("new InitAlpha(\"001_init_alpha\")"),
            "filename stem passed to constructor"
        );
        assert(
            body1.contains("new MigrationAddBeta(\"2026_11_20_063000_add_beta\")"),
            "timestamp stem passed to constructor"
        );
        assert(body1.contains("fn main()"), "generated registry includes runner entrypoint");

        // No-op re-sync
        assert(sync_registry(mdir, reg, false, false) == 0, "idempotent sync returns 0");

        // Incremental addition picked up automatically
        let f3 = "2026_11_20_063005_add_gamma";
        write_file("{mdir}/{f3}.matrix", sample_migration_source("MigrationAddGamma", f3));
        assert(
            sync_registry(mdir, reg, false, false) == 1,
            "incremental sync detects 1 new file"
        );
        let body2 = string(read_file(reg).unwrap());
        assert(body2.contains("2026_11_20_063005_add_gamma"), "new file added to registry");

        // Stale entry pruned on file delete
        file_delete("{mdir}/{f3}.matrix");
        let _ = sync_registry(mdir, reg, false, false);
        let body3 = string(read_file(reg).unwrap());
        assert(
            not body3.contains("2026_11_20_063005_add_gamma"),
            "deleted file pruned from registry"
        );
    } finally {
        cleanup_temp_dir(base);
    }
    println("  [PASS] test_registry_sync_sorted_incremental_and_prune");
}

fn test_route_resource_and_bootstrap_app() {
    Route.clear();
    let app = create_application();
    let client = app.test_client();

    client.get("/").assert_ok();
    client.get("/health").assert_ok().assert_json_path("status", "ok");
    client.get("/info").assert_ok();

    app.resource("/api/widgets", new WidgetController());
    client.get("/api/widgets").assert_ok().assert_json_path("action", "index");
    client.post("/api/widgets").assert_created().assert_json_path("action", "store");
    client.get("/api/widgets/42").assert_ok().assert_json_path("id", "42");
    client.put_json("/api/widgets/42", {
    }).assert_ok().assert_json_path("action", "update");
    client.patch_json("/api/widgets/42", {
    }).assert_ok().assert_json_path("id", "42");
    client.delete("/api/widgets/42").assert_ok().assert_json_path("action", "destroy");

    Route.clear();
    println("  [PASS] test_route_resource_and_bootstrap_app");
}

fn main() {
    println("\n=== Running Phase 3 Structure & Registry Suite ===");
    test_registry_sync_sorted_incremental_and_prune();
    test_route_resource_and_bootstrap_app();
    DB.close();
    println("=== All Phase 3 Structure Tests Passed! ===\n");
    return 0;
}

// tests/test_model.matrix
// Unit tests for the Spid3rWeb Model layer: lifecycle events, eager
// loading, transactions, pagination cap.
//
// Uses its own mb_* tables (created via the Schema builder) so it never
// disturbs the shared users/sessions schema.

import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Database/Schema";
import "framework/Database/Model";
import "framework/Events/Event";

class MBPost extends Model {
    fn new () {
        let _ = self;
        super("mb_posts", [], {
        });
    }
}

class MBComment extends Model {
    fn new () {
        let _ = self;
        super("mb_comments", [], {
        });
    }
}

fn setup_tables() {
    Schema.dropIfExists("mb_comments");
    Schema.dropIfExists("mb_posts");
    Schema.create("mb_posts", fn(t) {
        t.id();
        t.string("title");
        t.timestamps();
    });
    Schema.create("mb_comments", fn(t) {
        t.id();
        t.integer("post_id");
        t.string("body");
        t.timestamps();
    });
}

fn teardown_tables() {
    Schema.dropIfExists("mb_comments");
    Schema.dropIfExists("mb_posts");
    DB.close();
}

fn make_post(title: string) {
    let p = new MBPost();
    p.set("title", title);
    let saved = p.save();
    assert(saved != false, "post save succeeded: " + title);
    return p;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn test_model_created_events_fire_in_order() {
    Event.flush();
    let log = [];
    let sink = log;
    Event.listen("model.mb_posts.creating", fn(_m, _name) {
        sink.push("creating"); return null;
    });
    Event.listen("model.mb_posts.created", fn(_m, _name) {
        sink.push("created"); return null;
    });

    let p = make_post("events");
    assert(log[0] == "creating" and log[1] == "created", "creating fires before created");
    assert(p.exists, "model exists after save");

    // Update events
    Event.listen("model.mb_posts.updating", fn(_m, _name) {
        sink.push("updating"); return null;
    });
    Event.listen("model.mb_posts.updated", fn(_m, _name) {
        sink.push("updated"); return null;
    });
    p.set("title", "events2");
    p.save();
    assert(log[2] == "updating" and log[3] == "updated", "updating fires before updated");

    // Delete events
    Event.listen("model.mb_posts.deleting", fn(_m, _name) {
        sink.push("deleting"); return null;
    });
    Event.listen("model.mb_posts.deleted", fn(_m, _name) {
        sink.push("deleted"); return null;
    });
    let gone = p.delete();
    assert(gone == true, "delete returns true");
    assert(log[4] == "deleting" and log[5] == "deleted", "deleting fires before deleted");
    assert(not p.exists, "model marked deleted");
    Event.flush();
    println("  [PASS] test_model_created_events_fire_in_order");
}

fn test_model_event_vetoes() {
    Event.flush();

    // Veto the insert: save returns false, nothing lands in the table.
    Event.listen("model.mb_posts.creating", fn(_m, _name) {
        return false;
    });
    let blocked = new MBPost();
    blocked.set("title", "never");
    assert(blocked.save() == false, "vetoed creating returns false from save");
    assert(not blocked.exists, "vetoed model does not exist");
    assert(
        DB.table("mb_posts").where("title", "=", "never").count() == 0,
        "vetoed insert wrote nothing"
    );
    Event.forget("model.mb_posts.creating");

    // Veto the update: attributes changed, database unchanged.
    let p = make_post("veto-update");
    Event.listen("model.mb_posts.updating", fn(_m, _name) {
        return false;
    });
    p.set("title", "veto-update-changed");
    assert(p.save() == false, "vetoed updating returns false from save");
    assert(
        DB.table("mb_posts").where("title", "=", "veto-update-changed").count() == 0,
        "vetoed update wrote nothing"
    );
    assert(
        DB.table("mb_posts").where("title", "=", "veto-update").count() == 1,
        "original row untouched"
    );
    Event.forget("model.mb_posts.updating");

    // Veto the delete: row survives.
    Event.listen("model.mb_posts.deleting", fn(_m, _name) {
        return false;
    });
    assert(p.delete() == false, "vetoed deleting returns false from delete");
    assert(
        DB.table("mb_posts").where("id", "=", p.get("id")).count() == 1,
        "vetoed delete kept the row"
    );
    Event.forget("model.mb_posts.deleting");
    Event.flush();
    println("  [PASS] test_model_event_vetoes");
}

fn test_eager_load_has_many() {
    setup_tables();
    let p1 = make_post("one");
    let p2 = make_post("two");
    make_post("three");

    let c = new MBComment();
    c.set("post_id", p1.get("id"));
    c.set("body", "first!");
    c.save();
    let c2 = new MBComment();
    c2.set("post_id", p1.get("id"));
    c2.set("body", "second!");
    c2.save();
    let c3 = new MBComment();
    c3.set("post_id", p2.get("id"));
    c3.set("body", "on two");
    c3.save();

    // Model instances as input.
    let posts = [];
    let i = 0;
    while i < 3 {
        let m = new MBPost();
        m.set("id", i + 1);
        posts.push(m);
        i += 1;
    }
    load_has_many(posts, "mb_comments", "post_id", "comments");
    assert(posts[0].get("comments").len() == 2, "post 1 eager-loaded 2 comments");
    assert(posts[1].get("comments").len() == 1, "post 2 eager-loaded 1 comment");
    assert(posts[2].get("comments").len() == 0, "post 3 eager-loaded an empty list");
    assert(
        posts[0].get("comments")[0]["body"] == "first!",
        "comment rows carry their columns"
    );

    // Plain row maps as input (same loader must work).
    let rows = DB.table("mb_posts").get();
    load_has_many(rows, "mb_comments", "post_id", "comments");
    assert(rows[0]["comments"].len() == 2, "row-map input also hydrates");

    // to_map exposes the relation for JSON responses.
    let as_map = posts[0].to_map();
    assert(
        as_map.has("comments") and as_map["comments"].len() == 2,
        "to_map includes eager-loaded relations"
    );

    // Empty input list is a no-op, not an error.
    load_has_many([], "mb_comments", "post_id", "comments");
    println("  [PASS] test_eager_load_has_many");
}

fn test_eager_load_belongs_to() {
    let comments = DB.table("mb_comments").get();
    load_belongs_to(comments, "mb_posts", "post_id", "post");
    assert(comments[0]["post"]["title"] == "one", "comment 1 resolved its parent post");
    assert(comments[2]["post"]["title"] == "two", "comment 3 resolved its parent post");

    // A foreign key pointing nowhere resolves to null, not an error.
    let orphan = new MBComment();
    orphan.set("post_id", 999999);
    let wrap = [orphan];
    load_belongs_to(wrap, "mb_posts", "post_id", "post");
    assert(wrap[0].get("post") == null, "missing parent resolves to null");
    println("  [PASS] test_eager_load_belongs_to");
}

fn test_transaction_commit_and_rollback() {
    setup_tables();

    // Commit path: everything inside survives.
    DB.transaction(fn() {
        let a = new MBPost();
        a.set("title", "tx-a");
        a.save();
        let b = new MBPost();
        b.set("title", "tx-b");
        b.save();
        return true;
    });
    assert(DB.table("mb_posts").count() == 2, "committed transaction persisted both posts");

    // Rollback path: a throw inside reverts everything.
    let threw = false;
    try {
        DB.transaction(fn() {
            let c = new MBPost();
            c.set("title", "tx-c-rollback");
            c.save();
            throw "simulated failure";
        });
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw, "transaction re-raises the callback's exception");
    assert(
        DB.table("mb_posts").where("title", "=", "tx-c-rollback").count() == 0,
        "rolled-back insert left no row"
    );
    assert(DB.table("mb_posts").count() == 2, "earlier rows untouched by the rollback");
    println("  [PASS] test_transaction_commit_and_rollback");
}

fn test_pagination_cap() {
    let res = DB.table("mb_posts").paginate(1, 500);
    assert(res["per_page"] == 100, "per_page capped at 100");
    assert(res["total"] == 2, "total reflects the table");
    let tiny = DB.table("mb_posts").paginate(1, 0);
    assert(tiny["per_page"] == 1, "per_page floor at 1");
    let late = DB.table("mb_posts").paginate(99, 15);
    assert(late["data"].len() == 0, "page beyond the end is empty, not an error");
    println("  [PASS] test_pagination_cap");
}

fn main() {
    println("\n=== Running Model Layer Test Suite ===");
    setup_tables();
    test_model_created_events_fire_in_order();
    test_model_event_vetoes();
    test_eager_load_has_many();
    test_eager_load_belongs_to();
    test_transaction_commit_and_rollback();
    test_pagination_cap();
    teardown_tables();
    println("=== All Model Layer Tests Passed! ===\n");
    return 0;
}

// tests/test_modern_mvc.matrix
// Comprehensive Test Suite for Spid3rWeb Modern MVC Evolution (Phases 1-5)

import "framework/Web";
import "framework/Queue/Queue";

fn assert_eq(actual, expected, label: string) {
    let act = string(actual);
    let exp = string(expected);
    if act != exp {
        throw "FAIL [{label}]: expected '{exp}', got '{act}'";
    }
}

fn assert_true(cond: bool, label: string) {
    if not cond {
        throw "FAIL [" + label + "]: expected true, got false";
    }
}

fn main() {
    let app_env = env("APP_ENV").unwrap_or("").trim();
    if app_env == "" {
        env_set("APP_ENV", "testing");
    }
    println("\n=== Running Modern MVC & DX Test Suite ===");

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. Barrel Module, Direct Routing, Auto-Coercion & (req, res) Handlers

        let _ = req;
        return {
            "framework": "Spid3rWeb", "modern": true
        };
    });

        let _ = req;
        return "<h1>Hello Spid3r</h1>";
    });

        return res.status(201).json({
            "created": req.string("name", "anon")
        });

    .assert_ok()

    .assert_json_path("modern", true);

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

        return (req, next) => {

            res.header("x-onion-after", "completed");
            return res;
        };
    });

        return {
            "before": req.string("mw_before"), "has_req_id": req.header("x-request-id", "") != ""
        };

    .assert_json_path("has_req_id", true)

    let etag = r1.header("etag");
    assert_true(etag != "", "ETag header generated");
    assert_true(r1.header("x-request-id") != "", "X-Request-Id header generated");

    })

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    app.put("/spoofed-put", req => {
        "method": req.method, "orig": req.original_method
    });

        "method": req.method
    });

    .get("/read-signed")
    .assert_ok()
    .assert_json_path("prefs", "none");

    + "Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"caption\"\r\n\r\n"
    + "Profile Photo\r\n"
    + "--" + boundary + "\r\n"
    + "Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"avatar\"; filename=\"photo.png\"\r\n"

    + "@foreach(tag in tags)\n[{{ loop.iteration }}/{{ loop.count }}:{{ tag }}]@if(loop.last) (END)@endif\n@endforeach\n"
    + "<form>@csrf\n@method('PATCH')</form>\n"
    + "Shared: {{ app_title }}";

    let rendered = views().render_string(tpl, {

        "tags": ["fast", "clean"],

    });

    assert_true(rendered.contains("Grade: B"), "SpiderBD @elif with >= and ==");
    assert_true(rendered.contains("[1/2:fast]"), "SpiderBD loop.iteration and loop.count");
    assert_true(rendered.contains("[2/2:clean] (END)"), "SpiderBD loop.last conditional");
    assert_true(
        rendered.contains("name=\"_csrf\" value=\"tok_123\""),
        "SpiderBD @csrf directive"
    );
    assert_true(
        rendered.contains("name=\"_method\" value=\"PATCH\""),
        "SpiderBD @method directive"
    );
    assert_true(rendered.contains("Shared: Spid3rCloud"), "SpiderBD global share()");

    println("  [PASS] test_spiderbd_v2_directives_and_expressions");

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 10. Spid3r v1.1.1 Features: Streaming/SSE Responses & Graceful Queue Daemon
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    let st_res = send_stream(["chunk-1", "chunk-2"], "text/plain; charset=utf-8", 200);
    let st_http = st_res.to_http();
    assert_eq(st_http["status"], 200, "stream response status is 200");
    assert_true(st_http.has("stream"), "stream response emits http_stream map");
    assert_eq(
        st_http["headers"]["content-type"],
        "text/plain; charset=utf-8",
        "stream content-type preserved"
    );

    let sse_res = send_sse(fn() {
        return null;
    });
    let sse_http = sse_res.to_http();
    assert_true(sse_http.has("stream"), "SSE response emits http_stream map");
    assert_eq(
        sse_http["headers"]["content-type"],
        "text/event-stream; charset=utf-8",
        "SSE content-type"
    );
    assert_eq(sse_http["headers"]["cache-control"], "no-cache", "SSE cache-control");

    request_shutdown();
    let daemon_done = Queue.work_daemon(10);
    reset_shutdown();
    assert_eq(daemon_done, 0, "work_daemon exits immediately when shutdown_requested()");

    // Missing view must throw (visible HTTP 500 via ErrorRenderer) instead of
    // silently returning an invisible HTML comment with HTTP 200.
    let missing_threw = false;
    try {
        views().render("definitely_does_not_exist_xyz", {
        });
    } catch (ve) {
        missing_threw = string(ve).contains("not found");
    }
    assert_true(missing_threw, "missing BD view throws diagnostic error");

    println("  [PASS] test_v111_streaming_sse_and_graceful_daemon");

    db().close();
    println("=== All Modern MVC & DX Tests Passed! ===\n");
}

FILE: tests/test_multi_db_live.matrix

// tests/test_multi_db_live.matrix
// Live integration tests for PostgreSQL / MySQL / MariaDB drivers.
//
// These tests connect to REAL servers configured via environment variables.
// If a server is unreachable, that driver's tests print [SKIP] — the suite
// still passes so CI machines without database servers remain green.
//
// Environment:
//   SPID3R_DB_DRIVER=postgres | mysql | mariadb   (or run per-driver)
//   SPID3R_PG_HOST/PORT/USER/PASSWORD/DATABASE
//   SPID3R_MYSQL_HOST/PORT/USER/PASSWORD/DATABASE

import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Database/DatabaseManager";
import "framework/Database/Connection";

let passed_count = 0;
let skipped_count = 0;

fn report_pass(name: string) {
    passed_count = passed_count + 1;
    println("  [PASS] " + name);
}

fn report_skip(name: string, reason: string) {
    skipped_count = skipped_count + 1;
    println("  [SKIP] " + name + " (" + reason + ")");
}

// Open a connection for a driver, returning null when unreachable.
fn try_open(driver: string) {
    let mgr = new DatabaseManager();
    if mgr.driver_name() != driver {
        println(
            "  [FAIL] driver resolution mismatch: wanted " + driver + " got " + mgr.driver_name()
        );
        return null;
    }
    try {
        return mgr.connection();
    } catch (_e) {
        return null;
    }
}

fn teardown_probe(conn) {
    try {
        conn.statement("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS spid3rweb_driver_probe");
    } catch (_e) {
    }
}

// Full lifecycle per driver: DDL -> INSERT(last_id) -> SELECT -> UPDATE(changes)
// -> COUNT -> escaping round-trip -> DELETE(changes)
fn test_driver_lifecycle(driver: string) {
    let conn = try_open(driver);
    if conn == null {
        report_skip("lifecycle/" + driver, "server unreachable");
        return;
    }

    teardown_probe(conn);

    // 1. DDL via statement()
    let ddl = "";
    if driver == "postgres" {
        ddl = "CREATE TABLE spid3rweb_driver_probe (id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY, name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, payload VARCHAR(255), views INT DEFAULT 0, created_at TEXT)";
    } else {
        ddl = "CREATE TABLE spid3rweb_driver_probe (id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY, name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, payload VARCHAR(255), views INT DEFAULT 0, created_at VARCHAR(32))";
    }
    conn.statement(ddl);
    report_pass("ddl/" + driver);

    // 2. INSERT via run() -> last_id
    let r1 = conn.run(
        "INSERT INTO spid3rweb_driver_probe (name, payload, views, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
        ["first row",
        "it's a test",
        5,
        string(unix_time())]
    );
    assert(r1.has("last_id"), "insert result has last_id (" + driver + ")");
    let first_id = int(r1["last_id"]);
    assert(first_id > 0, "insert returns positive last_id (" + driver + ")");
    report_pass("insert-last-id/{driver} (id={first_id})");

    // 3. Escaping round-trip: nasty string goes in and comes back intact
    let nasty = "Robert'); DROP TABLE spid3rweb_driver_probe;-- \\ \"quotes\" \n newline";
    let r2 = conn.run(
        "INSERT INTO spid3rweb_driver_probe (name, payload, views, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
        ["nasty row",
        nasty,
        0,
        string(unix_time())]
    );
    let nasty_id = int(r2["last_id"]);
    assert(nasty_id > 0, "nasty insert succeeded (injection neutralized) (" + driver + ")");

    // Table must still exist and contain exactly the rows we inserted
    let rows = conn.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM spid3rweb_driver_probe");
    assert(int(rows[0]["c"]) == 2, "table survived injection attempt (" + driver + ")");

    let back = conn.query("SELECT payload FROM spid3rweb_driver_probe WHERE id = ?", [nasty_id]);
    assert(back.len() == 1, "nasty row readable (" + driver + ")");
    assert(
        back[0]["payload"] == nasty,
        "nasty payload round-trips byte-identical (" + driver + ")"
    );
    report_pass("escaping-roundtrip/" + driver);

    // 4. Query with interpolated params
    let found = conn.query(
        "SELECT id, name, views FROM spid3rweb_driver_probe WHERE name = ?",
        ["first row"]
    );
    assert(found.len() == 1, "param query finds row (" + driver + ")");
    assert(found[0]["name"] == "first row", "param query row content (" + driver + ")");
    assert(int(found[0]["views"]) == 5, "int column typed correctly (" + driver + ")");
    report_pass("param-query/" + driver);

    // 5. UPDATE via run() -> changes
    let r3 = conn.run(
        "UPDATE spid3rweb_driver_probe SET views = ? WHERE name = ?",
        [99,
        "first row"]
    );
    assert(r3.has("changes"), "update result has changes (" + driver + ")");
    assert(int(r3["changes"]) >= 1, "update reports changed rows (" + driver + ")");
    let check = conn.query(
        "SELECT views FROM spid3rweb_driver_probe WHERE name = ?",
        ["first row"]
    );
    assert(int(check[0]["views"]) == 99, "update persisted (" + driver + ")");
    report_pass("update-changes/" + driver);

    // 6. DELETE via run() -> changes
    let r4 = conn.run("DELETE FROM spid3rweb_driver_probe WHERE id = ?", [nasty_id]);
    assert(int(r4["changes"]) == 1, "delete reports 1 changed row (" + driver + ")");
    let remaining = conn.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM spid3rweb_driver_probe");
    assert(int(remaining[0]["c"]) == 1, "delete persisted (" + driver + ")");
    report_pass("delete-changes/" + driver);

    teardown_probe(conn);
    conn.close();
}

// QueryBuilder integration against a live server (fresh DatabaseManager).
fn test_driver_query_builder(driver: string) {
    let mgr = new DatabaseManager();
    if mgr.driver_name() != driver {
        report_skip("query-builder/" + driver, "server unreachable");
        return;
    }

    let conn = null;
    try {
        conn = mgr.connection();
    } catch (_e) {
        report_skip("query-builder/" + driver, "server unreachable");
        return;
    }

    let ddl = "";
    if driver == "postgres" {
        ddl = "CREATE TABLE spid3rweb_driver_qb (id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY, title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, status VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT 'draft', created_at TEXT)";
    } else {
        ddl = "CREATE TABLE spid3rweb_driver_qb (id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY, title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, status VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT 'draft', created_at VARCHAR(32))";
    }
    conn.statement("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS spid3rweb_driver_qb");
    conn.statement(ddl);

    // QueryBuilder.insert -> last id
    let id1 = mgr.table("spid3rweb_driver_qb").insert({
        "title": "alpha", "status": "published", "created_at": string(unix_time())
    });
    assert(id1 > 0, "qb insert last_id (" + driver + ")");
    let id2 = mgr.table("spid3rweb_driver_qb").insert({
        "title": "beta's \"quoted\"",
        "status": "draft",
        "created_at": string(unix_time())
    });
    assert(id2 > id1, "qb second insert id increments (" + driver + ")");

    // where + first
    let row = mgr.table("spid3rweb_driver_qb").where("title", "=", "beta's \"quoted\"").first();
    assert(row != null, "qb where finds escaped title (" + driver + ")");
    assert(row["status"] == "draft", "qb row content (" + driver + ")");

    // orderBy + limit + get
    let rows = mgr.table("spid3rweb_driver_qb").orderBy("id", "DESC").limit(1).get();
    assert(
        rows.len() == 1 and rows[0]["title"] == "beta's \"quoted\"",
        "qb orderBy+limit (" + driver + ")"
    );

    // count with where
    let c = mgr.table("spid3rweb_driver_qb").where("status", "=", "published").count();
    assert(c == 1, "qb count (" + driver + ")");

    // update through QueryBuilder
    let changes = mgr.table("spid3rweb_driver_qb").where("id", "=", id1).update({
        "status": "archived"
    });
    assert(int(changes) >= 1, "qb update changes (" + driver + ")");

    // select specific columns
    let sel = mgr.table("spid3rweb_driver_qb").select(["id", "title"]).where("id", "=", id2).first();
    assert(
        sel.has("title") and not sel.has("status"),
        "qb select columns (" + driver + ")"
    );

    // delete through QueryBuilder
    let deleted = mgr.table("spid3rweb_driver_qb").where("id", "=", id2).delete();
    assert(int(deleted) == 1, "qb delete (" + driver + ")");

    conn.statement("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS spid3rweb_driver_qb");
    report_pass("query-builder/" + driver);
}

// Migrations table DDL compiles and applies on this driver.
fn test_driver_migrations_table(driver: string) {
    let conn = try_open(driver);
    if conn == null {
        report_skip("migrations-table/" + driver, "server unreachable");
        return;
    }
    // Same DDL the MigrationRunner uses per dialect
    let ddl = "";
    if driver == "postgres" {
        ddl = "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS migrations_test_probe (id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY, migration TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, batch INTEGER NOT NULL, applied_at TEXT NOT NULL)";
    } else {
        ddl = "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS migrations_test_probe (id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY, migration VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL UNIQUE, batch INT NOT NULL, applied_at VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL)";
    }
    conn.statement(ddl);
    conn.statement(
        "INSERT INTO migrations_test_probe (migration, batch, applied_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
        ["001_test",
        1,
        string(unix_time())]
    );
    let rows = conn.query(
        "SELECT migration, batch FROM migrations_test_probe WHERE migration = ?",
        ["001_test"]
    );
    assert(
        rows.len() == 1 and int(rows[0]["batch"]) == 1,
        "migrations table insert/select (" + driver + ")"
    );
    conn.statement("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS migrations_test_probe");
    conn.close();
    report_pass("migrations-table/" + driver);
}

fn main() {
    println("\n=== Running Multi-DB Live Integration Suite ===");
    println("Detecting available servers via SPID3R_DB_DRIVER...\n");

    let driver = "sqlite";
    let d = env("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER");
    if d.is_some() {
        let raw = string(d.unwrap()).trim().lower();
        if raw == "postgres" or raw == "postgresql" or raw == "pg" {
            driver = "postgres";
        }
        elif raw == "mysql" {
            driver = "mysql";
        }
        elif raw == "mariadb" {
            driver = "mariadb";
        }
    }

    if driver == "sqlite" {
        println("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER not set to a server driver — nothing to test live.");
        println(
            "Run with: SPID3R_DB_DRIVER=postgres|mysql|mariadb + SPID3R_PG_* / SPID3R_MYSQL_* env vars"
        );
        println("=== Live Multi-DB Suite Finished (0 run / 0 skipped) ===\n");
        return 0;
    }

    println("Target driver: " + driver + "\n");

    test_driver_lifecycle(driver);
    test_driver_query_builder(driver);
    test_driver_migrations_table(driver);

    println("");
    println(
        "=== Live Multi-DB Suite Finished ({passed_count} passed / {skipped_count} skipped) ==="
    );
    println("");
    if passed_count == 0 and skipped_count > 0 {
        println("WARNING: all tests skipped — server may be down but env vars were set.");
        return 1;
    }
    return 0;
}

// tests/test_phase0_safety.matrix
// Regression suite for Phase 0 (Safety) fixes:
//   B1 - Multiple Set-Cookie headers emitted individually (never comma-joined)
//   B2 - Queue optimistic CAS reclaims stale reserved_at jobs after retry_after
//   S1 - APP_ENV defaults to "production" with debug=false; APP_DEBUG overrides
//   S2 - Unified APP_KEY / SPID3R_APP_KEY resolution; fail-closed in production

import "http";
import "framework/Web";
import "framework/Foundation/Application";
import "framework/Http/Request";
import "framework/Http/Response";
import "framework/Foundation/Config";
import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Queue/Queue";
import "framework/Auth/Jwt";

fn assert_eq(actual, expected, label: string) {
    let act = string(actual);
    let exp = string(expected);
    if act != exp {
        throw "FAIL [{label}]: expected '{exp}', got '{act}'";
    }
}

fn assert_true(cond: bool, label: string) {
    if not cond {
        throw "FAIL [" + label + "]: expected true, got false";
    }
}

fn wait_until(cond_fn, timeout_ms: int) -> bool {
    let waited = 0;
    while waited < timeout_ms {
        if cond_fn() {
            return true;
        }
        sleep_ms(20);
        waited = waited + 20;
    }
    return cond_fn();
}

fn read_opt_env(name: string) -> string {
    let v = env(name);
    if v.is_some() {
        return string(v.unwrap());
    }
    return "";
}

fn test_multi_cookie_headers_and_wire() {
    let app = web();
    app.router.clear();

    app.get("/two-cookies", (req, res) => {
        let _ = req;
        return res.cookie("c1", "v1").cookie("c2", "v2").json({
            "ok": true
        });
    });

    app.get("/cookie-and-session", (req, res) => {
        req.session().put("x", 42);
        return res.cookie("theme", "dark").json({
            "saved": true
        });
    });

    app.get("/wire-cookies", (req, res) => {
        let _ = req;
        return res.cookie("w1", "one").cookie("w2", "two").text("wire-ok");
    });

    let client = app.test_client();

    // 1. Response.cookie() x2 produces two distinct Set-Cookie headers and preserves CORS on JSON
    let r1 = client.get("/two-cookies").assert_ok();
    let sc1 = r1.header_values("set-cookie");
    assert_eq(sc1.len(), 2, "two-cookies emits 2 set-cookie headers");
    assert_true(sc1[0].starts_with("c1=v1;"), "first set-cookie is c1=v1");
    assert_true(sc1[1].starts_with("c2=v2;"), "second set-cookie is c2=v2");
    assert_true(not sc1[0].contains(", c2="), "first set-cookie is not comma-joined");
    r1.assert_header("access-control-allow-origin", "*");

    // 2. Response cookie + SessionStore cookie emit two distinct Set-Cookie headers
    let r2 = client.get("/cookie-and-session").assert_ok();
    let sc2 = r2.header_values("set-cookie");
    assert_eq(sc2.len(), 2, "cookie + session emits 2 set-cookie headers");
    let has_theme = false;
    let has_sid = false;
    for h in sc2 {
        if h.starts_with("theme=dark;") {
            has_theme = true;
        }
        if h.starts_with("spid3r_sid=sid_") {
            has_sid = true;
        }
        assert_true(not h.contains(", spid3r_sid="), "session cookie is not comma-joined");
    }
    assert_true(has_theme, "theme=dark Set-Cookie present");
    assert_true(has_sid, "spid3r_sid Set-Cookie present");

    // 3. Real TCP HTTP/1.1 wire test: verify two separate "Set-Cookie:" lines on the socket
    let lr = tcp_listen("127.0.0.1:0");
    assert_true(lr.is_ok(), "tcp_listen binds ephemeral port");
    let listener = lr.unwrap();
    let addr = string(tcp_local_addr(listener).unwrap());
    let wire_app = app;
    let srv_stats = {
        "served": -1
    };
    spawn fn() {
        srv_stats["served"] = serve_listener(listener, fn(raw_req) {
            return wire_app.handle_request(raw_req);
        }, {
            "watch_signals": false, "access_log": false, "keep_alive": false
        }, 1);
        return 0;
    };

    let cr = tcp_connect(addr);
    assert_true(cr.is_ok(), "tcp_connect connects to server");
    let conn = cr.unwrap();
    tcp_set_timeout(conn, 5000);
    let wr = tcp_write(
        conn,
        "GET /wire-cookies HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n"
    );
    assert_true(wr.is_ok(), "tcp_write sends HTTP request");

    let raw_resp = "";
    while true {
        let rr = tcp_read(conn, 4096);
        if rr.is_err() {
            break;
        }
        let chunk = string(rr.unwrap());
        if chunk == "" {
            break;
        }
        raw_resp = raw_resp + chunk;
    }
    tcp_close(conn);
    assert_true(wait_until(fn() {
        return srv_stats["served"] == 1;
    }, 3000), "wire server served 1 connection");
    tcp_close(listener);

    let sc_lines = [];
    for line in raw_resp.split("\r\n") {
        if line.lower().starts_with("set-cookie:") {
            sc_lines.push(line);
        }
    }
    assert_eq(sc_lines.len(), 2, "HTTP/1.1 wire response contains 2 Set-Cookie header lines");
    assert_true(sc_lines[0].contains("w1=one;"), "wire line 1 has w1=one");
    assert_true(sc_lines[1].contains("w2=two;"), "wire line 2 has w2=two");
    assert_true(raw_resp.contains("wire-ok"), "wire body received");

    println("  [PASS] test_multi_cookie_headers_and_wire");
}

fn test_queue_stale_reservation_reclaim() {
    let q = "phase0-queue-test";
    DB.table("jobs").where("queue", "=", q).delete();

    Queue.push("log.message", {
        "message": "stale test"
    }, q);
    assert_eq(Queue.size(q), 1, "initial queue size is 1");

    let first = Queue.pop(q);
    assert_true(first != null, "first pop reserves job");
    assert_eq(int(first["attempts"]), 1, "first pop increments attempts to 1");
    assert_true(Queue.pop(q) == null, "active reserved job is not immediately re-popped");
    assert_eq(Queue.size(q), 0, "reserved job does not count in available size");

    // Simulate worker crash: backdate reserved_at well past retry_after (default 90s)
    let stale_ts = int(unix_time()) - 300;
    DB.table("jobs").where("id", "=", first["id"]).update({
        "reserved_at": stale_ts
    });

    assert_eq(Queue.size(q), 1, "stale reserved job counts as available again");
    let reclaimed = Queue.pop(q);
    assert_true(reclaimed != null, "stale reserved job is reclaimed by pop()");
    assert_eq(int(reclaimed["id"]), int(first["id"]), "reclaimed job has same id");
    assert_eq(int(reclaimed["attempts"]), 2, "reclaimed job increments attempts to 2");
    assert_true(Queue.pop(q) == null, "reclaimed job cannot be double-popped immediately");

    DB.table("jobs").where("queue", "=", q).delete();
    println("  [PASS] test_queue_stale_reservation_reclaim");
}

fn test_production_defaults_and_app_key() {
    let orig_env = read_opt_env("APP_ENV");
    let orig_debug = read_opt_env("APP_DEBUG");
    let orig_key = read_opt_env("APP_KEY");
    let orig_spid3r_key = read_opt_env("SPID3R_APP_KEY");
    let orig_jwt = read_opt_env("JWT_SECRET");
    let orig_cfg_env = Config.get("app.env", "");
    let orig_cfg_key = Config.get("app.key", "");

    try {
        // S1: Unset APP_ENV/APP_DEBUG defaults to production with debug=false
        env_set("APP_ENV", "");
        env_set("APP_DEBUG", "");
        Config.set("app.env", "");
        assert_eq(resolve_app_env(), "production", "unset APP_ENV defaults to production");
        assert_true(is_production(), "is_production() true by default");
        assert_true(not resolve_app_debug(), "debug defaults to false in production");
        let prod_app = new Application();
        assert_eq(prod_app.env, "production", "Application.env defaults to production");
        assert_true(not prod_app.debug, "Application.debug defaults to false");

        // Development env turns debug=true unless APP_DEBUG overrides
        env_set("APP_ENV", "development");
        assert_true(resolve_app_debug(), "debug true in development");
        assert_true(new Application().debug, "Application.debug true in development");
        env_set("APP_DEBUG", "false");
        assert_true(not resolve_app_debug(), "APP_DEBUG=false overrides development");
        env_set("APP_ENV", "production");
        env_set("APP_DEBUG", "true");
        assert_true(resolve_app_debug(), "APP_DEBUG=true overrides production");
        env_set("APP_DEBUG", "");

        // S2: Production requires strong non-default APP_KEY / SPID3R_APP_KEY
        env_set("APP_ENV", "production");
        env_set("APP_KEY", "");
        env_set("SPID3R_APP_KEY", "");
        env_set("JWT_SECRET", "");
        Config.set("app.key", "");

        let threw_cfg = false;
        try {
            assert_app_key_configured();
        } catch (_e) {
            threw_cfg = true;
        }
        assert_true(threw_cfg, "assert_app_key_configured throws without key in production");

        let threw_app = false;
        try {
            prod_app.check_production_ready();
        } catch (_e) {
            threw_app = true;
        }
        assert_true(
            threw_app,
            "Application.check_production_ready throws without key in production"
        );

        let threw_cookie = false;
        try {
            response("").signed_cookie("k", "v");
        } catch (_e) {
            threw_cookie = true;
        }
        assert_true(threw_cookie, "signed_cookie throws without key in production");

        let threw_jwt = false;
        try {
            Jwt.sign({
                "id": 1
            });
        } catch (_e) {
            threw_jwt = true;
        }
        assert_true(threw_jwt, "Jwt.sign throws without key in production");

        // Known placeholder or short key must be rejected in production
        for bad_key in ["spid3rweb-default-cookie-secret-key", "short"] {
            env_set("APP_KEY", bad_key);
            let rejected = false;
            try {
                assert_app_key_configured();
            } catch (_e) {
                rejected = true;
            }
            assert_true(rejected, "production rejects insecure key: " + bad_key);
        }

        // Legacy SPID3R_APP_KEY alias works seamlessly across cookies & JWT in production
        env_set("APP_KEY", "");
        env_set("SPID3R_APP_KEY", "hex:0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef");
        assert_app_key_configured();
        prod_app.check_production_ready();

        let res_sc = response("").signed_cookie("prefs", "v_prod");
        let http_sc = res_sc.to_http();
        let raw_pair = string(http_sc["headers"]["set-cookie"]).split(";")[0];
        let signed_val = raw_pair.replace("prefs=", "");
        let probe_req = new Request({
            "method": "GET", "path": "/", "headers": {
                "cookie": "prefs=" + signed_val
            }
            , "body": ""
        });
        assert_eq(
            probe_req.signed_cookie("prefs", ""),
            "v_prod",
            "signed_cookie verifies with SPID3R_APP_KEY in production"
        );

        let tok = Jwt.sign({
            "id": 99
        });
        let claims = Jwt.verify(tok);
        assert_true(
            claims != null and int(claims["id"]) == 99,
            "JWT round-trips with SPID3R_APP_KEY in production"
        );

        // Testing mode uses ephemeral random key (never the old hardcoded string)
        env_set("APP_ENV", "testing");
        env_set("APP_KEY", "");
        env_set("SPID3R_APP_KEY", "");
        let eph = resolve_app_key();
        assert_true(
            eph != "" and eph != "spid3rweb-default-cookie-secret-key",
            "ephemeral dev/test key is non-empty and not hardcoded"
        );
        let eph_res = response("").signed_cookie("dev", "ok").to_http();
        let eph_val = string(eph_res["headers"]["set-cookie"]).split(";")[0].replace("dev=", "");
        let eph_req = new Request({
            "method": "GET", "path": "/", "headers": {
                "cookie": "dev=" + eph_val
            }
            , "body": ""
        });
        assert_eq(
            eph_req.signed_cookie("dev", ""),
            "ok",
            "ephemeral key round-trips signed cookies in testing"
        );
        let eph_tok = Jwt.sign({
            "sub": "tester"
        });
        let eph_claims = Jwt.verify(eph_tok);
        assert_true(
            eph_claims != null and eph_claims["sub"] == "tester",
            "ephemeral key round-trips JWT in testing"
        );
    } finally {
        env_set("APP_ENV", orig_env);
        env_set("APP_DEBUG", orig_debug);
        env_set("APP_KEY", orig_key);
        env_set("SPID3R_APP_KEY", orig_spid3r_key);
        env_set("JWT_SECRET", orig_jwt);
        Config.set("app.env", orig_cfg_env);
        Config.set("app.key", orig_cfg_key);
    }

    println("  [PASS] test_production_defaults_and_app_key");
}

fn main() {
    let app_env = env("APP_ENV");
    if app_env.is_none() or string(app_env.unwrap()).trim() == "" {
        env_set("APP_ENV", "testing");
    }
    println("\n=== Running Phase 0 Safety Regression Suite ===");
    test_multi_cookie_headers_and_wire();
    test_queue_stale_reservation_reclaim();
    test_production_defaults_and_app_key();
    DB.close();
    println("=== All Phase 0 Safety Tests Passed! ===\n");
    return 0;
}
