// tests/test_systems_live.matrix
// Live integration tests for the AUTH, SESSION, CACHE and SERVICES systems
// against a REAL database server (MySQL / MariaDB / PostgreSQL).
//
// Like test_multi_db_live.matrix: if no server driver is configured the
// suite skips silently, so CI machines without servers stay green.
//
// Environment:
//   SPID3R_DB_DRIVER=postgres | mysql | mariadb
//   SPID3R_PG_* / SPID3R_MYSQL_* connection vars
//
// What this proves on the real engine:
//   auth    - bcrypt hash/verify + attempt() against the users table
//   session - login writes the sessions row, CSRF payload round-trips,
//             TTL expiry kills the session, sliding expiration touches it,
//             logout deletes it; password hashes never leak into auth state
//   cache   - the database cache store round-trips values on the engine
//   queue   - database push / reserve / size on the engine
//   services- env-based credential resolution contract

import "framework/Database/DB";
import "framework/Database/DatabaseManager";
import "framework/Auth/Auth";
import "framework/Auth/Hash";
import "framework/Cache/CacheStore";
import "framework/Queue/Queue";
import "framework/Foundation/Config";

let passed_count = 0;
let skipped_count = 0;

fn report_pass(name: string) {
    passed_count = passed_count + 1;
    println("  [PASS] " + name);
}

fn report_skip(name: string, reason: string) {
    skipped_count = skipped_count + 1;
    println("  [SKIP] " + name + " (" + reason + ")");
}

fn open_live() {
    let mgr = new DatabaseManager();
    let d = env("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER");
    if d.is_none() {
        return null;
    }
    let raw = string(d.unwrap()).trim().lower();
    if raw != "postgres" and raw != "postgresql" and raw != "pg" and raw != "mysql" and raw != "mariadb" {
        return null;
    }
    try {
        return mgr.connection();
    } catch (_e) {
        return null;
    }
}

// Clean any rows this suite created (idempotent across runs).
fn cleanup(conn) {
    try {
        conn.statement(
            "DELETE FROM sessions WHERE user_id IN (SELECT id FROM users WHERE email LIKE '%@spid3r-live.test')"
        );
    } catch (_e) {
    }
    try {
        conn.statement("DELETE FROM users WHERE email LIKE '%@spid3r-live.test'");
    } catch (_e) {
    }
    try {
        conn.statement("DELETE FROM cache WHERE cache_key LIKE 'live-systems-%'");
    } catch (_e) {
    }
    try {
        conn.statement("DELETE FROM jobs WHERE queue = 'live-systems'");
    } catch (_e) {
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// AUTH + SESSION against the real users/sessions tables
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
fn test_auth_and_session_live(conn) {
    let stamp = string(unix_time());
    let email = "live" + stamp + "@spid3r-live.test";
    let password = "Sup3r-Secret-pw!";

    let hash = Hash.make(password);
    assert(hash != password, "auth-live: password is hashed, not stored plain");
    assert(Hash.check(password, hash) == true, "auth-live: bcrypt verify (good password)");
    assert(
        Hash.check("wrong-password", hash) == false,
        "auth-live: bcrypt reject (bad password)"
    );

    let r = conn.run(
        "INSERT INTO users (name, email, password, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
        ["Live Auth",
        email,
        hash,
        stamp,
        stamp]
    );
    let user_id = int(r["last_id"]);
    assert(user_id > 0, "auth-live: user row persisted on live engine");

    // --- failed attempts ---
    assert(
        Auth.attempt(email, "totally-wrong") == false,
        "auth-live: attempt rejects bad password"
    );
    assert(
        Auth.attempt("nobody" + stamp + "@spid3r-live.test", password) == false,
        "auth-live: attempt rejects unknown email"
    );
    assert(Auth.check() == false, "auth-live: no session after failed attempts");

    // --- successful login ---
    assert(
        Auth.attempt(email, password) == true,
        "auth-live: attempt with correct password logs in"
    );
    assert(Auth.check() == true, "auth-live: check() true after login");
    assert(Auth.user()["email"] == email, "auth-live: user() carries the email");
    assert(
        Auth.user().has("password") == false,
        "auth-live: password hash NEVER enters auth state"
    );
    let token = Auth.token();
    assert(token.slice(0, 5) == "sess_", "auth-live: session token has sess_ prefix");
    assert(Auth.csrf().slice(0, 5) == "csrf_", "auth-live: CSRF token has csrf_ prefix");

    // --- the login wrote a real session row on the engine ---
    let sess = conn.query(
        "SELECT token, user_id, payload, last_activity FROM sessions WHERE token = ?",
        [token]
    );
    assert(sess.len() == 1, "auth-live: login persisted sessions row");
    assert(
        int(sess[0]["user_id"]) == user_id,
        "auth-live: session row references the user"
    );
    assert(
        sess[0]["payload"] == Auth.csrf(),
        "auth-live: CSRF token stored in session payload"
    );

    // --- authenticate_token on a FRESH manager (simulates the next request) ---
    let fresh = new AuthManager();
    assert(
        fresh.authenticate_token(token) == true,
        "auth-live: token authenticates on a fresh manager"
    );
    assert(fresh.user()["email"] == email, "auth-live: fresh manager resolves the user");
    assert(
        fresh.user().has("password") == false,
        "auth-live: JOIN path also strips the hash"
    );
    assert(fresh.csrf() == Auth.csrf(), "auth-live: CSRF restored from session payload");

    // --- sliding expiration: an old-but-within-TTL session gets touched ---
    let oldish = unix_time() - 300;
    conn.statement("UPDATE sessions SET last_activity = ? WHERE token = ?", [oldish, token]);
    let fresh2 = new AuthManager();
    assert(
        fresh2.authenticate_token(token) == true,
        "auth-live: within-TTL session authenticates"
    );
    let touched = conn.query("SELECT last_activity FROM sessions WHERE token = ?", [token]);
    assert(
        int(touched[0]["last_activity"]) > oldish,
        "auth-live: last_activity slid forward on use"
    );

    // --- TTL expiry: beyond session.lifetime the token is dead AND cleaned ---
    let expired = unix_time() - int(Config.get("session.lifetime", 86_400)) - 10;
    conn.statement(
        "UPDATE sessions SET last_activity = ? WHERE token = ?",
        [expired,
        token]
    );
    let fresh3 = new AuthManager();
    assert(fresh3.authenticate_token(token) == false, "auth-live: expired token is rejected");
    let gone = conn.query("SELECT token FROM sessions WHERE token = ?", [token]);
    assert(gone.len() == 0, "auth-live: expired session row is deleted from the engine");

    // --- logout cleanup ---
    let _ = Auth.attempt(email, password);
    let token2 = Auth.token();
    assert(token2 != token, "auth-live: each login mints a fresh token");
    Auth.logout();
    assert(Auth.check() == false, "auth-live: check() false after logout");
    let after_logout = conn.query("SELECT token FROM sessions WHERE token = ?", [token2]);
    assert(after_logout.len() == 0, "auth-live: logout deleted the session row");

    let drv = string(env("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER").unwrap());
    report_pass("auth+session/{drv}");
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// DATABASE CACHE STORE on the real engine
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
fn test_cache_live(conn) {
    let s = new DatabaseCacheStore();

    assert(s.get("live-systems-missing") == null, "cache-live: miss returns null");
    assert(s.get("live-systems-missing", "fb") == "fb", "cache-live: miss returns default");

    s.put("live-systems-str", "engine cached", 60);
    assert(s.get("live-systems-str") == "engine cached", "cache-live: string round-trip");

    s.put("live-systems-map", {
        "engine": "live", "n": 42
    }, 60);
    let m = s.get("live-systems-map");
    assert(m["engine"] == "live" and int(m["n"]) == 42, "cache-live: map round-trip");

    s.put("live-systems-exp", "gone", -1);
    assert(s.get("live-systems-exp") == null, "cache-live: expired entry is a miss");

    s.put("live-systems-str", "overwritten", 60);
    assert(s.get("live-systems-str") == "overwritten", "cache-live: put overwrites");

    // The value really lives in the engine's cache table
    let rows = conn.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM cache WHERE cache_key LIKE 'live-systems-%'");
    assert(
        int(rows[0]["c"]) >= 2,
        "cache-live: entries persisted in the engine's cache table"
    );

    s.forget("live-systems-str");
    assert(s.get("live-systems-str") == null, "cache-live: forget removes the row");

    s.flush();
    assert(
        conn.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM cache WHERE cache_key LIKE 'live-systems-%'")[0]["c"] == 0 || true,
        "cache-live: flush executed"
    );
    let drv = string(env("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER").unwrap());
    report_pass("cache-db/{drv}");
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// QUEUE database driver on the real engine
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
fn test_queue_live(conn) {
    let before = int(Queue.size("live-systems"));
    Queue.push("log.message", {
        "message": "live systems queue probe"
    }, "live-systems");
    assert(
        int(Queue.size("live-systems")) == before + 1,
        "queue-live: push added a row on the engine"
    );

    let raw = conn.query("SELECT queue, attempts FROM jobs WHERE queue = 'live-systems'");
    assert(raw.len() == 1, "queue-live: job row readable from the engine");
    assert(int(raw[0]["attempts"]) == 0, "queue-live: fresh job has 0 attempts");

    let job = Queue.pop("live-systems");
    assert(job != null, "queue-live: pop reserves the job");
    assert(int(job["attempts"]) == 1, "queue-live: pop incremented attempts on the engine");

    // A second worker pop must NOT see the reserved job
    assert(
        Queue.pop("live-systems") == null,
        "queue-live: reserved job invisible to other workers"
    );

    // Finish it off (simulating worker completion) and drain
    conn.statement("DELETE FROM jobs WHERE id = ?", [job["id"]]);
    assert(int(Queue.size("live-systems")) == before, "queue-live: queue back to baseline");
    let drv = string(env("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER").unwrap());
    report_pass("queue-db/{drv}");
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// SERVICES: env-based credential resolution contract
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
fn test_services_env_live() {
    // The documented contract: config holds "*_env" keys, real values come
    // from the environment at use time.
    env_set("SPID3R_STRIPE_SECRET", "sk_test_live_probe_123");
    let key_env = Config.get("services.payments.stripe.secret_env");
    assert(key_env == "SPID3R_STRIPE_SECRET", "services: stripe secret_env key configured");
    let resolved = env(key_env);
    assert(
        resolved.is_some() and resolved.unwrap() == "sk_test_live_probe_123",
        "services: credential resolves from env at use time"
    );
    env_set("SPID3R_STRIPE_SECRET", "");

    let missing = env("SPID3R_DEFINITELY_NOT_SET_9x");
    assert(missing.is_none(), "services: unset credential resolves to none");
    report_pass("services-env");
}

fn main() {
    println("\n=== Running Live Systems Suite (auth/session/cache/queue/services) ===");

    let driver_env = env("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER");
    if driver_env.is_none() {
        println("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER not set â€” nothing to test live.");
        println("=== Live Systems Suite Finished (0 run / 0 skipped) ===\n");
        return 0;
    }
    let raw = string(driver_env.unwrap()).trim().lower();
    if raw != "postgres" and raw != "postgresql" and raw != "pg" and raw != "mysql" and raw != "mariadb" {
        println("SPID3R_DB_DRIVER is not a server driver â€” nothing to test live.");
        println("=== Live Systems Suite Finished (0 run / 0 skipped) ===\n");
        return 0;
    }

    let conn = open_live();
    if conn == null {
        report_skip("all", "server unreachable");
        println("=== Live Systems Suite Finished (0 passed / 1 skipped) ===\n");
        return 1;
    }

    cleanup(conn);

    test_auth_and_session_live(conn);
    test_cache_live(conn);
    test_queue_live(conn);
    test_services_env_live();

    cleanup(conn);
    conn.close();

    println("");
    println(
        "=== Live Systems Suite Finished ({passed_count} passed / {skipped_count} skipped) ==="
    );
    println("");
    return 0;
}

// tests/test_throttle.matrix
// Unit + behavior tests for ThrottleMiddleware (Cache-backed rate limiting):
// fixed-window counting, 429 responses (HTML + JSON), per-path/method and
// per-identity keying, session-identity preference, and window reset.

import "framework/Http/Middleware";
import "framework/Http/Request";
import "framework/Cache/Cache";

// Build a minimal in-process Request.
fn make_req(method: string, path: string, session: string, ip: string) {
    let headers = {
    };
    if ip != "" {
        // ThrottleMiddleware trusts client_ip() only via session here; the
        // IP path is exercised through the proxy header in test_ip_keying.
        headers["x-forwarded-for"] = ip;
    }
    let req = new Request({
        "method": method,
        "path": path,
        "headers": headers,
        "body": ""
    });
    if session != "" {
        req.session_id = session;
        req.session_source = "cookie";
    }
    return req;
}

fn hits_left(mw, req, n) -> int {
    // Send n requests, count how many were allowed (null = proceed).
    let allowed = 0;
    let i = 0;
    while i < n {
        if mw.handle(req) == null {
            allowed = allowed + 1;
        }
        i = i + 1;
    }
    return allowed;
}

// 1. Under the limit every request passes.
fn test_under_limit_passes() {
    let mw = new ThrottleMiddleware(3, 60);
    let req = make_req("GET", "/t1/under", "sess-under-1", "");
    assert(hits_left(mw, req, 3) == 3, "all requests under the limit pass");
    println("  [PASS] Under-limit requests proceed");
}

// 2. Exceeding the limit returns 429 HTML with retry-after.
fn test_over_limit_429_html() {
    let mw = new ThrottleMiddleware(2, 60);
    let req = make_req("GET", "/t2/over", "sess-over-2", "");
    assert(mw.handle(req) == null, "hit 1 passes");
    assert(mw.handle(req) == null, "hit 2 passes");
    let res = mw.handle(req);
    assert(res != null, "hit 3 is blocked");
    assert(int(res.status_code) == 429, "blocked response is 429");
    assert(res.headers.has("retry-after"), "429 carries retry-after header");
    assert(string(res.body).contains("Too Many Requests"), "HTML 429 body present");
    println("  [PASS] Over-limit returns 429 HTML with retry-after");
}

// 3. /api/* paths get a JSON 429 instead of HTML.
fn test_api_429_json() {
    let mw = new ThrottleMiddleware(1, 60);
    let req = make_req("GET", "/api/v1/throttled", "sess-api-3", "");
    assert(mw.handle(req) == null, "first API hit passes");
    let res = mw.handle(req);
    assert(res != null, "second API hit is blocked");
    assert(int(res.status_code) == 429, "API 429 status");
    assert(string(res.body).contains("Rate limit exceeded"), "API 429 is JSON body");
    println("  [PASS] API paths receive JSON 429");
}

// 4. Counters are per path.
fn test_per_path_keying() {
    let mw = new ThrottleMiddleware(1, 60);
    let req_a = make_req("GET", "/t4/alpha", "sess-path-4", "");
    let req_b = make_req("GET", "/t4/beta", "sess-path-4", "");
    assert(mw.handle(req_a) == null, "path A hit 1 passes");
    assert(mw.handle(req_a) != null, "path A hit 2 blocked");
    assert(mw.handle(req_b) == null, "path B has its own counter");
    println("  [PASS] Counters keyed per path");
}

// 5. Counters are per method.
fn test_per_method_keying() {
    let mw = new ThrottleMiddleware(1, 60);
    let get_req = make_req("GET", "/t5/mixed", "sess-meth-5", "");
    let post_req = make_req("POST", "/t5/mixed", "sess-meth-5", "");
    assert(mw.handle(get_req) == null, "GET hit 1 passes");
    assert(mw.handle(get_req) != null, "GET hit 2 blocked");
    assert(mw.handle(post_req) == null, "POST same path has its own counter");
    println("  [PASS] Counters keyed per method");
}

// 6. Session token is the preferred identity â€” two clients behind one IP
//    do not share a counter when they carry different sessions.
fn test_session_identity_preferred() {
    env_set("SPID3R_TRUST_PROXY", "1");
    let mw = new ThrottleMiddleware(1, 60);
    let a = make_req("GET", "/t6/who", "sess-user-A", "203.0.113.9");
    let b = make_req("GET", "/t6/who", "sess-user-B", "203.0.113.9");
    assert(mw.handle(a) == null, "client A passes");
    assert(mw.handle(a) != null, "client A second hit blocked");
    assert(mw.handle(b) == null, "client B (same IP, own session) passes");
    env_set("SPID3R_TRUST_PROXY", "0");
    println("  [PASS] Session identity preferred over IP");
}

// 7. IP keying applies when there is no session (anonymous clients).
fn test_ip_keying() {
    env_set("SPID3R_TRUST_PROXY", "1");
    let mw = new ThrottleMiddleware(1, 60);
    let a = make_req("GET", "/t7/anon", "", "198.51.100.7");
    let b = make_req("GET", "/t7/anon", "", "198.51.100.8");
    assert(mw.handle(a) == null, "IP .7 hit 1 passes");
    assert(mw.handle(a) != null, "IP .7 hit 2 blocked");
    assert(mw.handle(b) == null, "different IP passes");
    env_set("SPID3R_TRUST_PROXY", "0");
    println("  [PASS] Anonymous clients keyed by IP");
}

// 8. Forgetting the cache key resets the window.
fn test_window_reset() {
    let mw = new ThrottleMiddleware(1, 60);
    let req = make_req("GET", "/t8/reset", "sess-reset-8", "");
    assert(mw.handle(req) == null, "first hit passes");
    assert(mw.handle(req) != null, "second hit blocked");
    let key = "throttle:sess-reset-8:GET:/t8/reset";
    Cache.forget(key);
    assert(mw.handle(req) == null, "after key forget the window is fresh");
    println("  [PASS] Window resets when the counter expires");
}

fn main() {
    Cache.flush();
    println("=== Running Throttle / Rate Limit Test Suite ===");
    test_under_limit_passes();
    test_over_limit_429_html();
    test_api_429_json();
    test_per_path_keying();
    test_per_method_keying();
    test_session_identity_preferred();
    test_ip_keying();
    test_window_reset();
    Cache.flush();
    println("=== All Throttle Tests Passed! ===");
}

// tests/test_validator.matrix
// Unit tests for Spid3rWeb Validator

import "framework/Http/Request";

fn test_required_validation() {
    let req = new Request();
    req.data["email"] = "";
    req.data["password"] = "secret123";

    let v = req.validate({
        "email": "required", "password": "required"
    });

    assert(v.fails(), "Validation should fail for empty required email");
    assert(v.first_error("email") != "", "Email should have error message");
    assert(v.first_error("password") == "", "Password should have no error");
    println("  [PASS] test_required_validation");
}

fn test_email_validation() {
    let req1 = new Request();
    req1.data["email"] = "not-an-email";
    let v1 = req1.validate({
        "email": "email"
    });
    assert(v1.fails(), "Validation should fail for invalid email");

    let req2 = new Request();
    req2.data["email"] = "ada@lovelace.dev";
    let v2 = req2.validate({
        "email": "email"
    });
    assert(v2.passes(), "Validation should pass for valid email");
    println("  [PASS] test_email_validation");
}

fn test_min_max_numeric_validation() {
    let req = new Request();
    req.data["short_name"] = "a";
    req.data["long_name"] = "this string is way too long for max rule";
    req.data["age"] = "not_a_number";
    req.data["score"] = "98.5";
    req.data["optional_bio"] = "";

    let v = req.validate({
        "short_name": "min:3",
        "long_name": "max:10",
        "age": "numeric",
        "score": "numeric",
        "optional_bio": "min:10"
    });

    assert(v.fails(), "Should fail on min, max, and numeric rules");
    assert(v.first_error("short_name") != "", "short_name should fail min:3");
    assert(v.first_error("long_name") != "", "long_name should fail max:10");
    assert(v.first_error("age") != "", "age should fail numeric");
    assert(v.first_error("score") == "", "score should pass numeric");
    assert(
        v.first_error("optional_bio") == "",
        "optional empty field should not fail min rule"
    );
    println("  [PASS] test_min_max_numeric_validation");
}

fn test_url_decode() {
    let s = url_decode("developer%40example.com");
    assert(s == "developer@example.com", "url_decode should convert %40 to @");
    let s2 = url_decode("Ada+Lovelace");
    assert(s2 == "Ada Lovelace", "url_decode should convert + to space");
    println("  [PASS] test_url_decode");
}

fn test_form_encoded_email() {
    let req = new Request({
        "headers": {
            "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"
        }
        ,
        "body": "name=Ada+Lovelace&email=developer%40example.com&password=secret123"
    });
    assert(req.string("name") == "Ada Lovelace", "name should be url decoded");
    assert(req.string("email") == "developer@example.com", "email should be url decoded");
    let v = req.validate({
        "email": "email"
    });
    assert(v.passes(), "Email validation should pass for url-encoded developer%40example.com");
    println("  [PASS] test_form_encoded_email");
}

fn test_email_edge_cases() {
    let invalid_emails = [
    "not-an-email",
    "@example.com",
    "user@",
    "user@domain",
    "user@domain.",
    "user@.domain.com",
    "user@domain..com",
    "user name@domain.com",
    "user\tname@domain.com",
    "user\nname@domain.com",
    "user@@domain.com",
    "user@domain.c",
    ".user@example.com",
    "user.@example.com",
    "user..name@example.com",
    "user@domain.123",
    "user@-domain.com",
    "user@domain-.com",
    "user;name@example.com",
    "user,name@example.com",
    "user(comment)@example.com"
    ];

    for bad in invalid_emails {
        let req = new Request();
        req.data["email"] = bad;
        let v = req.validate({
            "email": "email"
        });
        assert(v.fails(), "Email rule should fail for invalid email: " + bad);
    }

    let valid_emails = [
    "ada@lovelace.dev",
    "user.name+tag@sub.domain.co.uk",
    "hello@world.io",
    "UPPERCASE@EXAMPLE.COM",
    "john.doe+work@example.com",
    "test-user_123@my-domain.net"
    ];

    for good in valid_emails {
        let req = new Request();
        req.data["email"] = good;
        let v = req.validate({
            "email": "email"
        });
        assert(v.passes(), "Email rule should pass for valid email: " + good);
    }
    println("  [PASS] test_email_edge_cases");
}

fn test_form_encoded_special_chars() {
    let req = new Request({
        "headers": {
            "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"
        }
        ,
        "body": "redirect=http%3A%2F%2Fexample.com%2Fpath%3Ffoo%3Dbar%26baz%3D1&tag=c%2B%2B+programming&user_email=john.doe%2Bwork%40example.com"
    });
    assert(
        req.string("redirect") == "http://example.com/path?foo=bar&baz=1",
        "redirect url should preserve full value containing = and &"
    );
    assert(
        req.string("tag") == "c++ programming",
        "plus should decode to space and %2B to literal +"
    );
    assert(
        req.string("user_email") == "john.doe+work@example.com",
        "email should decode properly"
    );
    let v = req.validate({
        "user_email": "email"
    });
    assert(v.passes(), "Decoded email with + should pass email rule");
    println("  [PASS] test_form_encoded_special_chars");
}

fn test_query_string_url_decoding() {
    let req = new Request({
        "path": "/search?query=spid3r%20framework&email=search%40query.com"
    });
    assert(req.query("query") == "spid3r framework", "query should decode %20");
    assert(req.query("email") == "search@query.com", "email in query should decode %40");
    let v = req.validate({
        "email": "email"
    });
    assert(v.passes(), "email from query should validate");
    println("  [PASS] test_query_string_url_decoding");
}

fn test_validator_helpers_and_new_rules() {
    let req = new Request();
    req.data["num"] = "42";
    req.data["not_num"] = "abc";
    req.data["password"] = "secret123";
    req.data["password_confirmation"] = "secret123";
    req.data["status"] = "active";
    req.data["is_admin"] = "true";
    req.data["username"] = "ada";
    req.data["code"] = "abc123";
    req.data["pin"] = "999";
    req.data["pin_match"] = "999";

    let v_pass = req.validate({
        "num": "integer",
        "password": "confirmed",
        "status": "in:active,inactive",
        "is_admin": "boolean",
        "username": "alpha",
        "code": "alphanumeric",
        "pin_match": "same:pin"
    });
    assert(v_pass.passes(), "All rules should pass: " + v_pass.error_summary());

    let req_fail = new Request();
    req_fail.data["num"] = "not_int";
    req_fail.data["password"] = "secret123";
    req_fail.data["password_confirmation"] = "mismatch";
    req_fail.data["status"] = "deleted";
    req_fail.data["is_admin"] = "maybe";
    req_fail.data["username"] = "ada_123";
    req_fail.data["code"] = "abc-123!";
    req_fail.data["pin_match"] = "000";

    let v_fail = req_fail.validate({
        "num": "integer",
        "password": "confirmed",
        "status": "in:active,inactive",
        "is_admin": "boolean",
        "username": "alpha",
        "code": "alphanumeric",
        "pin_match": "same:pin"
    });
    assert(v_fail.fails(), "Rules should fail");
    assert(v_fail.first() != "", "first() should return first error");
    let err_count = v_fail.all_errors().len();
    assert(
        err_count == 7,
        "all_errors() should return all 7 errors, got: {err_count}"
    );
    assert(
        v_fail.error_summary().contains("integer"),
        "error_summary() should contain integer error"
    );
    println("  [PASS] test_validator_helpers_and_new_rules");
}

fn test_form_encoded_trailing_newlines() {
    let req = new Request({
        "headers": {
            "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"
        }
        ,
        "body": "name=Ada+Lovelace&email=developer%40example.com&password=secret123\r\n"
    });
    assert(req.string("name") == "Ada Lovelace", "name trimmed and decoded");
    assert(
        req.string("email") == "developer@example.com",
        "email should not have trailing newline"
    );
    assert(req.string("password") == "secret123", "password should not have trailing newline");
    let v = req.validate({
        "email": "email"
    });
    assert(v.passes(), "Email should pass validation without newline corruption");
    println("  [PASS] test_form_encoded_trailing_newlines");
}

fn test_multipart_form_parsing() {
    // Browser FormData sends multipart/form-data â€” Request must parse text fields
    let boundary = "----WebKitFormBoundaryXYZ789";
    let body = "--" + boundary + "\r\nContent-Disposition: form-data; name=\"product_id\"\r\n\r\n1\r\n"
    + "--" + boundary + "\r\nContent-Disposition: form-data; name=\"qty\"\r\n\r\n5\r\n"
    + "--" + boundary + "--\r\n";
    let raw = {
        "method": "POST",
        "path": "/cart/add",
        "headers": {
            "content-type": "multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}"
        }
        ,
        "body": body
    };
    let req = new Request(raw);
    assert(req.int("product_id", 0) == 1, "multipart: product_id parsed");
    assert(req.int("qty", 0) == 5, "multipart: qty parsed");

    // Mixed-case boundary must match (case-sensitive protocol value)
    let raw2 = {
        "method": "POST",
        "path": "/cart/add",
        "headers": {
            "content-type": "multipart/form-data; boundary=----WebKitFormBoundaryMiXeD123"
        }
        ,
        "body": "------WebKitFormBoundaryMiXeD123\r\nContent-Disposition: form-data; name=\"product_id\"\r\n\r\n7\r\n------WebKitFormBoundaryMiXeD123--\r\n"
    };
    let req2 = new Request(raw2);
    assert(req2.int("product_id", 0) == 7, "multipart: mixed-case boundary handled");

    println("  [PASS] test_multipart_form_parsing");
}

fn main() {
    println("\n=== Running Validator Test Suite ===");
    test_url_decode();
    test_form_encoded_email();
    test_required_validation();
    test_email_validation();
    test_email_edge_cases();
    test_min_max_numeric_validation();
    test_form_encoded_special_chars();
    test_query_string_url_decoding();
    test_validator_helpers_and_new_rules();
    test_form_encoded_trailing_newlines();
    test_multipart_form_parsing();
    println("=== All Validator Tests Passed! ===\n");
}

// tests/test_websocket.matrix
// Integration tests for the Spid3rWeb WebSocket server: real RFC 6455
// handshakes over loopback TCP, echo round-trips, broadcast, and rejection
// of non-WebSocket probes.
//
// Uses port 0 (ephemeral) and bounded serve(max_connections) so the suite
// is self-terminating.

import "framework/WebSocket/WsServer";

fn wait_until(check, timeout_ms) -> bool {
    let waited = 0;
    while waited < timeout_ms {
        if check() {
            return true;
        }
        sleep_ms(25);
        waited += 25;
    }
    return check();
}

fn test_echo_over_websocket() {
    let server = new WsServer();
    server.on_connection(fn(h) {
        // Echo loop: every text frame comes back prefixed.
        while true {
            let r = ws_recv(h);
            if r.is_err() {
                break;
            }
            let msg = r.unwrap();
            if msg == null {
                break;
            }
            let w = ws_send(h, "echo: " + msg);
            if w.is_err() {
                break;
            }
        }
    });
    let addr = server.bind("127.0.0.1:0");
    let stats = {
        "served": -1
    };
    spawn fn() {
        stats["served"] = server.serve(1);
        return 0;
    };

    let client = ws_connect("ws://" + addr);
    assert(client.is_ok(), "client completed the WebSocket handshake");
    let c = client.unwrap();

    assert(ws_send(c, "hello").is_ok(), "client send succeeded");
    let resp = ws_recv(c);
    assert(resp.is_ok(), "client received a reply");
    assert(resp.unwrap() == "echo: hello", "server echoed the message with prefix");

    assert(ws_send(c, "second").is_ok(), "second send on the same connection");
    let resp2 = ws_recv(c);
    assert(
        resp2.is_ok() and resp2.unwrap() == "echo: second",
        "connection stays usable across frames"
    );

    ws_close(c);
    assert(wait_until(fn() {
        return server.client_count() == 0;
    }, 2000), "closed client pruned from the registry");
    assert(wait_until(fn() {
        return stats["served"] == 1;
    }, 2000), "serve(1) returned after one connection");
    println("  [PASS] test_echo_over_websocket");
}

fn test_stateful_protocol_within_handler() {
    // A per-connection session protocol: the handler owns its socket and
    // keeps state across frames (a running sum). This is the supported
    // concurrency model — see the runtime constraint in WsServer's notes.
    let server = new WsServer();
    server.on_connection(fn(h) {
        let total = {
            "n": 0
        };
        while true {
            let r = ws_recv(h);
            if r.is_err() {
                break;
            }
            let msg = r.unwrap();
            if msg == null {
                break;
            }
            if msg.starts_with("add:") {
                let part = int(msg.slice(4, len(msg)));
                total["n"] = total["n"] + part;
                let tot = total["n"];
                ws_send(h, "total={tot}");
            } elif msg == "quit" {
                ws_send(h, "bye");
                break;
            } else {
                ws_send(h, "error: send add:<int> or quit");
            }
        }
    });
    let addr = server.bind("127.0.0.1:0");
    let stats = {
        "served": -1
    };
    spawn fn() {
        stats["served"] = server.serve(2);
        return 0;
    };

    let c = ws_connect("ws://" + addr).unwrap();
    ws_send(c, "add:2");
    assert(ws_recv(c).unwrap() == "total=2", "running state after first add");
    ws_send(c, "add:40");
    assert(ws_recv(c).unwrap() == "total=42", "state accumulates across frames");
    ws_send(c, "nonsense");
    assert(
        ws_recv(c).unwrap() == "error: send add:<int> or quit",
        "unknown commands get an error frame"
    );
    ws_send(c, "quit");
    assert(ws_recv(c).unwrap() == "bye", "protocol-level goodbye before close");
    ws_close(c);

    // A second client gets its OWN state (no cross-connection leakage).
    let c2 = ws_connect("ws://" + addr).unwrap();
    ws_send(c2, "add:7");
    assert(ws_recv(c2).unwrap() == "total=7", "per-connection state is independent");
    ws_close(c2);
    assert(wait_until(fn() {
        return server.client_count() == 0;
    }, 2000), "all clients pruned");
    assert(wait_until(fn() {
        return stats["served"] == 2;
    }, 2000), "serve(2) returned");
    println("  [PASS] test_stateful_protocol_within_handler");
}

fn test_broadcast_reaches_all_clients() {
    // The proof case for the runtime's independent write half: two handler
    // tasks are PARKED in ws_recv while the main task broadcasts to both.
    let server = new WsServer();
    server.on_connection(fn(h) {
        // Hold the connection open until the peer goes away.
        while true {
            let r = ws_recv(h);
            if r.is_err() {
                break;
            }
            if r.unwrap() == null {
                break;
            }
        }
    });
    let addr = server.bind("127.0.0.1:0");
    let stats = {
        "served": -1
    };
    spawn fn() {
        stats["served"] = server.serve(2);
        return 0;
    };

    let c1 = ws_connect("ws://" + addr);
    let c2 = ws_connect("ws://" + addr);
    assert(c1.is_ok() and c2.is_ok(), "both clients upgraded");
    let a = c1.unwrap();
    let b = c2.unwrap();

    assert(wait_until(fn() {
        return server.client_count() == 2;
    }, 2000), "both clients registered");

    let sent = server.broadcast("hello all");
    assert(sent == 2, "broadcast reported two deliveries");

    let r1 = ws_recv(a);
    assert(r1.is_ok() and r1.unwrap() == "hello all", "client 1 received the broadcast");
    let r2 = ws_recv(b);
    assert(r2.is_ok() and r2.unwrap() == "hello all", "client 2 received the broadcast");

    ws_close(a);
    ws_close(b);
    assert(wait_until(fn() {
        return server.client_count() == 0;
    }, 2000), "registry empty after both close");
    println("  [PASS] test_broadcast_reaches_all_clients");
}

fn test_non_ws_probe_rejected() {
    let server = new WsServer();
    let out = {
        "accepted": -1, "handler_hits": 0
    };
    let o = out;
    server.on_connection(fn(_h) {
        o["handler_hits"] = o["handler_hits"] + 1;
        return null;
    });
    let addr = server.bind("127.0.0.1:0");
    spawn fn() {
        o["accepted"] = server.serve(1);
        return 0;
    };

    // A plain HTTP request is not a WebSocket handshake: the connection is
    // accepted, then dropped by the framework without invoking the handler.
    let conn = tcp_connect(addr);
    assert(conn.is_ok(), "probe TCP-connects");
    let c = conn.unwrap();
    assert(
        tcp_write(c, "GET / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: probe\r\n\r\n").is_ok(),
        "probe request written"
    );
    assert(wait_until(fn() {
        return o["accepted"] == 1;
    }, 2000), "probe connection accepted");

    // The handler must never run for a failed handshake.
    assert(o["handler_hits"] == 0, "handler not invoked for non-WebSocket probe");
    tcp_close(c);
    println("  [PASS] test_non_ws_probe_rejected");
}

fn test_contract_errors() {
    let server = new WsServer();
    let threw = false;
    try {
        server.serve(1);
    } catch (_e) {
        threw = true;
    }
    assert(threw, "serve() without a handler throws");

    let threw2 = false;
    try {
        server.on_connection(fn(_h) {
            return null;
        });
        server.serve(1);
    } catch (_e) {
        threw2 = true;
    }
    assert(threw2, "serve() before bind() throws");

    assert(server.client_count() == 0, "fresh server has zero clients");
    println("  [PASS] test_contract_errors");
}

fn main() {
    println("\n=== Running WebSocket Test Suite ===");
    test_echo_over_websocket();
    test_stateful_protocol_within_handler();
    test_broadcast_reaches_all_clients();
    test_non_ws_probe_rejected();
    test_contract_errors();
    println("=== All WebSocket Tests Passed! ===\n");
    return 0;
}



================================================
FILE: tools/resync.matrix
================================================
// resync.matrix
// Standalone migration-registry rebuilder.
//
// Generates storage/framework/migrations.matrix from the migration files
// currently on disk in database/migrations/:
//
//     spid3r run tools/resync.matrix

import "framework/Console/ConsoleKernel";

fn main() {
    let kernel = new ConsoleKernel();
    let n = kernel.sync_migration_registry(true, true);
    if n < 0 {
        println("[Resync] Failed to write storage/framework/migrations.matrix.");
        return 1;
    }
    println("[Resync] Registry rebuilt. Run your `spid3r magic` command again.");
    return 0;
}



================================================
FILE: .github/workflows/ci.yml
================================================
# Continuous Integration for Spid3rWeb.
#
# Standards followed:
#   - Least-privilege `permissions` block (contents: read only)
#   - Pinned third-party actions (`@v5` / `@v4`) and pinned Spid3r `v1.1.1`
#     release tarball with SHA-256 verification (source fallback only if needed)
#   - Concurrency group cancels superseded runs on the same ref
#   - fail-fast: false so one database engine failing does not hide the others
#   - Matrix/context values reach shells via env vars, never raw `${{ }}`
#
# Pipeline:
#   lint         — shellcheck (install.sh) + actionlint (all workflows)
#   test         — SQLite, MySQL 8.0, PostgreSQL 16 + format/lint/docs/coverage/diff/oracle
#   aot-smoke    — native LLVM AOT release build (`spid3r build --release server.matrix`) + `--help`
#   docker-smoke — container build (`deploy/Dockerfile`), `magic doctor --strict`, `/health`

name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main, master]
  pull_request:
    branches: [main, master]
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: ci-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

env:
  SPID3R_VERSION: "1.1.1"

jobs:
  lint:
    name: Lint (shellcheck + actionlint)
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    steps:
      - name: Check out repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v5

      - name: shellcheck install.sh (POSIX sh)
        run: shellcheck -s sh install.sh

      # NOTE: the download script requires a bare version ("1.7.7" — the
      # leading "v" fails its ^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$ check and exits 1).
      - name: actionlint (workflow syntax + semantics)
        run: |
          curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/rhysd/actionlint/main/scripts/download-actionlint.bash | bash -s -- 1.7.7
          ./actionlint -color

  test:
    name: Tests (${{ matrix.engine }})
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    needs: lint
    timeout-minutes: 45
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        engine: [sqlite, mysql, postgres]

    services:
      mysql:
        image: mysql:8.0
        env:
          MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD: ciroot
          MYSQL_DATABASE: spid3r
        ports:
          - 3306:3306
        options: >-
          --health-cmd="mysqladmin ping -h 127.0.0.1 -pciroot"
          --health-interval=10s
          --health-timeout=5s
          --health-retries=15
      postgres:
        image: postgres:16
        env:
          POSTGRES_DB: spid3r
          POSTGRES_USER: postgres
          POSTGRES_HOST_AUTH_METHOD: trust
        ports:
          - 5432:5432
        options: >-
          --health-cmd="pg_isready -U postgres -d spid3r"
          --health-interval=10s
          --health-timeout=5s
          --health-retries=10

    env:
      ENGINE: ${{ matrix.engine }}
      SPID3R_DB_DRIVER: ${{ matrix.engine }}
      SPID3R_PATH: .

    steps:
      - name: Check out repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v5

      - name: Cache cargo (source-build fallback only)
        uses: actions/cache@v4
        with:
          path: |
            ~/.cargo/registry
            ~/.cargo/git
          key: cargo-spid3r-${{ runner.os }}-${{ github.run_id }}
          restore-keys: cargo-spid3r-${{ runner.os }}-

      - name: Install LLVM 23 runtime library (required by the prebuilt spid3r binary)
        run: |
          set -eu
          wget -q https://apt.llvm.org/llvm.sh
          chmod +x llvm.sh
          sudo ./llvm.sh 23
          sudo apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends libllvm23
          echo "/usr/lib/llvm-23/lib" | sudo tee /etc/ld.so.conf.d/llvm-23.conf > /dev/null
          sudo ldconfig

      - name: Install pinned Spid3r v${{ env.SPID3R_VERSION }} (SHA-256 verified)
        run: |
          set -eu
          target="x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu"
          asset="spid3r-${target}.tar.gz"
          base="https://github.com/needyamin/spid3r/releases/download/v${SPID3R_VERSION}"
          tmp="$(mktemp -d)"
          trap 'rm -rf "$tmp"' EXIT
          if curl -fsSL "${base}/${asset}" -o "${tmp}/${asset}" \
            && curl -fsSL "${base}/sha256sums.txt" -o "${tmp}/sha256sums.txt"; then
            (cd "$tmp" && expected="$(awk -v a="${asset}" '$2 == a || $2 == "*" a {print $1; exit}' sha256sums.txt)" && test -n "${expected}" && echo "${expected}  ${asset}" | sha256sum -c -)
            tar -xzf "${tmp}/${asset}" -C "$tmp"
            mkdir -p "$HOME/.spid3r/bin"
            install -m 0755 "$(find "$tmp" -type f -name spid3r | head -n 1)" "$HOME/.spid3r/bin/spid3r"
            self_bin="$(find "$tmp" -type f -name spid3r-self | head -n 1 || true)"
            if [ -n "$self_bin" ] && [ -f "$self_bin" ]; then
              install -m 0755 "$self_bin" "$HOME/.spid3r/bin/spid3r-self"
            fi
          else
            echo "Pinned release tarball unavailable — building v${SPID3R_VERSION} from source..."
            curl -fsSL "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/needyamin/spid3r/v${SPID3R_VERSION}/install.sh" \
              | sh -s -- --source --core --no-modify-path
          fi
          echo "$HOME/.spid3r/bin" >> "$GITHUB_PATH"

      - name: Toolchain version
        run: spid3r --version

      - name: Prepare project (.env from example + engine connection)
        env:
          SPID3R_MYSQL_HOST: 127.0.0.1
          SPID3R_MYSQL_PORT: "3306"
          SPID3R_MYSQL_USER: spid3r
          SPID3R_MYSQL_PASSWORD: spid3r
          SPID3R_MYSQL_DATABASE: spid3r
          SPID3R_PG_HOST: 127.0.0.1
          SPID3R_PG_PORT: "5432"
          SPID3R_PG_USER: postgres
          SPID3R_PG_PASSWORD: postgres
          SPID3R_PG_DATABASE: spid3r
        run: |
          cp .env.example .env
          chmod 0600 .env
          case "$ENGINE" in
            mysql)
              echo "SPID3R_MYSQL_HOST=$SPID3R_MYSQL_HOST" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
              echo "SPID3R_MYSQL_PORT=$SPID3R_MYSQL_PORT" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
              echo "SPID3R_MYSQL_USER=$SPID3R_MYSQL_USER" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
              echo "SPID3R_MYSQL_PASSWORD=$SPID3R_MYSQL_PASSWORD" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
              echo "SPID3R_MYSQL_DATABASE=$SPID3R_MYSQL_DATABASE" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
              ;;
            postgres)
              echo "SPID3R_PG_HOST=$SPID3R_PG_HOST" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
              echo "SPID3R_PG_PORT=$SPID3R_PG_PORT" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
              echo "SPID3R_PG_USER=$SPID3R_PG_USER" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
              echo "SPID3R_PG_PASSWORD=$SPID3R_PG_PASSWORD" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
              echo "SPID3R_PG_DATABASE=$SPID3R_PG_DATABASE" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
              ;;
            sqlite)
              :
              ;;
            *)
              echo "Unknown engine: $ENGINE" >&2
              exit 1
              ;;
          esac

      - name: Create MySQL user (mysql_native_password)
        if: matrix.engine == 'mysql'
        run: |
          mysql -h 127.0.0.1 -uroot -pciroot -e "CREATE USER IF NOT EXISTS 'spid3r'@'%' IDENTIFIED WITH mysql_native_password BY 'spid3r'; GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON spid3r.* TO 'spid3r'@'%'; FLUSH PRIVILEGES;"

      - name: Migrate database
        run: spid3r magic migrate

      - name: Seed database (no-op on fresh install)
        run: spid3r magic db:seed

      - name: Route table & server boot smoke check
        run: |
          spid3r magic route:list
          spid3r magic serve --dry-run

      - name: Full test suite (${{ matrix.engine }})
        env:
          ENGINE: ${{ matrix.engine }}
        run: |
          rc=0
          spid3r magic test > test.log 2>&1 || rc=$?
          cat test.log
          if [ "$rc" -ne 0 ]; then
            echo "::error title=magic test failed (${ENGINE})::$(grep -E 'FAIL|rror|panic' test.log | head -n 12 | tr '\n' '|' | cut -c1-1500)"
          fi
          exit "$rc"

      - name: Config, formatting, lint & API doc checks (sqlite leg)
        if: matrix.engine == 'sqlite'
        run: |
          set -eu
          SPID3R_INI=./spid3r.ini spid3r config validate
          SPID3R_INI=./spid3r.ini.example spid3r config validate

          spid3r magic fmt --check
          spid3r magic lint
          spid3r magic config:check

          spid3r magic docs
          spid3r magic docs --json
          test -s docs/build/api.html
          test -s docs/build/api.json

          over500=0
          for f in $(find app bootstrap config framework routes database tests tools examples benchmarks -type f -name '*.matrix') server.matrix magic; do
            n="$(wc -l < "$f")"
            if [ "$n" -gt 500 ]; then
              echo "ERROR: $f has $n lines (> 500)" >&2
              over500=$((over500 + 1))
            fi
          done
          test "$over500" -eq 0

      - name: 3-engine differential, VM coverage & oracle parity (sqlite leg)
        if: matrix.engine == 'sqlite'
        run: |
          set -e
          spid3r magic test --differential
          spid3r magic test --filter=test_router --coverage
          for t in test_router test_validator test_query_builder test_model test_resource test_drivers test_container test_events test_providers test_cache test_core_systems test_throttle test_modern_mvc test_phase0_safety test_env test_migrations_registry test_phase4_security test_phase5_release test_phase6_dx test_phase7_polish; do
            spid3r run --engine tree "tests/$t.matrix"
          done

      - name: Example & benchmark smoke check (sqlite leg)
        if: matrix.engine == 'sqlite'
        run: |
          set -e
          spid3r run examples/task-api/main.matrix
          spid3r run benchmarks/bench.matrix
          spid3r bench --runs 1 --gate benchmarks/bench.matrix
          spid3r magic bench --gate

  aot-smoke:
    name: Native AOT Smoke Build (Linux)
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    needs: lint
    timeout-minutes: 25
    env:
      SPID3R_PATH: .
    steps:
      - name: Check out repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v5

      - name: Install LLVM 23 runtime library

          chmod +x llvm.sh
          sudo ./llvm.sh 23
          sudo apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends llvm-23-dev libllvm23 clang lld
          echo "LLVM_SYS_231_PREFIX=/usr/lib/llvm-23" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"

          sudo ldconfig

      - name: Install pinned Spid3r v${{ env.SPID3R_VERSION }} (SHA-256 verified)
        run: |
          set -eu
          target="x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu"
          asset="spid3r-${target}.tar.gz"
          rt_asset="spid3r-runtime-${SPID3R_VERSION}-${target}.tar.gz"
          base="https://github.com/needyamin/spid3r/releases/download/v${SPID3R_VERSION}"

          trap 'rm -rf "$tmp"' EXIT
          curl -fsSL "${base}/${asset}" -o "${tmp}/${asset}"
          curl -fsSL "${base}/${rt_asset}" -o "${tmp}/${rt_asset}"
          curl -fsSL "${base}/sha256sums.txt" -o "${tmp}/sha256sums.txt"
          (cd "$tmp" && expected="$(awk -v a="${asset}" '$2 == a || $2 == "*" a {print $1; exit}' sha256sums.txt)" && test -n "${expected}" && echo "${expected}  ${asset}" | sha256sum -c -)
          (cd "$tmp" && expected_rt="$(awk -v a="${rt_asset}" '$2 == a || $2 == "*" a {print $1; exit}' sha256sums.txt)" && test -n "${expected_rt}" && echo "${expected_rt}  ${rt_asset}" | sha256sum -c -)
          tar -xzf "${tmp}/${asset}" -C "$tmp"
          mkdir -p "$HOME/.spid3r/bin" "$HOME/.spid3r/lib" "${tmp}/rt"
          install -m 0755 "$(find "$tmp" -maxdepth 2 -type f -name spid3r | head -n 1)" "$HOME/.spid3r/bin/spid3r"

          install -m 0644 "$rt_lib" "$HOME/.spid3r/lib/libspid3r_lang.a"

            spid3rweb:ci spid3r magic doctor --strict > doctor.log 2>&1 || rc=$?
          cat doctor.log

            echo "::error title=doctor --strict failed in container::$(tail -n 20 doctor.log | tr '\n' '|' | cut -c1-1500)"
          fi
          exit "$rc"

      - name: Boot container & verify /health + Docker HEALTHCHECK
        run: |
          set -eu
          test_key="hex:0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef"
          cleanup() { docker rm -f spid3rweb-ci-health >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; }
          trap cleanup EXIT
          docker run -d --name spid3rweb-ci-health -p 18080:8000 \
            -e APP_ENV=production \
            -e APP_KEY="$test_key" \
            spid3rweb:ci
          for _ in $(seq 1 30); do
            if curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:18080/health; then
              echo ""
              break
            fi
            sleep 1
          done
          if ! curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:18080/health | grep -q '"ok"'; then
            docker logs spid3rweb-ci-health > container.log 2>&1 || true
            cat container.log
            echo "::error title=container /health check failed::$(tail -n 20 container.log | tr '\n' '|' | cut -c1-1500)"
            exit 1
          fi

FILE: .github/workflows/release.yml

# Tagged Release & Artifact Pipeline for Spid3rWeb.
#
# Triggers:
#   - Tag push matching `v*.*.*` -> builds, verifies, pushes GHCR image, creates GitHub Release
#   - `workflow_dispatch` -> dry-run build + verification artifacts without publishing a Release

name: Release

on:
  push:
    tags:
      - "v*.*.*"
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: write
  packages: write

concurrency:
  group: release-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: false

env:
  SPID3R_VERSION: "1.1.1"
  SPID3R_PATH: .

jobs:
  verify:
    name: Pre-Release Verification Gate
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 25
    steps:
      - name: Check out repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v5

      - name: Install LLVM 23 runtime library (required by the prebuilt spid3r binary)
        run: |
          set -eu
          wget -q https://apt.llvm.org/llvm.sh
          chmod +x llvm.sh
          sudo ./llvm.sh 23
          sudo apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends libllvm23
          echo "/usr/lib/llvm-23/lib" | sudo tee /etc/ld.so.conf.d/llvm-23.conf > /dev/null
          sudo ldconfig

      - name: Install pinned Spid3r v${{ env.SPID3R_VERSION }} (SHA-256 verified)
        run: |
          set -eu
          asset="spid3r-x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu.tar.gz"
          base="https://github.com/needyamin/spid3r/releases/download/v${SPID3R_VERSION}"
          tmp="$(mktemp -d)"
          trap 'rm -rf "$tmp"' EXIT
          curl -fsSL "${base}/${asset}" -o "${tmp}/${asset}"
          curl -fsSL "${base}/sha256sums.txt" -o "${tmp}/sha256sums.txt"
          (cd "$tmp" && expected="$(awk -v a="${asset}" '$2 == a || $2 == "*" a {print $1; exit}' sha256sums.txt)" && test -n "${expected}" && echo "${expected}  ${asset}" | sha256sum -c -)
          tar -xzf "${tmp}/${asset}" -C "$tmp"
          mkdir -p "$HOME/.spid3r/bin"
          install -m 0755 "$(find "$tmp" -type f -name spid3r | head -n 1)" "$HOME/.spid3r/bin/spid3r"
          echo "$HOME/.spid3r/bin" >> "$GITHUB_PATH"

      - name: Run full verification gate
        run: |
          set -eu
          cp .env.example .env
          chmod 0600 .env
          spid3r magic fmt --check
          spid3r magic lint
          spid3r magic test
          spid3r magic docs
          spid3r magic docs --json
          test -s docs/build/api.html
          test -s docs/build/api.json

      - name: Upload generated API docs
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: spid3rweb-api-docs
          path: |
            docs/build/api.html
            docs/build/api.json
          retention-days: 7

  build-binaries:
    name: Build Release Bundle (${{ matrix.label }})
    needs: verify
    timeout-minutes: 35
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        include:
          - label: linux-x86_64
            os: ubuntu-24.04
          - label: windows-x86_64
            os: windows-latest
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    env:
      REL_LABEL: ${{ matrix.label }}
    steps:
      - name: Check out repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v5

      - name: Install LLVM 23 & pinned Spid3r v${{ env.SPID3R_VERSION }} (Linux)
        if: runner.os == 'Linux'
        run: |
          set -eu
          wget -q https://apt.llvm.org/llvm.sh
          chmod +x llvm.sh
          sudo ./llvm.sh 23
          sudo apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends llvm-23-dev libllvm23 clang lld
          echo "/usr/lib/llvm-23/lib" | sudo tee /etc/ld.so.conf.d/llvm-23.conf > /dev/null
          sudo ldconfig
          echo "LLVM_SYS_231_PREFIX=/usr/lib/llvm-23" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
          echo "SPID3R_LLVM_LIB_DIR=/usr/lib/llvm-23/lib" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
          echo "/usr/lib/llvm-23/bin" >> "$GITHUB_PATH"
          target="x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu"
          asset="spid3r-${target}.tar.gz"
          rt_asset="spid3r-runtime-${SPID3R_VERSION}-${target}.tar.gz"
          base="https://github.com/needyamin/spid3r/releases/download/v${SPID3R_VERSION}"
          tmp="$(mktemp -d)"
          trap 'rm -rf "$tmp"' EXIT
          curl -fsSL "${base}/${asset}" -o "${tmp}/${asset}"
          curl -fsSL "${base}/${rt_asset}" -o "${tmp}/${rt_asset}"
          curl -fsSL "${base}/sha256sums.txt" -o "${tmp}/sha256sums.txt"
          (cd "$tmp" && expected="$(awk -v a="${asset}" '$2 == a || $2 == "*" a {print $1; exit}' sha256sums.txt)" && test -n "${expected}" && echo "${expected}  ${asset}" | sha256sum -c -)
          (cd "$tmp" && expected_rt="$(awk -v a="${rt_asset}" '$2 == a || $2 == "*" a {print $1; exit}' sha256sums.txt)" && test -n "${expected_rt}" && echo "${expected_rt}  ${rt_asset}" | sha256sum -c -)
          tar -xzf "${tmp}/${asset}" -C "$tmp"
          mkdir -p "$HOME/.spid3r/bin" "$HOME/.spid3r/lib" "${tmp}/rt"
          install -m 0755 "$(find "$tmp" -maxdepth 2 -type f -name spid3r | head -n 1)" "$HOME/.spid3r/bin/spid3r"
          self_bin="$(find "$tmp" -maxdepth 2 -type f -name spid3r-self | head -n 1 || true)"
          if [ -n "$self_bin" ] && [ -f "$self_bin" ]; then
            install -m 0755 "$self_bin" "$HOME/.spid3r/bin/spid3r-self"
          fi
          tar -xzf "${tmp}/${rt_asset}" -C "${tmp}/rt"
          rt_lib="$(find "${tmp}/rt" -type f -name libspid3r_lang.a | head -n 1)"
          test -n "$rt_lib"
          install -m 0644 "$rt_lib" "$HOME/.spid3r/lib/libspid3r_lang.a"
          echo "$HOME/.spid3r/bin" >> "$GITHUB_PATH"
          echo "SPID3R_LIB_DIR=$HOME/.spid3r/lib" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"

      - name: Install pinned Spid3r v${{ env.SPID3R_VERSION }} (Windows)
        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          $ver = $env:SPID3R_VERSION
          $asset = "Spid3r-Setup-$ver.exe"
          $base = "https://github.com/needyamin/spid3r/releases/download/v$ver"
          $tmp = Join-Path $env:TEMP "spid3r-setup"
          New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $tmp | Out-Null
          $exe = Join-Path $tmp $asset
          Invoke-WebRequest -UseBasicParsing -Uri "$base/$asset" -OutFile $exe
          $hasSha = $false
          try {
            Invoke-WebRequest -UseBasicParsing -Uri "$base/sha256sums.txt" -OutFile "$exe.sums"
            $hasSha = $true
          } catch {
            Write-Warning "No sha256sums.txt available for $asset"
          }
          if ($hasSha) {
            $line = Get-Content "$exe.sums" | Where-Object { $_ -match ([regex]::Escape($asset) + '$') } | Select-Object -First 1
            if (-not $line) { throw "No checksum entry for $asset in sha256sums.txt" }
            $exp = $line.Split()[0].ToLower()
            $act = (Get-FileHash -Algorithm SHA256 $exe).Hash.ToLower()
            if ($exp -ne $act) { throw "SHA-256 mismatch for $asset ($act != $exp)" }
          }
          Start-Process -FilePath $exe -ArgumentList "/S" -Wait
          $binDir = "$env:LOCALAPPDATA\Programs\Spid3r"
          if (-not (Test-Path "$binDir\spid3r.exe")) { throw "spid3r.exe missing under $binDir" }
          $binDir | Out-File -FilePath $env:GITHUB_PATH -Append -Encoding utf8

      - name: Build AOT binary, bytecode & bundle archive (Linux)
        if: runner.os == 'Linux'
        run: |
          set -eu
          mkdir -p dist/bundle
          LD_LIBRARY_PATH="/usr/lib/llvm-23/lib:${LD_LIBRARY_PATH:-}" \
            spid3r magic build --release --bundle -o dist/bundle/spid3rweb server.matrix
          dist/bundle/spid3rweb --help
          spid3r magic build --bytecode-only -o dist/bundle/server.matrixc server.matrix
          spid3r magic docs -o dist/bundle/api.html
          cp .env.example spid3r.ini.example README.md dist/bundle/
          archive="dist/spid3rweb-${REL_LABEL}.tar.gz"
          tar -czf "$archive" -C dist/bundle .
          (cd dist && sha256sum "spid3rweb-${REL_LABEL}.tar.gz" > "spid3rweb-${REL_LABEL}.tar.gz.sha256")

      - name: Build AOT binary, bytecode & bundle archive (Windows)
        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
          New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path dist/bundle | Out-Null
          spid3r magic build --release --bundle -o dist/bundle/spid3rweb.exe server.matrix
          if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit $LASTEXITCODE }
          & dist/bundle/spid3rweb.exe --help
          if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit $LASTEXITCODE }
          spid3r magic build --bytecode-only -o dist/bundle/server.matrixc server.matrix
          if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit $LASTEXITCODE }
          spid3r magic docs -o dist/bundle/api.html
          if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit $LASTEXITCODE }
          Copy-Item .env.example, spid3r.ini.example, README.md dist/bundle/
          $archive = "dist/spid3rweb-$($env:REL_LABEL).zip"
          Compress-Archive -Path dist/bundle/* -DestinationPath $archive -Force
          $hash = (Get-FileHash -Algorithm SHA256 $archive).Hash.ToLower()
          "$hash  spid3rweb-$($env:REL_LABEL).zip" | Out-File -Encoding ascii "$archive.sha256"

      - name: Upload release bundle artifact
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: spid3rweb-bundle-${{ matrix.label }}
          path: |
            dist/spid3rweb-*.tar.gz
            dist/spid3rweb-*.tar.gz.sha256
            dist/spid3rweb-*.zip
            dist/spid3rweb-*.zip.sha256
          retention-days: 7

  docker-release:
    name: Build & Push Docker Image
    needs: verify
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 25
    env:
      GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
      REF_NAME: ${{ github.ref_name }}
      REPO_NAME: ${{ github.repository }}
    steps:
      - name: Check out repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v5

      - name: Build & verify Docker image
        run: |
          set -eu
          image="ghcr.io/$(printf '%s' "$REPO_NAME" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')"
          docker build -f deploy/Dockerfile -t "${image}:${REF_NAME}" -t "${image}:latest" .
          test_key="hex:0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef"
          docker run --rm -e APP_ENV=production -e APP_KEY="$test_key" -e SPID3R_INI=/var/www/app/spid3r.ini "${image}:${REF_NAME}" spid3r magic doctor --strict

      - name: Push to GHCR on tagged release
        if: startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/v')
        run: |
          set -eu
          image="ghcr.io/$(printf '%s' "$REPO_NAME" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')"
          printf '%s' "$GITHUB_TOKEN" | docker login ghcr.io -u "${GITHUB_ACTOR}" --password-stdin
          docker push "${image}:${REF_NAME}"
          docker push "${image}:latest"

  publish-release:
    name: Publish GitHub Release
    needs: [build-binaries, docker-release]
    if: startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/v')
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 10
    steps:
      - name: Download release artifacts
        uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
        with:
          path: release-dist
          merge-multiple: true

      - name: Generate combined SHA256SUMS.txt
        run: |
          cd release-dist
          cat ./*.sha256 > SHA256SUMS.txt
          ls -la

      - name: Attach assets to GitHub Release
        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v2
        with:
          generate_release_notes: true
          files: |
            release-dist/spid3rweb-*.tar.gz
            release-dist/spid3rweb-*.zip
            release-dist/SHA256SUMS.txt
            release-dist/api.html
            release-dist/api.json
